01重点

1
Biopharmaceuticals / Biodrugs 基础（Turbica + QC 课）

1.1 定义 & 分类（必背）

  • Biopharmaceuticals / Biodrugs definition
    Biopharmaceuticals are pharmaceuticals manufactured by biotechnology methods and clearly of biological origin, usually involving living organisms or their components.
    → 生物制品 / 生物药：用生物技术方法生产、明确来源于生物体系的药物，一般涉及活体或其活性成分。
  • Biodrug key features
    • Drug substance produced by or extracted from biological source
    • Well-characterized manufacturing & purification process
    • Characterization / QC requires combined physical, chemical and biological assays
    → 关键点：生物来源 + 工艺描述清楚 + 需要多种理化 / 生物方法表征和质控。
  • Main classes of biodrugs（按课件图记结构）
    1. Natural / extraction products
      • Vaccines, toxins, antibiotics
      • Enzymes, hormones, polyclonal antibodies
      • Blood-derived products
        → 自然 / 提取：疫苗、毒素、抗生素、酶激素、多克隆抗体、血源制品等。
    2. Recombinant / DNA technology
      • Recombinant proteins, therapeutic proteins
      • Monoclonal antibodies, recombinant vaccines
      • Nucleic acids: plasmids, oligonucleotides, DNA vaccines, gene therapies
        → 重组 / 基因工程：重组蛋白、单抗、重组疫苗、核酸药（质粒、寡核苷酸、DNA 疫苗、基因治疗）。
    3. Cell & tissue therapies
      • Somatic cell therapy, tissue-engineered products
        → 细胞与组织治疗：体细胞治疗、组织工程产品。
  • Origins / production systems
    1. Extraction from human/animal tissues & fluids
    2. Cell culture: prokaryotes (bacteria) & eukaryotes (yeast, fungi, insect, mammalian)
    3. Transgenic animals & plants
    4. Live organisms (e.g. larvae, eggs for vaccines)
      → 来源：提取 / 细胞培养 / 转基因动植物 / 活体系统。
2
Upstream & Downstream Process（以 EPO / Vaccine 为例）

2.1 通用流程（必背）

Upstream process (USP)

Cell line & plasmid construct

→ Cell banking (MCB/WCB)

→ Seed train

→ Scale-up in bioreactors (batch / fed-batch / perfusion)

→ Controlled culture (pH, T, DO, agitation, nutrients, feed strategy)

上游（USP）

细胞系 + 质粒构建

→ 建立细胞库（主细胞库 / 工作细胞库）

→ 种子扩增

→ 生物反应器放大培养（批 / 补料 / 灌流）

→ 控制培养条件（pH、温度、溶氧、搅拌、营养、补料策略）

Downstream process (DSP)

Harvest (centrifugation / filtration, recover supernatant or cells)

→ Clarification

→ Concentration

→ Purification (chromatography / filtration / precipitation)

→ Viral removal / inactivation (if needed)

→ Formulation

→ Sterile filtration

→ Filling / lyophilization

下游（DSP）

收获（离心 / 过滤，获得上清或细胞）

→ 澄清

→ 浓缩

→ 纯化（各类层析 / 过滤 / 沉淀）

→ 去除 / 灭活病毒（如需要）

→ 配制

→ 无菌过滤

→ 分装 / 冻干

问Where does DSP start?
答

Bioreactor end → Harvest / recovery of culture fluid (removal of cells / biomass) = start of DSP.

DSP 起点：

生物反应结束 → 从反应器收集培养液并去除细胞 / 生物量 = DSP 开始。

2.2 EPO 生产流程（case study 对应）

EPO process overview（Neorecormon 示例）Cell culture (engineered CHO cells) → Recovery of extracellular fluid → Sequence of chromatography steps → Formulation + fill & freeze-dry.→ EPO（CHO 分泌型）：细胞培养 → 上清收集 → 蓝色 Sepharose / 离子交换 / 反相 / HA 等层析 → 配制、分装、冻干。

Why no cell lysis for EPO?rEPO is secreted into extracellular medium by CHO cells, so no lysis step is needed; harvest = supernatant.→ 因为重组 EPO 为分泌型糖蛋白，直接在上清中，故不用裂解细胞。

3
QC of Therapeutic Proteins（Taverna & Smadja + mock Q3/Q4）

3.1 四大必控项目 = mock Q4 标准答案

4 main characteristics to be controlled

IdentityConfirm that the batch contains the correct therapeutic protein and structural variants.→ 身份：确认批次确实是目标蛋白（原始结构 + 相关变体）。

PurityBiopharmaceutical must be free from contaminating substances (host cell proteins & DNA, degraded forms, aggregates, process-related impurities…).→ 纯度：去除宿主蛋白 / DNA、降解产物、聚集体、工艺残留等。

Dosage (Content / Assay)Determine accurate concentration of active protein in drug substance / product to ensure correct dose delivered.→ 含量：准确测定活性成分浓度，保证给药剂量正确。

StabilityCheck how the product reacts to stress (heat, pH changes, shaking, light) to decide the storage conditions and how long it remains stable.→ 稳定性：考察对温度、pH、剪切、光等的敏感性，确定贮存条件和有效期。

👉 建议背成一句：

“Identity–Purity–Dosage–Stability / 身份-纯度-含量-稳定性”。

3.2 主要杂质类别（Cervarix Q5 也考）

Categories of impurities (protein products & vaccines)

Product-related impuritiesDegraded protein (fragmented, oxidized, deamidated), misfolded variants, aggregates.→ 与产物相关：降解片段、氧化 / 去酰胺变体、构象异常、聚集体。

Host cell–related impuritiesHost cell proteins (HCP), host cell DNA/RNA, lipids, endotoxins.→ 宿主相关：宿主蛋白、宿主 DNA/RNA、脂类、内毒素等。

Process-related impuritiesChromatography leachable, solvents, salts, detergents, media components.→ 工艺相关：层析配体脱落、溶剂、盐、表面活性剂、培养基成分残留。

Microbial contaminantsBacteria, fungi, adventitious viruses, mycoplasma.→ 微生物污染：细菌、真菌、外源病毒、支原体。

用于 Vaccines 2022 Q5：

“Any micro-organisms; product-related; process-related; host cell–related” —— 就是这 4 类。

3.3 关键 QC 技术 & 对应用途（可用于简答）

可以背一个表式：

① Identity（身份）

Peptide mapping：enzymatic digestion + RP-HPLC fingerprint

Mass spectrometry (MS)：accurate mass of protein/peptides

IEF：separate proteins according to pI

👉 中文提示：肽图 + 质谱 + 等电聚焦，用来确认是不是对的蛋白。

② Purity / variants（纯度 & 变体）

SDS-PAGE：separate proteins by size → main band, fragments

SEC：size-exclusion → aggregates and fragments

Ion-exchange chromatography (IEX)：separate by charge → charge variants

中文提示：SDS-PAGE 看大小；SEC 看聚集体；IEX 看电荷变体。

③ Glycosylation（糖基化）

Monosaccharide / sialic acid assays：overall sugar & sialic-acid level

Released glycan mapping：profile of released N-glycans

Glycopeptide mapping：site-specific glycosylation

👉 中文提示：单糖/唾液酸定量 + 游离糖链图谱 + 糖肽图谱。

④ Dosage / Activity（含量 & 活性）

UV / colorimetric assays：total protein content

ELISA：specific quantification of active protein

Cell-based bioassays：biological activity / potency

👉 中文提示：UV/比色测总蛋白，ELISA 测特异蛋白，细胞实验看活性。

3.4 Mock exam Q3：Peptide mapping

Peptide mapping – definition & rolePeptide mapping is an identity control method where the protein is enzymatically digested (e.g. trypsin) and the resulting peptides are separated (RP-HPLC) and compared to a reference profile, possibly coupled with MS.→ 肽图：用酶解（如胰蛋白酶）把蛋白切成一系列肽段，通过 RP-HPLC（±MS）分离检测，将色谱 / 质谱指纹图与对照品比较，用于身份鉴别 + 结构完整性评估。

答题骨架（1–2 句话）：

Peptide mapping = enzymatic digestion + chromatographic separation of peptides to obtain a fingerprint chromatogram/mass pattern and compare with a reference, for identity and structural integrity control.

4
Glycosylation & Biobetters（mock Q1 + Biobetters slides）

4.1 Glycosylation 重要性（QC & EPO）

Why glycosylation is important• Solubility & stability• Proper folding• Bioactivity: receptor recognition, cell targeting• Pharmacokinetics: serum half-life, clearance (asialoglycoprotein receptor)• Immunogenicity→ 糖基化决定蛋白的溶解性 / 稳定性、正确折叠、生物活性、血药浓度-时间曲线（半衰期、清除）、免疫原性。

N- vs O-glycosylation• N-glycosylation: Asn in consensus sequence Asn-X-Ser/Thr• O-glycosylation: Ser / Thr→ N-糖：Asn-X-Ser/Thr；O-糖：Ser 或 Thr 残基。

4.2 Mock Q1：3 strategies to improve protein via glycosylation

Strategy 1 – Add new glycosylation sites (sequence engineering)

Introduce additional N-glycosylation consensus sequences in the protein sequence to increase sialylation and size.

Example: Darbepoetin (EPO biobetter) – extra glycosylation sites → longer half-life.→ 策略 1：通过点突变增加 N-糖基化位点（Asn-X-Ser/Thr），提高糖基化和唾液酸水平，延长半衰期。如 Darbepoetin。

Strategy 2 – Fusion with glycosylated domains / peptides

Fuse therapeutic protein with a glycosylated peptide region rich in Ser/Thr.

Example: CTP fusion (C-terminal peptide of hCG) or Fc fusion (IgG Fc is heavily glycosylated).→ 策略 2：与富含 Ser/Thr 的糖蛋白片段融合（如 CTP-hGH、Fc 融合蛋白），增加 O-/N-糖链和分子大小。

Strategy 3 – Choose / engineer host cells for better glycosylation

Use mammalian cells (e.g. CHO) instead of bacteria/yeast to obtain complex, sialylated glycans.

Host cell glycoengineering to humanize glycosylation (reduce immunogenic glycans).→ 策略 3：选择或工程化宿主细胞（CHO、人细胞等），获得更接近人类的复杂糖链、减少免疫原性、改善 PK。

（答题只要写 3 个策略 + 3 个例子 即可。）

4.3 Biobetters & half-life extension（mock Q2）

  • Biobetters definition
    Biobetters (biosuperiors) are improved versions of existing biological drugs, belonging to the same class but with better PK/PD, efficacy, safety, or convenience.
    → Biobetters = 在同一靶点 / 同类的改良版生物药，在 PK/PD、疗效、安全性或用药便利性上更好。
  • General strategies（可直接背 list）
    1. Modify coding sequence (mutations, fusion proteins, humanization, new glycosylation sites)
    2. Non-translational engineering (PEGylation, polymer conjugation, lipidation)
    3. Host cell engineering (glycoengineering)
    4. New formulations and routes of administration
      → 四大类策略：编码序列改造 / 非翻译修饰（PEG、聚合物、脂化）/ 宿主工程 / 新剂型与给药途径。

4.4 Mock Q2：Extend IFN-γ half-life（2 策略示范）

Strategy A – PEGylation

Covalent attachment of polyethylene glycol chains increases hydrodynamic radius, reduces renal clearance, and proteolysis.

Example: Peginterferon-alpha products.→ PEG 化：共价 PEG 修饰，增大水动力半径，减少肾清除和蛋白水解，延长半衰期。

Strategy B – Fusion to albumin or Fc

Create a fusion protein with human serum albumin (HSA) or IgG Fc domain.

Benefits: larger size, FcRn recycling, longer circulation time.

Example: Albuferon (IFN-α–albumin fusion), Fc-fusion biologics.→ 与白蛋白 / Fc 融合：增大分子、利用 FcRn 循环回收，提高半衰期，如 Albuferon。

（答题时写“strategy + short mechanism + example”就足够。）

5
Erythropoietin (EPO) Case Study（mock + case PDF）

5.1 生理作用 & 适应证

Physiological effect of EPOEPO is a glycoprotein cytokine produced mainly by the kidney; it stimulates erythropoiesis by promoting proliferation and differentiation of erythroid progenitors in bone marrow.→ 作用：肾脏分泌的糖蛋白细胞因子，促进骨髓红系祖细胞增殖与分化，增加红细胞生成。

Indications of rEPO therapy• Renal failure–associated anemia• Anemia of chronic disease / inflammation• Anemia associated with cancer and chemotherapy• Support of erythropoiesis (major surgery, bone marrow transplantation)→ 适应证：肾性贫血、慢性病 / 炎症性贫血、肿瘤及化疗相关贫血、大手术和造血干细胞移植中的促红细胞生成治疗。

5.2 结构特点 & 糖基化（case Part 1）

Structural characteristics of EPO• ~30 kDa glycoprotein, ~165 amino acids• Several N- and O-linked glycans (3 N-glycosylation + 1 O-glycosylation site)→ 结构：约 30 kDa、165 aa 的糖蛋白，含 3 个 N 糖位点 + 1 个 O 糖位点。

Post-translational modifications• Glycosylation is the major PTM: crucial for activity, half-life & solubility.→ PTM：以糖基化为主，决定活性与 PK。

Required host cell type for PTMEPO requires mammalian cells (e.g. CHO) providing complex, sialylated N-glycans; bacteria or simple yeasts cannot perform appropriate glycosylation.→ 必须用哺乳动物细胞（如 CHO）表达以获得合适的人样糖基化；细菌 / 简单酵母不行。

5.3 Host cell choice（mock case Q a）

Order of choice based on glycosylation quality

1
Mammalian cells (CHO, BHK, human cell lines) – complex, sialylated glycans
2
Insect cells – some complex glycans but less human-like
3
Yeast – high-mannose-type glycans (more immunogenic)
4
Bacteria – no glycosylation→ 选择顺序：哺乳动物细胞 > 昆虫细胞 > 酵母 > 细菌。

答题可以简化成：

“CHO or other mammalian cells, because they provide complex, sialylated glycosylation required for EPO activity and long half-life.”

5.4 Plasmid construct & control（case Q5–Q7 + mock Q b）

  • Key features of EPO expression plasmid
    • Strong promoter (e.g. CMV) → high transcription
    • EPO cDNA coding sequence
    • Polyadenylation signal (e.g. bovine growth hormone polyA)
    • Selection marker gene (antibiotic resistance)
    → 质粒要素：强启动子 + EPO cDNA + 多聚腺苷化信号 + 选择标记基因。
  • Steps to create expression plasmid from commercial backbone
    1. Design primers and amplify EPO cDNA
    2. Digest both cDNA and plasmid with restriction enzymes
    3. Ligate insert into plasmid at appropriate site
    4. Transform into bacteria and select colonies
    5. Screen and validate clones (restriction mapping, sequencing)
      → 制备步骤：PCR 扩增 cDNA → 限制性内切酶切割插入片段和载体 → 连接 → 转化 → 挑克隆 + 限切图谱 / 测序验证。
  • Methods to control recombinant plasmid
    • Restriction enzyme digestion + agarose gel (restriction map)
    • Sequencing
    → 质粒控制：做限制酶切图谱 + 测序确认序列正确。

5.5 Contaminants after bioproduction（EPO & L1 都通用）

直接套用 “4 类杂质”。

Host cell proteins & DNA

Product-related variants (aggregates, fragments, misfolded forms)

Process-related impurities (chromatography leachables, solvents)

Microbial contaminants & endotoxins

6
Vaccines & Cervarix 例题（Turbica Vaccine slides + 2022 exam）

6.1 基本定义 & 分类

Vaccine definition (WHO)Suspensions of killed or attenuated microorganisms, antigenic proteins, synthetic constructs or other biomolecular derivatives administered for prevention, amelioration or treatment of diseases.→ 疫苗：灭活 / 减毒微生物、抗原蛋白或其它生物大分子，用于预防或治疗疾病。

Main vaccine strategies（课件图）• Live attenuated• Inactivated (killed)• Sub-unit (purified or recombinant antigens, polysaccharidic, conjugated)• VLP (virus-like particles)• Vector vaccines (replicating / non-replicating viral vectors)• DNA / mRNA vaccines→ 记关键词：减毒、灭活、亚单位 / 多糖 / 结合、VLP、载体疫苗、DNA、mRNA。

6.2 Cervarix® – 2022 真题

题干关键信息：

L1 protein from HPV types 16 and 18

Recombinant production in insect cells

Aluminium adjuvant

Q1 – Vaccine category

Cervarix is a recombinant subunit vaccine based on VLPs (virus-like particles) composed of L1 proteins from HPV 16 and 18.

→ 分类：重组亚单位 VLP 疫苗。

Q2 – Role of aluminium adjuvant

Aluminium salts act as adjuvant to enhance immune response by promoting antigen uptake and activation of antigen-presenting cells and prolonging antigen release.→ 铝佐剂：增强 APC 激活和抗原递呈、延长抗原暴露，从而增强和持久化免疫应答。

问Q3 – Why insect cells?
答

L1 proteins must self-assemble into VLPs with correct conformation, requiring eukaryotic folding and PTM; insect cells allow proper assembly, unlike bacteria.→ 必须在昆虫细胞等真核系统中表达，才能正确折叠并自组装成 VLP，诱导有效免疫。

问Q4 – Where is L1 produced?
答

Because there is a cell lysis step in process, L1 is produced intracellularly and released after lysis.→ L1 在细胞内表达，通过细胞裂解释放。

Q5 – Impurity categories

直接用上面 QC 的 4 类（细菌 / 病毒；产品相关；工艺相关；宿主相关）。

问Q6 – How many processes?
答

Two independent production processes (one for L1 type 16 and one for L1 type 18), then mixing and formulation.→ 两条独立工艺链，分别生产 16 型和 18 型 L1，然后混合配制。

7
Somatic Cell Therapy & ATMPs（mock MCQ1 + Chaput slides）

7.1 核心概念（MCQ 必考点）

Somatic cellAny cell of the body except germ line cells.→ 体细胞：除生殖细胞外的所有细胞。

Somatic cell therapyTherapeutic use of living somatic cells, autologous, allogeneic or xenogeneic, manipulated before reintroduction.→ 体细胞治疗：将处理过的体细胞（自体 / 异体 / 异种）回输，用于治疗。

Autologous vs allogeneic• Autologous: patient’s own cells; minimal immune reaction; but individualized manufacturing for each patient.• Allogeneic: donor cells; easier large-scale production and “off-the-shelf”; but requires HLA compatibility and immunosuppression.→ 自体：免疫排斥少，但每人一批；异体：可规模化和库存，但存在免疫排斥和 HLA 匹配问题。

Cell-based therapyAdministration of living cells to patients.→ 细胞治疗 = 给病人输入活细胞。

Mock MCQ1 正确语句（直接背）：

B) Somatic cell is any cell of the body except germ line cells.

D) Cell-based therapies consist in administration of living cells.

E) Autologous cell therapy is most of the time well tolerated.

7.2 ATMP 分类（必背 3 大类）

Advanced Therapy Medicinal Products (ATMPs)• Gene therapy medicines• Somatic-cell therapy medicines• Tissue-engineered medicines→ ATMP = 基因治疗药、体细胞治疗药、组织工程药。

Regulatory differencesATMPs require full drug-like GMP (Part IV), authorized manufacturing sites and qualified person; simpler cell therapy products may follow specific cell therapy GMP.→ ATMP 监管更接近“药品”：Part IV GMP、合格负责药师、ANSM 授权等。

8
Viral Vectors for Gene Therapy（Bonte slides）

8.1 基本概念

Viral vector definitionGenetically modified virus used to transfer and express its own or foreign genes.→ 病毒载体：经过基因改造，用于递送并表达目标基因的病毒。

Main objectives of viral vectors• Protein production in cell culture• Vaccination• Gene therapy• Anticancer (oncolytic) applications→ 应用：重组蛋白生产、疫苗、基因治疗、溶瘤治疗。

8.2 Desired characteristics（背三大块）

Targeting & expression• Adaptable cellular tropism, high transduction efficiency• Controllable expression level and stability (integration vs episomal)

Safety• Minimal toxicity & immunogenicity• Low recombination & low RCV (replication competent virus) generation

Production• Easy and cost-effective production• High viral titer achievable→ 三大块：靶向 + 表达 / 安全性 / 生产性。

8.3 Main viral vectors & cargo size（常规考点）

Adenoviral vector

Retroviral / Lentiviral vector

rAAV vector

Remember approximate cargo limits:

AAV ~4.5 kb

Adenovirus 8–35 kb

Lentivirus ~8 kb→ 记住 AAV 载量小，Adenovirus 最大。

8.4 QC of viral vectors（大题可能会让你列）

Identity & purity• DNA identity: sequencing, restriction map• Physical titration: photometry• Biological titration: plaque assays, cytopathic effect, (RT-)PCR• Empty capsid assessment: photometry or density gradients

Activity• In vitro transgene transfer efficiency• Expression of protein of interest: SDS-PAGE, Western blot, ELISA, immunocytochemistry• Functional studies in cell / animal models

Safety / sterility• Sterility tests for bacteria, fungi• Endotoxin (LAL)• Mycoplasma detection (culture or PCR)

9
Non-viral Vectors & Nucleic Acid Drugs（Fay – non-viral + oligo）

9.1 Nucleic acids as therapeutics：关键类型

背这个总表就够用：

CRISPR/Cas9

Plasmids (DNA vaccines, gene addition)

siRNA / shRNA / miRNA / anti-miRNA

Antisense oligonucleotides (ASOs)

mRNA (vaccines, protein replacement)

Aptamers

→ 一句话：“CRISPR, plasmids, si/sh/miRNA, ASOs, mRNA, aptamers”。

9.2 Natural barriers for DNA/RNA delivery

Low stability (nuclease degradation)

High molecular weight

Negative charge

Hydrophilicity→ 自然障碍：不稳定 / 分子量高 / 带负电 / 亲水 → 需要修饰和载体化（vectorisation）。

9.3 Non-viral vector design criteria & LNP 组成（常考 Comirnaty）

Lipid nanoparticle composition• Ionizable lipid (pKa ~6) – interacts with RNA, endosomal escape• Helper lipid – supports membrane fusion, internalization• Cholesterol – stabilizes bilayer, fluidity• PEGylated lipid – colloidal stability, controls size and circulation→ LNP 四件套：可电离脂质 / 辅助脂质 / 胆固醇 / PEG-脂质。

Design criteria• Protect nucleic acid from degradation• Facilitate cellular uptake and endosomal escape• Enable targeted delivery• Maintain acceptable toxicity & immunogenicity

10
Molecular Tools & Plasmid / PCR（Gesbert + oligo QC）

10.1 Genetic engineering basics（常规简答）

Genetic engineering definitionUse, modification and reproduction of genomes to manipulate genetic material for research, therapy, diagnosis, etc.→ 基因工程：对基因组进行操控，用于研究 / 治疗 / 诊断等。

Recombinant DNA principleUse plasmid vectors and restriction enzymes to construct recombinant DNA; introduce into host cells (transformation / transfection) for amplification or protein expression.→ 重组 DNA：限制酶 + 连接酶构建含插入片段的质粒 → 转化 / 转染宿主细胞。

10.2 Plasmid QC（与 EPO plasmid & oligo QC 呼应）

Impurities in plasmid production• Host bacterial DNA• RNA• Proteins• Endotoxins• Incorrect plasmid isoforms (supercoiled, linear, nicked)

Recommended tests（要点）• Agarose gel electrophoresis (DNA, RNA, plasmid isoforms)• Southern blot / PCR for residual bacterial DNA• Protein assay (e.g. BCA) + SDS-PAGE• LAL test for endotoxins• Sterility test• Restriction map and sequencing to confirm DNA identity

11
总结：考试高频问答骨架（你可以重点先背这些）

我帮你把几个最像考题的模板再浓缩一下，你可以按“英文一句 + 中文一句”直接背：

  1. 四大 QC 特性
    • “QC of therapeutic proteins focuses on four main characteristics: identity, purity, dosage and stability.”
      → 治疗性蛋白质的质控主要关注四个方面：身份、纯度、含量和稳定性。
  2. 三种改善蛋白糖基化的策略
    • “Therapeutic proteins can be improved by adding new glycosylation sites through sequence engineering, fusing with glycosylated domains such as CTP or Fc, and choosing or engineering host cells that provide more human-like glycosylation.”
      → 可以通过增加糖基化位点、与糖蛋白片段融合、选择 / 工程化宿主细胞来改善治疗性蛋白的糖基化。
  3. IFN 半衰期延长策略
    • “To extend interferon half-life, we can PEGylate the protein to increase hydrodynamic radius and reduce renal clearance, or fuse it to albumin or Fc to benefit from FcRn-mediated recycling.”
      → 延长干扰素半衰期可以PEG 化减少肾清除，也可以与白蛋白或 Fc 融合利用 FcRn 循环回收。
  4. Peptide mapping 一句话
    • “Peptide mapping consists in enzymatically digesting the protein and separating the resulting peptides by RP-HPLC (±MS) to obtain a fingerprint that is compared with a reference for identity and structural integrity control.”
      → 肽图通过**酶解 + RP-HPLC（±质谱）**生成指纹图，与参考对比，用于身份和结构完整性控制。
  5. Cervarix 分类 + 铝佐剂
    • “Cervarix is a recombinant subunit vaccine based on L1 VLPs of HPV16 and 18 produced in insect cells, formulated with aluminium salts as adjuvant to enhance antigen presentation and boost the immune response.”
      → Cervarix 是基于 HPV16/18 L1 VLP 的重组亚单位疫苗，在昆虫细胞中生产，配以铝佐剂以增强抗原递呈和免疫应答。
  6. EPO 宿主选择
    • “Recombinant EPO must be produced in mammalian cells such as CHO, because it is a highly glycosylated glycoprotein and requires complex, sialylated N-glycans for activity and long half-life.”
      → 重组 EPO 必须在 CHO 等哺乳动物细胞中表达，因为它是高度糖基化的糖蛋白，需要复杂唾液酸化 N-糖来保持活性和长半衰期。
  7. Somatic cell therapy 核心句
    • “Somatic cell therapy uses living somatic cells—autologous or allogeneic—that are manipulated before reintroduction; autologous therapies are generally well tolerated, while allogeneic therapies allow off-the-shelf products but require immune compatibility.”
      → 体细胞治疗使用处理过的自体或异体体细胞；自体一般耐受性好，异体便于“现货化”但需考虑免疫相容性。
12
Extracellular Vesicles（EV，Obeid 课）

12.1 定义 & 分类（必背）

Definition

Extracellular vesicles are membrane-enclosed nanoparticles released by almost all cell types and found in all body fluids.

→ 细胞外囊泡是几乎所有细胞都会释放、存在于各种体液中的膜性纳米颗粒。

Main classes of EVs（记 3 类 + 尺寸 + 来源）

Exosomes – 30–150 nm, derived from endosomal multivesicular bodies (MVBs) that fuse with the plasma membrane.→ 外泌体：约 30–150 nm，来源于多囊泡体（MVB）与细胞膜融合后释放。

Microvesicles / Microparticles – 100–1000 nm, formed by outward budding and fission of the plasma membrane.→ 微囊泡 / 微粒：约 100–1000 nm，由细胞膜外向出芽形成。

Apoptotic bodies – 500–5000 nm, released by blebbing of apoptotic cells.→ 凋亡小体：约 500–5000 nm，由凋亡细胞起泡、断裂产生。

Typical markers（只要举几个）

Exosomes: tetraspanins (CD9, CD63, CD81), ESCRT proteins (Alix, TSG101), Rab GTPases, HSPs.→ 外泌体：四跨膜蛋白（CD9/63/81）、ESCRT 蛋白（Alix、TSG101）等。

Microvesicles: integrins, selectins, phosphatidylserine (Annexin V binding).→ 微囊泡：富含整合素、选择素、外翻磷脂酰丝氨酸（可被 Annexin V 识别）。

Apoptotic bodies: nuclear fragments, histones, chromatin.→ 凋亡小体：常含有核碎片、组蛋白、染色质。

考点模式：

“Name the three main types of EVs and their origin.”

→ 答：exosomes（endosomal）、microvesicles（plasma membrane budding）、apoptotic bodies（apoptotic blebbing）。

12.2 组成 & 生物学功能

Composition

EVs contain a specific combination of lipids, proteins, and nucleic acids that reflect the cell of origin.

→ EV 携带的脂质、蛋白和核酸具有**“来源细胞指纹”**。

Lipids: cholesterol, sphingomyelin, phosphatidylserine-rich membrane.→ 脂质：胆固醇、鞘磷脂、磷脂酰丝氨酸等。

Proteins: tetraspanins, ESCRT components, integrins, adhesion molecules, enzymes.→ 蛋白：四跨膜蛋白、ESCRT 成分、整合素、黏附分子、酶等。

Nucleic acids: mRNA, miRNA, lncRNA, rRNA, and sometimes DNA.→ 核酸：mRNA、miRNA、长链非编码 RNA、rRNA，甚至 DNA 片段。

Functions

EVs mediate short- and long-distance intercellular communication by transferring bioactive cargo to recipient cells.

→ EV 通过“运送货物”（蛋白/脂质/核酸）实现近距离和远距离的细胞间通信。

They can:

Modulate gene expression and signaling in target cells.→ 调节受体细胞的基因表达和信号通路。

Participate in immune regulation, angiogenesis, coagulation, tissue repair, and tumor progression.→ 参与免疫调节、血管生成、凝血、组织修复以及肿瘤进展等。

12.3 EV-based therapy vs Cell-based therapy（优缺点比较）

Advantages of EV-based therapy

EVs are non-replicating and cannot divide, reducing the risk of tumor formation compared to cell therapy.→ EV 不会增殖，相比细胞治疗肿瘤形成风险更低。

Lower immunogenicity and easier to store, freeze, and standardize.→ 免疫原性相对较低，更易于冷冻保存与标准化生产。

Better tissue penetration due to their small size and ability to cross biological barriers.→ 体积小，可穿越多种生物屏障，组织渗透性更好。

Limitations / Challenges

Heterogeneity in EV populations and difficulty to fully define composition.→ 群体高度异质，难以完全表征。

Large-scale, GMP-compliant production and purification are technically challenging.→ 放大生产和 GMP 纯化难度大。

Lack of harmonized regulatory guidelines compared to classical biologics.→ 相比传统生物药，监管框架尚不完善。

典型问法：

“Cite two advantages of EV-based therapies compared to cell-based therapies, and one major challenge.”

→ 优点：更安全（不增殖）、免疫原性低、易保存 / 标准化、组织渗透性好；

→ 挑战：大规模生产和纯化困难、群体异质、缺乏统一监管标准。

12.4 EV 的临床应用潜力（生物标志物 & 治疗）

EVs as biomarkers

Because EVs circulate in blood and other fluids and carry cell-type-specific cargo, they are attractive non-invasive biomarkers (“liquid biopsy”).

→ EV 存在于血液等体液中、携带来源细胞特异“指纹”，非常适合作为无创液体活检生物标志物。

They have been associated with many diseases (cancer, cardiovascular, neurodegenerative and inflammatory diseases).

→ 在肿瘤、心血管、神经退行性、炎症性疾病中均有数量或组成改变。

Therapeutic use（记 3 大类）

Therapeutic EVs can be:

1
Native EVs from therapeutic cells (e.g. MSC-derived exosomes with anti-inflammatory properties).→ 来自治疗性细胞（如 MSC）的“天然 EV”，本身具有免疫调节 / 组织修复作用。
2
EVs from genetically modified cells expressing specific therapeutic proteins or RNAs.→ 来源细胞经过基因修饰，使 EV 携带特定蛋白或 RNA。
3
Engineered EVs loaded with drugs or nucleic acids (e.g. siRNA, miRNA mimics).→ 后期工程化装载小分子药物或核酸（siRNA、miRNA 模拟物等）的 EV。

可以背一句：

“EVs can be used both as diagnostic biomarkers in liquid biopsy and as therapeutic carriers delivering proteins, lipids, or nucleic acids to target tissues.”

→ EV 既可作为液体活检的诊断标志物，也可作为递送蛋白 / 脂质 / 核酸的治疗载体。

12.5 EV 的制备与纯化方法（一句话记忆）

Main isolation methods (exam-style list)

Differential ultracentrifugation and density gradient centrifugation.→ 差速 + 密度梯度离心。

Size-exclusion chromatography (SEC).→ 体积排阻色谱（SEC）。

Ultrafiltration / tangential-flow filtration (TFF).→ 超滤 / 切向流过滤。

Polymer-based precipitation methods.→ 聚合物沉淀法。

Immunoaffinity capture and microfluidic-based platforms.→ 免疫亲和捕获、微流控平台。

一句：

“EV isolation methods combine size-based, density-based and affinity-based strategies such as ultracentrifugation, SEC, ultrafiltration and immunoaffinity capture.”

→ EV 纯化一般结合粒径、密度和亲和性原理，如超速离心、SEC、超滤和免疫亲和捕获。

12.6 EV 的表征 & 质控（QC）

Physical characterization

Size distribution and concentration: Nanoparticle Tracking Analysis (NTA), Dynamic Light Scattering (DLS), Tunable Resistive Pulse Sensing (TRPS).→ 物理表征：NTA、DLS、TRPS 测粒径和浓度。

Morphology: Transmission Electron Microscopy (TEM), cryo-EM, Atomic Force Microscopy (AFM).→ 形态：透射电镜、冷冻电镜、原子力显微镜。

Molecular characterization

Surface and cargo proteins: Western blot, flow cytometry, mass spectrometry.→ 蛋白质：Western blot、流式细胞术、质谱。

Nucleic acids: qPCR, RNA-seq for miRNA/mRNA.→ 核酸：qPCR、RNA-测序。

Purity & safety

Assess co-isolated proteins, lipoproteins, and non-EV particles; test for sterility and endotoxin.→ 纯度与安全：评估共分离的蛋白 / 脂蛋白等杂质，并进行无菌与内毒素检测。

可能的简答模板：

“To characterize EVs, we combine nanoparticle tracking analysis and electron microscopy for size and morphology, Western blot or flow cytometry for EV markers, and nucleic-acid-based methods to analyse their RNA cargo.”

→ EV 表征通常结合 NTA+电镜看粒径和形态，WB/流式看标志蛋白，再用核酸检测方法分析 RNA 货物。

12.7 EV 典型考题模板（背题用）

EV-Q1：Definition & classes

“Define extracellular vesicles and cite the three main classes with their origin.”

→ 定义细胞外囊泡，并列出三类及其来源。

答题骨架：

EV = membrane-enclosed nanoparticles released by cells into body fluids, carrying proteins, lipids and nucleic acids.→ EV 是细胞释放到体液中的膜性纳米颗粒，携带蛋白、脂质和核酸。

Classes: exosomes (endosomal origin), microvesicles (plasma membrane budding), apoptotic bodies (apoptotic cell blebbing).→ 三类：外泌体（内体来源）、微囊泡（细胞膜出芽）、凋亡小体（凋亡起泡）。

EV-Q2：Advantages vs cell therapy

“Give two advantages of EV-based therapies compared to cell-based therapies, and one limitation.”

→ 写出 EV-治疗相对细胞治疗的两个优势和一个缺点。

答题骨架：

Advantages: non-replicating / lower tumor risk; lower immunogenicity; easier storage and standardization; better tissue penetration.→ 优势：不增殖、致瘤风险低；免疫原性较低；易保存与标准化；组织渗透性好。

Limitation: large-scale GMP production and purification remain challenging; EV populations are heterogeneous and difficult to fully characterize.→ 缺点：大规模 GMP 生产和纯化困难；群体异质，难以完全表征。

EV-Q3：EVs as biomarkers

“Explain why EVs are attractive biomarkers for liquid biopsy.”

→ 解释为何 EV 适合作为液体活检的标志物。

骨架：

Widely present in body fluids and can be obtained non-invasively.→ 广泛存在于体液，可无创获取。

Carry cell-type-specific proteins and nucleic acids reflecting the physiological or pathological state.→ 携带来源细胞特异的蛋白和核酸，反映疾病状态。

Protected by lipid bilayer, which stabilizes their cargo.→ 双层膜保护内部 cargo，更稳定。

13
真题常见模板（补差用）

13.1 Ion-exchange chromatography（IEX）原理（EPO 纯化常考）

Definition & principle Ion-exchange chromatography separates proteins according to their net charge at a given pH by reversible binding to oppositely charged groups on the resin. → 离子交换色谱根据蛋白在特定 pH 下的净电荷差异来分离，通过与载体上**相反电荷的官能团可逆结合。

How it works（一句话骨架） The protein of interest binds to the charged resin, impurities are washed out, then the protein is eluted by increasing salt concentration or changing pH. → 目标蛋白先吸附在带电填料上，杂质被洗脱掉，然后通过升高盐浓度或改变 pH把目标蛋白解吸洗脱下来。

13.2 SDS-PAGE：原理 + 如何用来看纯度

Principle SDS-PAGE separates proteins mainly according to their molecular weight: SDS coats proteins with a uniform negative charge, so separation depends on size when they migrate in the polyacrylamide gel. → SDS-PAGE 主要按分子量分离蛋白：SDS 给蛋白包上一层均一负电荷，在聚丙烯酰胺凝胶中迁移速度主要取决于大小。

Purity assessment from a gel A pure protein gives a single band at the expected molecular weight, whereas impurities appear as additional bands at different sizes or as smears. → 单一、清晰的一条带（在预期分子量位置）代表比较纯；多条带或拖尾 / 抹带说明存在其他蛋白或降解产物。

如果考题给三条泳道（supernatant / intermediate / final）：

Crude supernatant: many bands → very impure.

Intermediate: fewer bands → increased purity.

Final purified: single major band → high purity. → 用话术：“从左到右杂带减少，最终样品只剩一条主带，说明纯度逐步提高。”

13.3 如何证明“无某种微生物污染”（sterility / specific test）

Generic answer To verify that the batch is not contaminated with a given micro-organism, we perform sterility tests by incubating the product in growth media, and we can combine this with a specific detection method such as PCR or immunoassay targeting this organism. → 要证明批次没有被某种微生物污染，可以：

1
做无菌检查：在适当培养基中培养，观察是否有生长；
2
再加一个特异性检测（如针对该微生物的 PCR 或免疫检测）。

你答题时可以写成两点：

Sterility test in appropriate culture media.

Specific PCR / immunological assay for this microorganism. → 无菌检查 + 针对该菌的 PCR / 免疫检测。

13.4 “RNA-based alternative to rEPO”（开放题模板）

mRNA–LNP encoding EPO

An RNA-based alternative would be an mRNA therapy encoding EPO, formulated in lipid nanoparticles (LNPs). After injection, patient cells translate the mRNA and produce EPO in vivo instead of injecting the protein itself.

→ 用装在 LNP 里的 EPO 编码 mRNA，让病人细胞在体内自己产 EPO，而不是打现成蛋白。

Main advantages

This approach can simplify manufacturing and allows transient, controllable expression of EPO.

→ 优点：生产更简单，表达是短期且可控的。

13.5 动物实验曲线解读模板（以 EPO 抗肥胖为例）

常见题型：给你几条曲线（体重变化、食物摄入），问你“How do you interpret these results?”。

通用英文骨架 In the high-fat diet group, mice treated with EPO gained less body weight than controls, while food intake was similar (or only slightly reduced). This suggests that EPO limits weight gain mainly by modulating metabolism rather than by decreasing food consumption. → 在高脂饮食组中，EPO 处理的小鼠体重增加更少，而食物摄入量相近（或仅轻微下降），说明 EPO 主要通过影响代谢来减少体重增加，而不只是让小鼠吃得更少。

你可以把第一句背熟，再根据具体图改两个词：

gained less body weight

food intake was similar

13.6 Protein separation: which properties?（MCQ 模板）

Key properties used for protein separation Protein separation techniques are mainly based on size, charge, hydrophobicity and specific binding affinity. → 蛋白分离方法主要根据分子大小、带电性质、疏水性和特异性亲和力来设计。

可以列成：

Size → gel filtration / SEC

Charge → ion-exchange, CZE

Hydrophobicity → RP-HPLC, HIC

Affinity → affinity chromatography (ligand–receptor) → 一看到选项就往这四类上靠，像“viscosity / color / taste 这种肯定就是错的”。

02基因克隆与表达全流程

1.全流程总览（Overview）
  • A获取目标基因 (Target Gene Acquisition)

→ B. 克隆到表达载体 (Cloning into Expression Vector)

→ C. 转染/转化宿主细胞 (Transfection/Transformation of Host Cells)

→ D. 建立主细胞库MCB (Establish Master Cell Bank, MCB)

→ E. 建立工作细胞库WCB (Establish Working Cell Bank, WCB)

→ F. 小规模细胞扩增 (Small-scale Cell Expansion)

→ G. 工业放大培养 (Large-scale Cell Culture)

→ H. 下游纯化流程 (Downstream Purification Steps)

→ I. 终产品制剂 (Final Product Formulation)

→ J. 包装与质控 (Packaging & Quality Control)

1.基因与细胞系开发（Gene & Cell Line Development）
  • A获取编码目标蛋白的cDNA (Obtain cDNA encoding target protein)

→ B. 插入质粒，形成表达载体 (Insert into plasmid; build expression vector)

→ C. 转染进入大肠杆菌/酵母/昆虫/哺乳动物细胞 (Transfer to E. coli/yeast/insect/mammalian cells depending on protein needs)

→ D. 筛选高效表达克隆 (Screen for high-expression clones)

→ E. 建立主细胞库MCB (Establish Master Cell Bank, MCB)

1.细胞银行建立与放大（Cell Banking & Scale-up）
  • A通过高表达克隆→建MCB（冻存保存）(High-expression clone → freeze as MCB)

→ B. 由MCB扩增并建立WCB (Amplify from MCB to build Working Cell Bank, WCB)

→ C. 质控指标
细胞身份、活力、纯度、无病毒/微生物 (QC: Identity, viability, purity, no virus/microbes)

→ D. 从WCB扩种至摇瓶/波动袋/反应器 (Expand from WCB: flask → wavebag → bioreactor)

1.工艺放大与反应器（Scale-up & Bioreactor）
  • A小体积细胞培养 (Small-scale culture, e.g. 200mL bottle)

→ B. 波动袋培养 (Wavebag, e.g. 20L–200L)

→ C. 大型反应器培养 (Large bioreactor, e.g. 10,000 L stainless steel tank)

→ D. 反应器参数控制
温度、pH、溶氧、搅拌、营养投加 (Parameters: Temp, pH, DO, stirring, feeding)

→ E. 工艺持续自动监控 (Real-time automated control)

1.培养模式选择（Culture Modes）
  • A批次培养（Batch: 一次性加料到终止）

→ B. 补料分批（Fed-batch: 中间多次加营养，延长生产期，提高产量）

→ C. 连续（Continuous/Perfusion: 持续加新鲜培养基与收集产物，细胞持续生长）

1.下游纯化流程（Downstream Purification）
  • A移除细胞与碎片（离心/过滤） (Cell and debris removal: centrifugation/filtration)

→ B. 初步纯化（如层析、析出） (Primary purification, e.g., chromatography, precipitation)

→ C. 精细纯化（亲和、离子交换、凝胶过滤等） (Fine purification: affinity, ion exchange, gel filtration, etc.)

→ D. 杂质去除 (Host cell proteins, DNA, endotoxins, process residuals, product-related aggregates/unfolded)

→ E. 浓缩与最终纯化 (Concentration and final purification)

→ F. 病毒去除/灭活（低pH, 过滤） (Viral removal/inactivation: low pH, filtration)

1.杂质与质量控制（Impurities & QC）
  • A检测宿主相关杂质：DNA、蛋白、脂类 (Host impurities: DNA, proteins, lipids)
→ B. 检测工艺相关杂质
缓冲液残留、色谱填料、金属、聚合物 (Process impurities: buffers, chromatography resin, metals, polymers)
→ C. 检测产品相关杂质
聚集体、截短体、错误折叠 (Product-related: aggregates, truncation, misfolded protein)

→ D. 检测病毒、内毒素、微生物 (Virus, endotoxin, microorganism tests)

1.终产品制剂与储存（Final Formulation & Storage）
  • A纯化蛋白配制药用缓冲液 (Buffer formulation)

→ B. 无菌过滤 (Sterile filtration)

→ C. 灌装/分装/包装 (Filling & Packaging)

→ D. 低温/冻干储存 (Low-temperature/lyophilized storage)

1.质量表征与合规（QC & Compliance）
  • A结构分析（一级、二级、糖基化）(Structural: primary, secondary, glycosylation)

→ B. 纯度分析（SDS-PAGE, HPLC等）(Purity: SDS-PAGE, HPLC, etc.)

→ C. 功能活性（结合、细胞/动物功能）(Activity: binding, cell/animal assays)

→ D. 安全性（病毒、内毒素、宿主DNA等）(Safety: virus, endotoxin, host DNA)

→ E. GMP记录与溯源全程合规 (Full GMP record & traceability)

1.典型案例：青霉素/重组蛋白（Classic Example: Penicillin/Protein）
  • A菌种库扩增（母本）(Strain bank amplification)

→ B. 发酵罐大规模培养 (Large-scale fermentation)

→ C. 发酵结束，分离胞外/内产物 (Harvest extracellular/intracellular product)

→ D. 提取纯化（溶剂、层析/结晶等工艺） (Extraction/Chromatography/Crystallization as needed)

→ E. 最终制剂与包装 (Formulation & Packaging)

1
细胞培养优化与放大
A→B流程（生产工艺放大）
  • A起始细胞扩增 Single cell expansion

→ B. 建立主细胞库（MCB）Master Cell Bank (MCB)

→ C. 建立工作细胞库（WCB）Working Cell Bank (WCB)

→ D. 小体积培养（瓶、波动袋）Small-scale culture (flask, wavebag)

→ E. 工业大规模反应器 Large-scale bioreactor (hundreds/thousands liters)

→ F. 过程参数优化如温度、pH、溶氧、搅拌速率 Process parameter control: temperature, pH, DO, stirring

→ G. 产量及细胞密度动态监测 Yield & cell density monitoring

产物表达系统的选择

  • A分析蛋白结构与功能需求 Analyze protein structure/function

→ B. 选择表达系统（如大肠杆菌/酵母/昆虫/哺乳动物细胞）Select system: E.coli/yeast/insect/mammalian

→ C. 载体与细胞株构建 Construct expression vector & cell line

→ D. 筛选高表达克隆 Select high-producer clones

→ E. 建立稳定细胞库 Cell bank construction

代表性案例简述
青霉素生产
  • A菌种扩增 Seed expansion

→ B. 发酵罐培养 Fermentation

→ C. 静止期产物积累 Stationary phase for product synthesis

→ D. 离心与溶剂萃取/水相结晶 Extraction (centrifugation/solvent/crystallization)

→ E. 衍生物改造 Further semi-syntheses (ampicillin等)

干扰素α生产
  • A基因工程转染表达 Construction via genetic engineering

→ B. 上游细胞工厂 Upstream culture, secretion to medium

→ C. 离心去除细胞 Remove cells by centrifuge

→ D. 分步纯化（层析等）Stepwise purification (chromatography, filtration)

→ E. 病毒安全及表征 Virus safety & characterization

1.蛋白结构和功能表征
  • A一级结构氨基酸/测序 Primary structure (AA sequence, sequencing)

→ B. 二级/高级结构分析 Secondary/tertiary structure (CD, NMR)

→ C. 糖基化/翻译后修饰分析 PTMs analysis (glycosylation)

→ D. 纯度聚集体分析 Purity & aggregates (SDS-PAGE, HPLC)

→ E. 体外活性/结合试验/细胞活性 Functional assays (in vitro binding, cell-based)

1.法规与全流程合规
  • AGMP全流程体系 GMP compliance & batch traceability

→ B. 研发、中试、商业化均需文档和质控 Records and QC across R&D/pilot/commercial

→ C. 工艺节点留样、批次编号、原材料等全程可追溯 Sample/batch/reagent/operator traceability

→ D. 所有验证实验及数据归档 Documentation of all qualification/validation data

1.下游工艺——细胞收获/澄清/初步纯化Downstream Process: Cell Harvesting / Clarification / Initial Purification
  • A细胞培养结束（达到静止/目标阶段）Culture ends (stationary/target phase)

→ B. 离心去除细胞和大颗粒杂质

- Centrifugation removes cells and coarse particles

→ C. 过滤（微滤等）确保进一步清除细胞碎片

- Filtration (microfiltration, etc.) for extra debris removal

→ D. 如为胞内蛋白，还需要细胞破碎（如高压均化）

- If intracellular
cell disruption (e.g. high pressure homogenization)
→ E. 澄清后进行初步纯化
盐析/超滤

- Clarified supernatant: preliminary purification (salting out/ultrafiltration)

1.病毒安全控制的典型流程 Viral Safety Control: Typical Steps
  • A选择专用病毒过滤（纳米级过滤器去除病毒粒子）

Select dedicated viral filtration (nano-scale filter)

B. 酸处理（低pH处理灭活病毒）

Acidification (virus inactivation at low pH)

C. 添加溶剂/去垢剂去病毒包膜（仅限某些蛋白耐此处理）

Solvent/detergent viral inactivation (optional; depends on protein stability)

D. 关键节点均需病毒核酸+感染性检测

Virus PCR/infectivity tests at all critical steps

1.典型案例：“干扰素α”下游工艺 Case: Interferon-Alpha Downstream Processing
  • A培养上清→离心移除细胞→超滤浓缩

Supernatant → centrifugation → ultrafiltration

B. 亲和层析纯化→阴离子交换层析→凝胶过滤层析

Affinity chromatography → anion exchange chromatography → gel filtration

C. 病毒过滤→无菌灌装

Viral filtration → sterile filling

D. 批间表征
结构测序、糖型分析、活性测定

Batch characterization: structure, glycoform, activity

1.产品最终配制与包装 Final Formulation and Packaging
  • A配制稳定缓冲液（调节pH、渗透压等）

Prepare stable buffer: adjust pH, osmolarity

B. 灭菌过滤

Sterile filtration

C. 分装/灌装（小瓶、西林瓶等）

Filling/vialing (small vials etc.)

D. 冻干/低温保存

Lyophilization/cold storage

E. 包装、标签、批号、合规文档归档

Packaging, labeling, batch documentation

1.产品表征与合规思路 Product Characterization and Regulatory Compliance
  • A产品一级/高级结构要与设计一致

Structural identity must match intended design

B. 纯度无聚集体，活性可复现

Purity
no aggregates, reproducible activity

C. 病毒、微生物等杂质小于法规限值

Viruses/microbes under regulatory threshold

D. 每批追踪号、文件归档，可应对审计

Full batch traceability and regulatory records

1.工艺优化与研发节点 Process Optimization & R&D Control Points
  • A各核心节点均要小试→中试→放大工艺验证

Lab scale → pilot scale → scale-up process validation

B. 关键过程参数设定与归档（如温度、pH、添加剂浓度等）

Key process parameters set and recorded (temp, pH, feeds, etc.)

C. 设定工艺控制点，建成工艺流程卡与审计追踪链

Workflow cards and audit chains

1
质量表征、法规与行业趋势小结 QC, Regulation and Industry Trend Summary
- 下游工艺越来越综合
需多步纯化、严格除杂，并与法规要求紧密结合

- 质控点贯穿全过程，数据留档和批间一致性最为关键

- 表格资料与工艺流程“中英对照”，便于国际申报/合规与查阅

1.工艺优化与生产放大
  • A小规模实验室工艺验证
→ B. 工艺优化
关键参数如温度、pH、溶氧等范围摸索
→ C. 放大试验
逐级扩大体积，记录及修正流程

→ D. 批量生产与记录，每批留存收率、纯度、活性等溯源数据

→ E. 工艺放大的关键
保持质控、一致性与法规合规[@ref_36][@ref_45]

1.下游工艺关键参数与监控 Critical Process Parameters and Monitoring
  • A工艺各环节需设定关键控制点（如pH、温度、溶氧、流速）
  • B实时监控（inline sensors），确保工艺偏差可控
  • C记录所有参数，便于溯源/申报/一致性评估
  • D有工艺偏差（如温度突变、色谱失灵）需要立刻评估对终产品影响

1.病毒安全与监管节点（含典型检测项目表）Viral Safety Steps & Test Table
  • A细胞库建立前、生产/纯化中、成品后反复病毒检测
  • B检测项目包括：

- 动物源/人源病毒（HIV, HBV, HCV...)

- 植物/真菌/细菌病毒

- 内源/支原体污染等

C. 专用病毒去除/灭活工艺：

- 低pH、溶剂/去垢剂、纳滤等

- 定期批间/批内病毒PCR、感染性试验[@ref_44][@ref_47][@ref_52]

1.纯化工艺与杂质去除步骤表 Purification Steps & Impurity Removal
  • A离心/过滤：去细胞杂质
  • B盐析/超滤/缓冲更换：初步去杂
  • C主体层析（亲和/离子交换/凝胶）：高效纯化
  • D病毒过滤/灭活：病毒安全
  • E终灭菌/分装/冻干：无菌及稳定化

1.产品表征与稳定性分析Characteristics & Stability
  • A一级/高级结构：AA测序、CD、NMR等验证设计一致性
  • B功能活性：结合/细胞/动物实验检定
  • C样品纯度与杂质含量HPLC/SDS-PAGE/质谱等方法
  • D稳定性：加速、长期（温度、光等）实验确认产品合格性、货架期
8.工艺与产品法规合规总结 Compliance & Documentation
  • A所有批次工艺参数、样品检测、原材料/人员均全程追踪
  • B留样管理和数据归档，定期批间一致性比对
  • C产品注册/申报前须全部质控/安全/表征/工艺记录齐全，并通过GLP/GMP审查

03Study Guide（英文）

MOLECULAR TOOLS IN BIOTECHNOLOGY - AN OVERVIEW

Biotechnology

The application of science and technology to living organisms, as well as parts, products and models

Alter living or non-living materials for the production of knowledge, goods and services.

Genetic engineering

  • Use, modify, or reproduce the genomes of living organisms
  • Manipulate genetic material for the purposes of:
    • Therapy
    • Research
    • Enhance farming efficiency
    • Diagnostic
    • Prognostic
    • Cloning for comfort (pets)

The concept of transformation - The Giffrick experiment

  • 1928: Frederick Griffith demonstrate transformation in Streptococcus pneumoniae
  • Pathogenicity: Due to key enzyme involved in the synthesis of bacterial capsule
  • Two different alleles:
    • Smooth allele (S): smooth surface → pathogenic
    • Rough allele (R) → rough surface → non-pathogenic
  • Four different mice injection treatments:
    • Treatment 1: Living S strain (pathogenic) → Die
    • Treatment 2: Living R strain (non-pathogenic) → Lives
    • Treatment 3: Heat treated S strain → Lives
    • Treatment 4: Heat treated S + Living R → Dies

→ A chemical substance from 1 cell has the ability to transform another cell

DNA is the nature of the transforming substance

1944
Avery, MacLeod & McCarty

Culture S. pneumoniae (S strain - pathogenic)

Heat kill, homogenize + R strain → Transformation occurs

Treat with protease + R strain → Transformation occurs

Treat with ribonuclease + R strain → Transformation occurs

Treat with deoxyribonuclease + R strain → Don’t transform

→ The chemical nature of the transforming substance is DNA

DNA structure & organization

DNA
deoxyribonuclease
RNA
ribonuclease

→ Polymers of nucleotides

Nucleotide

Sugar
deoxyribose (DNA) / ribose (RNA)

Phosphate group

Nitrogenous base

Nitrogenous bases

  • Purines (2 rings → larger)
    • Adenine (A)
    • Guanine (G)
  • Pyrimidines (1 ring → smaller)
    • Cytosine (C)
    • Uracil (U)
    • Thymine (T)

Nucleotide metabolism - Biosynthesis pathways of nucleic acids

  • De novo pathway:
    • Synthesis of nucleic acids from amino acids
    • Requires ATP
    • High in proliferating tissues (bone marrow, gut, tumors)
  • Salvage pathway:
    • Recycled bases / nucleosides
    • Low ATP expenditure
    • High in non-dividing tissues (brain, RBCs)

Hybridoma selection by HAT medium

Hybridoma
B cell + myeloma (cancer) cell

→ Produce antibody without dying

  • HAT medium purpose: hybridoma selection after B cell and myeloma cell fusion
  • Properties of cells:
    • B cell: HGPRT+
    • Myeloma cell: HGPRT-
  • HAT medium components:
    • Hypoxanthine: purine precursor
    • Aminopterin: blocks de novo synthesis
    • Thymine: pyrimidine precursor
  • During selection:
    • B cell: HGPRT+ → Die after a while (not-immortal)
    • Myeloma cell: HGPRT- → Die (no de-novo synthesis)
    • Hybridoma: HGPRT+ & immortal → Live forever

DNA double helix

2 complementary & antiparallel strand

Bacterial DNA
unique, circular, supercoiled (due to the action of gyrase - type II topoisomerase)
Eukaryotic DNA
linear DNA double helix, histone & non-histone proteins

Chromatin = DNA + histone protein + non-histone proteins

Nucleosome
Chromatin coiled around 8 histones

Study methods of nucleic acids

Extraction & purification

Denaturation & renaturation

Chemical & enzymatic hydrolysis

Action of modification enzymes

Physico-chemical properties

  • Water soluble
  • Precipitate in EtOH
  • Insoluble in organic solvents
  • DNA extraction & purification
    • Chemical lysis: break the cellular membrane & allow content to release
    • Binding: allow nucleic acid binding to the column
    • Washing: wash out other components, allowing only DNA to bind to the silica column
    • Elution: release the DNA into the elution buffer → concentrated DNA solution

Charge

  • Negatively charged at pH 7
  • Separation according to size:
    • Electrophoresis:
      • Agarose gel, TAE / TBE buffer
      • Resolution: 20 - 30 b
  • SDS-PAGE:
    • Acrylamide gel, resolution: 1 b

Electrophoresis

Separation of nucleic acids based on their size

DNA, RNA: negative charge at pH 7

Migrate from (-) electrode to (+) electrode

Separate according to size in agarose / acrylamide gel

SDS-PAGE

  • Sodium dodecyl sulfate polyacrylamide gel electrophoresis
  • Separation of macroproteins from 5 - 250 kDa
  • Sodium dodecyl sulfate: anion detergent
    • Denatures & linearizes proteins
    • Mask the proteins’ intrinsic charges → Apply an even negative charge to the proteins

→ Separate proteins according to their sizes

DNA & pH

pH < 2
DNA degradation into phosphate, bases & d-ribose
2 < pH < 7
apurinic sites (sites missing purine bases due to glycosidic bond cleavage, is prone to mutations)
pH > 7
DNA denaturation → ssDNA

RNA & pH

Acidic / alkaline pH can degrade ssRNA into phosphate, bases, and ribose

Spectral properties

Nucleic acids show an absorption peak at 260 nm

UV absorption by the cyclic structure of nitrogenous bases

Reversible denaturation capacity

Progressive denaturation → renaturation is possible

Aggressive denaturation → renaturation is impossible

Thermal denaturation & Hyperchromicity

  • Hyperchromicity: At 260 nm, ssDNA absorbs more UV light than dsDNA (unstacked, exposed bases absorb more UV light)
  • Melting temperature (Tm):
    • The temperature at which 50% of a DNA solution is ssDNA.
    • Greater GC content → Higher melting temperature

DNA replication

  • DNA synthesis: DNA polymerase
  • Matrix: ssDNA
  • Primer: short oligonucleotide (DNA / RNA), paired with template
  • Precursors: dNTP (dATP, dCTP, dGTP, dTTP) - nucleotides
    • dNTP: deoxyribonucleoside triphosphate
    • dATP: deoxyadenosine triphosphate
    • dCTP: deoxycytidine triphosphate
    • dGTP: deoxyguanosine triphosphate
    • dTTP: deoxythymidine triphosphate

Nitrogenous bases, Nucleotides & Nucleosides

  • Nucleotide: sugar + nitrogenous base + phosphate group
    • dNTP: deoxyribonucleoside triphosphate
    • dATP: deoxyadenosine triphosphate
    • dCTP: deoxycytidine triphosphate
    • dGTP: deoxyguanosine triphosphate
    • dTTP: deoxythymidine triphosphate
  • Nucleoside: sugar + nitrogenous base
    • Adenine (base) → Adenosine
    • Guanine (base) → Guanosine
    • Thymine (base) → Thymidine
    • Cytosine (base) → Cytidine

Sanger sequencing

  • Elongation termination method
  • Each tube: DNA, DNA polymerase, dNTP & 1 type of ddNTP
  • dd: di deoxy (removed another -OH group → termination of elongation when ddNTP is reached)
  • Read Sanger sequencing result
    • Read bottom of the gel up (5’ - 3’ of the other strand)
    • Reverse complement → 5’ - 3’ of the template strand

→ Still the standard sequencing method today

RAS-V12

G → T point mutation → Gly → Val (in the HRAS gene)

Point mutation that leads to carcinoma oncogene

Sequencing

Evolution towards high-throughput Next Generation Sequencing (NGS) → Whole genome sequencing, Exome sequencing, RNA-seq, CHIP-seq, SNP/Indel

Next Generation Sequencing

Library preparation — fragment nucleic acids and ligate sequencing adapters.

Cluster generation / amplification — amplify library fragments on the flow cell.

Sequencing-by-synthesis — detect bases as they are incorporated.

Bioinformatics analysis — perform read alignment, variant calling, and annotation.

Comparative genomic hybridization - CGH

  • DNA microarray - DNA chip
  • DNA microarray principle:
  • Fluorescence label:
    • Patients DNA: red
    • Reference DNA: green
  • Hybridize, wash & scan:
    • Normal: green = red → yellow
    • Deletion: green (gene is only present in reference DNA)
    • Duplication: red (gene is only present in patient DNA)
  • Applications:
    • Detect copy number variations (CNVs) such as deletions and duplications.
    • Identify chromosomal imbalances in congenital disorders and developmental delay.
    • Profile tumor genomes to find gains, losses, and amplifications in cancer.

Tumor mutational burden & signature percentages

TMB tells you how many mutations the tumor carries (mutations per megabase).

Mutational signatures tell you why those mutations occurred (e.g., UV, smoking, MMR deficiency).

Together, they show the tumor’s mutation load + its underlying mutational processes.

Real-time qPCR

  • Quantitative method to quantify the amplified amount of DNA
  • CT (threshold cycle) is the cycle number at which fluorescence first crosses the detection threshold.
    • Lower CT = more starting template
    • Higher CT = less starting template
  • Sybr Green: Quencher - Reporter model
    • The probe has a fluorophore and a quencher close together, so no light is emitted.
    • When Taq polymerase cuts the probe during PCR, the fluorophore is released.
    • Once separated from the quencher, the fluorophore lights up, showing that the target was amplified.

DNA array applications

Identify modified expression of genes between experimental samples

Organize samples according to expression profiles

Establish expression profiles of diseases & stages of diseases

Detect mutations / single-nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs)

Pathogen analysis

→ Transcriptome analysis

The “omics” cascade

Genomics
what may happen
Transcriptomics
what seems to happen
Proteomics
what makes this happen
Metabolomics
what happened

Genetic engineering

  • 1972: Creation of the first recombinant DNA by Paul Berg
  • Recombinant DNA → Recombinant protein
  • Expression vector:
    • Promoter + regulatory elements — drive transcription of the inserted gene.
    • Multiple cloning site (MCS) — region containing restriction sites for gene insertion.
    • Selection marker + origin of replication — allow selection and replication of the plasmid in host cells.
