BIOTECHNOLOGY MOCK EXAM

Glycosylation

  • Is a major post-translational modification (PTM)
  • 1 glycoprotein = mixture of different glycoforms in a given & constant proportion
  • Why is it important for biopharmaceuticals?
    • Solubility & stability of product
    • Affects folding of protein
    • Ensuring the bioactivity of the biopharmaceutical:
      • Cell recognition (ligand - receptor binding)
      • Pharmacokinetics
      • in vivo clearance
      • Antigenicity

Two types of glycosylation

N-glycosylation

Happens on Asparagine (Asn).

Requires the motif Asn-X-Ser/Thr (X ≠ Pro).

Starts in the ER, continues in the Golgi.

A big sugar tree is added all at once (Glc₃Man₉GlcNAc₂).

All N-glycans share a common core (Man₃GlcNAc₂).

Glycans are large and branched.

Common on antibodies (Fc region), hormones, secreted proteins.

Important for
folding, stability, trafficking, immune effector functions, half-life.

Very important in QC: glycan mapping, CZE, HILIC, MS.

Strong impact on PK/PD.

The 3 Types of N-Glycans

  • Oligomannose N-glycans
  • Mostly mannose residues on the core.
  • The structure stays “mannose-rich.”
  • Not many additional sugars (Gal, GlcNAc, Sialic acid).
  • Early glycan processing stage.
  • Complex N-glycans
    • Mannose residues are trimmed
    • The glycan is extended with:
      • GlcNAc
      • Galactose
      • Sialic acid
      • Fucose
  • Highly branched, most diverse.
  • Very important for PK, stability, and immunogenicity.
  • Hybrid N-glycans
  • A mix of both types:
    • One branch looks like high-mannose
    • The other branch looks complex
  • Represents an intermediate processing pattern.

O-glycosylation

Happens on Serine or Threonine (Ser/Thr).

No specific consensus sequence.

Occurs only in the Golgi.

Sugars are added one-by-one, not pre-assembled.

The first sugar is GalNAc.

O-glycans are shorter and more variable.

Common in mucins, membrane glycoproteins, some receptors.

Important for
protease resistance, local stability, cell–cell interactions.

QC relies on glycopeptide LC-MS/MS, or β-elimination to release O-glycans.

More limited impact on PK but important for local structure and protection.

Question 1
Strategies to improve a therapeutic protein by acting on its glycosylation, illustrate each strategy by an example
BIOBETTERS
STRATEGIES TO IMPROVE BIOLOGICALS

Definition of biobetters:

Improved visions of an existing biological drug

Improve its pharmacokinetics / efficacy / mode of administration / toxicity / immunogenicity

Reasons to improve therapeutic proteins:

Increase stability

Increase solubility

Pharmacokinetics, pharmacodynamics

Efficacy

Patient compliance (administration route, frequency)

Reduce production cost

A biobetter may provide one or more of the following advantages over the reference biologic

Greater efficacy

Greater purity

Longer product half-life

Less frequent dosing

Lower likelihood of aggregation

Fewer adverse events

Streamlined manufacturing

Longer shelf-life and greater stability

Easier administration & packaging improvements

Improvement of pharmacokinetic properties of a recombinant protein - Human interferon-alpha (IFN-α)

Question 1
Human interferon-alpha (h-INF-α)
问What is human interferon-alpha (h-INF-α)?
答

Type I antiviral cytokine

Produced by plasmacytoid dendritic cells

Binds to the IFNAR receptor → Activate the JAK-STAT pathway

→ Induce IFN-stimulated genes that block viral replication & modulate the immune response

问What is its mechanism of action?
答
  • Bind to receptor on target cells: INF-α binds to the cell-surface receptor IFNAR (type I interferon receptor)
  • Activation of JAK/STAT pathway
    • JAK1 & TYK2 (associated kinases) get activated
    • Phosphorylate transcription factors STAT1 & STAT2
    • STAT1 & STAT2 dimerizes, then associate with IRF9 to form the transcriptional complex ISGF3 (Interferon-Stimulated Gene Factor 3)
  • Translocation to the nucleus & induction of antiviral genes
    • ISGR3 complex translocates to cell nucleus, binds to ISRE (Interferon-Stimulated Response Elements)
    • Production of proteins that degrade viral RNA, inhibit viral protein synthesis, block viral particle assembly or release
问How is recombinant h-INF-α administered?
答

Subcutaneous (SC), Intramuscular (IM), or intravenously (IV)

问What is its half-life time?
答

2 - 3 hours after SC or IM injection

Question 2: Pegylation of human interferon-alpha (h-INF-α)

问What does pegylation mean?
答
  • PEGylation: covalently attaching polyethylene glycol (PEG) chains to a therapeutic protein
  • Purposes:
    • Increase the stability & half-life of h-INF-α (increase size)
    • Decrease immunogenicity
    • Increase resistance to proteolysis

Comment the figure

The figures show the plasma concentration of different recombinant protein products

PEGylation increases the volume of the recombinant proteins, increasing the half-life time of the product in the human body

Branched PEGylation is more effective in increasing half-life time than linear PEGylation, as seen in the final figure.

Other PEGylated pharmaceutical products

  • Pegasys ® (PEG-IFN-α2a)
    • 40 kDa branched PEG
    • Treatment of Hepatitis B & C
  • PegIntron ® (PEG-IFN-α2b)
    • 12 kDa linear PEG
    • Treatment of Hepatitis C

Pegylation

  • Non-toxic, hydrophilic, uncharged molecule
  • Increase half-life in vivo (4 - 400x)
  • Reduce the risk of immunogenicity
  • Increase resistance to proteases
  • Improves protein stability & solubility
  • Sites of PEGylation (virtually any site)
    • N-terminal
    • C-terminal
    • Amino acid
    • Disulfide bonds
  • Variability of PEGylation
    • Linear or branched PEGs
    • Variable size
    • Variable positions
    • Variable chemistry

Approved PEGylated proteins

Interferon alfa-2a → Chronic hepatitis B & C

Interferon alfa-2b → Chronic hepatitis C

Erythropoietin → Anemia

Human growth hormone → Growth hormone deficiency

Coagulation factor VIII → Hemophilia A

Question 3: Interferon-CTP (C-terminal peptide)

问What is a fusion protein?
答
  • Fusion protein: a protein created by joining 2 different protein sequences at the DNA level so that they are expressed as one continuous protein
  • Purpose of fusion proteins:
    • Extend half-life (by increasing the size)
    • Improve stability of the recombinant protein
问What is the C-terminal peptide (CTP)?
答

Short amino-acid sequence naturally found at the C-terminus of the β-subunit of human chorionic gonadotropin (hCG)

CTP from hCG has multiple O-glycosylation sites

→ CTP adds extra O-glycosylation

→ Increases half-life of recombinant protein

问How is IFN-CTP obtained?
答

Genetic fusion at the DNA level of the genes expressing interferon-alpha and C-terminal peptide in hCG

Insert the fused gene into an expression vector, transfect into mammalian host cell (CHO cells)

Expression in a mammalian host cell → Perform O-glycosylation

Host cell secretion of IFN-CTP

问What properties of the therapeutic molecule are targeted by the addition of CTP?
答

Addition of multiple O-glycosylation with sialic acids

→ Increase hydrodynamic size

→ Prevent rapid renal clearance

→ Protection from proteolysis

→ Decrease immunogenicity

The results of this pre-clinical studies

CTP addition to IFN increases the circulation time of the recombinant protein

The addition site of CTP has an effect on IFN

More CTP → Higher plasma circulation time (bigger size)

However, more is not necessarily better. The CTP attachment site affects the bioactivity of CTP-IFN, and more CTP sites decrease the bioactivity of CTP-IFN (CTP-IFN-CTP)

Question 4: Albuferon

Albuferon: single polypeptide molecule combining the sequence of interferon alpha with human albumin serum (HSA)

问What is the strategy behind the development of Albuferon?
答
  • Genetic fusion of HSA gene & interferon-alpha gene
  • Insert the fused gene into an expression vector, transfect the vector into a mammalian host cell
  • Mammalian host cell will secrete Albuferon (fused HSA & interferon-alpha) with the correct PTMs
  • Why?
    • HSA: high half-life (456 hours - 19 days)
    • Interferon-alpha half-life: 5 hours

→ Fusion to increase half-life

Analyze and make comments about the figure

  • UV-CD: ultraviolet circular dichroism, used to analyze the secondary & tertiary structure of proteins
  • Mechanism: Measure the different absorption of left- and right- circularly polarized UV light
  • This picture shows the spectra of HSA-IFN combined with the spectral sums of HSA and IFN
  • Analysis:
    • α-helix: double minima at 208 & 222 nm
    • β-sheet: single minimum around 215 nm
    • Random coil: minimum around 198 nm

→ Confirm correct folding, compare structural integrity & detect structural changes

Fusion proteins & biobetters

  • Fusion with protein
  • Fusion with Fc: human IgG Fc region (half-life: 14 days)
  • Polyamino acid fusion protein
  • Fusion with CTP
  • Many biobetters are in clinical trials right now
  • Purposes
    • Increase in size and hydrodynamic radius
    • Recycling via FcRn
    • Increase of negative charges via sialylation

(sialylation: addition of sialic acid residues at the terminal ends of N- or O-linked glycans → increases half-life & stability)

Question 5: Albuferon - Figure 4 analysis

Albuferon has comparable antiviral activity compared to other IFN biobetter strategies.

The relative antiviral potency is given on a molar basis, given the differences in molecular weight of several IFN formulations

Different strategies to improve therapeutic proteins

  • Modifications of the coding sequence
    • Mutations, deletions
    • Fusion proteins
    • Humanization (of antibodies)
    • Modification of glycosylation sites
  • Non-translational engineering
    • Conjugation with hydrophilic polymers
    • Conjugation to an active molecule (cytotoxic)
    • Modification of already formed glycan chains
    • Lipid binding
  • New production technologies
    • Host cell engineering - Glycosylation improvement (glycoengineering)
  • New formulations & new routes of administration

Examples of commercially available biobetters

Erythropoietin alpha → ARANESP ® → Reduce dosing frequency

Filgrastim → NEULASTA ® → Reduce chemotherapy cycle

Follicle stimulating hormone → ELONVA ® → Reduce injection time

Trastuzumab → KADCYLA ® → Improved breast cancer treatment efficacy

Rituximab → GAZVYA ® → Improved pharmacokinetics

Biobetter summary

Strategies for biobetter development from a native protein:

PEGylation: + PEG chain

Lipidation: + FA chain

Glycosylation: + oligosaccharide

Fc-fusion: + Human IgG Fc domain

Albumin fusion: +HSA (human serum albumin)

Current challenges of biobetter development

Decreased tissue uptake

Immunogenicity

Functional heterogeneity

Modified functional activity

QUALITY CONTROL OF BIOPHARMACEUTICALS

Biopharmaceuticals

  • Pharmaceuticals manufactured by biotechnology methods
  • Source: biological (involving organisms / active components)
    • Extraction from animal tissues / human fluids
    • Cell culture production:
      • Prokaryotic cells: bacteria
      • Eukaryotic cells: fungi, insects, animals
    • From transgenic animals or plants
    • Living organisms (larvae, eggs, …)
  • Classification:
    • Not obtained from recombinant DNA
      • Vaccines
      • Toxins & Antibiotics
      • Enzymes, Hormones & Polyclonal Abs
      • Blood-derived proteins
      • Cell therapies
    • Obtained from recombinant DNA
      • Therapeutic proteins (Growth Factors, Hormones, Cytokines, Enzymes, Blood factors)
      • Monoclonal antibodies
      • Recombinant therapeutic vaccines

Therapeutic proteins are different compared to chemically synthesized molecules

Size difference: much bigger than normal drug molecules

High structural complexity (MANDATORY for their activity)

Heterogeneity (1 glycoprotein = mixture of glycoforms in a CONSTANT proportion)

Production methods

Glycosylation

  • Major post translational modification (PTM)
  • Important for biopharmaceuticals:
    • Improve solubility & stability
    • Enhance protein folding
    • Improve biological activity
      • Improve targeting (ligand - receptor interaction)
      • Pharmacokinetics
      • Slower in vivo clearance rate
      • Effect on antigenicity
  • N-glycosylation:
    • Adds a glycan to the nitrogen (N) of an Asparagine (Asn) residue in the Asn-X-Ser/Thr consensus sequence
    • Occurs in the ER → Golgi apparatus
    • Produces large branched glycans
    • Strongly affects folding, stability & half-life
    • Three types of N-glycans:
      • Oligomannose N-glycans: least processed type, only mannose residues
      • Complex N-glycans: fully processed branches, containing sugars like GlcNAc, galactose, fucose & sialic acid
      • Hybrid N-glycans: somewhere in between, 1 branch is mannose rich, the other is complex
  • O-glycosylation:
    • Adds a short glycan to the oxygen (O) of Serine (Ser) or Threonine (Thr) side chains
    • Starts in the Golgi apparatus
    • Shorter glycans, more diverse → High variability

Quality control of recombinant proteins

  • Is necessary to ensure safety & efficiency
  • Active product is heterogenous & structurally complex
  • Needs several complementary techniques to address:
    • Purity
    • Identity
    • Dosage
    • Stability
    • Activity
  • At which step?
    • In process (performed at critical decision-making steps)
    • Purified protein
    • Formulated product
    • Compounding product

Quality control - Objectives

  • Quality control (QC):
    • Assess and ensure the safety & efficacy of the product
    • Verify lot-to-lot consistency
  • Objectives of quality control:
    • Identity test:
      • Making sure the product batch contains the correct therapeutic protein

→ Might decrease biological activity or generate

unwanted immunogenicity

    • Purity:
      • The biopharmaceutical product must be free of contaminating substance

→ Impact protein performance, immunogenicity

  • Dosage:
    • Determine exact purified product concentration in the final product

→ Correct amount of API delivered to patient

  • Stability:
    • Evaluate how well the protein product tolerate external stressors
    • Define optimal storage condition

Evaluation of the desired product is a challenge because

  • A protein is a complex & heterogenous mixture
  • Many impurities:
    • Process-related impurities:
      • Cell-derived: host cell proteins, nucleic acids, endotoxins
      • Cell-culture medium-derived: inducers, antibiotics, enzymes, serum, bacteria
      • Downstream-derived: chemicals & biochemicals, inorganic salts, solvents, residual components
    • Product-related impurities:
      • Related forms & variants: deamidation, oxidation, isomerisation, racemization, glycated, altered PTMs
      • Truncated forms, fragments
      • Dimers, oligomers, aggregates
      • Misfolded / Unfolded proteins
      • Cys-linked variants, scrambling

Degradation pathways

Chemical degradation pathways: deamidation, isomerization, oxidation, fragments / oligomers

Physical degradation pathways: aggregates, oligomers

Complementary techniques required for QC

  • Electrophoresis-based techniques
    • Gel electrophoresis
    • Capillary electrophoresis (CE)
    • Capillary electrophoresis - Mass spectrometry (CE-MS)
  • Liquid chromatography techniques
    • All modes / Mass spectrometry (MS)
  • Spectrophotometric techniques: Circular dichroism, Fluorescence, UV absorbance, IRTF, DSC, light diffusion, …

Three approaches for mAb analysis

Top-down: Intact mAb → mass, overall heterogeneity

Middle-up: Large Ab fragments → Fc glycans, charge variants, regional modifications

Bottom-up: Peptides (digested mAbs) → Sequence confirmation, site-specific PTMs

Glycosylation analysis can be performed at different levels

Intact proteins

Glycopeptides

Released glycans

Monosaccharides

Quality control methods

问How do charge variant analysis of monoclonal antibodies by IEX-HPLC contribute to their QC?
答
  • Charge variant analysis of mAbs
    • mAbs are heterogeneous molecules. Even if they have the same amino acid sequence, they can differ in charge because of:
      • PTMs (deamidation, C-terminal lysine clipping, glycosylation, sialylation)
      • Chemical degradation (oxidation, N-terminal pyroglutamate)
      • Manufacturing conditions (temperature, pH, cell culture process)
  • IEX-HPLC:
    • Separate molecules based on net surface charge
    • Cation exchange (CEX): binds positively charged variants (common for mAbs) (column is negatively charged)
    • Anion exchange (AEX): binds negatively charged variants (column is positively charged)
    • Elutes variants from weakest to strongest binding
  • How this contributes to QC:
    • Confirms product identity & consistency: Each commercial mAb has a characteristic charge-variant profile → Ensure batch-to-batch consistency
    • Detect critical quality attributes (CQAs): Many charge variants directly affect the potency, stability & immunogenicity (acidic variants may indicate fragmentation, basic variants may indicate amidation or incomplete processing)
    • Monitor degradation during manufacturing & storage: Detect changes caused by temperature stress, freeze-thaw cycles, oxidation → Used in stability studies
问What does size exclusion chromatography (SEC-HPLC) bring to the purity control of therapeutic proteins?
答
  • Size exclusion chromatography:
    • Diffusion through a column matrix of porous beads (no adsorption)
    • Bigger molecules can only move around the beads at a slow pace → travel slower
    • Smaller molecules can travel around and between the pores of the beads → travel faster
    • Enables detection of impurities that can affect the safety & stability of proteins
    • Detection with UV detector → Chromatogram with distinct peaks
    • Strong reproducibility, no need binding interactions
    • Lower selectivity compared to other methods, limited separation for similar-sized proteins
  • Purity control of therapeutic proteins:
    • Quantification of percentage of monomer vs. impurities
    • Detection of aggregation (dimer, trimer, or oligomer formation)
    • Detection of protein fragmentation
问What kind of chemical degradation variants of therapeutic proteins can be controlled by RP-HPLC?
答
  • RP-HPLC:
    • Reverse-phase high performance liquid chromatography
    • Uses hydrophobic stationary phase & hydrophilic mobile phase
    • Separation mechanism:
      • For small molecules:
        • Continuous partitioning, separated based on hydrophobic interaction
        • Hydrophilic molecules elute first, more hydrophobic ones elute later
      • For polypeptides:
        • Adsorb to the hydrophobic phase
        • Desorb from the surface at critical organic modifier concentration → gradient elution
  • Chemical degradation variants of therapeutic proteins
    • Can detect chemical degradation variants that alter protein hydrophobicity, such as oxidation, deamidation, isomerization, N/C-terminal clipping, peptide-bond hydrolysis, and certain soluble aggregates or glycation forms → Appear as shifted or additional peaks in the chromatogram and are key QC markers in therapeutic protein analysis
问How does peptide mapping of therapeutic proteins by RP-HPLC contribute to their identity control?
答
  • Peptide mapping:
    • A rapid protein fingerprinting method
    • Uses an analytical technique to characterize the primary structure of proteins
    • The analyzed therapeutic protein is denatured, linearized, then digested with enzymatic reactions
    • RP-HPLC is performed, then the peak heights & location are compared to the reference sequence to verify protein identity
    • Protocol:
      • Evaporate & dissolve IgG
      • Reduce disulfide bonds
      • S-Carboxymethylate
      • Desalt & exchange into digestion buffer
      • Digest with trypsin
      • Run RP-HPLC
      • Result analysis
  • RP-HPLC:
    • Reverse-phase high performance liquid chromatography
    • Uses hydrophobic stationary phase & hydrophilic mobile phase
    • Separation mechanism:
      • For small molecules:
        • Continuous partitioning, separated based on hydrophobic interaction
        • Hydrophilic molecules elute first, more hydrophobic ones elute later
      • For polypeptides:
        • Adsorb to the hydrophobic phase
        • Desorb from the surface at critical organic modifier concentration → gradient elution
  • In QC context:
    • Identity test for routine QC
    • Structural characterization technique

→ Identity control of the produced therapeutic protein

问How can capillary zone electrophoresis be used for the glycosylation assessment of therapeutic glycoproteins?
答
  • Capillary zone electrophoresis
    • Separates molecules based on their charge-to-size ratio in an electric field
    • Glycans strongly influence charge, especially sialic acids, which add negative charges

→ CZE can resolve glycoforms as different electrophoretic mobility peaks

  • Glycosylation assessment of therapeutic glycoproteins - CZE can be used to measure:
    • Sialylation level: The more sialylated a glycoform is, the more negative it is → The faster it migrates toward the anode

→ Important because sialylation impacts half-life, clearance, and receptor binding

Overall glycoform distribution: CZE can resolve high-mannose variants, complex glycoforms, hybrid types, partially processed glycoforms

Charge variant profiling: Glycans contribute to acidic charge variants → Detects acidic / basic glycoforms (more / less sialylated)

→ Charge heterogeneity

Batch-to-batch comparison: CZE fingerprints are used to compare biosimilars & their reference product, monitor manufacturing changes, evaluate sample stability

问Why can capillary gel electrophoresis be used for both identity and purity control of therapeutic proteins?
答
  • Capillary gel electrophoresis:
    • Separates proteins by their molecular size in a sieving polymer matrix
    • Similar to other electrophoresis techniques but with higher resolution
    • This size-based separation allows CGE to verify what the protein is (identity) and which impurities are present (purity)
  • Purity control & Identity control
    • Identity control: CGE generates a reproducible electropherogram peaks that correspond to the proteins molecular weight, expected subunits & migration times

→ Confirms identity by matching the protein’s size profile to

a reference standard

Purity control: CGE resolves impurities & degradation products (fragmentation, aggregates, mispaired chains, process impurities, …)

→ Quantifies purity & detects degradation pathways

  • Why CGE is useful in QC:
    • High resolution: detection of low-abundance variants
    • Highly quantitative: directly reflects mass fraction, enabling precise purity determination
    • Automated & compliant

ERYTHROPOIETIN CASE STUDY

Part 1: rhEPO and plasmid construct

问1. What is the in vivo physiological effect of EPO? What are the indications for using EPO therapy?
答
  • Physiological effect of EPO: Stimulates erythropoiesis by promoting survival, proliferation and differentiation of erythroid precursors in the bone marrow
  • Indications:
    • Treatment of anemia (CKD related anemia, chemo-induced anemia)
    • Treatment of chronic inflammatory / hematologic disorders
1
Structural characteristics of EPO

165-aa glycoprotein with 3 N-glycosylation sites & 1 O-glycosylation site (as shown on the primary structure of EPO)

问1. Does EPO undergo post-translational modifications? Which ones?
答

Yes, EPO undergoes N-glycosylation, O-glycosylation, and terminal sialylation, which strongly affect solubility, stability & in vivo clearance

问1. What type of host cell is required for this post-translational modification?
答

A mammalian expression system, especially CHO cells, is required to obtain human-like glycosylation

问1. What are the specificities of the plasmid used?
答

CMV promoter for high transcription level

EPO cDNA inserted at the restriction site

Selection gene for stable cell selection

Poly A signal for correct mRNA termination

问1. How can this specific construct be created from commercial plasmid?
答

RE digestion of the backbone, insertion of the EPO cDNA, and ligation, followed by transformation into host cells

问1. What methods can be used to control the recombinant plasmid?
答

Restriction digest analysis → Band patterns after RE digest

Colony PCR → Confirm the presence and insert site

Sanger sequencing → Verify sequence integrity

PRODUCTION OF THERAPEUTIC INTEREST BIOMOLECULES

Bioproduction: The production of biologics-based therapeutic drugs

Biodrug:

Drug substance produced / extracted from a biological source

Well characterized & described manufacturing & purification process

Characterization & quality assessment (a combination of physical chemical & biological assays)

Biodrug classification

  • Natural bioproducts
    • Vaccines, enzymes, toxins, drugs from blood extraction
    • Cell & tissue therapies
  • Recombinant DNA technology
    • Nucleic acids
    • Recombinant proteins
      • Oligonucleotides & plasmids
      • DNA vaccines
      • Gene therapies
      • Therapeutic vaccines
  • Biodrugs origin:
    • Extraction: from human / animal tissues & fluids
    • Cell substances:
      • Prokaryotic → Bacteria
      • Eukaryotic → Fungi, insects, mammals, multiple secretion types:
      • Natural secretions: toxins or bacterial enzymes (botulism toxin, tetanus toxin, streptokinase, …)
      • Recombinant secretions after genetic engineering
    • Transgenic animals / plants

012025-10-07（第 1 讲）

第2页：生物药物定义

1
生物药物是指通过生物技术，比如基因重组、细胞培养等生产的大分子药物，例如蛋白、抗体、疫苗等。

Biopharmaceuticals are large molecule drugs produced by biotechnology, such as recombinant DNA techniques and cell culture; examples include proteins, antibodies, and vaccines.

2
与化学合成的小分子药物相比，生物药物具有结构更大、更复杂的特点。

Compared to chemically synthesized small molecule drugs, biopharmaceuticals are larger and more structurally complex.

3
生物药在不同批次之间会有微小差异，这对质量控制提出更高要求。

There can be slight differences between batches of biopharmaceuticals, which raises the standard for quality control.

4
生物药的生产过程包括细胞系构建、细胞培养、提纯、配制和质检等多个环节。

The production process of biopharmaceuticals includes cell line development, cell culture, purification, formulation, and quality checks.

5
老师强调，这个生产流程通常至少持续2~3个月，每一步都可能影响最终产品杂质的种类和含量。

The professor emphasized that this production workflow usually lasts at least 2–3 months, and each step can affect the types and amounts of impurities in the final product

第3页：杂质类型分类

1
生物药物的杂质可以分为工艺相关杂质和产品相关杂质两大类。

Impurities in biopharmaceuticals can be classified into two major categories: process-related and product-related impurities.

2
工艺相关杂质是指来自生产用的宿主细胞（如蛋白、DNA）、培养基成分、反应试剂、内毒素等。

Process-related impurities refer to contaminants from production, such as host cell proteins, DNA, media components, process chemicals, and endotoxins.

3
产品相关杂质主要包括蛋白降解产物、聚集体、错误的糖基化、氧化/脱酰胺/异构体和蛋白片段等。

Product-related impurities mainly consist of protein degradation products, aggregates, incorrect glycosylation, oxidized/deamidated/isomerized variants, and protein fragments.

4
老师举例道，E. coli表达系统常带来细胞碎片、培养基残留、降解小片段等工艺相关杂质。

The professor gave examples, stating that E. coli expression systems often bring process-related impurities like cell debris, medium residues, and small degraded fragments.

5
同时，蛋白本身“脆弱点”决定了其更容易产生哪些产品相关杂质，如易脱酰胺、易氧化、易聚集等。

Meanwhile, the protein’s own "weak spots" determine which product-related impurities are most likely, such as being prone to deamidation, oxidation, or aggregation.

6
控制杂质既要根据流程预判，也要通过应激实验（高温、pH、光照等）测试蛋白的稳定性及相关杂质。

Impurity control requires both anticipation from process knowledge and stress-testing experiments (e.g., heat, pH, light) to assess protein stability and related impurities[

第4页
治疗性蛋白的特殊性（Specificities of Therapeutic Proteins）
1
分子量大、结构复杂
治疗性蛋白和化学合成药最大差别
分子量远大、结构更复杂（如干扰素α有165个氨基酸，分子量19,625Da；阿司匹林仅180Da）。

结构具有一级、二级（α螺旋/β折叠）、三级结构，这些结构的正确折叠决定蛋白活性。 Therapeutic proteins differ significantly from chemical drugs: they are much larger and structurally more complex (interferon α: MW 19,625 Da, aspirin: MW 180 Da) with essential secondary and tertiary structures required for activity.

2
高度异质性（Heterogeneity）

比如糖蛋白是多种不同糖基化形式（glycoforms）的混合物，每批的微小差异都可能影响药物性能。

这类异质性是天然存在且不可避免的，需要严格检测和控制。 Glycoproteins are always a mixture of glycoforms; subtle differences exist even between batches.

3
生产方式不同

大部分生物药依赖活细胞表达，工艺链条长，涉及细胞系构建、培养、纯化、制剂和质控，通常周期需要数月。 Unlike small molecules, biopharmaceuticals are produced in living cells, and manufacturing takes several months from genetic engineering to the final product.

图片说明

[典型图片
结构图、对比]一侧显示小分子药（阿司匹林），一侧展示蛋白质的复杂折叠与分子图（可在复习时脑补或翻看PPT）。
[老师讲解思路]
不断强调“复杂性”和“异质性”是蛋白药管控的核心难点。
第5页
治疗性蛋白的质量属性和控制要点（Quality Attributes & Control）
1
质量属性五大核心

纯度（Purity）：是否纯净，有无杂质，如降解产物、聚集体等。

活性（Activity）：蛋白是否有应有的生物活性功能。

结构完整性与同一性（Identity）：包括一级、二级、三级结构及糖基化等修饰的完整性和可重复性。

含量（Dosage）：定量检测每剂量中的蛋白含量。

稳定性（Stability）：在不同条件（温度、pH、光照等）下，蛋白药的物理和化学稳定性如何。 The five key quality attributes are: purity, activity, identity (structure), dosage, and stability.

2
质量控制的不同环节

生产过程控制（In-process）：在多个生产关键节点快速检测，确保及时发现异常，防止大批量报废。

终产品质控（Purified product/Formulated product）：对每一批次终产品进行深入分析，尤其是对结构和功能要求极高的生物药。

医院末端复配（Compounding）：如抗体等高值药物，医院临用前还会做再次确认。 In-process controls are performed at critical decision steps, while end-product control ensures that the final protein drug meets all quality specifications.

图片说明与讲解思路

[图片]
质量属性的冰山图（冰山上面是我们能检测到的属性，下面是还没检测到/潜在的隐患）。
[老师思路]
用“冰山理论”比喻已知和未知属性；强调检测手段与参考物质（标准品）对比的重要性——如使用FDA/EMA认证参考品或企业自建参考蛋白。

复习小结 & 建议

生物药的复杂性和异质性决定了其质量控制比小分子药物难得多，需多技术手段配合检测。

课上老师提及“每个质量属性都对药效和安全性至关重要”，复习建议结合蛋白结构知识理解每种检测方法的意义。

第6页：重组蛋白的质量控制

Quality control of recombinant proteins

要点总结/Notes：

质量控制是保证安全性与有效性的必要措施。 QC is mandatory to ensure safety and efficacy.

活性产物具有异质性与结构复杂性。 The active product is heterogeneous and structurally complex.

通常需要多种互补技术来进行全面检测，涵盖：纯度(Purity)、同一性(Identity)、含量(Dosage)、稳定性(Stability)、活性(Activity)。 Several complementary techniques are needed to address: purity, identity, dosage, stability, and activity.

问质量控制的环节 (At which step?)
答

生产过程控制 (In-process)： 关键决策节点进行快速检测，确保生产过程中一致性。 In-process tests are performed at critical decision-making steps to confirm process consistency.

纯化蛋白阶段 (Purified protein)

制剂阶段 (Formulated Product)

临床配伍/医院环节 (Compounding product at hospital)

老师讲解角度：

一般会强调“越早发现问题，越能节省成本；多环节质控是保障每一批产品一致性的关键”。

第7页：质量控制的目标与主要检测内容

Quality control - Objectives

总目标(Objectives)

确保治疗性蛋白药的安全性与有效性（assess and ensure safety and efficacy）

验证批次间一致性（verify lot-to-lot consistency）

关键检测项目 (核心四性)：

1
Identity test（同一性鉴定）

确认每批产品是否为正确的治疗性蛋白

检查生物活性和免疫原性 Establish the batch contains the correct therapeutic protein, check bioactivity and immunogenicity

2
Purity（纯度）

是否有杂质（污染物）——直接影响蛋白性能，也可能引发免疫性不良反应 Product must be free of contaminants, which may impact performance or cause serious side effects (immunogenicity)

3
Dosage/含量

精确定量纯化蛋白浓度，确保每位患者接收到准确剂量 Accurately determine protein concentration in final product, ensure correct amount delivered to the patient

4
Stability（稳定性）

评估蛋白在不同应激条件下的稳定性，制定最优储存条件 Evaluate susceptibility to stresses, define optimal storage conditions

教师常提
“生物药复杂且多变，批间一致性靠的就是全流程严格质控。”
第8页
重组蛋白的质量控制的特殊难点The Challenges of Quality Control for Recombinant Proteins核心要点
1
高度复杂和异质（Heterogeneous and Complex）

蛋白不是单一成分，而是多种变体（主成分+各种天然及人工变体+杂质）的混合物。

“纯化”后的蛋白制品，往往仍包含多种微小分子差异的变体（isoforms, glycoforms, etc）。

冰山模型常被用来比喻
能检测的只是“冰山一角”，更多微观异质变化难以完全检测。
与化学药物本质不同
化学合成药（如阿司匹林）结构单一、批间一致。蛋白药则复杂得多。
2
杂质来源多样（Sources of Impurities）

产品相关杂质（Product-Related Impurities）

主体蛋白本身的结构变体
截短、多聚体（聚集）、错误折叠、错配二硫键等。

翻译后修饰变异（如糖基化变异、氧化、去酰胺化等）。

工艺相关杂质（Process-Related Impurities）

来源于宿主细胞、培养基或生产下游化学品等，有些理论上可去除，但难以100%清除。

3
批间一致性难（Batch-to-batch Consistency is Hard）

每次生产都要关注所有这些微小变体，不只是目标分子含量，更要关心所有可能存在的杂质。

多种杂质和微差会直接影响生物活性和安全性，易引起免疫反应甚至疗效失败。

English Highlights

Recombinant proteins are heterogeneous and complex.

QC challenge
Not a single molecule but a mixture (main + variants + impurities).

Both product-related (fragments, aggregates, misfolded, PTMs) and process-related impurities (host cell, medium).

Each batch may have subtle differences; lot-to-lot consistency is critical and hard.

第9页
蛋白药质量控制的目标与检测内容Quality Control Objectives & Key Tests

质量控制的核心目标（QC Objectives）

1
安全性与有效性（Safety & Efficacy）

只有满足质量和一致性，才能保证临床使用安全、有效。

2
验证批间一致性（Lot-to-lot Consistency）

不同批次间保持质量属性一致，否则直接影响疗效与病人安全。

主要检测内容及项目

同一性（Identity）确认是目标蛋白，包括一级序列、部分结构和生物活性、免疫原性鉴定。

纯度（Purity）检查杂质（分解产物、多聚体、碎片等）含量。

含量/剂量（Dosage）定量测定主蛋白的精确浓度，保障患者剂量准确。

稳定性（Stability）评估蛋白在不同外界条件下的稳定性，确定最优储存方式。

老师讲解思路/比喻

用“每一批产品都是一只‘五十指的手’”类比：每一根“手指”都对应蛋白一个细微特征，要确保和参考品完全一致。

每个项目都是“守门员”，只有全部检测通过才能进入市场。

强调制药行业采用权威参考品（FDA、EMA）或自建参考品做对比**40。

English summary

QC’s main goal
Ensure safety, efficacy, batch consistency.

Four key parameters: Identity, purity, dosage, stability.

Essential to compare all batches with a well-characterized reference standard.

Many analytical methods (HPLC, electrophoresis, mass spectrometry, etc.) used in combination.

第9页：化学降解——电荷变体

Charge Variants（老师强调易混、细微变化）

1
常见化学降解机制

脱酰胺（Deamidation）

主要发生在天冬酰胺（Asn）和谷氨酰胺（Gln）残基上。

过程
侧链酰胺水解，生成天冬氨酸（Asp）或谷氨酸（Glu），同时质量+1 Da，带入一个新负电荷。
老师比喻
蛋白质的小变化如“50指的手有1只不一样”，非常难区别，需要高分辨本领。

异构化（Isomerization）

主要在天冬氨酸（Asp）或脱酰胺后，中间体形成琥珀酰亚胺环并可能开环生成isoAsp。

结构变化往往不改变质量但可能影响电荷、蛋白构象和功能。

\text{Asn (N) + H_2O} \rightarrow \text{Asp 　 } (\Delta m = +1 \text{Da})
答案 D
2
老师课堂强化的问题与检测难点

这些电荷差异极其微小，检测“+1 Da”变化需要非常灵敏的仪器（如高分辨质谱、离子交换色谱等）。

虽然这些微小变化很难发现，但可能会导致免疫原性或活性丧失；所以企业和监管机构都高度重视。

3
相关分析方法（技术原理）

离子交换色谱（IEX），区分不同电荷状态变体；最常用@老师提及

等电聚焦电泳（IEF/CIEF）/毛细管电泳（CZE）

HPLC-反相高效液相色谱（RP-HPLC）（脱酰胺也可分离）

质谱（MS），检测微小质量变化（如+1+1 Da，看出deamidation）

第11页
化学降解途径——片段/寡聚体Chemical degradation pathways: Fragments/Oligomers
1
机制与类型 Mechanisms & Types

水解 Hydrolysis

肽键断裂，蛋白主链断裂为片段。

机制
水分子攻击肽键，生成多个小片段。
重点
在贮存、pH、温度等应激下更易发生。

β-消除 β Elimination

Cys, Ser, Thr, Phe, Lys残基易发生，通过β-消除断裂主链。

课堂补充
老师反复强调，一些特定氨基酸序列会更易发生这种降解，是蛋白易“断裂点”。

二肽哌嗪（DKP）形成 Formation of a Diketopiperazine (DKP)

一种N末端特异性降解方式，常见于蛋白加工/储存过程。

二硫键交换/共价寡聚 Covalent Oligomerisation / Exchange of disulfide bond

错误二硫键交换可导致多聚体形成，是片段、寡聚体杂质的来源。

2
检测方法 Analytical Methods

RP-HPLC 反相高效液相色谱

SEC-HPLC 分子排阻色谱（Size Exclusion）

毛细管凝胶电泳（CGE）Capillary gel electrophoresis

质谱分析（MS）Mass spectrometry

老师课堂强调：

这些片段的“分子量、带电情况”变化不大，非常难检测，需多个方法联合。例如：

“Sometimes you only get one Dalton difference—hard to see! You must use a good mass spectrometer and high-performance chromatography to distinguish these small fragments.”

英文速记（First Page）

Hydrolysis and β-elimination result in fragmentation of peptide chains.

DKP formation may occur at the N-terminus.

Covalent oligomerization involves disulfide exchanges.

Detected by
RP-HPLC, SEC-HPLC, CGE, Mass spec.

Fragments/oligomers are critical QC issues—they may be nearly invisible by simple methods!11

第12页：物理降解途径——聚集体/寡聚体

Physical degradation pathways: Aggregates/Oligomers

1
机制 Mechanisms

去折叠/变性 Denaturation

蛋白失去天然结构，暴露疏水区，容易异常自聚、功能丧失。

诱因
温度、pH、离子强度或变性剂（chaotropic agents）等7。

聚集与沉淀 Aggregation/Precipitation

部分变性、搅拌、冷冻-复融，蛋白易聚集，形成高分子量复合物甚至沉淀。

老师课堂补充
搅拌、冻干、冷冻→复融过程中的结构扰动，都极易诱发蛋白聚集、沉淀。

吸附 Adsorption

蛋白吸附在容器表面造成活性损失或部分浓度下降。

2
影响因素 Factors

关键参数 Critical parameters

温度、溶剂环境、缓冲液pH与离子强度、变性剂。

聚集易发因素

高浓度蛋白、搅拌、冷冻、复融等各环节应力。[老师现场点名：高浓度、机械扰动，是聚集和疾病（如自体免疫副反应）重要隐患。]

3
检测与方法 Methods
检测聚集与变性的典型方法
SEC-HPLC、SDS-PAGE、动态光散射等。

必须多方法结合，防漏检！

英文速记（Second Page）

Denaturation exposes aggregation-prone domains; triggers: heat, solvent, pH, ionic

strength.

Aggregation/precipitation favored by high protein concentration, stirring, freeze-thaw, lyophilization.

Surface adsorption gives protein loss.

Monitoring
size exclusion chromatography, PAGE, DLS, etc.

Physical aggregation = QC must! Prof says: “Never rely on a single test—combine methods since some aggregates are invisible!

第13页 物理降解途径：聚集体/寡聚体

Physical degradation pathways: aggregates, oligomers

1
变性 Denaturation
定义/Definition
蛋白质从天然三维结构变为无序或部分展开结构，暴露疏水区。
结果/Consequences
易导致后续聚集、自我结合、功能丧失。

影响因素/Critical parameters：

温度 (temperature)

有机溶剂 (solvents)

缓冲液pH和离子强度 (buffer pH, ionic strength)

变性剂 (chaotropic agents)

录音补充：

“像你不喜欢晒太阳一样，每种蛋白都有自己的脆弱点，就是这些物理化学因素”。

2
聚集与沉淀 Aggregation, precipitation

机制/Mechanism：

蛋白变性或部分折叠异常后，疏水区暴露，相互吸引结合，形成更大聚集体；进一步可形成沉淀。

诱因/Factors that favor aggregation：

高浓度的蛋白溶液 (high concentration of proteins)

搅拌、晃动 (stirring)

冷冻/冻干/复融 (freezing, freeze-drying, thawing)

易考提示/老师补充：

“搅拌、冷冻、冻干这些动作在实际生产/运输第一常见，也是导致聚集的高风险操作”。

聚集/沉淀影响药物安全性和免疫原性，是临床副作用的重要根源

3
吸附 Adsorption

机制/Mechanism：

蛋白疏水氨基酸暴露后，易吸附在容器表面（如玻璃、塑料）疏水区域，造成蛋白实际浓度下降、活性丧失。

检测意义/Significance：

难以直接检测，但对药物实际有效浓度、稳定性高度重要。

重点整理 / High-Yield Summary
中文English注释/老师tips
1. 变性Denaturation主要受温度、pH、变性剂影响
2. 聚集/沉淀Aggregation, precipitation高浓度、搅拌、冷冻等最常见物理应激（考试常考）
3. 吸附Adsorption多发生在储存、运输，实际少量即可影响药效

老师课堂录音要点摘录

“Every protein has its own weak spot, like you don’t like sun, proteins are fragile to different parameters: temperature, pH, agitation... That’s why stability tests are so important for storage and development.” 47

“Never rely on only one test. For aggregates, at least combine SEC-HPLC and one more orthogonal technique.” 54

中文考场速记模板

聚集/沉淀通常由于蛋白浓度高、搅拌、冷冻/冻干/复融等物理应激导致，是影响药物稳定性、安全性的关键问题。

变性和吸附也会降低蛋白有效性。通常需要结合多种方法综合检测（如SEC-HPLC、动态光散射、PAGE等）。

English answer template

Physical degradations such as denaturation, aggregation/precipitation, and adsorption are triggered by temperature, pH, solvents, high protein concentration, stirring, freezing, freeze-drying, and container surface effects.

Aggregates jeopardize drug safety and efficacy, hence multiple complementary analytical methods (SEC-HPLC, DLS, PAGE) are required for thorough QC

第14页：互补分析技术的重要性

Page 1
Importance of Complementary Analytical Techniques
问1. 为什么需要互补技术？
问Why are complementary techniques necessary?
答

蛋白药物高度异质，杂质、微变体类型繁多，任何单一技术都难以全面检出，容易遗漏关键信息。

Protein biopharmaceuticals are highly heterogeneous, with many impurities and micro-variants. No single technique can detect all of them—key information is easily missed.

老师强调：“有的降解产物分子量、带电几乎没有区别，非常难检出。分析化学进步才能推动质控进步。”

“Some degradation products have almost no difference in size and charge, very hard to detect. Only advances in analytical chemistry lead to QC progress.” 1

2
常用互补分析方法（对应英文）

Common complementary QC methods (point-by-point correspondence)

电泳法（如SDS-PAGE、毛细管电泳CE、CE-MS）：按分子量/电荷分离，用于检测碎片、变体等。

Electrophoresis (e.g., SDS-PAGE, capillary electrophoresis CE, CE-MS): Separates by size/charge, detects fragments and variants.

色谱法（如SEC-HPLC、离子交换CEX、反相RP-HPLC）：按大小、电荷、疏水性分离杂质/变体。

Chromatography (SEC-HPLC, CEX, RP-HPLC): Separates by size, charge, hydrophobicity to detect impurities/variants.

光谱/光度法（如CD、荧光、UV、DSC、光散射）：检测蛋白二、三级结构、聚集状态和稳定性。

Spectroscopy/Photometry (CD, fluorescence, UV, DSC, light scattering): Assesses secondary/tertiary structure, aggregation, and stability.

老师重点：“每种方法能检测的类型有限，只靠一种肯定不够，必须联合至少两种，才能保证不遗漏关键微变体。”

"Each technique only detects limited types; never rely on one, at least two are required to avoid missing important micro-variants." 4113

3
检测方法与微变体类别一览

Techniques vs. Micro-variants Table

中文方法面向杂质/变体对应英文配对
SDS-PAGE骨架碎片/分子量异构体skeleton fragments/mass variants
CEX (阳离子交换)去酰胺、氧化、糖基化等电荷变体deamidation, oxidation, glycosylation (charge variants)
SEC-HPLC聚集体、片段（尺寸变体）aggregates, fragments (size variants)
RP-HPLC疏水性亚型、氧化产物hydrophobic variants, oxidized forms
CE/IEF电荷/糖型微变体charge/glyco-micro-variants
光散射/DSC聚集、热稳定性aggregation, thermal stability

老师补充：“杂质类型多，单看一张图容易漏掉重要信息，必须交叉比照不同数据。”

"So many impurity types, reading one profile alone will miss critical data, always cross-compare different results." 4241

第15页：抗体分析的三种策略

Page 2
Three Analytical Strategies for Antibodies
1
定义与流程

Definitions and workflows

Top-down（全分子）：直接检测完整抗体分子（如IgG），主要分析主结构和大体修饰。

Top-down (whole molecule): Direct analysis of intact antibody (e.g., IgG), focusing on the main structure and major modifications.

Middle-up（酶切大片段）：先用限制性酶（如IdeS，Papain）部分切开，得到大块Fab/Fc片段，再分析。

Middle-up (large fragment): Restriction enzyme digestion (IdeS, papain) generates large Fab/Fc fragments, then analyze separately.

Bottom-up（全酶解+肽图）：完全水解抗体为小肽段，结合MS进行详细肽图分析及修饰鉴定。

Bottom-up (peptide mapping): Complete enzymatic digestion into small peptides, then detailed map and modification analysis by MS.

老师强调
“有时直接分析全分子，看不出微小差异。分段或肽图分析更灵敏，可以精确定量和定位修饰与杂质。”

"Direct whole protein analysis sometimes misses subtle differences. Fragment or peptide mapping is more sensitive for quantifying and localizing modifications/impurities." 227

2
三种方法适用场景对比
Comparison Table
Applications of Three Strategies
方法中文说明English Description优缺点/老师提示
Top-down全分子直接检测，速度快，宏观判别主结构、聚集Quick overview; intact structure/aggregation微小修饰变体难以分辨，灵敏度有限
Middle-up酶切Fab/Fc等大片段，定向分析关键区域Analyze large, functionally relevant domains检测特定区域变体或修饰；适合发现功能相关区域变异
Bottom-up完全消化，MS肽图分析每个位点、PTMFull mapping of peptides/PTMs by MS最详细、灵敏度最高，适合全面鉴别微变体与翻译后修饰
3
答题高分模板

中文

蛋白药物质控需多种互补技术（电泳、色谱、谱学等）联合应用，才能全面检测纯度、结构异质性和安全性。抗体样品分析采用top-down（全分子）、middle-up（大片段分析）、bottom-up（完全酶解+MS）三种策略，彼此互补，根据分析需求选择合适组合。老师特别强调“至少联合两种手段，避免漏检微变体”。

English

Quality control of biopharmaceuticals requires combined complementary techniques (electrophoresis, chromatography, spectrometry) for full assessment of purity, structure, and safety. Antibody analysis uses three approaches: top-down (intact), middle-up (large fragment), and bottom-up (peptide mapping with MS). These are complementary and chosen according to analytical needs. Teacher emphasizes: "Never rely on one method, always cross-check to avoid missing micro-variants."

第16页
Middle-up 方法检测抗体异构化和琥珀酰亚胺（实验解读）

中文拆解与英文对照

1
基本原理与流程（Principle & Workflow）
Middle-up法
抗体样品先用酶（如papain、IdeS）部分消化，切成较大片段（如Fab和Fc），再经疏水作用色谱（HIC）分离分析。
Middle-up approach
The antibody is partially digested with enzymes (papain, IdeS), generating large fragments (Fab, Fc), then separated with hydrophobic interaction chromatography (HIC).
2
目的及优势（Objective & Advantages）

高分辨检测抗体修饰，包括异构化（isomerisation）与琥珀酰亚胺（succinimide）杂质，如Asu74、isoAsp32、Asu32等。

High-resolution detection of antibody modifications, especially isomerisation and succinimide byproducts (e.g., Asu74, isoAsp32, Asu32).

3
图谱解析（Chromatogram Explanation）

不同峰代表不同变体/杂质：

Peak 1*
Asu74（琥珀酰亚胺, 由papain切割产生）
Peak 2
Asp32（有自由巯基的Asp32）
Peak 3
isoAsp32（异构化位点）
Peak 5
Asu32

Different peaks represent different variants/impurities:

Peak 1*
Asu74 (succinimide from papain cleavage)
Peak 2
Asp32 (Asp32 with a free thiol)
Peak 3
isoAsp32 (isomerisation site)
Peak 5
Asu32
4
结论与连接老师要点（Summary & Teacher’s Notes）
老师强调
分段分析（middle-up）能显著提升对修饰、异构体、微变体的分辨与定量灵敏度，相比全蛋白分析更具针对性。
Teacher’s point
Middle-up segmentation significantly enhances sensitivity and separative power for modifications and micro-variants compared with whole-molecule analysis.
第17页
生物药质控核心目标（Quality Control Objectives）

中文梳理与英文对照

1
总目标（Overall QC goals）

评估和确保蛋白药物的安全性、有效性及生产批间一致性

To assess and ensure the safety and efficacy of the therapeutic protein and to verify lot-to-lot consistency

2
四大核心质量指标与其意义（Four Key QC Criteria & Significance）
中文English解读&老师点拨
鉴别试验Identity test确认批次中含有目标治疗蛋白（含生物活性&免疫原性）<br>Ensure the batch contains the correct therapeutic protein (including bioactivity, immunogenicity)
纯度Purity产品中无污染杂质<br>A biopharmaceutical must be free of contaminating substances
剂量Dosage精确定量药品有效成分，确保患者获得正确剂量<br>Accurately determine active protein concentration to deliver correct dose

3
答题高分模板（中英模版）

中文

蛋白药物质控需涵盖四大目标
① 鉴别试验确保正确蛋白与生物活性；② 纯度检测保证无杂质；③ 剂量检测确保剂量准确；④ 稳定性测试优化储存和使用全程安全。通过结构分段（如middle-up）及多技术联合，可全方位提升检测灵敏度、确保药品安全有效。【老师强调每一步都要有据可依，不能仅凭单一试验判断药品合格与否。】

English

Quality control of therapeutic proteins covers four objectives: (1) Identity testing to verify the correct protein and biological activity; (2) Purity assessment to exclude contaminants; (3) Dosage determination for accurate delivery; (4) Stability tests for storage/safety optimization. By combining segmentation approaches (e.g., middle-up) and complementary methods, QC achieves high sensitivity and robustness. Teacher emphasizes: every criterion needs robust evidence—never rely on a single test for product qualification.

第18页
Impurities and methods to control the purity

杂质来源与纯度控制方法

一、杂质分类与检测方法

Types of impurities and methods of detection

1
宿主细胞蛋白杂质 (Host cell proteins)

检测方法:

SDS-PAGE/Coomassie blue staining

HCP (host cell protein) ELISA tests

解析
非特异性，总检测宿主蛋白，SDS-PAGE分离不同分子量蛋白，ELISA针对来源细胞特异检测。
2
降解蛋白杂质 (Degraded proteins)

检测方法 & 解析:

RP-HPLC/IEX
检测脱酰胺、氧化变体 (deamidated, oxidized forms)

IEF/Capillary electrophoresis：检测电荷变体/糖型异构体

Size exclusion chromatography (SEC)：检测聚集体或片段(碎片)

HIC
检测折叠异常或疏水区暴露
专用颗粒检测技术
如光散射
总结
每种方法面向一种结构/杂质类型，需互补组合以覆盖所有潜在杂质。
3
DNA 残留 (Residual DNA)

Hybridization method using radiolabelled probes

保证安全，防止转染等风险

4
致热原/内毒素 (Pyrogenic substances, endotoxins)
检测方法
LAL test (用鲎试剂，检测内毒素活性)
5
病毒 (Virus)

需专门筛查（未在slide详述）

第19页
Approaches for Identity Control

蛋白药物分子身份鉴别途径

一、一级结构确认（Primary sequence）

方法:

氨基酸组成分析 (amino acid composition)

自动测序 (EDMAN)

肽图谱比对 (peptide mapping)

二、理化性质确认（Physicochemical parameters）

分子量测定:

SDS-PAGE, SEC, 毛细管电泳，质谱（MALDI，ESI）

等电点测定:

凝胶等电聚焦 (IEF), 毛细管等电聚焦 (CIEF)

疏水性检测:

RP-HPLC

多参数组合分析:

PAGE, 毛细管区带电泳

三、二级/三级结构分析（Secondary/Tertiary structure）

方法:

圆二色谱 　
答案 CD

红外光谱 (IR)

四、翻译后修饰（PTMs，主要关注糖基化）

糖基成分分析/结构异构/杂合型蛋白测定:

单糖组成、唾液酸检测

Glycan mapping, glycopeptide mapping, glycoform heterogeneity analysis

五、生物学和免疫活性（Bio/immuno activity）

功能性实验，最终保证药用特性

模板总结

中文

蛋白药物纯度控制需针对不同来源杂质（如宿主蛋白、降解片段、DNA、内毒素等）采取多种互补分析技术，包括SDS-PAGE、SEC-HPLC、RP-HPLC、IEF、质谱和专用免疫检测等。同时，蛋白身份鉴别需从一级结构、理化性质、二/三级结构、翻译后修饰（如糖基化）以及生物学功能多层次确认。各方法原理互补，确保药物安全有效。

English

Quality control of biopharmaceuticals requires complementary techniques to assess different sources of impurities such as host cell proteins, degradation fragments, residual DNA, and endotoxins. Methods include SDS-PAGE, SEC-HPLC, RP-HPLC, IEF, mass spectrometry, and specific immunoassays. Identity control relies on verifying the primary sequence, physicochemical parameters, secondary/tertiary structure, post-translational modifications (especially glycosylation), and biological/immunological activity, with each method targeting specific attributes for comprehensive verification.

第20页Glycosylation Analysis at Different Levels糖基化分析的多层级思路

1
Glycosylation is a key post-translational modification for therapeutic proteins.

糖基化是治疗性蛋白药物中最重要的翻译后修饰之一。

2
To fully characterize glycosylation, multi-level analysis is required.

要全面表征糖基化，必须采用多层级的检测分析。

分析层级 Level英文解释中文解释方法与分析内容
Intact protein levelAnalyze full protein全蛋白层级：分析完整蛋白全貌LC-MS, CESI-MS等，揭示全蛋白糖异构体分布和总体异质性
Glycopeptide levelAnalyze glycopeptides糖肽层级：分析带糖肽段酶切后LC-MS/MS，定位具体糖基化位点和各位点上微异质性
Released glycan levelAnalyze released glycans游离糖链层级：分析游离糖链结构HILIC-HPLC, MALDI-TOF，获得全面糖链结构和成分组成
Monosaccharide levelAnalyze monosaccharides单糖层级：分析单糖成分酸水解后高效离子色谱/GC-MS，透明定量/定性各类单糖
3
Each level provides complementary information.

每一级分析都提供互补且独立的重要信息：

Intact protein analysis gives a global view of glycoform heterogeneity. 全蛋白分析可快速获得整体糖型构成和异质性。

Glycopeptide analysis identifies glycosylation sites and site-specific variants. 糖肽分析可精准定位糖基化修饰位点及对应的差异。

Released glycan analysis reveals the detailed glycan structures and their relative abundances. 游离糖链分析揭示各种糖链的详细结构及比例。

Monosaccharide analysis quantifies different sugar residues in the glycan moiety. 单糖分析用来明确各种单糖残基的种类与含量。

4
Complementary, multi-level analysis is required for regulatory compliance and ensures drug quality and efficacy.

多层级、互补的糖基化分析是药品质量控制和法规符合性的必备条件，可以保证生物药的安全性和有效性。

5
老师课堂强调

“要全面评估蛋白的糖型，一定不能只停留在某一个检测层面。” "To fully evaluate protein glycoforms, one cannot rely on a single analytical level."

“这些检测不是简单重复，而是信息互补，是监管绝对要求。” "These techniques are not redundant; they are complementary and absolutely required by regulation."

“每个层级聚焦不同，但缺一不可。” "Each level focuses on different aspects; none can be skipped."

Glycosylation analysis can be performed at different levels糖基化分析可在不同层级进行

Intact proteins全蛋白层级分析

Direct analysis of the protein with all glycans attached, providing an overview of glycoform heterogeneity.

对带全部糖链的蛋白进行直接分析，得到糖型异质性整体分布情况。

常用方法
高分辨质谱（MS）、LC-MS56。

Glycopeptides糖肽层级分析

Enzymatic digestion generates glycopeptides, which allow identification of glycosylation sites and site-specific glycan heterogeneity.

通过酶切获得糖肽，可定位糖基化位点和每个位点的糖链种类及异质性。

常用方法
LC-MS/MS、酶解结合色谱。

Released glycans游离糖链层级分析

Glycans are enzymatically or chemically released from the protein, permitting detailed structural elucidation of the glycans.

将糖链从蛋白上释放下来，详尽解析糖链的结构组成和比例。

常用方法
HILIC-HPLC、MALDI-TOF MS56。

Monosaccharides单糖层级分析

Analysis of individual monosaccharide composition after hydrolysis, providing quantitative data on each sugar type.

水解后分析组成单糖，获得各单糖类型的含量和比例。

常用方法
离子色谱、GC-MS56。

补充（老师讲解要点）：

“每个层级补充不同信息，只有结合结果评估，才能全面掌握蛋白糖基化状态，满足生物药质量和法规要求。” "Each level adds unique information; only by combining results can we comprehensively assess protein glycosylation and meet regulatory requirements."

Analytical techniques for QC of common variants of biopharmaceuticals

生物药常见变体的质量控制分析技术

ModificationsAnalytical techniques
Charge variantsDeamidation, Oxidation, GlycosylationCEX, IEF, CIEF, CZE; HILIC-HPLC
电荷变体脱酰胺、氧化、糖基化阳离子交换色谱、等电聚焦、毛细管等电聚焦、毛细管区带电泳；亲水作用色谱
Size variantsFragmentation, AggregationSEC-HPLC, SDS-PAGE, CGE
分子大小变体断裂、聚集凝胶过滤色谱、聚丙烯酰胺凝胶电泳、毛细管凝胶电泳
ConformersMisfolding / DenaturationHIC (not in a QC context)
构像变体错误折叠/变性疏水作用色谱（QC中不常用）

老师课堂补充说明（逐行对应）

1
Charge variants / 电荷变体
英文
These include deamidation, oxidation, and glycosylation, which affect the protein's overall charge. 中文：包括脱酰胺、氧化、糖基化，这些修饰会改变蛋白整体电荷状态。
英文
Key analytical methods are ion exchange chromatography (CEX), isoelectric focusing (IEF/CIEF/CZE), and hydrophilic interaction chromatography (HILIC-HPLC). 中文：主要分析方法包括离子交换色谱（CEX）、等电聚焦（IEF/CIEF/CZE），以及亲水作用色谱（HILIC-HPLC）。
补充
“这些手段可细致分离、鉴别细微电荷异构体，是质量控制不可或缺的。”
2
Size variants / 分子大小变体
英文
Fragmentation and aggregation influence the size and molecular weight of proteins. 中文：分子断裂和聚集会影响蛋白的分子大小和分子量。
英文
Gel filtration chromatography (SEC-HPLC), polyacrylamide gel electrophoresis (SDS-PAGE), and capillary gel electrophoresis (CGE) are commonly used. 中文：常用方法有凝胶过滤色谱（SEC-HPLC）、聚丙烯酰胺凝胶电泳（SDS-PAGE）、毛细管凝胶电泳（CGE）。
补充
“分子大小分析对监测蛋白碎片和聚集体尤为重要，有助于发现降解产物与安全风险。”
3
Conformers / 构像变体
英文
Misfolded or denatured proteins have altered higher-order structure. 中文：错误折叠或变性的蛋白，其高级结构发生改变。
英文
Hydrophobic interaction chromatography (HIC) is used mainly in research, not routine QC. 中文：疏水作用色谱（HIC）多用于结构研究，在QC中较少使用。
补充
“构像异常虽对疗效和免疫原性影响大，但现阶段仅限科研和深度分析。”

知识点总结（中英对照）

Each analytical method targets specific modification types, ensuring comprehensive quality control for biopharmaceuticals. 每种分析手段对应特定的修饰种类，保证生物药质量控制的全面性。

To fulfill regulatory requirements and ensure patient safety, multiple complementary techniques are essential. 为满足法规要求和患者安全，必须多方法互补分析。

Group 1:

How charge variants analysis of monoclonal antibodies by IEX-HPLC contributes to their QC (T1)

Group 2:

What does Size Exclusion Chromatography (SEC-HPLC) bring for the purity control of therapeutic proteins (T1)

Group 3:

What kind of chemical degradation variants of therapeutic proteins can be controlled by RP-HPLC

Group 4:

How peptide mapping of therapeutic proteins by RP-HPLC contributes to their identity control (T2)

Group 5:

How capillary zone electrophoresis can be used for the glycosylation assessment of therapeutic glycoproteins (T2)

Group 6:

Why capillary gel electrophoresis can be used for both identity and purity control of therapeutic proteins (T2)
