对于总RNA，完整的eukaryote RNA样品在凝胶上呈现清晰的28S和18S条带，28S:18S约为2:1，提示RNA完整。 For total eukaryotic RNA, an intact sample shows clear 28S and 18S bands on the gel, with a 28S:18S ratio of about 2:1, indicating good RNA integrity.
6
如果RNA降解，表现为条带变弱、拖尾、28S条带降低甚至消失，28S/18S比值下降。 If RNA is degraded, bands become faint and smeared, with a decrease or loss of the 28S band and a reduced 28S/18S ratio.
老师强调
降解的主要标志有片段化（fragmentation）、28S条带损失（loss of 28S）、28S/18S比值下降，这些均通过EtBr染色显现。 The instructor highlighted that main signs of degradation are fragmentation, loss of the 28S band, and decreased 28S/18S ratio, all revealed by EtBr staining.

(第二页/Second slide)

8
荧光染料试剂盒可提高DNA/RNA检测的灵敏度和特异性，温和区分DNA与RNA信号。 Fluorogenic dye kits improve the sensitivity and specificity of DNA/RNA detection and allow a clear distinction between DNA and RNA signals.
9
左图（E. coli DNA试剂盒）：DNA含量与荧光信号强度呈线性相关，RNA单独基本无信号，显示高度特异性。 Left panel (E. coli DNA kit): DNA content shows a linear relationship with fluorescence intensity, while RNA alone yields negligible signal, indicating high specificity.
10
右图（E. coli rRNA试剂盒）：RNA与荧光信号相关，而DNA影响较小，能区分RNA和DNA。 Right panel (E. coli rRNA kit): RNA content correlates with fluorescence, whereas DNA yields little signal, enabling differentiation between RNA and DNA.
结果说明
试剂盒方法具有极佳的灵敏度和特异性，适用于微量样品的准确定量。 This demonstrates that the kit method provides excellent sensitivity and specificity, suitable for accurate quantification of minute samples.
12
一般每个样品检测10 μL，荧光分为DNA（485-530 nm）和rRNA（630-680 nm）测定。 Typically, 10 μL samples are used, with fluorescence detection at 485-530 nm for DNA and 630-680 nm for rRNA.
老师补充
目前这是检测核酸的最佳灵敏度和特异性方法，极适合科学研究和临床检测。 The instructor added: This is currently the best method for nucleic acid detection in terms of both sensitivity and specificity, making it highly suitable for research and clinical diagnostics3034.

UV吸收法与荧光法测定RNA比较 UV Absorbance vs Fluorimetry to Measure RNAs

听课笔记 | Lecture Notes

1
UV吸收法（ABS）优点： Advantages of UV Absorbance (ABS):

简单且便宜。 Simple and cheap.

可检测污染物。 Contaminant detection.

可用于浓度估算。 Allows concentration estimation.

可用于纯度的控制。 Allows control of purity.

2
UV吸收法（ABS）缺点： Drawbacks of UV Absorbance (ABS):

若未知类型，无法区分DNA和RNA。 Cannot discriminate between DNA and RNA if not known.

无法判断降解情况。 No information on degradation.

存在杂质时可能过高估算含量。 Possible overestimation if there are contaminants.

3
荧光法（FLUO）优点： Advantages of Fluorimetry (FLUO):

灵敏度极高。 Very high sensitivity.

针对多核苷酸链特异性强。 Highly specific to poly-nucleotide chains.

染料可根据核酸类型专一性识别。 Dyes are specific for each type of nucleic acid.

可进行特异性定量。 Allows specific quantification.

4
荧光法（FLUO）缺点： Drawbacks of Fluorimetry (FLUO):

价格高，需要染料和仪器。 Expensive due to dyes/instruments.

无法检测污染物（如核苷酸、寡聚物、蛋白质、盐等）。 Cannot detect contaminants (nucleotides, oligos, proteins, salts, etc.).

课件与讲解总结
UV法可检测污染、粗略估算浓度和纯度，但对物种和降解无判别力，适合初步筛查。荧光法灵敏、专一，可精确定量特定核酸但无法识别杂质，价格较高。两者优势互补，实验时应结合使用以获得最可靠结果。 Summary from slide and lecture: UV absorbance is suitable for assessing purity, contamination, and rough concentration but cannot distinguish species nor assess degradation; it's best for initial screening. Fluorimetry is highly sensitive and specific, allows precise quantification of a given nucleic acid, but cannot detect contaminants and is more expensive. The two approaches are complementary and should be combined for reliable results.

032025-10-09（第 2 讲）

核酸药物的作用机制与天然屏障 Mechanisms of Nucleic Acid Drugs & Natural Barriers

基本策略
添加/移除/取代DNA与mRNA，以及与蛋白互作 |
Basic strategies
Add/remove/replace DNA and mRNA, and react with proteins

添加DNA Add DNA

移除DNA Remove DNA

替换DNA Replace DNA

添加mRNA Add mRNA

破坏mRNA（防止翻译） Destroy mRNA (prevent translation)

修饰mRNA（改变翻译） Modify mRNA (modify translation)

与蛋白相互作用，调控生理反应 React with protein to regulate physiological response

问2. 自然屏障？Natural barriers?
答

药物在体内投递面临多种天然障碍。 Delivery of drugs in the body faces various natural hurdles.

3
自然屏障：生理屏障

Natural barriers: Physiological barriers

系统性给药后，需穿越体外和体内多级屏障。 After systemic administration, drugs must cross multiple extracellular and intracellular barriers.

体外屏障包括： Extracellular barriers include:

血清核酸酶分解 Degradation by serum endonucleases

巨噬细胞吞噬 Uptake by macrophages

血管壁穿透（逸出作用） Extravasation through blood vessel walls

基质障碍 Extracellular matrix barriers

进入靶细胞后，需要： Once inside the target cell, the agent must:

通过受体介导的内吞作用进入细胞 Enter via receptor-mediated endocytosis

在内体内避免降解并实现逃逸 Escape from endosomes to avoid degradation

DNA药物需被运送至细胞核，并通过核孔复合体 DNA drugs need to be transported into the nucleus via nuclear pore complexes

mRNA/siRNA/miRNA等需在胞质中才能被核糖体识别 mRNA/siRNA/miRNA need to be recognized by ribosomes in the cytoplasm

4
自然屏障：DNA/RNA的生物化学性质

Natural barriers: DNA/RNA Biochemistry

DNA与RNA分子具有碱基对和带负电的糖磷酸骨架 DNA and RNA molecules have base pairs and a negatively charged sugar-phosphate backbone

这种结构导致其稳定性较低且极易被降解 This structure leads to low stability and high susceptibility to degradation

5
分子量对比

Molecular weight comparison

阿司匹林（小分子药物）：0.18 kDa Aspirin (small molecule drug): 0.18 kDa

siRNA（小干扰RNA，核酸药物）：约10 kDa siRNA (small interfering RNA, nucleic acid drug): ~10 kDa

mRNA（信使RNA，核酸药物）：约400 kDa mRNA (messenger RNA, nucleic acid drug): ~400 kDa

抗体（大分子生物药物）：150 kDa Antibody (large biologic drug): 150 kDa

6
课堂补充

Supplemented by classroom recording

老师强调RNA和mRNA药物实际分子量远高于一般小分子药物，稳定性和给药难度更大。 The lecturer emphasized that RNA and mRNA drugs have much higher molecular weight than regular small molecule drugs, making them less stable and harder to deliver.

以阿司匹林（小分子）、siRNA（中分子）、抗体（大分子）举例对比，让大家不要把RNA药物当作普通小分子药物看待。 By comparing aspirin (small), siRNA (medium), and antibodies (large), the lecturer reminded students not to treat RNA drugs as conventional small molecules.

RNA药物因体积大、不稳定，在血液中容易被降解，体内运送需克服更多天然屏障。 Due to their large size and instability, RNA drugs are prone to degradation in blood and must overcome more natural barriers for delivery.

Natural barriers——DNA/RNA的生物化学性质与载体策略 Natural barriers——DNA/RNA Biochemistry & Vectorization Strategies

1
DNA/RNA的天然生物化学屏障

Natural Biochemical Barriers of DNA/RNA

DNA和RNA分子低稳定性，容易被体内酶降解。 DNA and RNA molecules have low stability and are easily degraded by enzymes in vivo.

分子量很大，导致它们难以轻松通过细胞膜。 They have high molecular weight, making it difficult for them to cross cell membranes easily.

带有负电荷的糖磷酸骨架，使DNA/RNA与同样带负电的细胞表面互相排斥。 The sugar-phosphate backbone carries a negative charge, causing DNA/RNA to repel the negatively charged cell surface.

亲水性强，限制了其跨越细胞膜的能力。 They are highly hydrophilic, which restricts their ability to penetrate cell membranes.

2
解决上述障碍的方法——修饰和/或载体化

Solutions to These Barriers—Modification and/or Vectorization

需要通过化学修饰结构或使用载体系统，增强其稳定性与细胞摄取。 Structural chemical modification or the use of vector systems is required to enhance their stability and cellular uptake.

否则，DNA/RNA药物将很难高效进入细胞并发挥药效。 Otherwise, DNA/RNA drugs will have difficulty efficiently entering cells and exerting therapeutic effects.

3
病毒载体优缺点分析

Advantages and Disadvantages of Viral Vectors

优点：

转染效率高，可高效将核酸导入细胞。 High transfection efficiency, can deliver nucleic acids into cells efficiently.

天然趋向性，可感染多种细胞类型。 Natural tropism, able to infect various cell types.

天生具有促进内体逃逸的机制。 Evolved mechanisms for endosomal escape.

具备将DNA递送到细胞核的天然运输机制。 Have natural transportation mechanisms for DNA into the nucleus.

缺点：

易引发强烈免疫反应，无法多次给药。 Elicit strong immune reactions, prohibiting multiple administrations.

有遗传物质插入染色体和激活原癌基因的潜在风险。 Possibility of chromosomal insertion and proto-oncogene activation.

合成过程复杂、成本高。 Complicated synthesis process and high costs.

基因大小受限，不能递送大分子。 Limited gene size, cannot deliver large genes.

可能存在活病毒污染和毒性问题。 Potential toxicity and contamination of live virus.

4
非病毒载体——递送设计标准

Non-viral Vectors—Delivery Design Criteria

DNA、mRNA、siRNA等大分子，需跨越多个体内外屏障： Macromolecules like DNA, mRNA, siRNA must cross multiple extra- and intracellular barriers:

血清内核酸酶降解 Degradation by serum endonucleases

巨噬细胞吞噬 Uptake by macrophages

血管通透与组织渗出 Vascular permeability and tissue extravasation

基质屏障 Extracellular matrix

结合细胞表面受体-内吞作用 Binding to cell surface receptor and internalization (endocytosis)

内体阻遏与降解 Endosomal trapping and degradation

DNA需运送至细胞核，mRNA/siRNA需释放到胞质 DNA must be transported into the nucleus, while mRNA/siRNA need to be released into the cytosol for function.

因此，设计非病毒载体时要综合考虑这些关键屏障，优化递送系统，实现高效、安全的核酸药物递送。 Therefore, when designing non-viral vectors, these key barriers must be considered to optimize delivery systems for efficient and safe nucleic acid drug delivery.

5
课堂要点补充

Key Supplemen

老师强调，DNA和RNA药物体积大且不稳定，加上负电荷与亲水性，使它们难以通过带负电的细胞膜，需要修饰或载体帮助递送。 The lecturer emphasized that DNA and RNA drugs are large and unstable; their negative charge and hydrophilicity make it hard to cross negatively charged cell membranes, thus requiring modification or vectors for delivery.

如果直接用DNA/RNA药物注射体内，基本会被降解，无法进入细胞或发挥作用。 If DNA/RNA drugs are injected directly into the body, they will mostly be degraded and will not enter cells or be effective.

这也是为什么科研和临床上重点发展各类载体（如病毒和非病毒载体）技术。 This is why various vector technologies (viral and non-viral) are being developed in research and clinical settings.

非病毒载体 Non-viral vectors

1
商品化转染试剂实例 Commercial Transfection Reagents Examples

常用的非病毒基因递送试剂包括Invitrogen与Polyplus等公司生产的转染试剂。 Commonly used non-viral gene delivery reagents include transfection reagents produced by companies such as Invitrogen and Polyplus.

实验室级别的体外细胞转染常选用这些商品化试剂，它们包括阳离子脂质体及聚合物复合物，用于将DNA、mRNA或siRNA导入哺乳动物细胞。 These commercial reagents are commonly chosen for in vitro transfection in the laboratory, including cationic liposomes and polymer complexes, for delivering DNA, mRNA, or siRNA into mammalian cells.

2
非病毒载体类型 Types of Non-viral Vectors

非病毒递送载体分为几大类： Non-viral delivery vectors can be divided into major types:

脂质类纳米颗粒（Lipid-based nanoparticles） Liposome（脂质体 Liposome）、LNP（脂质纳米颗粒 LNP）、Lipopolyplex（脂质-聚合物复合物 Lipopolyplex） Liposome, LNP (Lipid Nanoparticles), Lipopolyplex

高分子纳米颗粒（Polymer-based nanoparticles） 包括聚合物纳米颗粒、聚合物胶束、树枝状聚合物复合物等。 Including polymeric NPs, polymeric micelles, dendriplex, etc.

siRNA-配体缀合物（siRNA-ligand conjugates） siRNA可与多种靶向分子偶联（如适配体、GalNAc、肽、抗体、胆固醇等），提高递送的特异性和效率。 siRNA can be conjugated with various targeting molecules (like aptamers, GalNAc, peptide, antibody, cholesterol) to improve specificity and efficiency of delivery.

外泌体（Exosomes） 外泌体是天然分泌的小囊泡，可作为生物来源递送载体，具有低免疫原性等优点。 Exosomes are naturally secreted small vesicles and can serve as bio-origin delivery vectors with low immunogenicity and other advantages 9。

3
课堂补充 Lecture Key Points

老师强调很多同学实验用过这些转染试剂（如Lipofectamine、jetPRIME），这是利用人工合成的脂质体或聚合物构建纳米颗粒，将核酸包裹递送进细胞。 The lecturer emphasized that many students have used these transfection reagents (such as Lipofectamine, jetPRIME), which utilize artificial lipid or polymer particles to encapsulate and deliver nucleic acids into cells.

这些方法在体外效率较高，但体内递送还需解决免疫清除、血浆蛋白吸附等生理屏障难题。 These approaches are quite efficient in vitro, but for in vivo delivery, physiological barriers like immune clearance and plasma protein absorption still need to be addressed.

递送体系可根据需求选择—有的用于增强内吞和胞内释放，有的用于特异性靶向特定组织或细胞。 Delivery systems can be chosen based on specific needs—some enhance endocytosis and intracellular release, others are designed for targeted delivery to specific tissues or cells.

4
图示归纳 Summary of Figure

脂质类与聚合物类纳米颗粒、核酸-配体偶联体、外泌体为主的递送体系共同组成了当前非病毒递送工具箱，提供多样化选择。 Lipid-based and polymer-based nanoparticles, nucleic acid-ligand conjugates, and exosomes together form the current non-viral delivery toolkit, offering diverse options.

脂质纳米颗粒的组成及结构特征

Lipid NanoParticles: Composition and Structural Features

1
可离子化脂质 Ionisable lipid

摩尔比例为30-50%。 % molar ratio: 30-50%.

pKa<7pKa​<7，常见约为6，在中性pH下为中性，酸性环境下变为正电荷。 pKa<7pKa​<7, usually around 6, neutral at physiological pH and positively charged in acidic conditions.

与RNA发生静电相互作用，实现有效包裹递送。 Forms electrostatic interactions with RNA for efficient encapsulation and delivery.

促进内涵体逃逸，帮助核酸释放入胞质。 Facilitates endosomal escape, aiding nucleic acid release into the cytosol.

课堂补充：这种pH响应机制是核酸递送成功的关键，临床中mRNA疫苗、siRNA药物均采用。 Class supplement: This pH responsiveness is crucial for successful nucleic acid delivery and is used in clinical mRNA vaccines and siRNA drugs 72116.

2
帮助脂质 Helper lipid

摩尔比例为10-30%。 % molar ratio: 10-30%.

影响LNP结构的稳定性。 Influences the stability of the LNP structure.

参与内涵体逃逸。 Involved in endosomal escape.

参与表面蛋白相互作用，可能影响体内行为。 Interacts with surface proteins, possibly affecting in vivo behavior.

课堂补充：一般采用磷脂类成分，兼具结构稳定与功能性。 Class addition: Commonly uses phospholipids, which provide both structural support and functionality 217.

3
胆固醇 Cholesterol

摩尔比例为30-50%。 % molar ratio: 30-50%.

提升稳定性与膜的流动性。 Increases stability and membrane fluidity.

促进细胞摄取，有助于内化作用。 Enhances cellular uptake and internalization.

课堂补充：胆固醇起“胶水”作用，使膜特性更优、更易被细胞识别。 Class supplement: Cholesterol acts like "glue," optimizing membrane properties for easier cellular recognition 721.

4
PEG化脂质 PEGylated lipid

摩尔比例<2%。 % molar ratio: <2%.

促进胶体稳定性，防止颗粒聚集。 Promotes colloidal stability and prevents particle aggregation.

防止血浆蛋白吸附，延长在体内循环时间。 Prevents plasma protein adsorption, extends circulation time in vivo.

可调节颗粒大小，提高转染效率。 Can modulate particle size and improve transfection efficiency.

课堂补充：PEG化策略显著降低纳米颗粒被免疫系统清除的速率。 Class supplement: PEGylation markedly reduces the rate of nanoparticle clearance by the immune system 2114.

5
结构区别：脂质纳米颗粒 vs. 脂质体

Structural Differences: Lipid Nanoparticle vs. Liposome

LNP具有更复杂的内核结构，由多种脂质、胆固醇、PEG化脂质组成，用于包载RNA等核酸药物。 LNPs have a more complex core composed of various lipids, cholesterol, and PEGylated lipids, designed to encapsulate RNA and other nucleic acids.

脂质体多为双层，主要由磷脂和胆固醇组成，可包载小分子或某些大分子。 Liposomes are mostly bilayered, primarily made up of phospholipids and cholesterol, for encapsulation of small molecules or some macromolecules.

电子显微镜下LNP呈实心或海绵状核结构，脂质体表现为空心囊泡。 Under electron microscopy, LNPs usually show a solid or sponge-like core, while liposomes appear as hollow vesicles 12.

6
mRNA LNP典型处方举例 Examples of mRNA LNP Formulations

以Patisiran（Onpattro）、Tozinameran（Comirnaty，辉瑞/BioNTech）、Elasomeran（Spikevax，Moderna）为代表，各自采用不同离子化脂质和辅脂肪、PEG脂质组合。 Representative formulations include Patisiran (Onpattro), Tozinameran (Comirnaty, Pfizer/BioNTech), and Elasomeran (Spikevax, Moderna), each uses different ionisable lipids, helper lipids, and PEG-lipids.

四大类核心材料在各种mRNA/siRNA药物LNP中共同体现。 The four core LNP materials are fundamental across all mRNA/siRNA drug formulations 17.

7
课堂整体总结 Class Overall Summary

脂质纳米颗粒实现高效核酸递送的关键在于不同组分的协同设计，每种组分针对递送中的不同障碍发挥作用。 The success of LNPs for efficient nucleic acid delivery relies on the synergistic design of each component, targeting different delivery challenges.

pH敏感性（由可离子化脂质提供）、胶体稳定性（PEG化脂质）、膜特性与细胞识别（胆固醇、帮助脂质）等均为递送成败的决定因素。 pH sensitivity (from ionisable lipid), colloid stability (from PEGylated lipid), membrane properties, and cellular recognition (from cholesterol and helper lipids) are all decisive for delivery efficacy

自然屏障与内在障碍：脂质纳米颗粒递送及胞内释放机制

Natural barriers & Physiological barriers: LNP Delivery and Endosomal Escape

1
递送核酸面临的自然屏障

Natural Barriers Faced by Nucleic Acid Delivery

DNA、mRNA、siRNA或miRNA均需通过多重体内屏障才能实现有效递送。 DNA, mRNA, and siRNA/miRNA all need to cross multiple physiological barriers for effective delivery.

体外，主要障碍有巨噬细胞吞噬和血清核酸酶降解，导致核酸药物极易失活。 Extracellularly, the main barriers are macrophage phagocytosis and degradation by serum endonucleases, resulting in rapid inactivation of nucleic acid therapeutics.

细胞内，首先要求药物被细胞摄取（内吞），然后需要跨越内涵体并释放到胞质。 Intracellularly, the drug first needs to be taken up by endocytosis and then escape the endosome to release into the cytoplasm1224.

2
脂质纳米颗粒制备与pH调控机制

LNP Preparation and pH-Responsive Mechanism

脂质和siRNA在pH 4环境下混合，脂质分子带正电，能有效吸附带负电的核酸，形成均匀纳米颗粒。 Lipids and siRNA are mixed at pH 4, where the lipid molecules are positively charged and can efficiently absorb negatively charged nucleic acids, forming uniform nanoparticles144.

随后，体系通过透析调至pH 7.4，此时LNP表面趋于中性，有利于体内递送，减少被血浆蛋白结合和免疫系统清除。 The system is then dialyzed to pH 7.4, making the LNP surface more neutral, which favors in vivo delivery by reducing plasma protein binding and immune system clearance144.

3
PEG修饰脂质的作用

Role of PEGylated Lipids

PEG化脂质（摩尔比<2%）包被在LNP表面，能提高胶体稳定性，防止颗粒聚集。 PEGylated lipid (<2% molar ratio) coats LNP surfaces to enhance colloidal stability and prevent particle aggregation15.

还能有效减少血浆蛋白吸附和靶向/非靶向细胞的捕获，从而显著延长在体内的循环时间。 They also effectively inhibit plasma protein adsorption and capture by (non-)targeted cells, thereby significantly prolonging circulation time in vivo15.

PEG修饰还能调节LNP粒径，提高转染效率。 PEGylation can also modulate LNP particle size and improve transfection efficiency15.

4
细胞摄取与内涵体障碍

Cellular Uptake and Endosomal Barriers

LNP通过受体介导的内吞作用进入细胞，随后被包裹在内涵体内。 LNPs enter cells via receptor-mediated endocytosis, becoming enclosed in endosomes24.

内涵体pH降低，铁离子和蛋白水解酶等丰富，若药物未及时释放将被降解。 As the endosome becomes more acidic and enzyme-rich, drugs that are not promptly released are degraded14.

5
可离子化脂质与内涵体逃逸机制

Ionizable Lipids and Endosomal Escape

可离子化脂质分子pKa为6.2-6.5，在生理pH下电中性，在内涵体酸性环境（pH约5）下带正电。 Ionizable cationic lipids (pKa 6.2–6.5) are neutral at physiological pH, but become positively charged in the acidic endosomal environment (pH ~5)174.

此时可离子化脂质与内源性阴离子脂质相互作用，诱导膜结构从双层向六角HII型（非双层）转化，破坏膜结构，实现核酸药物释放。 At this point, ionizable lipids interact with endogenous anionic lipids, inducing a transition from bilayer to non-bilayer (hexagonal HII) structures, disrupting the membrane and releasing nucleic acids4.

这种逃逸过程是LNP药物递送成功的核心环节，但分子机制尚未完全阐明。 This escape process is crucial for successful LNP drug delivery, though the molecular mechanism is not fully understood41.

6
总结

Summary

LNP通过pH响应性和PEG修饰等策略，成功克服体内外多重障碍，实现高效核酸递送。 LNPs employ pH responsiveness and PEGylation strategies to overcome multiple physiological barriers, achieving efficient nucleic acid delivery.

可离子化脂质关键在于内涵体酸性环境下改变电性，诱导膜破坏并促进药物释放。 Ionizable lipids are key for changing charge in acidic endosomes and inducing membrane disruption and drug release

可离子化脂质的进化与Patisiran开发

Evolution of Ionizable Lipids during Patisiran Development

听课笔记（中英对照）：

1
Patisiran研发过程中的可离子化脂质结构演变 Evolution of ionizable lipid structures in Patisiran development

图A展示了四种典型可离子化脂质的结构：DLinDAP、DLinDMA、DLin-KC2-DMA和DLin-MC3-DMA。 Panel A shows molecular structures of four representative ionizable lipids: DLinDAP, DLinDMA, DLin-KC2-DMA, and DLin-MC3-DMA22。

这些脂质均含有pKa 6.2-6.5的“可离子化”特性，可在不同pH环境中变换电荷，实现siRNA的有效递送。 These lipids all exhibit pKa 6.2-6.5, meaning their ionization states change with pH, enabling efficient siRNA delivery22。

2
各代脂质递送siRNA的体内效果优化 Optimization of in vivo siRNA delivery using different lipid generations

图B反映了不同可离子化脂质包载siRNA时的体内基因沉默效率（以FVII残余百分比为指标）。 Panel B shows in vivo gene silencing efficiency (measured by % residual FVII) for siRNA delivered by different lipids22。

横轴为siRNA剂量（mg/kg），纵轴为体内剩余FVII百分比（残余越低表示沉默效果越好）。 The x-axis is siRNA dose (mg/kg), and the y-axis is % residual FVII in vivo (lower value = better silencing)。

DLinDAP和DLinDMA为早期结构，所需siRNA剂量高，沉默效率低。 DLinDAP and DLinDMA are early-generation, requiring higher doses and giving less efficient silencing22。

DLin-KC2-DMA、DLin-MC3-DMA为后期优化脂质，能极大提升递送效率，显著降低所需siRNA剂量。 DLin-KC2-DMA and DLin-MC3-DMA are later generations, showing much higher delivery efficiency and reduced required siRNA dose22。

其中DLin-MC3-DMA将递送效率提升了约1000倍（4个数量级），成就LNP递送siRNA的“金标准”脂质，且无毒性增加。 DLin-MC3-DMA improved delivery potency by ~1000-fold (4 orders of magnitude) without increased toxicity, becoming the gold standard for LNP siRNA delivery22。

MC3脂质被用于FDA批准siRNA药物Patisiran（Onpattro）临床配方。 MC3 lipid is used in the FDA-approved siRNA drug Patisiran (Onpattro) formulation22。

3
机理总结与课程强调 Summary of mechanism and class emphasis

可离子化脂质通过pH响应性，中性环境下不带电，酸性环境（如内涵体）下带正电，与核酸和阴离子成分协同，破坏内涵体膜，实现高效基因沉默。 Ionizable lipids are pH-responsive: neutral at physiological pH, positively charged in acidic (endosomal) environments, synergizing with nucleic acids and anionic components to disrupt endosomal membranes and enable efficient gene silencing22。

教师补充：MC3等后代脂质的发现极大推动了LNP递送体系的发展，是现代基因沉默与mRNA药物递送领域的里程碑。 Teacher’s emphasis: Discovery of MC3 and other advanced lipids has greatly propelled the LNP delivery system, marking a milestone for gene silencing and mRNA therapeutics22。

纵向梳理要点总结

Key Takeaways:

Patisiran等LNP药物的关键在于可离子化脂质的结构与理化性质优化。 The key to LNP drugs like Patisiran lies in structural and physicochemical optimization of ionizable lipids22。

早期脂质效率低、副作用大，MC3等现代脂质实现了低剂量、高效率、低毒性的平衡。 Early lipids were less efficient and more toxic, but modern lipids like MC3 balance low dose, high efficiency, and low toxicity22。

可离子化脂质的科学进步直接带来了临床突破。 Advances in ionizable lipid chemistry drove direct clinical breakthroughs22。

042025-10-10

疫苗是独特的生物制药产品Vaccines are biopharmaceutical products unlike any others

1
疫苗定义与作用目标
1
Definition and goals of vaccines
疫苗
是由灭活或减毒的微生物（如细菌、病毒、真菌、原生动物）、抗原蛋白、合成构建体或其他生物分子衍生物组成的悬浮液，用于预防、改善或治疗感染性及其他疾病（WHO）。
Vaccines
Suspensions of killed or attenuated microorganisms (bacteria, viruses, fungi, protozoa), antigenic proteins, synthetic constructs, or other biomolecular derivatives, administered for the prevention, amelioration, or treatment of infectious and other diseases (WHO).
目标
安全地复现由病原体激发的免疫应答。
Goal
Safe reproduction of the immune response elicited by a pathogen.

➔ 诱导在接触到毒性强的野生型病原体时能够被激活的记忆性免疫应答。

➔ Induction of a memory immune response that is activated upon contact with the virulent wild-type pathogen.

2
生物药（Biodrug）定义 2. Definition of Biodrug
生物药
由或来源于生物来源的药物物质。
Biodrug
Drug substance produced by or extracted from a biological source.
要求
必须对生产和纯化过程有明确描述和良好表征。
Requirements
Well characterized & described manufacturing and purification process.

对药物表征和质量评估需要结合物理、化学和生物学方法进行。

Characterization and quality assessment need a combination of physical, chemical, and biological assays.

3
相关法规参考 3. Relevant regulatory reference

欧盟生物制品定义（Directive 2001/83/EC修订版，Annex 1 Active substance 3.2.1.1.b）。

EU definition of Biologic (Directive 2001/83/EC as amended, Annex 1 Active substance 3.2.1.1.b).

疫苗的开发周期与特点 Long development and characteristics of vaccines

4
开发周期长 4. Long development

临床实验周期长。

Long clinical experimentation.

制造过程复杂。

Complex manufacturing process.

对质量和控制环境的要求极高，确保最大安全性。

Highly regulated quality and control environments for maximum safety.

5
预防性策略特征 5. Features of the preventive approach

针对健康个体（包括儿童群体），要求极高的收益/风险比。

Target healthy individuals (including pediatric population), which demands high benefit/risk ratio.

获益感知不明显（不是即时可见）。

Perception of benefit is not visible (not immediate).

涉及大规模健康人群及儿童群体。

Large treated populations + pediatric populations.

不可接受副作用。

Side effects not accepted.

6
课堂补充与重点 6. Class highlights and supplements

老师强调，疫苗的给药对象大多为健康人群及儿童，因此社会对疫苗的安全要求极高，副作用几乎不可接受。

The instructor emphasized that vaccines are mostly given to healthy individuals and children, so the public demands extremely high safety, and side effects are hardly tolerated.

疫苗目标在于模拟自然感染时的免疫记忆建立，防止未来真正感染时的发病。

The goal of vaccines is to mimic the immune memory establishment seen in natural infection, thereby preventing illness upon real future exposure.

由于疫苗的效果无法立刻被感知（不像患病后药物治疗能立见疗效），故公众对其效果的感知并不直观。

Since the effect of vaccination is not immediately visible (unlike therapy for illness where the effect is direct), the perception of benefit is not intuitive for the public.

疫苗的生产与质控流程极为复杂且受到严格监管，其过程不同于一般小分子药物的生产。

The vaccine manufacturing and quality control process is extremely complex and strictly regulated, which is different from the production of common small molecule drugs.

【笔记小结 Note Summary】

总结
疫苗是一类与传统药物有本质不同的高标准生物制药产品，开发和生产难度大，对安全性和收益/风险比要求极高，多用于健康人群且效果难以立刻感知，因此任何副作用都难以被接受。
Summary
Vaccines are biopharmaceutical products fundamentally different from traditional drugs; their development and production are difficult with high safety and benefit/risk standards, usually used in healthy populations where effects are not immediately perceptible, and any side effects are hardly acceptable.

1

疫苗作用机制总览General vaccine mechanism of action

不同疫苗策略Different vaccine strategies

已商品化的疫苗Commercialized vaccines

1
疫苗激发免疫反应的基本机制 General mechanism by which vaccines elicit immune responses

疫苗接种的目标是激发特异性与适应性免疫应答，以防止细菌或病毒感染，这一过程叫作主动免疫。 The goal of vaccination is to induce specific and adaptive immune responses to protect against bacterial or viral infections; this is known as active immunization.

疫苗注射后，抗原首先被树突状细胞　和巨噬细胞等抗原提呈细胞摄取。 After injection, vaccine antigens are first taken up by antigen-presenting cells (APCs) such as dendritic cells (DCs) and macrophages.
答案 CD

这些细胞通过吞噬作用摄取并加工抗原，并将其呈递给T细胞。 These cells engulf and process the antigens via phagocytosis, then present them to T cells.

抗原提呈细胞激活后迁移至淋巴结，与特异性B细胞和T细胞相遇，触发特异性免疫反应。 Upon activation, antigen-presenting cells migrate to lymph nodes and interact with specific B cells and T cells, thus triggering a specific immune response.

T细胞帮助B细胞成熟为浆细胞，产生针对抗原的抗体，实现体液免疫。 T cells help B cells mature into plasma cells, which produce antibodies specific for the antigen, enabling humoral immunity.

适应性免疫产生记忆B细胞和T细胞，实现长期免疫保护。 Adaptive immunity generates memory B cells and T cells, leading to long-term immunological protection.

2
不同类型的疫苗策略 Different vaccine strategies

当前疫苗有多种类型，包括减毒活疫苗、灭活疫苗、亚单位疫苗、病毒样颗粒疫苗、mRNA疫苗、DNA疫苗、载体疫苗等。 There are multiple types of vaccines, such as live attenuated, inactivated (killed), subunit, virus-like particle (VLP), mRNA, DNA, and vector-based vaccines.

亚单位疫苗可以是纯化抗原、重组抗原、多糖或结合疫苗。 Subunit vaccines may consist of purified antigens, recombinant antigens, polysaccharides, or conjugated forms.

多价疫苗针对同一病原体的不同血清型或株，例如23价肺炎球菌疫苗。 Multivalent vaccines target different serotypes or strains of the same pathogen, such as the 23-valent pneumococcal vaccine.

联合疫苗包含多种病原体的抗原，例如百白破-脊髓灰质炎联合疫苗同时预防多种疾病。 Combined vaccines contain antigens from several pathogens, like the DTP-polio combination vaccine that prevents multiple diseases at once.

需要注意的是，并非所有疫苗策略都已商品化。 Note that not all vaccine strategies are commercially available.

3
已商品化的主要疫苗类型 Major types of commercialized vaccines

灭活（非活性）疫苗包括伤寒、霍乱、鼠疫、百日咳、流感、脊灰（注射）、乙肝、甲肝等。 Inactivated (killed) vaccines include those for typhoid, cholera, plague, pertussis, influenza, injectable polio, hepatitis A, etc.

减毒活疫苗包括天花、卡介苗（BCG）、狂犬病、黄热病、口服脊灰、麻疹、腮腺炎、风疹、腺病毒、水痘、轮状病毒等。 Live attenuated vaccines include smallpox, BCG, rabies, yellow fever, oral polio (Sabin), measles, mumps, rubella, adenovirus, chickenpox, rotavirus, etc.

亚单位疫苗可为重组（乙肝、HPV、无细胞百日咳），纯化多糖类（脑膜炎球菌、肺炎球菌、伤寒），结合型（Hib、脑膜炎球菌、肺炎球菌），类毒素（破伤风、白喉）。 Subunit vaccines can be recombinant (hepatitis B, HPV, acellular pertussis), purified polysaccharides (meningococcus, pneumococcus, typhoid), conjugated (Hib, meningococcus, pneumococcus), or anatoxins (tetanus, diphtheria).

mRNA疫苗和部分非复制载体疫苗，已用于新冠病毒预防（COVID-19）。 mRNA vaccines and some non-replicating vector vaccines have been used for COVID-19 prevention.

疫苗生产总体流程General scheme for vaccine production

第一部分：疫苗开发总体流程

Part 1
General scheme for a vaccine development
1
疫苗的平均开发周期约为12年，总体研发投入通常超过5亿欧元。 The average development time for a vaccine is about 12 years, with total R&D investment usually exceeding 0.5 billion euros24。
2
整个开发流程中，约70%的时间都用于质量控制。 Around 70% of the total timeline is dedicated to quality control27。
3
研发阶段包括实验阶段、临床前阶段、I-III期临床试验，这一部分一般持续9-14年。 The R&D phase includes experimental, preclinical, and clinical phases I to III, typically lasting 9-14 years24。
4
实验和工艺开发完成后，需进行产品注册和上市许可，然后进入工业化操作。 After experimental work and process development, registration and market authorization are required before entering industrial operations24。
5
工业化阶段涵盖了种子批生产、工艺开发、产业化可行性验证、生产微生物（或原材料）、收获、纯化、灭活、组装价次、配方、冻干、包装、分发和最终批次放行。 The industrial phase includes first batch making, process development, industrial feasibility, germ biomass production, harvest, purification, inactivation, valence assembly, formulation, lyophilization, conditioning, distribution, and batch release24。
6
商业化阶段包括市场需求评估、目标产品和人群确定及疫苗商业化，最终进入市场。 The commercial phase covers evaluation of needs, definition of product target profile and population, and commercialization of vaccine batches into the market24。
7
整个过程中，质量控制与药物警戒持续进行，确保每一批次疫苗的安全性和有效性。 Quality control and pharmacovigilance are performed throughout the process to ensure safety and efficacy of each vaccine batch27。
补充
老师口头强调
Supplement
Teacher's verbal highlights
8
疫苗必须面对健康大人和儿童，接种对象普遍且对风险容忍度极低，因此质量标准非常严苛。 Vaccines are given to healthy adults and children, covering a wide population with very low risk tolerance; thus, the quality standards are extremely strict2。
9
质量控制伴随疫苗从研发到市场全生命周期，包括研发、生产、包装、上市后的每一个环节。 Quality control accompanies the vaccine through the entire lifecycle from R&D to market, covering every step in development, production, packaging, and post-marketing627。

第二部分：疫苗生产总体流程（工业化操作）

Part 2
General scheme for vaccine production (industrial operations)
1
工业化流程分为上游（USP，微生物/细胞生物量生产）和下游（DSP，收获、纯化、灭活、过滤）操作。 The industrial process is split into upstream (USP, germ biomass production) and downstream (DSP, harvest, purification, inactivation, filtration) operations24。
2
下游工艺第一步是通过离心与过滤进行收获，所需部分可以是上清液或细胞。 The first DSP step is harvest using centrifugation and filtration, keeping either the supernatant or the cells depending on the product24。
3
纯化步骤采用层析或沉淀等技术，去除杂质。 Purification is performed using chromatography or precipitation to remove impurities24。
4
灭活步骤（仅针对灭活疫苗）可用过氧化氢、甲醛、戊二醛、胃蛋白酶（pH4）、潮湿加热等方法处理。 Inactivation (only for inactivated vaccines) uses agents like hydrogen peroxide, formaldehyde, glutaraldehyde, pepsin pH4, or damp heat14。
5
活疫苗则无灭活步骤，直接进入后续加工环节。 Live attenuated vaccines skip inactivation and proceed directly to later steps14。
6
过滤用于最终清除反应残留及微生物杂质，保证疫苗成品纯净。 Filtration is used at the end to remove residuals and ensure final product purity24。
补充
老师口头强调
Supplement
Teacher's verbal highlights
7
每个步骤都有严格的质量把控标准，且工艺复杂、周期长。 Each step has strict quality control standards, and the process is complex and time-consuming136。
8
工业化生产和监管要求通常导致一个疫苗从研发到上市需十年以上时间，投资巨大。 Industrial production and regulatory requirements mean that it often takes more than a decade and major investment to bring a vaccine from discovery to market25。

第一页：配方Formulation

配方的主要目的是维持活性成分的结构和稳定性。 The main purpose of formulation is to maintain the structure and stability of the active substance.

配方可显著提高疫苗的稳定性和持续效力。 Formulation can significantly enhance the sustained stability of vaccines.

佐剂的加入能提升免疫效果、并减少副作用。 The addition of adjuvants increases vaccine potency and minimizes adverse effects.

常用稳定剂包括乳糖和山梨醇。 Common stabilisers include lactose and sorbitol.

欧盟药典建议每剂疫苗中铝（Al3+）的最大用量为1.25mg，2岁以下儿童为0.125-0.82mg。 The European Pharmacopoeia recommends a maximum content of 1.25mg aluminum (Al3+) per dose, and 0.125-0.82mg for children under 2 years old.

常见佐剂分为盐类（如明矾）和油包水乳剂（如MF59、AS03、AF03等）。 Common adjuvants are divided into salts (e.g., alum) and oil-in-water emulsions (e.g., MF59, AS03, AF03).

佐剂通过刺激树突状细胞和巨噬细胞增强免疫反应，部分可引发轻微炎症作为正常免疫激活现象。 Adjuvants stimulate dendritic cells and macrophages to enhance immune responses, and some may cause mild local inflammation as part of normal immune activation 1611.

第二页
灌装、冻干、包装Filling, lyophilisation, packaging

小瓶（VIAL）疫苗包装流程包括清洗、灭菌、灌装、冻干、加盖、目检/自动检视和贴标签。 The vial packaging process includes cleaning, sterilization, filling, lyophilization, capping, visual/automatic inspection, and labelling.

注射器（SYRINGE）包装则是：灭菌、灌装、目检/自动检视、贴标签。 The syringe packaging process includes sterilization, filling, visual/automatic inspection, and labelling.

灭菌和无菌灌装是确保疫苗不被污染的关键步骤。 Sterilization and aseptic filling are essential to prevent vaccine contamination.

灭菌、冻干和封盖后需进行严格目视或自动检验，剔除不合格品。 After sterilization, lyophilization, and capping, strict visual or automatic inspection is performed to remove defective products.

最后贴上标签，完成最终产品出厂。 Finally, labels are applied to complete the finished product.

第三页
实例1：Infanrix Hexa（联合疫苗）Example 1: Infanrix Hexa (combined vaccine)

Infanrix Hexa是由多种成分组成的联合疫苗。 Infanrix Hexa is a combination vaccine containing multiple components.

白喉类毒素和破伤风类毒素均由细菌培养纯化获得，分别用于预防白喉和破伤风。 Diphtheria and tetanus anatoxins are purified from bacterial cultures to prevent diphtheria and tetanus.

百日咳抗原来源于细菌培养，包括PT（百日咳毒素）、HA（丝状血凝素）、PRN（黏附素），用于预防百日咳。 Bordetella pertussis antigens include PT (pertussis toxin), HA (filamentous haemagglutinin), and PRN (pertactin), all purified from cultures for whooping cough prevention.

乙肝表面抗原（HBs抗原）为重组蛋白，来源于基因工程，用于乙型肝炎预防。 Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) is a recombinant protein produced by genetic engineering for hepatitis B prevention.

灭活脊髓灰质炎病毒组分分别来自三种不同毒株，均经过纯化和灭活处理。 Inactivated poliovirus components are purified and inactivated strains of types 1, 2, and 3.

b型流感嗜血杆菌多糖结合蛋白组分采用纯化多糖与类毒素结合，增强免疫反应。 Haemophilus influenzae type b component is a purified polysaccharide conjugated with tetanic toxoid to enhance immune response.

赋形剂包括无水乳糖、氯化钠、氨基酸、矿物盐、维生素等，保证制剂稳定。 Excipients include anhydrous lactose, sodium chloride, amino acids, mineral salts, vitamins, etc., for formulation stability.

可能残留微量甲醛、新霉素和多粘菌素，用于制造过程中。 Trace amounts of formaldehyde, neomycin, and polymyxin may remain from the manufacturing process11。

联合疫苗六种成分的生产与放行流程Production and Release Process of Six Components in Combination Vaccine

该图展示了一个联合疫苗中六种成分的生产、质控、配方及最终放行的全过程。 This diagram shows the entire process for producing, quality controlling, formulating, and ultimately releasing six components in a combination vaccine.

联合疫苗包括六个主要成分，分别通过六条独立生产线（P1至P6）制备：白喉类毒素、破伤风类毒素、乙肝表面抗原、百日咳抗原、灭活脊髓灰质炎病毒（1/2/3型）、和Hib多糖。 The combination vaccine contains six main components, each produced on an independent production line (P1 to P6): diphtheria toxoid, tetanus toxoid, hepatitis B surface antigen, pertussis antigens, inactivated poliovirus types 1/2/3, and Hib polysaccharide.

各组分在生产过程中都要经过严格的质量控制（QC1-QC6），确保其纯度、安全性和活性达标。 Each component undergoes strict quality control in its respective line (QC1 to QC6) to ensure purity, safety, and potency.

如果配方中有佐剂，也必须进行独立的质量控制流程。 If adjuvants are included in the formulation, they too require separate quality control steps.

所有通过质控的成分（即各自的valence批次）会被释放（released batches）用于最终配方。 All components that pass quality control (i.e., valence batches) are released for use in the final formulation.

这些组分被合并、配伍，形成最终的疫苗制剂（Formulation: Drug Product），再进行后续灌装、包装和分发等操作。 These components are combined and formulated into the final drug product, followed by subsequent filling, packaging, and distribution procedures.

在最终制剂灌装前，还需进行综合质量检查（CQ），确保所有成分相容且没有相互干扰。 Before filling, a comprehensive quality check (CQ) is performed to ensure all combined components are compatible and do not interfere with each other.

最后产品需通过卫生主管部门的独立放行（Heath authorities release），只有通过所有质量体系审核后才能上市。 The final product must pass release by health authorities, and only after meeting all quality requirements can it be marketed.

整个流程从各组分生产到最终放行，通常需耗时24-36个月。 The entire process from raw component production to final release typically takes 24-36 months.

重组乙肝表面抗原的生产过程 Recombinant HBs Antigen Production Process

乙型肝炎表面抗原（HBsAg）是一种由三种多肽——L、S和M组成的蛋白。 Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) is a protein composed of three polypeptides: L, S, and M.

首先从乙肝病毒中分离出表面抗原的mRNA，并将其逆转录为cDNA。 First, the mRNA encoding the surface antigen is isolated from the HBV and reverse-transcribed into cDNA.

将含有HBsAg编码序列的cDNA插入到质粒载体中，构建表达载体。 The cDNA encoding HBsAg is inserted into a plasmid to construct an expression vector.

该表达载体经转化导入酵母细胞，实现目的蛋白的生物合成。 This expression vector is introduced into yeast cells to enable biosynthesis of the target protein.

酵母细胞通过发酵生产大量的HBsAg蛋白。 Yeast cells are used in a fermentation process to produce large amounts of HBsAg.

生产工艺首先通过微滤收获发酵产物，回收酵母细胞。 The first production step is harvesting the fermentation product by microfiltration, collecting yeast cells.

加入蛋白酶抑制剂，防止蛋白在随后的处理过程中被降解。 Protease inhibitors are added to prevent degradation of the protein during subsequent steps.

使用匀浆机破碎细胞，释放胞内的HBsAg蛋白。 A homogenizer is used to disrupt cells and release intracellular HBsAg protein.

用Triton X-100（0.2-0.5% v/v）提取蛋白，溶解细胞膜和包膜杂质。 Triton X-100 (0.2-0.5% v/v) is used to extract the protein and solubilize membrane and envelope impurities.

再次采用微滤澄清体系，去除大颗粒杂质。 A second microfiltration (clarification) is performed to remove large particle impurities.

使用XAD-4树脂去除Triton X-100等表面活性剂残留。 XAD-4 resin is applied to remove residual Triton X-100 and other surfactants.

蛋白吸附到胶体硅上，进行进一步纯化。 The protein is adsorbed onto colloidal silica for further purification.

接着进行疏水作用色谱（HIC），纯化和分离目标蛋白。 Hydrophobic interaction chromatography (HIC) is then performed to purify and separate the target protein.

用硫氰酸钾进一步去除杂质。 Potassium thiocyanate treatment is used for removal of remaining impurities.

最终，将纯化的HBsAg蛋白与明矾佐剂配制成疫苗制剂。 Finally, the purified HBsAg protein is formulated with alum adjuvant to make the vaccine product.

在酵母中表达的HBsAg能自组装形成类病毒颗粒（VLP），大大增强了其免疫原性。 Yeast-expressed HBsAg can self-assemble into virus-like particles (VLPs), greatly enhancing immunogenicity.

生产中“上游工艺”指发酵及初步收获，“下游工艺”包括细胞破碎、蛋白提取与多步纯化。 The "upstream process" refers to fermentation and harvesting, while the "downstream process" includes cell disruption, protein extraction, and multi-step purification.

HBsAg蛋白位于酵母细胞内，因此需通过细胞破碎来释放，再进行提取。 HBsAg is produced intracellularly in yeast, so cell disruption is required for release prior to extraction.

每一步操作都需严格质量控制，确保蛋白的有效性和安全性，最终产品需接受监管部门的放行。 All steps require strict quality control to ensure efficacy and safety, with final product release subjected to health authority approval.

P4
百日咳博德特氏菌组份产物的生产（PT，FHA，PRN）
P4
Bordetella pertussis valences production: pertussic anatoxin (PT), Filamentous haemagglutinin (FHA) and pertactin (PRN) = purified proteins

听课笔记 Lecture Notes

1
百日咳博德特氏菌的培养

培养百日咳博德特氏菌。

Culture Bordetella pertussis.

2
分离菌体与上清液

通过离心或过滤，将发酵液分为上清液和沉淀（菌体）。

Separate culture into supernatant and pellet (cells) by centrifugation or filtration.

上清液路线
PT和FHA的提纯

（1）化学解毒（过氧化氢、福尔马林、戊二醛）去除毒性。

Apply chemical detoxification (hydrogen peroxide, formalin, glutaraldehyde) to remove toxicity.

（2）硫酸铵沉淀，初步富集蛋白。

Precipitate proteins with ammonium sulfate for initial enrichment.

（3）离子交换色谱纯化分离PT和FHA。

Purify and separate PT and FHA by ion exchange chromatography.

4
沉淀（菌体）路线：PRN的提纯

（1）60°C热提取从沉淀中析出PRN蛋白。

Extract PRN protein from pellet by heat extraction at 60°C.

（2）双离子交换色谱进一步纯化。

Further purify by dual-ion exchange chromatography.

（3）疏水作用色谱（HIC）最终纯化，得到高纯度PRN。

Final purification is performed by hydrophobic interaction chromatography (HIC) to obtain high-purity PRN.

5
各步关键点与回收率

初始阴离子交换色谱将产物浓缩至14.6%，回收率80%。 Initial anion exchange chromatography concentrates to 14.6%, with 80% recovery.

第二步阳离子交换色谱纯度提升至33%，回收率83%。 Second cation exchange chromatography increases purity to 33%, 83% recovery.

最终疏水色谱纯度达96%。三步总回收率61%。 Final hydrophobic interaction chromatography yields 96% purity; total recovery of three columns is 61%.

6
总结

三种蛋白PT、FHA和PRN经多步分离纯化工艺分别获得。

Three proteins (PT, FHA, PRN) are obtained separately through multi-step purification processes.

百日咳博德特氏菌价生产（PT、FHA、PRN）=纯化蛋白  Bordetella pertussis valence production (PT, FHA, PRN) = purified proteins

脊髓灰质炎价生产（3种病毒型）P5: Poliomyelitis valences production (3 virus types)

1
采用微载体培养VERO细胞，并将规模从2升扩大到1500升。 VERO cells are cultured on microcarriers, with scale-up from 2L to 1500L.
2
使用三种不同的病毒种子批次（1、2、3）分别感染VERO细胞。 Three different viral seed batches (1, 2, 3) are used to infect VERO cells separately.
3
对于每一种病毒型，单独在1500升VERO细胞体系中进行感染。 For each virus type, infection is carried out separately in 1500L of VERO cells.
4
感染后，通过过滤去除细胞杂质，收集病毒上清液。 After infection, filtration is used to remove cell debris and collect the virus-containing supernatant.
5
上清液依次经过浓缩、纯化。 The supernatant is then subjected to concentration and purification steps.
6
纯化后的病毒经甲醛灭活，确保彻底失去感染性和致病性。 The purified virus is inactivated using formaldehyde to ensure complete loss of infectivity and pathogenicity.
7
三种病毒型分别独立生产与灭活后，混合得到包含三价成分的灭活脊灰疫苗。 After the three virus types are independently produced and inactivated, they are mixed to obtain a trivalent inactivated poliovirus vaccine.
8
生产过程要求采用无菌操作，防止交叉污染，且每种病毒分开处理直至最终混合。 Aseptic procedures are crucial throughout production to avoid cross-contamination, and the three virus types are handled separately until the final mixing step.
9
该工艺确保了每种病毒型的稳定性和灭活效果，满足多价疫苗的质量与安全要求。 This process guarantees the stability and proper inactivation of each virus type, meeting the quality and safety standards of multivalent vaccines.

Hib多糖疫苗生产/纯化（纯化多糖与载体的结合）

P6
Production/Purification of Haemophilus influenza type b polyoside (Combination of purified polysaccharide with a carrier)
1
以冻干Hib菌株为种子，在1200升发酵罐中培养产生细菌。 Starting from the lyophilized Hib seed, bacteria are cultured in a 1200-liter fermenter.
2
通过甲醛灭活Hib细菌，确保致病性消除。 Hib bacteria are inactivated by formaldehyde to ensure elimination of pathogenicity.
3
灭活后回收菌体，为后续多糖纯化做准备。 After inactivation, the bacterial cells are recovered for subsequent polysaccharide purification.
4
多糖纯化步骤包括使用变性洗涤剂、色谱和过滤，以除去杂质并富集多糖组分。 The polysaccharide is purified using chaotropic detergents, chromatography, and filtration to remove impurities and enrich the polysaccharide.
5
接着对多糖进行化学修饰，产生能与载体结合的活性基团。 The purified polysaccharide is then chemically modified to generate reactive groups that can bind to carrier proteins.
6
载体蛋白（如破伤风毒素、白喉毒素或H. influenzae的D蛋白）由Clostridium tetani等菌株经发酵、甲醛灭活、上清液回收和毒素纯化获得。 Carrier proteins, such as tetanic toxin, diphtheria toxin (native or mutated), or D protein from H. influenzae, are obtained by fermenting bacteria (e.g., Clostridium tetani), inactivating them with formaldehyde, recovering the supernatant, and purifying the toxin.
7
多糖与载体蛋白通过化学共价结合，形成多糖-蛋白偶联物（即疫苗活性物质）。 The polysaccharide and carrier protein are chemically conjugated to form a polysaccharide-protein conjugate, which is the active substance of the vaccine.
8
最后，将多糖-蛋白偶联物用硫酸铝吸附，完成最终疫苗的配制。 The final formulation is achieved by adsorbing the conjugate onto aluminium sulfate, finishing the vaccine preparation.
9
该偶联多糖疫苗可显著提高免疫原性，适用于婴幼儿群体接种。 This conjugate polysaccharide vaccine significantly enhances immunogenicity and is suitable for immunization of infants and young children.
补充说明
老师强调

多糖疫苗若不与载体蛋白偶联，免疫原性低，婴幼儿主体不能有效激发保护性应答。 Teacher emphasized that unconjugated polysaccharide vaccines have low immunogenicity and cannot elicit effective protective responses in infants.

共轭后，疫苗可通过T细胞辅助途径诱导强烈、持久的免疫反应，产生免疫记忆。 After conjugation, the vaccine induces a strong and durable immune response via T cell help, resulting in immunological memory.

工艺链条需严格无菌、过程受控，保证最终疫苗安全高效。 The entire process must be strictly aseptic and controlled to ensure vaccine safety and efficac

疫苗多价产品的生产与配制（Formulation of Multivalent Vaccines）Formulation of Multivalent Vaccines多价疫苗的配制

1
生产地点分散，需全球统筹管理 多价疫苗的生产常常在地理上分散的地方，甚至不同国家进行，这涉及到市场准入、技术以及知识产权等多因素。 The manufacturing operations for multivalent vaccines are often carried out in geographically distant sites, or even in different countries, due to factors such as market access, technology, and intellectual property considerations.
2
物料与批次管理至关重要 在上述背景下，对物料流转、产品批次、质控试剂以及生产区域的管理，对疫苗分销能否成功至关重要。 In this context, the management of material flows, product batches, QC reagents, and production areas is fundamental for the success of vaccine distribution.
3
所有组分必须最终汇合配制成疫苗制品 所有活性药物成分和佐剂需有效集合、配制，才能制得最终疫苗制品。 All the drug substances and adjuvants must be brought together to produce (formulate) the final drug product—the vaccine.
4
配制时的关键约束（Formulation Constraints）

避免不同抗原、佐剂及防腐剂之间的不相容 Avoiding incompatibility between different antigens, adjuvants, and preservatives

避免表位降解、蛋白不稳定/聚集 Avoid degradation of epitopes, destabilisation or aggregation of proteins

避免诱发“对立型”免疫反应（如Th1 vs Th2） Avoid induction of "opposite" immune responses (i.e., Th1 vs Th2)

简明解读 | Summary:

多价疫苗的核心难点在于各成分在不同地点独立生产，最终需要统一质量标准并科学配方，保证配伍安全、有效与免疫应答方向的一致性，否则会出现组分间相互干扰或免疫效果打折等问题。

The core difficulty with multivalent vaccines is that each component is independently produced in different locations and must ultimately meet unified quality standards and scientifically sound formulation, ensuring compatibility, efficacy, and consistency of the immune response; otherwise, interference or compromised vaccine performance can occur.

mRNA疫苗的作用机制How mRNA vaccines work

1
mRNA疫苗通过注射进入宿主细胞，并被内吞至胞内囊泡。 The mRNA vaccine is delivered into host cells via injection and taken up into intracellular vesicles (endosomes).
2
mRNA释放到细胞质中后，被核糖体翻译为病毒蛋白抗原。 After release into the cytoplasm, mRNA is translated by ribosomes into viral protein antigens.
3
部分合成的抗原蛋白在胞内被蛋白酶体降解为肽片段，并由内质网及高尔基体处理后，通过MHC I分子递呈，激活CD8+ T细胞，诱导适应性细胞免疫。 Some synthesized antigen proteins are degraded by proteasomes into peptides, processed through the ER and Golgi apparatus, and presented via MHC I molecules to activate CD8+ T cells, leading to adaptive cellular immunity.
4
细胞通过先天免疫通路识别mRNA（如TLR介导），激活干扰素（IFN-I）及炎症因子，启动先天免疫反应。 Cells recognize mRNA via innate immune pathways (e.g., TLR-mediated), triggering interferons (IFN-I) and proinflammatory cytokines to initiate innate immune responses.
5
一部分抗原通过MHC II途径被抗原递呈细胞递呈给CD4+ T细胞，进一步诱导适应性免疫（辅助T细胞反应），推动B细胞活化。 Some antigens are presented via the MHC II pathway by antigen-presenting cells to CD4+ T cells, further inducing adaptive immunity (helper T cell response) and facilitating B cell activation.
6
最终，B细胞被激活生成浆细胞，分泌特异性抗体，实现体液免疫。 Finally, activated B cells differentiate into plasma cells and secrete specific antibodies, achieving humoral immunity.

要点总结 | Key Points

mRNA疫苗通过表达抗原蛋白，激活先天和适应性免疫（包括细胞免疫和体液免疫）。 mRNA vaccines induce both innate and adaptive immunity (cellular and humoral) by expressing antigenic proteins.

先天免疫可增强疫苗效力，但过度激活需控制。 Innate immune activation can enhance vaccine efficacy but must be balanced.

适应性免疫保证长期保护，抗体产生是关键终点。 Adaptive immunity ensures long-term protection, with antibody production as a key endpoint.

老师讲解补充：

教师强调，mRNA疫苗不同于传统疫苗，无需病毒或蛋白纯化，而是直接利用宿主细胞产生抗原。这种方式可以诱导广泛又强烈的免疫反应，尤其是辅助T细胞（Th）、细胞毒性T细胞和B细胞抗体应答。

Teacher highlighted that mRNA vaccines, unlike traditional vaccines, do not require viral or protein purification, but use the host cell to produce antigens. This approach elicits broad and robust immune responses, especially helper T cell (Th), cytotoxic T cell, and B cell antibody responses

mRNA疫苗生产实例Example 2: mRNA vaccines production

1
整体流程分为“制造、纯化、配制”三大阶段。

The overall process consists of three main stages: "Make, Purify, Formulate".

2
首先，以含有目标抗原的质粒DNA为起始（双链DNA编码药物活性成分）。

First, production starts from plasmid DNA containing the coding sequence for the immunogen (double-stranded DNA coding for the drug substance) 8。

3
主要步骤如下：

The main steps are as follows:

⑴ 质粒DNA线性化。 (1) Linearization of plasmid DNA.

⑵ 色谱和/或超滤浓缩操作去除杂质，收集纯化的线性DNA。 (2) Chromatography and/or ultrafiltration/diafiltration (UF/DF) to remove impurities and collect pure linear DNA.

⑶ 体外转录，利用RNA聚合酶将DNA模板转录为mRNA。 (3) In vitro transcription using RNA polymerase to produce mRNA from the DNA template.

⑷ 经色谱及超滤/浓缩（UF/DF）再度纯化。 (4) Further purification by chromatography and UF/DF.

⑸ 酶促加帽，即在mRNA 5'端加上帽结构，增强稳定性及翻译效率。 (5) Enzymatic capping adds a cap to the 5' end of the mRNA, improving stability and translation efficiency.

⑹ 终末色谱纯化，进一步除去杂质。 (6) Final purification by chromatography.

⑺ 超滤/浓缩（UF/DF）作为收尾纯化步骤。 (7) Final UF/DF purification step.

4
mRNA被包封/配制与递送系统，并通过无菌过滤制备最终成品。

The purified mRNA is encapsulated and formulated—with delivery systems (like lipid nanoparticles)—and then subjected to final sterile filtration for producing the drug product 8。

5
结构说明（图下部模块）：

Explanation of structure (bottom schematic):

5'非翻译区(5' UTR): 提高翻译效率。 5' UTR: Untranslated region, for translation efficacy.

RDRP区段
编码RNA依赖的RNA聚合酶，增强自我复制能力。 RDRP complex: RNA-dependent RNA polymerase, enables self-replication.
Immunogen区段
编码病毒蛋白，是诱导免疫的核心。 Immunogen: Encodes the viral protein, the key immune-inducing element.

3'非翻译区(3' UTR): 也参与稳定/翻译调控。 3' UTR: Untranslated region, involved in stability/translation regulation.

(成熟产物还带有5'帽结构和3' polyA尾巴，加强mRNA稳定性和翻译)。 (The mature product has a 5' cap and a 3' polyA tail for enhanced stability and translation.)

6
关键术语提醒：

Key terms:

UTR
非翻译区，提升翻译效率。 UTR: Untranslated region, for translation efficacy.
RDRP
RNA依赖RNA聚合酶，用于自复制。 RDRP: RNA-dependent RNA polymerase, for self-replication.
Immunogen
即“免疫原”，为病毒蛋白编码区。 Immunogen: The viral protein coding sequence.
7
教师补充讲解要点：

Key teacher explanations:

所有步骤需严格控制杂质和无菌，纯化环节多次进行，保证最终mRNA药物的安全和高纯。 Strict impurity and sterility control is required for all steps, with multiple purification rounds to ensure high purity and safety of final mRNA product 8。

加帽与polyA尾巴对于mRNA疫苗表达与稳定性至关重要；不加帽的mRNA很快被降解，无法激发理想免疫反应。 Capping and polyA tails are critical for mRNA vaccine expression and stability; uncapped mRNA would rapidly degrade and fail to induce effective immune responses.

mRNA疫苗生产高度自动化且适于大规模生产，是现代疫苗技术的重要突破。 The production of mRNA vaccines is highly automated and suitable for large-scale manufacturing, marking a major advancement in modern vaccine technology 8。

Step 1
生产mRNA——听课笔记整理
Step 1
Producing the mRNA——Lecture Notes
1
质粒DNA的生产与纯化
1
Plasmid DNA Production and Purification

首先将目标基因的cDNA片段插入质粒载体，完成载体构建。 First, the cDNA fragment encoding the target is inserted into a plasmid vector, completing vector construction.

利用细菌对质粒进行扩增，收获含有多拷贝质粒的细菌。 Bacterial amplification is used to multiply the plasmid, harvesting bacteria containing multi-copy plasmid DNA.

经过发酵罐大规模培养后，进行细胞裂解和后续纯化，获得高纯度超螺旋质粒DNA作为后续模板。 After large-scale fermentation, cell lysis and subsequent purification steps are performed to obtain highly pure supercoiled plasmid DNA, which serves as the following template.

2
线性化DNA
2
DNA Linearization

采用限制性内切酶、BSA和缓冲液，将超螺旋质粒切割成为线性化的DNA，保证后续转录模板的一致性。 Use restriction enzyme, BSA, and buffer to digest the supercoiled plasmid, producing linearized DNA as a uniform transcription template.

3
体外转录mRNA
3
In vitro Transcription of mRNA

在线性化质粒为模板的基础上，添加RNA聚合酶、核苷三磷酸（NTP）和缓冲液，进行体外转录。 Using the linearized plasmid as a template, add RNA polymerase, NTP, and buffer to perform in vitro transcription.

产物为带有5’UTR、3’UTR、RDRP区段、免疫原区段的mRNA分子，还会有polyA尾巴。 The product is an mRNA molecule with 5' UTR, 3' UTR, RDRP region, and the immunogen region, as well as a polyA tail.

4
细菌毒素杂质需严格控制
4
Endotoxins Must Be Carefully Controlled

质粒扩增过程中，细菌来源会携带内毒素等杂质，需在纯化步骤严格去除。 Endotoxins and other impurities from bacterial sources must be strictly removed during purification.

典型产量为每升培养基可得200-300 mg质粒。 Typical yield is about 200-300 mg plasmid per liter of medium.

5
教师补充要点
5
Additional Teacher’s Notes

细胞库（主细胞库MCB、工作细胞库WCB）需长期保存且符合质量管控要求。 Cell banking (Master Cell Bank, MCB; Working Cell Bank, WCB) must be maintained and quality controlled.

每一步质粒的制备到纯化都需要质量检测，为疫苗生产后续步骤奠定基础。 Each step from plasmid preparation to purification must pass quality control, ensuring a solid foundation for the next steps in vaccine manufacturing.

纯化DNA的质量控制Quality control of purified DNA

1
需要检测的杂质及推荐检测方法

Impurities to be tested and recommended assays

杂质包括
细菌DNA、RNA、质粒异构体、蛋白质、内毒素、无菌性，以及DNA本身的身份鉴定。 Impurities include: bacterial DNA, RNA, plasmid isoforms, proteins, endotoxins, sterility, and identification of DNA itself.
2
各项杂质的检测方法与合格标准

Detection methods and acceptance criteria for each impurity

细菌DNA
琼脂糖凝胶电泳、Southern Blot 或PCR检测，要求小于0.01μg/μg质粒。 Bacterial DNA: Detected by agarose gel electrophoresis, Southern Blot, or PCR; should be undetectable, less than 0.01 μg/μg plasmid.
RNA
琼脂糖凝胶电泳检测，要求不可检出。 RNA: Detected by agarose electrophoresis; should be undetectable.
质粒异构体
琼脂糖凝胶电泳检测，比例应低于5%。 Plasmid isoforms: Detected by agarose electrophoresis; should be less than 5%.
蛋白质
蛋白定量和SDS-PAGE银染检测，要求不可检出。 Proteins: Detected by protein dosage and SDS-PAGE plus Ag staining; should be undetectable.
内毒素
LAL试验检测，要求低于0.1 U/μg质粒。 Endotoxins: Tested by LAL test; should be less than 0.1 U/μg plasmid.
无菌性
要求无微生物检出。 Sterility: No micro-organisms should be detected.
DNA身份鉴定
通过限制酶图谱和测序，要求与参考分子一致。 DNA identity: Verified by restriction map and sequencing; should match the reference molecule.
3
教师补充讲解

Additional teacher highlights

教师强调，要严格去除杂质，特别是内毒素和外源细菌核酸残留，因为这些会影响疫苗安全性和有效性。 The teacher emphasized the strict removal of impurities, especially endotoxins and residual exogenous bacterial nucleic acids, as these can affect vaccine safety and efficacy.

每一步纯化后都要进行质量检测，确保最终得到高纯、高安全性的DNA作为下游mRNA合成的模板。 Quality control must be conducted after each purification step to ensure highly pure and safe DNA, which serves as the template for downstream mRNA synthesis.

4
质量检测的重要意义

Importance of quality control

只有各项指标均达标，才能进入mRNA生产和疫苗后续环节。 Only when all parameters meet the criteria can the DNA enter subsequent mRNA production and vaccine manufacturing steps.

化学修饰、进一步纯化和配制Next steps: chemical modification, further purification and formulation

1
纯化和配制的工艺流程

Purify and formulate process flow

首先通过色谱或超滤/置换（UF/DF）进行粗纯，去除杂质。 First, rough purification is carried out using chromatography or ultrafiltration/diafiltration (UF/DF) to remove impurities.

然后进行酶促加帽，常加入GTP、DNA酶和缓冲液，确保mRNA 5'端有帽结构。 Next, enzymatic capping is performed—adding GTP, DNase, and buffer—to ensure a 5' cap structure on the mRNA.

再次通过色谱进一步纯化。 Further purification is done by chromatography.

采用超滤/置换（UF/DF）进行最后的缓冲更换、浓缩。 Final buffer exchange and concentration are achieved by UF/DF.

mRNA被包封并配制成脂质纳米粒（LNP）等递送系统。 The mRNA is encapsulated and formulated into delivery systems, such as lipid nanoparticles (LNPs).

终末无菌过滤，获得药物成品。 Final sterile filtration yields the drug product.

2
mRNA结构和加帽的重要性

mRNA structure and importance of capping

mRNA包括5’帽结构、5’非翻译区（5'UTR）、RDRP区段（如有）、免疫原区段、3’非翻译区（3'UTR）和polyA尾巴。 The mRNA includes a 5’ cap, 5' untranslated region (5' UTR), RDRP region (if present), immunogen region, 3' untranslated region (3' UTR), and a polyA tail.

加帽可保护mRNA并促进其成熟和高效翻译。 The cap structure protects mRNA and enables its maturation and efficient translation.

3
配制成脂质纳米粒系统

Formulation into lipid nanoparticle (LNP) system

mRNA最终包裹于脂质纳米粒，包括PEG化脂质、阳离子脂、DSPC脂、胆固醇。 The finalized mRNA is encapsulated in lipid nanoparticles, consisting of PEG-lipid, cationic lipid, DSPC lipid, and cholesterol.

LNP能有效递送mRNA至细胞内，保证疫苗递送和免疫效果。 LNPs enable efficient delivery of mRNA into cells, ensuring vaccination efficacy.

4
教师补充讲解

Teacher’s additional explanation

加帽反应通常和去除DNA模板（如加DNase处理）同步进行，避免杂质残留。 The capping step is usually combined with DNase treatment to remove the DNA template, minimizing residual impurities.

反复纯化和最终无菌过滤对保障mRNA疫苗安全性和有效性至关重要。 Repeated purification and final sterile filtration are critical for the safety and efficacy of mRNA vaccines.

5
工艺小结（图示对照）

Process summary (Diagram illustration reference)

上方流程图依次展示了“酶促加帽→色谱纯化→超滤→包载与最终无菌过滤”的完整工艺。 The diagram at the top illustrates the complete process: enzymatic capping → chromatography purification → UF/DF → encapsulation and final sterile filtration.

下方结构图例展示了脂质纳米粒包裹mRNA的组成。 The structural diagram at the bottom shows the composition of the lipid nanoparticle encapsulating the mRNA.

体细胞治疗简介与主要内容索引 Introduction to Somatic Cell Therapy & Main Contents

体细胞治疗简介 Introduction to Somatic Cell Therapy

1
体细胞治疗是一种利用人体自身（除生殖系细胞外）来源的活细胞，经过一定操作后再回输患者体内的治疗方式。 Somatic cell therapy uses living cells from any part of the body (excluding germ line cells) that have been manipulated before reintroduction into patients.
2
体细胞治疗所用细胞可以来源于自身（自体）、他人（异体）或异种（如动物）来源。 These therapeutic cells can be autologous, allogeneic, or xenogeneic in origin.

主要内容结构 Main Contents

1
细胞治疗基础知识
1
Cell Therapy Fundamentals

了解体细胞、不同类型的细胞治疗、自体和异体来源区别。 Understanding somatic cells, therapy types, and the difference between autologous and allogeneic approaches.

2
法规框架 2. Regulatory Framework

探讨细胞治疗产品与高级治疗药物（ATMPs）法规的区别。 Exploring the distinction between cell therapy products and Advanced Therapy Medicinal Products regulations.

3
生产工艺 3. Production Processes

概述生产流程、质量控制、在开发过程的关键质量属性（CQAs）。 Overview of manufacturing workflows, quality controls, and critical quality attributes in therapy development.

4
临床应用 4. Clinical Applications

展示获批的细胞治疗案例，包括HSCT、Amtagvi、Ebvallo、Holoclar、Spherox。 Real-world examples of approved therapies including HSCT, Amtagvi, Ebvallo, Holoclar, Spherox treatments.

体细胞治疗机制及类型 Understanding Somatic Cell Therapy

1
【核心定义】体细胞治疗采用来自身体任何部位（不含生殖细胞）的经过操作的活细胞，这些细胞用于患者回输治疗。 Core Definition: Somatic cell therapy uses living cells from any part of the body (excluding germ line cells) that have been manipulated before reintroduction into patients.
2
这些治疗细胞可分为自体（自体细胞治疗）、异体（异体细胞治疗）、异种（异种细胞治疗）三类。 These therapeutic cells can be autologous, allogeneic, or xenogeneic in origin.
自体细胞治疗
使用患者自身细胞，可避免免疫排斥，但每个病人都需单独加工细胞。 Autologous Therapy: Using patient’s own cells eliminates immune rejection risks but requires individual cell processing for each patient treatment.
异体细胞治疗
利用供体细胞，可以实现批量生产和快速供货，但需考虑免疫相容及使用免疫抑制剂。 Allogeneic Therapy: Using donor cells allows mass production and immediate availability but requires immune compatibility considerations and immunosuppression.

法规分级与监管要求 Regulatory Classification Framework

细胞治疗产品
指未发生实质性改造、保留原生理功能的细胞产品，如用于血液重建的造血干细胞移植。 Cell Therapy Products: Cells without substantial modification retaining original physiological functions (e.g., hematopoietic stem cell transplantation for blood reconstitution).
2
高级治疗药物（ATMPs）：包括实质性改造的细胞，或用于全新生理功能的细胞，按药品管理。 ATMPs - Advanced Therapies: Substantially modified cells or those intended for different physiological functions. Regulated as medicinal products with drug status.
3
两者都需关键质量属性（CQAs）监控、产品放行标准和稳定性考察，但受不同级别的法规监管。 Both require critical quality attributes monitoring, release specifications, and stability profiles, but with different regulatory oversight levels.
4
细胞治疗需在细胞制备中心完成，ATMPs则需在药企生产，均需良好生产规范（GMP）和合格人员。 Cell therapy requires processing facilities, while ATMPs need pharmaceutical manufacturing, both with Good Manufacturing Practice compliance and qualified personnel.
法国法规示例
细胞治疗需法国药监局（ANSM）批准中心，ATMPs需药厂、专门药品GMP，且部门负责人资格有所区别。 France example: Cell therapy products require ANSM-authorized cell processing facility, while ATMPs need a pharmaceutical company with Part IV GMP for drugs and a qualified person (PharmD, MD, PhD, etc.); directors must hold relevant qualifications 11421.

ATMPs三大类 Three Main Categories of ATMPs

体细胞治疗药物
含有经过操作的细胞或组织，应用于疾病的治疗、诊断或预防。 Somatic-Cell Therapy Medicines: Therapies contain cells or tissues that have been manipulated or function differently from their original role. Applications include treating, diagnosing, or preventing diseases through cellular modification and transplantation.
基因治疗药物
含有设计用于具有治疗、预防或诊断作用基因的产品，通过重组基因实现治疗。 Gene Therapy Medicines: Contain genes designed for therapeutic, prophylactic, or diagnostic effects; use recombinant genes to address genetic disorders, cancer, and chronic diseases.
组织工程药物
包含经过改造的细胞/组织，旨在修复或替换受损组织与器官，实现结构和功能恢复。 Tissue-Engineered Medicines: Contain modified cells or tissues engineered for repairing, regenerating, or replacing damaged human tissue and restoring structure and function 15.
小结
本部分为体细胞治疗与再生医学发展提供了基础知识、监管框架和ATMPs的初步结构。
Summary
This section introduces the fundamentals of somatic cell therapy, regulatory frameworks, and the three major ATMPs categories for regenerative medicine.

生产工艺总览 Production Process Overview

1
起始材料收集 Starting Materials Collection 捐赠的细胞、组织或细胞系在作为原材料前需按明确标准进行质量认证。 Donated cells, tissues, or banked cell lines undergo qualification as raw materials with defined specifications. 细胞来源可为疾病患者、健康志愿者，或来源于细胞库。 Sources can be diseased patients, healthy donors, or banked cell lines. 这是所有细胞治疗产品的基础，只有合格的起始材料才能进入下一步操作。 This is the foundation for all cell therapy products; only qualified starting materials progress to the next stage. ——— 老师补充：任何起始材料的质量标准都要明确，例如细胞的鉴定、纯度、无菌等。[老师录音/课堂强调] — Teacher emphasized: The quality standards for any starting materials must be defined, such as cell identification, purity, and sterility. 2316
2
药物活性成分生产 Drug Substance Production 通过细胞培养、扩增、修饰等流程制备药物活性成分，并在中间环节进行质量监控。 Active pharmaceutical ingredient is developed through cell culture, expansion, and modification, with in-process quality controls. 包括细胞的增殖、诱导、或遗传修饰等，期间应设定关键控制点。 Steps include cell proliferation, induction, and possible genetic modification; critical control points must be established. ——— 老师强调：如诱导分化、激活、或敲入/敲除特定基因都属于“实质性改造”，这一阶段决定产品类型合规性。 — Teacher explained: Actions like induction, activation, or gene editing count as substantial manipulation and determine regulatory classification. 116
3
药物产品制剂 Drug Product Formulation 通过加入辅料、稳定剂和冷冻保护剂进行最终配方，保障给药安全性和完整表征。 Final formulation includes excipients, stabilizers, and cryopreservatives for patient administration with complete characterization. 包括细胞悬液/冻存液的配制，确保其运输和储存安全。 This includes preparation of cell suspensions or cryopreserved products, ensuring safe shipping and storage. ——— 关键在于所有组分都需可追溯，并记录在案。 — All components must be traceable and documented. 316
4
质量放行检测 Quality Release Testing 需要评估关键质量属性（CQA），如细胞活性、效能、纯度及安全性（包括微生物污染），获得临床放行。 Critical quality attributes (CQAs) are assessed, including viability, potency, purity, and safety (e.g., microbial contamination), before clinical release. ——— 老师强调：必须设定释放标准，且稳定性、有效性都要有数据支持。 — Teacher emphasized: Release specifications must be set, and data is required to support stability and efficacy. 关键质量属性直接关联患者的安全性与有效性，是全流程的重点环节。 CQAs are directly linked to patient safety and efficacy and are crucial throughout the process. 116

小结

生产工艺包括起始材料收集、活性成分制备、最终制剂配制和质量检测，流程每一步都需设定标准和监控点，确保最终产品安全有效合规。

Summary

The production process covers starting materials collection, drug substance production, formulation, and release testing. Every step requires defined standards and monitoring to ensure the final product’s safety, efficacy, and compliance.

生产工艺中的关键质量属性与放行标准Critical Quality Attributes and Release Specifications in Production Process

1
关键质量属性（CQAs）的定义 Definition of Critical Quality Attributes (CQAs) CQAs是指与最终产品安全性和有效性密切相关的重要指标，如细胞活性、纯度、效力和微生物安全性。 CQAs refer to key indicators closely related to the safety and efficacy of the final product, such as cell viability, purity, potency, and microbial safety.
2
CQAs设定与监控 Setting and Monitoring of CQAs 生产全过程需明确这些CQAs，制定相应的检测方法与放行标准。 It is necessary to define and monitor these CQAs throughout production, and establish appropriate test methods and release criteria.
3
活性评估 Potency Assessment 老师强调，应设计特异性方法来验证细胞的功能活性，如造血干细胞需要CFU-GM（集落形成单位-粒细胞/巨噬细胞）实验来证明其分化能力。 The teacher emphasized that specific assays should be designed to verify cellular functional activity, e.g., for hematopoietic stem cells, the CFU-GM (colony forming unit-granulocyte/macrophage) assay is needed to prove their differentiation potential.416
4
放行检测项目举例 Examples of Release Testing Parameters 包括细胞活性（Viability）、白细胞计数（Number of leukocytes）、造血干细胞数（Number of HSC, CD34+/kg）、CFU-GM（clonogenicity）、污染物、微生物学检测等。 Includes viability, number of leukocytes, number of HSC (CD34+/kg), CFU-GM (clonogenicity), contaminants, and microbiological testing, etc.
5
质量放行的难点 Challenges in Quality Release 老师讲到，理想情况下放行指标要直接与产品疗效和安全性挂钩，但实际临床新型细胞治疗很难做到完全对应，部分指标只能间接反映。 The teacher mentioned that ideally, release specifications should be directly tied to efficacy and safety, but for novel cell therapies this is not fully achievable; some parameters are only indirectly indicative.6
6
放行流程 Release Procedure 每批制品在输注给患者前，必须依据CQAs进行终极检验，只有达标才能发放临床使用。 Each batch must pass final CQAs-based tests before being released for clinical use; only qualified products can be given to patients.134
7
放行周期 Release Timeline 老师补充，根据产品类型、检测项目不同，整个制品从生产到放行可能仅需一天，也可能要一个月。 The teacher added that the total time from production to release may range from one day to one month depending on product type and testing complexity.4
8
典型指标（以造血干细胞为例） Representative Indicators (e.g., for Hematopoietic Stem Cells)
活性
Viability
白细胞数/纯度
Number of leukocytes/purity

HSC数量（CD34+计数）：Number and percentage of HSC (CD34+ counts)

CFU-GM（克隆形成能力）：CFU-GM (clonogenicity)

微生物学
Microbiology
污染物
Contaminants
其他功能或安全性检测
Others (as needed)

检验证明需在产品放行前出具。

Certificate of analysis required before product release.1116

小结 Summary:

关键质量属性（CQAs）和产品放行标准是保障细胞治疗产品安全性、有效性的核心，每一步都需设定标准、严控过程并做好详尽检测。检测项目覆盖细胞活性、功能、纯度、污染及微生物，放行标准最终决定制品能否进入临床使用。

Critical quality attributes (CQAs) and release specifications are core to ensuring the safety and efficacy of cell therapy products. Every step requires standards, process controls and detailed testing. Test items cover viability, function, purity, contaminants, and microbiology; the release specification is ultimately decisive for clinical application.

细胞治疗的临床应用（FDA/EMA）Clinical Applications in Cell Therapy (FDA/EMA)

1
该表总结了几种已获FDA/EMA认证的细胞治疗药物，包括其商品名、细胞来源（自体/异体）、以及细胞类型。 This table summarizes several FDA/EMA-approved cell therapy products, including trade names, cell sources (autologous/allogeneic), and cell types.
2
Ryoncil是一种来源于成人骨髓的间充质干细胞（MSC）产品，属于异体干细胞治疗。 Ryoncil is a mesenchymal stromal cell (MSC) product derived from adult bone marrow and is an allogeneic stem cell therapy.
3
Omisirge是一种采用烟酰胺修饰的造血祖细胞的异体治疗产品，也属于异体干细胞治疗。 Omisirge is an allogeneic therapy using nicotinamide-modified hematopoietic progenitor cells; it is also an allogeneic stem cell therapy.
4
Lantidra为异体胰岛细胞移植，用于补充或替代患者胰岛细胞，细胞类型为分化细胞。 Lantidra is an allogeneic pancreatic islet therapy that supplements or replaces patients’ islet cells, classified as differentiated cells.
5
Ebvallo是一种针对Epstein-Barr病毒（EBV）的特异性异体T淋巴细胞，用于治疗相关疾病，属于异体分化细胞治疗。 Ebvallo is comprised of Epstein-Barr virus (EBV)-specific allogeneic T lymphocytes for treating related diseases; it is an allogeneic differentiated cell therapy.
6
Provenge为自体单核细胞产品，激活于PAP-GM-CSF，用于个性化免疫治疗，细胞类型为分化细胞。 Provenge is an autologous mononuclear cell product activated with PAP-GM-CSF for personalized immunotherapy, using differentiated cells.
7
Amtagvi是一种自体肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞（TIL）疗法，利用患者自身TIL进行肿瘤靶向治疗，属于分化细胞。 Amtagvi is an autologous tumor infiltrating lymphocyte (TIL) therapy, using the patient’s TILs for tumor-targeted treatment as differentiated cells.
8
Laviv为自体成纤维细胞产品，主要用于组织修复与再生，也属于分化细胞治疗。 Laviv is an autologous fibroblast product for tissue repair and regeneration, classified as differentiated cell therapy.
老师补充
这些上市细胞治疗产品覆盖多种细胞类型（包括干细胞和分化细胞），来源既有自体也有异体，适应症和适用人群各不相同，体现了细胞治疗领域的多样性。 Teacher’s note: These approved cell therapy products cover various cell types (stem cells and differentiated cells), come from both autologous and allogeneic sources, and serve different indications and patient populations, reflecting the diversity in cell therapy applications.

小结

该页PPT总结了已批准细胞治疗产品的类型、来源与应用方向，有助于了解当前细胞治疗领域的代表性产品和发展趋势。

Summary

This slide summarizes types, sources, and application directions of approved cell therapy products, providing an overview of representative products and development trends in the current field of cell therapy.

细胞治疗的临床应用举例 Clinical Applications in Cell Therapy, some examples

1
造血干细胞移植HSCT Hematopoietic Transplant

法国每年约有5000例造血干细胞移植。 Around 5,000 hematopoietic stem cell transplants are performed annually in France.

可为异体或自体治疗。 This therapy can be either allogeneic or autologous.

使用造血干细胞实现血液系统重建，是应用最成熟的细胞治疗。 Hematopoietic stem cells are used for hematological reconstitution and represent the most established form of cell therapy.

自1959年以来成为金标准。 It has been considered the gold standard since 1959.

老师补充，造血干细胞移植主要用于重建因化疗损伤或先天性疾病导致的造血功能障碍；自体移植无免疫排斥风险，而异体移植需要关注配型和免疫问题。 The teacher added that HSCT is mainly for reconstructing hematopoietic function damaged by chemotherapy or congenital diseases; autologous transplants avoid immune rejection, while allogeneic require attention to matching and immunity issues.

2
Ebvallo免疫治疗 Ebvallo Immunotherapy

该疗法基于针对EB病毒的特异性异体T淋巴细胞。 This therapy uses Epstein-Barr virus (EBV)-specific allogeneic T lymphocytes.

属于异体细胞治疗。 It is an allogeneic cell therapy.

使用的是分化细胞（即功能成熟的T淋巴细胞）。 Differentiated cells (functionally mature T lymphocytes) are used.

老师指出，该疗法是针对EBV相关肿瘤或疾病治疗，通过扩增供者的特异性T细胞以增强免疫清除能力。 The teacher pointed out that this is used to treat EBV-related tumors/diseases, by expanding donor-derived, antigen-specific T cells to enhance immune clearing power.1421

3
Lifileucel免疫治疗 Lifileucel Immunotherapy

该疗法基于肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞（TILs），为自体细胞治疗。 This therapy uses tumor infiltrating lymphocytes (TILs) and is an autologous cell therapy.

所选TILs来自患者自身肿瘤组织，经体外扩增后回输。 Selected TILs are taken from the patient’s own tumor tissue, expanded ex vivo, and reinfused.

采用的是分化细胞。 Differentiated (mature) cells are used.

老师特别补充，Lifileucel适用于既往治疗失败的黑色素瘤患者，是FDA批准的T细胞疗法代表，用于个体化治疗难治性实体瘤。 The teacher specifically added that Lifileucel is for patients with previously treated melanoma as an FDA-approved T cell therapy, representing personalized therapy for refractory solid tumors.1624

小结 Summary

本页PPT展示了细胞治疗领域最具代表性和临床价值的三种治疗思路，分别涵盖了造血干细胞移植（自体和异体）、病毒特异性T细胞和肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞等技术路线，体现了细胞治疗的多样性和精准化方向。

This slide presents three representative and clinically valuable approaches in cell therapy: hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (autologous and allogeneic), virus-specific T cells, and tumor infiltrating lymphocytes, reflecting diversity and precision in cell therapy development.

HSCT造血干细胞移植
细胞治疗产品 HSCT Hematopoietic Transplant: Cell therapy product
1
造血干细胞移植是目前最成熟的细胞治疗方式，自1959年以来被视为金标准。 HSCT is the most established form of cell therapy and has been considered the gold standard since 1959.

1959年，第一例同卵双胞胎骨髓移植帮助白血病患儿恢复血液系统功能。 In 1959, the first bone marrow transplant from a healthy identical twin restored the blood system of a leukemic child.

3
法国每年大约有5000例造血干细胞移植执行。 Approximately 5,000 HSCT procedures are performed annually in France alone.
4
造血干细胞移植包括异体和自体两种类型。 HSCT includes both allogeneic and autologous transplant types.
5
造血干细胞（HSC）为多能细胞，可以分化为血液系统的所有细胞。 Hematopoietic stem cells (HSCs) are multipotent and can differentiate into all cell types of the hematopoietic system.
6
这些特性推动了“造血功能重建”有关的HSCT方案发展。 These properties led to the development of HSCT protocols for hematopoietic reconstitution.
7
自体HSCT的主要目标是重建再生障碍性患者的造血组织。 The main objective of autologous HSCT is to reconstruct hematopoietic tissue in aplastic patients.
老师补充
自体移植不涉及免疫排斥风险，常用于放化疗后患者；当中性粒细胞>0.5 G/L、血小板>20 G/L 时，感染、出血、血流动力学风险明显降低。 Teacher’s note: Autologous transplants avoid immune rejection and are often used after chemotherapy; when neutrophils >0.5 G/L and platelets >20 G/L, infectious, hemorrhagic, and hemodynamic risks are reduced.
9
异体HSCT的目标包括重建造血功能和发挥“移植物抗白血病”作用（见于肿瘤血液科应用）。 Objectives of allogeneic HSCT include hematopoietic reconstruction and exerting “graft versus leukemia” activity (used in onco-hematology).
10
自体和异体HSCT的主要临床适应症为血液恶性肿瘤，占比约90%。 The main clinical indication for both autologous and allogeneic HSCT is hematological malignancies, accounting for about 90%.
11
老师强调，HSCT广泛用于重建因化疗、放疗及先天性疾病破坏的造血功能，并能显著提高患者存活率和生活质量。 The teacher emphasized that HSCT is widely used to reconstruct hematopoiesis damaged by chemotherapy, radiotherapy, or congenital disease, improving survival and quality of life.

小结 Summary

HSCT是细胞治疗领域应用最成熟、操作流程最规范的治疗方式，以重建造血功能和治疗血液系统肿瘤为主要目标，自体型和异体型各有适应人群和临床优势。

HSCT represents the most mature and standardized cell therapy, mainly aiming for hematopoietic reconstitution and treatment of hematological malignancies, with distinct advantages for both autologous and allogeneic types.

细胞治疗的临床应用举例 Clinical Applications in Cell Therapy, some examples

1
造血干细胞移植（HSCT）是应用最广泛、最成熟的细胞治疗方法之一，主要用于血液系统疾病的治疗。 Hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT) is one of the most widely used and established cell therapies, mainly for treating hematologic diseases.
2
法国每年约有5000例HSCT，分为自体和异体两种。 About 5,000 HSCT procedures are performed each year in France, including both autologous and allogeneic transplants.
3
HSCT利用造血干细胞的多能性实现血液系统的再生与重建，是许多血液系统恶性肿瘤的标准治疗。 HSCT takes advantage of the multipotency of hematopoietic stem cells to regenerate and reconstitute the hematopoietic system, being the standard therapy for many hematological malignancies.
4
自体移植来自患者自身，几乎无免疫排斥风险，常用于放化疗后重建造血功能。 Autologous transplants use the patient’s own cells, carrying almost no risk of immune rejection, and are often performed to restore hematopoietic function after chemoradiotherapy.
5
异体移植来自健康供者，除重建造血功能外，还可通过“移植物抗肿瘤”效应（如抗白血病）提高治疗效果，但需注意配型和免疫并发症。 Allogeneic transplants come from healthy donors. Besides restoring hematopoiesis, they provide a graft-versus-tumor effect (e.g., against leukemia), but require attention to matching and immune complications。
6
Ebvallo是一种基于EB病毒特异性异体T淋巴细胞的免疫治疗产品，用于EB病毒相关移植后淋巴增生性疾病等难治性疾病。 Ebvallo is an allogeneic immunotherapy product based on EBV-specific T lymphocytes, used for refractory diseases such as EBV-associated post-transplant lymphoproliferative disease.
7
Ebvallo通过供者PBMC中分离并扩增针对EBV的T细胞，实现精确的抗病毒和抗肿瘤作用，尤其在配型匹配基础上实现个体化治疗。 Ebvallo isolates and expands EBV-targeted T cells from donor PBMCs, providing precise antiviral and antitumor activity, and achieves personalized therapy based on HLA matching。
8
Lifileucel（Amtagvi）是首个FDA批准的一次性肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞（TIL）疗法，适用于黑色素瘤患者，属于自体细胞免疫治疗。 Lifileucel (Amtagvi) is the first FDA-approved one-time TIL therapy for melanoma, representing autologous cell immunotherapy.
9
其核心步骤为从患者肿瘤组织分离TIL，体外扩增后一次性回输，具有高度个体化和针对难治性肿瘤的优势。 The main steps include isolating TILs from the patient’s tumor, in vitro expansion, and one-time reinfusion, offering highly personalized treatment for refractory tumors。
10
老师强调，这些产品代表了细胞治疗临床应用的多样化与精准化趋势，不同类型（未分化干细胞、分化T细胞等）及自体/异体来源决定了适应症、效果和风险控制方法。 The teacher emphasized that these products demonstrate the diversity and precision of cell therapy in the clinic; different cell types (undifferentiated stem cells, differentiated T cells, etc.) and autologous/allogeneic sources determine indications, effectiveness, and risk control326。

小结 Summary

本页展示了造血干细胞移植、特异性T淋巴细胞治疗和肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞疗法等多种代表性细胞治疗产品，覆盖了干/祖细胞与分化细胞，以及自体和异体多种策略，体现了细胞治疗领域的技术进步和精准医学方向。

This slide presents representative cell therapies including HSCT, virus-specific T-cell therapies, and TIL therapy, covering strategies using progenitor/stem cells and differentiated cells from both autologous and allogeneic sources, reflecting advances and precision medicine in cell therapy

自体新鲜造血干细胞移植放行证书示例 Example of release certificate for an autologous Fresh HSC transplantation

1
性能与放行指标分为关键质量属性（CQA）和放行标准。 The performance and release criteria are divided into Critical Quality Attributes (CQAs) and release specifications.
CQA包括
CQAs include:

产品中的总细胞数； Total cells in the product;

产品中的血小板数； Platelets in the product;

CD45+与CD34+（造血干细胞）活性； Viability of CD45+ and CD34+ (HSCs);

白细胞总数（CD45/µL, CD45×10^8/kg）； Number of leukocytes (CD45/µL, CD45×10^8/kg);

HSC百分比及数量（CD34+/kg）； % and number of HSCs (CD34+/kg);

集落形成单位（CFU-GM 10^4/kg）及克隆形成能力； CFU-GM (10^4/kg) and clonogenicity;

杂质（粒细胞）； Contaminants (granulocytes);

微生物学检测； Microbiology;

其他（如转化率等）。 Others (such as CD34+ transformation rate, if applicable) 29。

放行标准包括
Release specifications include:

细胞活性（Viability）； Viability;

CFU-GM（克隆形成能力）； CFU-GM (clonogenicity);

杂质（粒细胞）； Contaminants (granulocytes);

微生物学。 Microbiology 29。

4
录音补充说明，老师强调： From the lecture, the teacher emphasized:

即使不是药物也需严密质控，确保产品有重建造血组织的能力且无不良事件。 Even if it is not classified as a drug, strict quality controls are required to ensure the product can reconstitute hematopoietic tissue and does not induce adverse events 1。

需对每批次产品进行详尽检测，包括细胞活性、CFU-GM、细胞杂质及微生物；具体项目都必须有合格标准，否则不得用于临床。 Each batch must undergo detailed testing (viability, CFU-GM, contaminant levels, microbiology), and all must meet defined specifications for clinical use 32。

指标划分为疗效相关（如活性与克隆形成能力）、安全相关（如杂质、微生物），二者同等重要。 Criteria are split into efficacy-related (e.g., viability and clonogenic potential) and safety-related (e.g., contaminants, microbiology), and both are equally critical 3。

表格数据举例
Some data example from the chart:

活性CD45+为99.5%，放行标准为＞95%； Viability CD45+ is 99.5%, meeting the release criterion of >95%;

CD34+活性为95.6%； CD34+ viability is 95.6%;

集落形成能力要求≥10%； Clonogenic capacity requirement is ≥10%;

粒细胞杂质要求＜50%； Granulocyte contaminants must be <50%;

微生物检测必须合格。 Microbiology results must be validated as conforming 2229。

小结 Summary

本页以放行证书示例，详细展示自体新鲜造血干细胞移植相关的关键质控要素及合格标准，强调每项指标都需达标以保障产品疗效与患者安全。

This slide uses a release certificate example to detail the critical quality attributes and standards for autologous fresh HSC transplantation, highlighting that every parameter must be qualified to ensure product efficacy and patient safety

Tabelecleucel（Ebvallo）免疫治疗：ATMP

Tabelecleucel (Ebvallo) Immunotherapy: ATMP

1
Ebvallo是一种体细胞治疗药物，用于特异性针对EB病毒（Epstein-Barr virus, EBV）的异体T淋巴细胞免疫治疗。 Ebvallo is a somatic cell therapy product for allogeneic immunotherapy with Epstein-Barr virus (EBV)-specific T lymphocytes.
2
该产品属于异体细胞治疗，所用细胞来源于健康EBV血清阳性供者，通过体外激活和扩增获得。 This is an allogeneic cell therapy: T cells are derived from healthy EBV-positive donors and are activated and expanded in vitro.
3
Ebvallo用于治疗≥2岁的成人及儿童反复或难治性EBV阳性移植后淋巴增生性疾病（PTLD）。 Ebvallo is indicated for adult and pediatric patients (≥2 years) with recurrent or refractory EBV-positive post-transplant lymphoproliferative disease (PTLD).
4
该病进展迅速、不治疗时中位生存期仅为4.1个月，属于治疗紧急状态，需“按需”提供此种异体免疫治疗。 This disease progresses rapidly and has a median survival without treatment of less than 4.1 months, making it a therapeutic emergency where “on demand” allogeneic immunotherapy is needed.
Ebvallo治疗的基本流程
从EBV阳性供者采集PBMC，经体外共培养、刺激，扩增获得高纯度抗EBV T细胞。 The therapeutic process involves collecting PBMCs from EBV-positive donors, co-culturing and stimulating in vitro, then expanding to obtain high-purity anti-EBV T cells.
6
对这些T细胞进行全面特性鉴定，包括HLA配型和EBV杀伤能力，确保疗效和安全性。 Comprehensive characterization is performed for these T cells, including HLA restriction and EBV cytotoxicity, to ensure efficacy and safety.
7
由于制备过程中包括体外激活、扩增环节，属于“实质性改造”，按欧盟规定，该产品归类为ATMP（高级治疗药物）。 Because the preparation involves substantial modifications—namely in vitro activation and expansion—it is classified as an Advanced Therapy Medicinal Product (ATMP) under EU regulations.
8
老师在录音中强调，区别细胞治疗产品和ATMP的关键就在于是否对细胞有“实质性改造”，如激活、扩增等操作，Ebvallo正是一个典型案例。 In the lecture, the teacher emphasized that the key distinction between cell therapy products and ATMPs lies in whether there is “substantial modification” of the cells, such as activation or expansion, and Ebvallo exemplifies this25。
9
老师补充指出，Ebvallo这种ATMP的开发为治疗极危重的移植后EBV相关疾病提供了精准且“按需”的解决方案，反映了细胞治疗技术和监管的前沿进展。 The teacher further pointed out that ATMPs like Ebvallo provide an “on demand,” precise solution for critically ill patients with post-transplant EBV-associated disease, reflecting the cutting edge of both cell therapy technology and regulation25。

小结 Summary

本页以Ebvallo为例，展示了异体T细胞经体外激活与扩增后治疗EBV-PTLD的全过程，突出其“实质性改造”属性及ATMP监管归属。老师强调此类ATMP在难治性移植后疾病中的临床价值及安全、疗效评估的严格性。

This slide uses Ebvallo as an example to illustrate the entire process of treating EBV-PTLD with allogeneic T cells after substantial in vitro modification, highlighting its ATMP classification. The teacher emphasized the clinical value of such ATMPs in refractory post-transplant diseases and the strict evaluation for both safety and efficacy25

Tabelecleucel（Ebvallo）免疫治疗：HLA相容性

Tabelecleucel (Ebvallo) Immunotherapy: HLA compatibility

1
Ebvallo是一种异体T细胞免疫治疗产品，通过从不同HLA分型的健康EBV阳性供者外周血中分离并体外扩增T细胞制备得到。 Ebvallo is an allogeneic T cell immunotherapy product, manufactured by isolating and expanding T cells ex vivo from peripheral blood of healthy EBV-positive donors with various HLA types.
2
制备过程中，T细胞会被特异性筛选和鉴定，确保其对EB病毒有特异性杀伤作用，并严格确认HLA限制性。 During manufacturing, T cells undergo specific selection and characterization to ensure EBV-restricted cytotoxicity and verified HLA restriction.
3
这些T细胞不经过基因修饰，仅通过体外扩增和功能筛选获得。 These T cells are not genetically modified, only expanded and functionally selected ex vivo.
4
产品批次被储存在一个“随需应变”的生物样本库（biobank）中，并在有患者需求时做好随时调配的准备。 Product batches are kept in an "on-demand" biobank and are ready for dispatch whenever needed for a patient.
5
当有患者需要治疗时，医生会将患者的临床和HLA相关信息输入专门的在线平台，检索并匹配合适的Ebvallo产品。 When a patient is in need, clinicians enter the patient's clinical and HLA data into a secure online platform to search and match for a suitable Ebvallo product.
6
匹配成功后，对应的Ebvallo批次会通过物流系统迅速发运至患者所在医院。 Once matched, the corresponding Ebvallo batch is rapidly shipped to the patient's hospital.
7
