这样，实现了“按需”个体化治疗，整个过程可以在几天内完成，极大满足了临床上危急重症患者的时间要求。 This enables "on-demand" individualized treatment, with the entire process possible within a few days, which is critical for patients in urgent clinical situations.
8
课堂录音中，老师特别强调，该流程的核心优势在于建立了多HLA型的细胞库和快速的匹配及供应链，突破了自体细胞治疗时效差、异体细胞免疫排斥等局限，实现高效精准治疗。 In the lecture, the teacher highlighted that the core advantage of this process is the establishment of a diverse multi-HLA cell bank and a rapid matching and delivery system, overcoming the limitations of autologous cell therapy turnaround time and issues of immune rejection for allogeneic products, thus achieving efficient and precise treatment

Tabelecleucel（Ebvallo）免疫治疗作用机制

Tabelecleucel (Ebvallo) Immunotherapy: Mechanism of Action

1
Ebvallo用于治疗因器官移植（SOT）或造血干细胞移植（HSCT）后发生且对既往治疗无响应的EBV阳性移植后淋巴增生性疾病（PTLD）患者。 Ebvallo is used to treat EBV-positive post-transplant lymphoproliferative disease (PTLD) in patients who have not responded to prior therapies after solid organ transplantation (SOT) or hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT)。
2
Ebvallo为异体来源的抗EBV特异性细胞毒性T细胞，通过适当的HLA匹配和限制性，针对性清除EBV感染的B淋巴细胞。 Ebvallo consists of allogeneic anti-EBV-specific cytotoxic T cells, which are specifically matched by HLA type and restriction to target and eliminate EBV-infected B lymphocytes28。
治疗过程
首先选择合适HLA匹配的T细胞，然后在第1、8、15天静脉输注（2×10^6活性T细胞/kg），以实现体内对EBV+细胞的特异杀伤。 Treatment involves selecting T cells matched for HLA, then administering Ebvallo intravenously (2×10^6 viable T cells/kg) on days 1, 8, and 15, allowing for targeted cytotoxicity against EBV+ cells28。
4
Ebvallo通过其T细胞受体识别EBV表位及HLA形成复合物后，特异性杀伤携带EBV抗原的靶细胞，实现抗肿瘤作用。 Ebvallo’s T cell receptors recognize EBV epitopes bound to HLA on tumor cells, mediating specific cytolysis of EBV-expressing cells and thereby exerting anti-tumor effects28。
5
在III期ALLELE多中心临床研究中，30名未响应前序治疗的PTLD患者接受Ebvallo治疗，依据HLA限制性进行个体化产品匹配。 In the phase III ALLELE multicenter trial, 30 PTLD patients non-responsive to previous therapies received Ebvallo, with personalized product selection based on HLA restriction28。
6
试验方案为第1、8、15天注射，28天评估应答，结果显示器官移植后患者（SOT）的应答率为56%，造血干细胞移植后患者（HSCT）应答率为50%。 The protocol consisted of infusions on days 1, 8, and 15, with response evaluated at day 28; results showed a 56% response rate in SOT cases and 50% in HSCT cases28。
7
经Ebvallo治疗后，所有应答的HSCT患者以及75%应答的SOT患者在一年后仍然存活，表明疗效显著、持久。 After Ebvallo treatment, all responding HSCT patients and 75% of responding SOT patients were still alive one year later, indicating significant and durable efficacy28。
8
老师在课堂讲解中补充强调，Ebvallo代表了一种利用异体、HLA精确匹配T细胞，针对病毒相关肿瘤开展“现成型”精准免疫治疗的成功范例。传统自体细胞治疗无法满足急危重症需求，而异体细胞库及精配流程大幅提升了治疗时效性与覆盖人群。 In class, the teacher further emphasized that Ebvallo exemplifies a successful “off-the-shelf” precision immunotherapy using allogeneic, HLA-matched T cells for virus-associated tumors. Unlike autologous therapies, this model meets the urgent needs of critical patients, with allogeneic cell banks and rapid matching expanding timely access and candidate coverage328。

小结 Summary

本页用图示和临床数据详细阐释了Ebvallo的作用机制——通过HLA限制下的异体抗EBV特异性T细胞快速清除病毒相关肿瘤细胞，并通过临床试验证明了其高效持久的疗效。课堂强调其在急重症免疫治疗中的独特优势。

This slide uses illustrations and clinical results to explain Ebvallo’s mechanism—rapid EBV tumor cell clearance by HLA-restricted, allogeneic, anti-EBV T cells—with clinical trials evidencing lasting efficacy. The class highlighted its unique advantage in urgent immunotherapy settings328。

Lifileucel（Amtagvi）免疫治疗：ATMP

Lifileucel (Amtagvi) Immunotherapy: ATMP

1
Lifileucel（Amtagvi）是一种源自肿瘤的自体T淋巴细胞（TIL）疗法。 Lifileucel (Amtagvi) is an autologous tumor-infiltrating lymphocyte (TIL) therapy22。
2
适应症为既往接受过抗PD-1抗体治疗（若为BRAFV600+患者需合用BRAF抑制剂±MEK抑制剂），且病灶无法切除或已转移的黑色素瘤成年患者。 It is indicated for adult patients with metastatic or unresectable melanoma who have previously received anti-PD-1 antibody therapy (and if BRAFV600+, a BRAF inhibitor ± MEK inhibitor)。
3
推荐给药剂量为7.5×10⁹至72×10⁹个活性T细胞。 The recommended dose is 7.5×10⁹ to 72×10⁹ viable T cells22。
4
该疗法属于自体细胞治疗，细胞来源于患者自身，因而免疫排斥风险低。 This is an autologous cell therapy; the cells are derived from the patient, minimizing the risk of immune rejection2237。
5
项目属于高级治疗药物（ATMP），其原理为回输经扩增的特异性TIL细胞，通过增强自身免疫系统杀伤肿瘤。 Amtagvi is classified as an Advanced Therapy Medicinal Product (ATMP), and the principle is to reinfuse expanded TILs to empower the patient's immune system against the tumor1622。
制备流程包括
外科手术获取肿瘤组织→在专门工厂中分离T细胞→体外扩增复苏T细胞至十亿级数量→最后制备成一次性输注药物。 The preparation process includes: surgically resecting tumor tissue → separating T cells in a specialized facility → rejuvenating and expanding T cells in vitro to billions → formulating into a one-time infusion product2416。
课堂讲解补充
老师强调，TIL疗法的关键在于从患者肿瘤微环境内分离能直接识别和攻击肿瘤细胞的T细胞，并通过体外大量扩增，极大增强其抗肿瘤效应。 In class, the teacher emphasized that the key of TIL therapy is isolating T cells from the patient's tumor microenvironment, which can directly recognize and attack tumor cells, and massively expanding these cells in vitro to greatly enhance their anti-tumor effect128。
8
由于每个患者均需个体化制备，制剂周期较长，但适用于常规疗法无效的晚期患者。 Because each dose is individually manufactured per patient, the preparation process is lengthy, but it's suitable for advanced cases unresponsive to standard therapies161。

小结 Summary

本页介绍了Lifileucel（Amtagvi）的自体来源、ATMP属性、关键工艺流程与适用人群，并结合课堂内容突出自体TIL疗法的肿瘤特异性和扩增优势。

This slide reviews the autologous origin, ATMP status, key manufacturing steps, and patient indications of Lifileucel (Amtagvi), and, incorporating classroom notes, highlights the tumor specificity and expansion power of autologous TIL therapy

Lifileucel（Amtagvi）免疫治疗作用机制与临床试验

Lifileucel (Amtagvi) Immunotherapy: Mechanism of Action & Clinical Trial

1
Lifileucel（Amtagvi）免疫治疗机制（第一页PPT）
1
Mechanism of Action of Lifileucel (Amtagvi) Immunotherapy (First Slide)

Lifileucel 是一种肿瘤浸润性淋巴细胞（TIL）衍生的自体细胞治疗产品。 Lifileucel is an autologous cell therapy product derived from tumor-infiltrating lymphocytes (TILs).

该疗法采用患者自身的肿瘤组织中分离出的TIL，体外扩增后回输患者，实现个体化治疗。 TILs are isolated from the patient's own tumor tissue, expanded ex vivo, and then reinfused for a personalized therapy.

回输后TIL随血液循环迁移到肿瘤部位，特异识别肿瘤细胞表位。 After reinfusion, TILs circulate in the bloodstream and migrate to the tumor site, specifically recognizing tumor cell epitopes.

TIL通过肽抗原-受体识别机制定位肿瘤细胞并激活效应，释放颗粒酶、穿孔素等杀伤因子诱导肿瘤细胞裂解。 TILs locate and activate against tumor cells via peptide antigen-receptor recognition, releasing factors like granzyme and perforin to induce tumor cell lysis1833.

TIL能够靶向并同时攻击肿瘤异质性中的多种新抗原，具有多克隆、广谱抗肿瘤作用。 TILs can target and attack multiple neoantigens within the heterogeneous tumor, providing a polyclonal and broad anti-tumor effect33.

老师补充
TIL疗法的关键是通过体外大规模扩增提高肿瘤特异性T细胞数量与功能，使其具备更强免疫清除能力。 Teacher’s note: The critical aspect of TIL therapy is large-scale ex vivo expansion to boost the number and function of tumor-specific T cells, enabling stronger immune clearance1.
2
Lifileucel（Amtagvi）临床试验设计与结果（第二页PPT）
2
Clinical Trial of Lifileucel (Amtagvi) (Second Slide)

In a phase II trial, 153名晚期黑色素瘤患者接受Lifileucel治疗，多数为四线及以上（多次前期免疫检查点抑制剂与靶向治疗失败）。 In a phase II trial, 153 advanced melanoma patients received Lifileucel, mostly as fourth-line or higher therapy (after failed immune checkpoint inhibitors and targeted therapies)30.

主要流程
前期先用化疗（环磷酰胺+氟达拉滨）清除内源免疫细胞，为TIL回输造就有利的扩增环境。 Protocol: Lymphodepleting chemotherapy (cyclophosphamide + fludarabine) is given prior to TIL infusion to create a favorable in vivo expansion environment3019.

第8天静脉回输7.5至72×10⁹个TIL细胞，随后使用大剂量IL-2加速扩增与效应。 On day 8, 7.5–72 × 10⁹ TIL cells are intravenously infused, followed by high-dose IL-2 to enhance expansion and effects3015.

疗效方面
总体客观缓解率（ORR）为31.4%，其中8例完全缓解，40例部分缓解；41.7%患者维持应答18个月以上。 Efficacy: Overall response rate (ORR) was 31.4%, with 8 complete and 40 partial responses; 41.7% of patients maintained response for over 18 months.

无进展生存期为4.1个月、中位总生存期为13.9个月。 Progression-free survival was 4.1 months, and median overall survival was 13.9 months.

安全性方面，100%患者出现治疗相关不良事件（TRAE）；超过30%的患者为3/4级不良反应。 Regarding safety, 100% experienced treatment-related adverse events (TRAEs); over 30% had grade 3/4 adverse reactions.

老师强调
TIL疗法不仅是晚期黑色素瘤的救命治疗选择，还建立了细胞免疫治疗的“个体化、靶向、高度强化”范式。 Teacher emphasized: TIL therapy is not only a life-saving option for advanced melanoma but also establishes a “personalized, targeted, and highly intensified” paradigm in cell immunotherapy18.

总结 Summary

该案例系统阐释了Lifileucel（Amtagvi）自体TIL免疫治疗从作用机制、制备流程、扩增和临床疗效，以及在晚期患者中的安全性和意义，并结合课堂讲解突出了该疗法对细胞免疫治疗领域的突破性贡献。

This case systematically illustrates Lifileucel (Amtagvi) autologous TIL immunotherapy, covering the mechanism, manufacturing, expansion, clinical efficacy, safety in advanced patients, and, with classroom comments, highlights its breakthrough for the field of cellular immunotherapy

组织工程药物临床应用Clinical Applications in Tissue-Engineered Medicinal Products

Holoclar角膜治疗Holoclar Corneal Therapy

Holoclar是一种基于组织工程的角膜上皮干细胞治疗产品。 Holoclar is a tissue-engineered corneal epithelial stem cell therapy product.

它通过体外扩增自体角膜缘上皮干细胞，最终形成用于移植的治疗片。 It uses ex vivo expanded autologous limbal epithelial stem cells to create a graft for transplantation.

主要用于治疗中重度角膜缘干细胞缺乏症所致的失明。 Mainly used to treat blindness caused by moderate to severe limbal stem cell deficiency.

属于组织工程药物（ATMP），2015年获得欧盟批准上市，适用于所有欧盟合作国家。 It is classified as an advanced therapy medicinal product (ATMP) and was approved by the European Commission in 2015 for use in all partner countries.

临床数据显示，12个月后，移植成功率为72%。 Clinical data show a 72% transplant success rate at 12 months.

老师补充
临床试验中，大约一半患者角膜透明度完全恢复，可以再次看见。 Teacher's note: In clinical trials, about half of the patients achieved complete corneal clarity and regained vision.

技术流程 Technical Procedure

治疗步骤包括
Procedure steps include:
1.角膜缘取2x2mm²小活检，并进行酶处理。 　 Biopsy of 2x2 mm² at a depth of 100-200 microns from the limbus, followed by enzymatic treatment.
答案 A
2
分离并扩增角膜缘干细胞（LSCs），在平板上培养初步扩增。 Isolate and expand limbal stem cells (LSCs) with primary culture on plate.
3
细胞冻存于冷冻管，备用。 Freeze the expanded cells in cryovials for storage.
4
使用认证的饲养层细胞系进行再培养，促进细胞扩增后，在纤维蛋白基质上培养，制成移植片。 Thaw and further culture on a certified feeder layer and then on fibrin to form the graft.
5
将制备好的Holoclar移植物移植到病变角膜表面。 Transplant the Holoclar graft onto the affected corneal surface.

手术过程 Surgical Procedure

　 首步取2x2 mm²角膜缘活检，深度约100-200微米（正常角膜厚度约540微米）。 (A) First, take a 2x2 mm² limbal biopsy at 100-200 microns depth (normal corneal thickness ~540 microns).
答案 A
　 第二步手术，去除纤维血管膜。 (B) In the second step, dissect the fibrovascular pannus from the corneal surface.
答案 B
　 第二步手术中将Holoclar治疗片安置于角膜表面。 (C) Place the Holoclar sheet on the corneal surface during the second surgery38.
答案 C

疗效和费用 Outcomes and Pricing

术后不同时间点可见移植区透明度恢复明显。 Corneal clarity in the treated area significantly improves over time after surgery.

术后荧光素与蓝光照射可以突出显示角膜上皮的损伤和恢复情况。 Fluorescein and blue light illumination highlights lesions and recovery of the corneal epithelium.

Holoclar对患者的视力恢复具有显著疗效。 Holoclar significantly restores vision in affected patients.

费用较高，每只眼睛在欧洲约10.5万美元。 High cost: approximately USD 105,000 per eye in Europe.

在法国未获医保报销，因为不被认为是“重大医疗需求”。 Not reimbursed in France as it’s not considered a major medical need38.

回顾性临床研究显示
104例接受移植患者，12个月移植物成功率为72.1%，49%患者视力改善。 Retrospective clinical study: Among 104 transplanted patients, 12-month graft success rate was 72.1%, with 49% showing improved visual acuity38.

组织工程药物临床应用——Spherox在软骨修复中的应用

Clinical Applications in Tissue-Engineered Medicinal Products: Spherox for Cartilage Repair

概述 Summary

Spherox是一种自体基质相关型软骨细胞球，用于关节软骨缺损的修复。 Spherox is an autologous matrix-associated chondrocyte spheroid product for the repair of articular cartilage defects.

该药物在法国未获医保报销，因为不被认为是“重大医疗需求”。 The medication is not reimbursed in France because it is not considered to be a major medical need.

制备与植入流程 Preparation and Implantation Process

先从患者关节软骨活检获取软骨组织。 Cartilage biopsy is performed to obtain cartilage tissue from the patient.

体外对软骨细胞进行单层扩增，并用自体血清培养（不添加外源因子、抗生素等）。 The chondrocytes are expanded in monolayer culture supplemented with autologous serum (no xenogenous products, growth factors, or antibiotics).

软骨细胞被培养成球形聚集体（spheroid culture），形成Spherox。 Chondrocytes are then cultured in spherical aggregates to form Spherox.

移植物自粘附，无需缝线、膜片或胶水即可通过关节镜下植入到缺损区域。 The spheroid implant is self-adhering and can be arthroscopically implanted into the cartilage defect with no need for sutures, membranes, or glue.

作用机制 Mechanism of Action

Spherox中的软骨细胞在体外球形聚集、分泌细胞外基质，在植入后为软骨下骨提供粘附基础。 Chondrocytes in Spherox aggregate and synthesize extracellular matrix in vitro, which provides adhesion to the subchondral bone after implantation.

其降解细胞外基质促进了缺损处新软骨形成、软骨再生修复。 The degradation of this extracellular matrix promotes new cartilage formation and facilitates regeneration in the defect area.

临床试验与疗效 Clinical Trial and Efficacy

进行了一项Ⅲ期、多中心、前瞻性、随机对照临床试验，比较了Chondrosphere（即Spherox）与微骨折（MF）技术在修复单侧软骨缺损（面积1-4cm²）中的疗效。 A prospective, randomized, multicenter phase III clinical trial compared Chondrosphere (Spherox) to microfracture (MF) in the treatment of unilateral cartilage lesions (1–4 cm²).

共纳入102例患者，其中52例接受了Chondrosphere治疗（每例剂量10–70个球体，按缺损面积调整）。 102 patients were enrolled, with 52 receiving Chondrosphere® (dosed at 10–70 spheroids per cm² according to defect size).

结果显示两组疗效均显著改善，Chondrosphere®组疗效不劣于微骨折组（MF）。 Clinical outcomes showed significant improvement in both groups, with Chondrosphere® demonstrating non-inferiority to MF.

机制分子标志及与再生能力关系 Molecular Markers and Regeneration Capacity

Spherox球体内聚集素（aggrecan）蛋白及其mRNA的表达与软骨修复能力密切相关。 The presence of aggrecan protein and its mRNA in spheroids is closely correlated with cartilage regenerative capacity.

软骨细胞分化越高，聚集素表达越强，提示分化程度高的细胞可能具有更好的再生能力。 The expression of aggrecan increases with the level of chondrocyte differentiation, suggesting that highly differentiated cells may have better regeneration potential.

细胞治疗市场现状与未来展望Current Market and Future Perspectives in Cellular Therapy

市场规模与批准产品 Market Size and Approved Products

2023年ATMP（先进治疗药物产品）市场规模为270.4亿美元。 The total ATMPs (Advanced Therapy Medicinal Products) market size in 2023 is $27.04 billion.

已有25种以上ATMP获批，其中约68%为基因疗法。 Over 25 ATMPs have been approved, about 68% of which are gene therapies.

全世界有1200多个相关临床试验正在进行。 More than 1200 clinical trials are ongoing globally.

科学挑战 Scientific Challenges

推进细胞治疗需克服制造复杂性，保证质量一致性，并开发具成本效益的生产方法。 Advancing cell therapy requires overcoming manufacturing complexity, ensuring consistent quality, and developing cost-effective production methods.

标准化不同细胞类型的生产工艺。 Standardize manufacturing processes across different cell types.

开发通用的质量控制与效力检测方法。 Develop universal quality control and potency assays.

在保证治疗效果的同时降低生产成本。 Reduce production costs while maintaining therapeutic efficacy.

监管演变 Regulatory Evolution

监管框架持续演进，以平衡创新速度与患者安全，适应新兴治疗方法。 Regulatory frameworks continue evolving to balance innovation acceleration with patient safety in emerging therapeutic approaches.

协调国际间监管标准与审批路径。 Harmonize international regulatory standards and approval pathways.

为个体化疗法建立灵活的临床试验设计。 Establish adaptive clinical trial designs for personalized therapies.

建立上市后长期安全监测体系。 Create post-market surveillance systems for long-term safety.

患者可及性 Patient Access

实现公平的患者可及性需解决成本障碍、报销政策及全球分发难题。 Ensuring equitable patient access requires addressing cost barriers, reimbursement policies, and global distribution challenges.

制定基于价值的创新疗法定价模式。 Develop value-based pricing models for innovative therapies.

扩大产能以满足不断增长的需求。 Expand manufacturing capacity to meet growing demand.

创设患者援助计划和保险覆盖。 Create patient assistance programs and insurance coverage.

“细胞”治疗有前景或正在兴起的疾病与状况Diseases and Conditions Where "Cellular" Treatment is Promising or Emerging

细胞治疗在多种疾病中展现出广阔前景，包括：中风、外伤性脑损伤、学习障碍、阿尔茨海默病、帕金森病。 Cellular therapies hold broad promises for many diseases, including stroke, traumatic brain injury, learning defects, Alzheimer's disease, and Parkinson's disease.

其他适应症还包括脱发、失明、失聪、肌萎缩侧索硬化症、心肌梗死、牙齿缺失。 Other indications include baldness, blindness, deafness, amyotrophic lateral sclerosis, myocardial infarction, and missing teeth.

细胞治疗还用于慢性伤口愈合、骨髓移植（已成熟）、脊髓损伤、骨关节炎、类风湿关节炎、Crohn病、糖尿病、肌营养不良和多种类型的癌症。 Cellular treatment is also applied for chronic wound healing, bone marrow transplantation (currently established), spinal cord injury, osteoarthritis, rheumatoid arthritis, Crohn’s disease, diabetes, muscular dystrophy, and multiple types of cancer.

老师补充
这些应用领域显示出细胞治疗的巨大潜力，未来有望扩展到更多慢性与疑难疾病。
Teacher’s note
These fields highlight the great potential of cell therapy, which is expected to expand to even more chronic and complex diseases in the future

062025-10-16

Biobetters改善生物制剂的策略｜Biobetters: Strategies to improve biologics

1
定义与基本概念 Definition and Core Concepts

Biobetters（又称Biosuperiors）是指在现有生物药基础上进行改进的药物，通过改善药代动力学、疗效、给药方式、毒性或免疫原性等特性，达到更优治疗效果。 Biobetters, also known as Biosuperiors, are improved versions of existing biological drugs, enhancing properties such as pharmacokinetics, efficacy, mode of administration, toxicity, or immunogenicity for better therapeutic outcomes.

Biobetters属于同一类重组蛋白药物，但并非与原药完全相同，而是在原有基础上进行了功能或质量改进。 Biobetters refer to recombinant protein drugs that belong to the same class as existing biopharmaceuticals, but are not identical—rather, they represent an improved version.

2
改善治疗性蛋白的原因 Reasons to Improve Therapeutic Proteins

稳定性（stability） Stability

溶解性（solubility） Solubility

药代动力学和药效动力学特性（如半衰期、分布、消除）（pharmacokinetics, pharmacodynamics） Pharmacokinetics and pharmacodynamics characteristics (such as half-life, distribution, elimination)

提高效力（例如增强与受体亲和力，更有针对性且不易降解）（efficacy） Improved efficacy (such as higher affinity for the receptor, more targeted delivery, reduced degradation)

提升患者依从性（例如改善给药途径或减少用药频次）（patient compliance） Increased patient compliance (such as easier administration or reduced dosing frequency)

降低生产成本（reduce production costs） Reduced production costs

3
Biobetters相较于参考生物药的可能优势 Possible Advantages of Biobetters over Reference Biologics

更高疗效 Greater efficacy

更高纯度 Greater purity

更长的产品半衰期 Longer product half-life

更少的用药频率 Less frequent dosing

更低的聚集风险（蛋白物理降解更少） Lower likelihood of aggregation (less physical degradation of the protein)

更少的不良事件 Fewer adverse events

精简制造流程 Streamlined manufacturing

更长的货架期和更优的稳定性 Longer shelf-life and greater stability

更便捷的给药或包装改进 Easier administration or packaging improvements

4
老师课堂重点补充 Teacher's Emphasis from Audio

Biobetters可以通过改变蛋白质的氨基酸组成或配方，提高纯度、延长半衰期、减少聚集或降解，这些都是控制蛋白稳定性的措施。 Biobetters can improve purity, extend half-life, and reduce aggregation or degradation by modifying amino acid composition or formulation; these approaches aim for better protein stability.

更好的患者友好性可通过简化给药设备和方式实现，比如减少需要随身携带的复杂设备，提高服药便利性。 Improved patient convenience may come from easier-to-use devices or administration methods, reducing the need for patients to carry complex devices with them.

易用性的提升也是包装优化的一种表现，有些产品因不易携带最终停产，说明便捷性对患者依从性至关重要。 Improvements in ease of use, such as better packaging, directly impact patient compliance; inconvenient forms of administration may even lead to product withdrawal from the market.

除了蛋白本身的改造，更新生产工艺以降低成本也是Biobetter的重要目标之一。 In addition to changes to the protein itself, upgrading the manufacturing process to reduce costs is also a key Biobetter strategy.

总结 Summary

通过改进生物药的分子、配方或生产流程，Biobetter旨在为患者和临床提供疗效更好、不良反应更少、使用更便利的药物选择。

By improving the molecule, formulation, or production process, Biobetters aim to offer better efficacy, fewer side effects, and greater convenience for patients and clinics.

重组蛋白药物药代动力学性质的改善
以干扰素α为例｜Improvement of pharmacokinetic properties of a recombinant protein: interferon-alpha, a case study
问1. 干扰素α是什么？
问What is interferon-alpha?
答

干扰素α是一种由抗原呈递细胞产生的细胞因子，用于应对病毒感染（如乙肝、丙肝）、肿瘤等环境刺激。 Interferon-alpha is a cytokine produced by antigen-presenting cells in response to environmental exposures including viral infections (such as hepatitis B and C), cancer, carcinoma.

干扰素分为I、II、III型，有多个亚型（如2a，2b）。 There are several types of interferon (I, II, III) and subtypes (such as 2a and 2b).

问2. 干扰素α的作用机制是什么？
问What is its mechanism of action?
答

抑制蛋白质合成并使病毒RNA失活。 It inhibits protein synthesis and inactivates viral RNA.

干扰素α由免疫细胞在识别到病原体后产生，结合I型干扰素受体，激活JAK-STAT信号通路，促使ISFG3复合物激活，进而作为转录因子（通过磷酸化和二聚化）促进B细胞和T细胞的募集。 Interferon alpha is produced by immune cells after recognizing pathogens; it binds to type I interferon receptors, activates the JAK-STAT signaling pathway, leads to the activation of the ISFG3 complex, and acts as a transcription factor (via phosphorylation and dimerization) to amplify B and T cell recruitment.

问3. 重组人干扰素α2（rh-IFN a2）的给药方式？
问How is rh-INF a2 administered?
答

仅能通过注射（肌肉注射），治疗肿瘤时每天一次，治疗病毒感染时每周注射三次。 It is only administered parenterally (intramuscularly): once a day for cancers, and three times per week for viral infections.

问4. 它的半衰期是多少？
问What is its half-life time?
答

一般为4-8小时，具体取决于给药方式。 In general, its half-life is 4-8 hours, depending on its mode of administration.

课堂补充讲解要点 Teacher’s Key Points

干扰素α需要注射给药，因蛋白质本身难以口服吸收以及分子容易被降解。 Interferon-alpha requires injection because protein drugs are difficult to absorb orally and prone to degradation.

半衰期较短，是由于分子小，容易被肾脏过滤清除，导致在体内维持时间有限。 The short half-life is due to its small molecular size, allowing for rapid filtration and clearance by the kidneys, thus limiting the duration in the body.

重组蛋白药物药代动力学性质的改善：以干扰素α为例 Improvement of pharmacokinetic properties of a recombinant protein: interferon-alpha, a case study

问1. 什么是PEG修饰？
问What does pegylation mean?
答

PEG修饰指将聚乙二醇(PEG)链共价连接到药物分子（如蛋白/多肽）上，以提高其半衰期、稳定性、溶解性，并降低免疫原性。 Pegylation means the covalent attachment of polyethylene glycol (PEG) chains to drug molecules (such as proteins/peptides) to increase half-life, stability, solubility and decrease immunogenicity.

PEG可以有不同的分子量、线型/分支结构、结合的位置和数量。 PEG can differ in molecular size, structure (linear or branched), conjugation site and number per protein.

2
图表分析及解读

Analyze and comment the figure below

未经PEG修饰的干扰素需多次给药且药物活性迅速恢复至基线。 Unpegylated interferon requires frequent administrations and the activity drops to baseline quickly.

加入线型PEG（5kDa、12kDa）后，药物活性维持时间延长，浓度下降速度减慢。 After linear PEG (5kDa, 12kDa) modification, the drug lasts longer in the body and activity decreases more slowly.

当用40kDa分支PEG修饰（如Pegasys®）时，半衰期进一步显著延长，可超过100小时。 With 40kDa branched PEG (e.g. Pegasys®), the half-life is greatly prolonged, exceeding 100 hours.

总结：PEG修饰越大（尤其是分支型且分子量大），药物作用持续时间越长，给药频率显著减少，提高患者依从性。 In summary: the larger the PEG (especially high molecular weight and branched), the longer the drug stays active and less frequent injections are needed, thus improving patient compliance.

3
其他已上市PEG化药物举例

Search for other pegylated pharmaceutical products

已上市的PEG化药物包括：PEG化的凝血因子VIII、干扰素、白介素6、重组人生长激素等。 Examples of marketed pegylated drugs: Pegylated Factor VIII, Interferon, Interleukin-6, Recombinant Growth Hormone.

它们的共同点是分子体积小，易被肾脏滤过，通过PEG修饰能大幅延长体内循环时间。 They share a small molecular size, prone to renal clearance, and pegylation greatly extends their in vivo circulation time.

4
补充课堂强调（结合老师讲解录音）

Supplementary Key Points from Lecture Audio

PEG修饰是化学修饰，不属于细胞自然过程，而是下游工艺完成的。 Pegylation is a chemical process, not a natural cellular event but performed in downstream processing.

PEG包裹蛋白后形成水合层，能保护蛋白免受蛋白酶降解，提高溶解性和稳定性，改善药效动力学。 After PEG surrounds the protein, a hydration layer is formed, protecting it from protease degradation, improving solubility, stability, and pharmacokinetics.

药物分子的体积和分子量越大，肾脏滤过越慢，循环半衰期延长。 The larger the molecular volume and mass, the slower the renal clearance and the longer the circulation half-life.

PEG化药物的一大创新是根据分子结构设计合适的PEG大小和连接方式，实现药物临床性能最优化。 Innovation in pegylated drugs lies in the rational design of PEG size/structure and conjugation strategy to optimize clinical performance.

Pegylation的作用与代表性产品 Pegylation and Representative Products

Pegylation的原理及优势 Principle and Advantages of Pegylation

聚乙二醇化（Pegylation）是指将非毒性、亲水性、中性（不带电）的聚乙二醇（PEG）链通过化学方法修饰到蛋白质或多肽药物分子上。 Pegylation refers to the chemical attachment of non-toxic, hydrophilic, uncharged polyethylene glycol (PEG) chains to a protein or peptide drug.

Pegylation带来的优势包括： The main advantages of pegylation are:

显著延长药物的半衰期（体内持续时间提升4-400倍）。 Significantly increases the half-life of therapeutic proteins in vivo (by 4–400 times).

降低免疫原性，减少患者免疫反应风险。 Reduces immunogenicity, lowering the risk of unwanted immune responses.

增强对蛋白酶降解的耐受性，提高蛋白稳定性。 Increases resistance to protease degradation and improves protein stability.

提高药物的水溶性和在体内的溶解度。 Enhances solubility and dissolution in the body.

提高生物利用度和患者依从性。 Increases bioavailability and patient compliance.

PEG分子可以线性或分支，分子量大小、连接位置、PEG数量等均可调控，实现个性化结构设计。 PEG chains can be linear or branched, with variable sizes, positions, and the number of PEGs per molecule, allowing for customizable structural designs.

PEG修饰位点及化学策略 Sites and Chemistry of PEGylation

PEG链常通过反应蛋白质分子的N端氨基、赖氨酸侧链氨基、半胱氨酸硫醇等处进行共价连接。 PEG is commonly attached via covalent bonds to the N-terminal amino group, lysine side-chain amines, or cysteine thiols of proteins.

这种修饰在蛋白质分子表面形成一层水合保护外壳，显著提升稳定性和耐受性。 This modification creates a hydrated protective shell on the protein surface, dramatically enhancing stability and resistance.

不同的PEGylation化学方法赋予制剂不同的药代和药效特性。 Different pegylation chemistries yield products with distinct pharmacokinetic and pharmacodynamic properties.

代表性上市PEG化药物 Approved PEGylated Drugs

下图表列举了多种已获批的PEG修饰蛋白及其适应症，展示了聚乙二醇化策略的广泛应用。 The table below lists various approved pegylated protein drugs and their indications, demonstrating the broad utility of pegylation strategies.

典型药物示例包括： Key examples include:

Adagen®（pegademase）：治疗ADA严重联合免疫缺陷。 Adagen® (pegademase): for ADA severe combined immunodeficiency.

Oncaspar®（pegaspargase）：治疗急性淋巴细胞白血病。 Oncaspar® (pegaspargase): for acute lymphoblastic leukemia.

ViraferonPeg®/Pegasys®（PEG-IFNα2b/2a）：治疗慢性丙型肝炎。 ViraferonPeg®/Pegasys® (PEG-IFNα2b/2a): for chronic hepatitis C.

Neulasta®（pegfilgrastim）：抗粒细胞生长因子，用于恶性肿瘤化疗后的粒细胞减少症。 Neulasta® (pegfilgrastim): G-CSF for chemotherapy-induced neutropenia.

Somavert®（pegvisomant）：治疗肢端肥大症。 Somavert® (pegvisomant): for acromegaly.

Jintrolong®（PEG-hGH）：重组人生长激素的PEG制剂。 Jintrolong® (PEG-hGH): pegylated recombinant human growth hormone.

Rebinyn®（Coagulation factor IX, pegylated）：用于血友病B。 Rebinyn® (pegylated coagulation factor IX): for hemophilia B.

这些药物通过PEG修饰显著延长了体内作用时间，减少用药频率，改善了患者的生活质量和依从性。 These drugs benefit from PEG modification by showing much longer in vivo action, reduced dosing frequency, and improved patient quality of life and compliance.

结论 Conclusion

PEG化是一种强大的生物制剂优化策略，被广泛应用于重组蛋白药物，以延长半衰期、增加稳定性和改善药物动力学特性，从而带来更好的治疗体验和效果。 Pegylation is a powerful strategy for optimizing biotherapeutics, widely applied in recombinant protein drugs to prolong half-life, increase stability, and improve pharmacokinetic characteristics, resulting in superior therapy and patient experience.

干扰素-CTP融合蛋白Q3 Interferon-CTP (C-terminal peptide)

问什么是融合蛋白？ What is a fusion protein?
答

融合蛋白是通过重组DNA技术将两段或多段基因序列转录生成一个蛋白。 A fusion protein is produced by transcribing two or more sequences into a single protein using recombinant DNA technology.

通过表达载体（如质粒、病毒载体、噬菌体等）导入目标序列，实现宿主细胞的生产。 The target sequences are inserted into expression vectors (plasmid, viral vectors, or bacteriophages) and introduced into a host cell for protein production.

实际生产时，从一开始就设计好融合序列，再用载体改造宿主进行表达。 In practical production, the fusion sequence is designed at the start, and a vector is used to modify the host cell for expression.

问什么是CTP肽？ What is the CTP peptide?
答

CTP是可以与蛋白融合、显著延长半衰期的C端肽。 CTP is a C-terminal peptide that can be fused to proteins to significantly increase their half-life.

CTP来源于孕期人体分泌的绒毛膜促性腺激素（hCG）。 CTP is a peptide from human chorionic gonadotrophin (HCG) produced during pregnancy.

它对应于hCG分子的C端，富含丝氨酸和苏氨酸残基，这些位点容易发生O-糖基化修饰。 It corresponds to the C-terminal part of hCG, which is rich in serine and threonine residues, allowing for extensive O-glycosylation.

问IFN-CTP是如何获得的？ How is IFN-CTP obtained?
答

选择能够进行糖基化修饰的宿主细胞进行表达。 A host cell capable of glycosylation is selected for expression.

将设计好的带有CTP融合序列的基因转入该宿主细胞，进行重组蛋白表达。 The gene encoding the fusion protein (with CTP) is introduced into this host for recombinant protein production.

问这种修饰主要影响蛋白的哪些药物性质？ What properties of the therapeutic molecule are targeted by this modification?
答

显著延长半衰期（通过降低肾小球滤过，提高分子量与糖基化）。 It markedly increases half-life (by decreasing glomerular filtration through higher molecular weight and glycosylation).

增大分子体积，提升体内稳定性。 Increases the size and improves in vivo stability.

同时CTP的糖基化特性可改变蛋白结合肝细胞的无酶受体，影响清除途径。 The glycosylation of CTP modifies the binding of the protein to hepatic asialoglycoprotein receptors, altering elimination routes.

补充老师强调： Additional points stressed by the teacher:

融合蛋白是在DNA水平就实现的结构优化，是上游设计，下游表达，区别于后期的化学修饰。 Fusion proteins represent structural optimization at the DNA level, achieved by upstream design and downstream expression, unlike post-expression chemical modifications.

CTP广泛的O-糖基化使蛋白抗酶解能力提升，并因带负电荷，可防止肾脏快速滤过。 The extensive O-glycosylation of CTP increases resistance to proteases and, due to the negative charge, prevents rapid renal filtration.

改变蛋白与肝细胞的受体结合，可延缓通过肝脏的清除，进一步延长血中持续时间。 Changes in glycosylation can alter receptor binding to hepatocytes, further prolonging circulation time by slowing hepatic clearance.

CTP融合是创新的分子改造方式，能显著改善蛋白药物的药代动力学属性，提高临床效果。 CTP fusion is an innovative molecular engineering strategy that significantly improves pharmacokinetic properties and clinical efficacy of protein drugs.

CTP融合技术及案例 Technology of Fusion with CTP

hCG与hLH的氨基酸序列极其相似，但只有hCG的β亚基C端28个氨基酸（CTP）带有4个O-糖链，hLH则没有。 The amino acid sequences of hCG and human luteinizing hormone (hLH) are almost identical, but only hCG’s β subunit contains a 28-amino-acid C-terminal peptide (CTP) with four O-glycans; hLH does not have this.

示例：将CTP嫁接于促卵泡激素（FSH）得到FSH-CTP，提升其稳定性和药效，ELONVA®（2010年EMEA批准）；生长激素-CTP技术用于提升生物药持续作用效果。 Example: FSH-CTP is obtained by fusing CTP to follicle-stimulating hormone, improving stability and efficacy (ELONVA®, approved by EMEA in 2010); similar technology is applied to growth hormone.

Albuferon的研发策略 Q4 Albuferon

Albuferon是通过将干扰素α与人血清白蛋白（HSA）基因融合，表达出的单一多肽链蛋白。 Albuferon is a single polypeptide combining the gene sequences of interferon α and human serum albumin (HSA).

策略核心是： The main strategy is: 利用白蛋白的长半衰期特性，将治疗性蛋白与其融合，极大延长药物体内持续时间。 To utilize albumin’s long half-life by fusing it with the therapeutic protein, thus significantly prolonging circulation time.

依据： Basis: 图1显示了基因融合的结构模式。 Figure 1 shows the schematic representation of genetic fusion. 图2表明人血清白蛋白半衰期远长于其他大多数蛋白（~19天），通过融合显著提升药物半衰期。 Figure 2 indicates that human serum albumin has a much longer half-life (~19 days) than most proteins, so fusion leads to marked increases in the therapeutic protein’s half-life.

图3为近/远紫外圆二色谱（UV-CD）光谱，显示融合蛋白保留两部分的主要结构特征。 Figure 3 shows the near- and far-UV CD spectra, demonstrating that the fusion protein preserves the main structural characteristics of both albumin and interferon α. 说明结构兼容性好，有利于功能表达。 This indicates good structural compatibility, which supports effective functional expression.

融合蛋白和生物改良药物 Fusion Proteins and Biobetters

融合蛋白的创新方式： Innovative ways to create fusion proteins: 包括与白蛋白、Fc段、多聚氨基酸等融合，或与CTP等小肽段融合。 Including fusion with albumin, Fc domains, polyamino acid linkers, or CTP and other short peptides.

主要优点： Main advantages: 融合可增加分子尺寸和水动力学半径，降低肾排泄速率。 Fusion increases molecular size and hydrodynamic radius, reducing renal clearance. Fc段可通过FcRn受体循环再吸收，延长半衰期。 Fc fusion enables recycling via the FcRn receptor, prolonging half-life. 通过唾液酸化等修饰增加负电荷，进一步延缓清除。 Sialylation increases negative charge, further delaying clearance. 许多生物改良药（biobetter）正在临床试验中。 Many biobetters are in clinical trials.

Albuferon的研发策略与结构分析 The Strategy Behind the Development of Albuferon & Analysis of Its Structure

问基于图1和图2，Albuferon的研发策略是什么？
问Based on Figures 1 and 2, What Is the Strategy Behind the Development of Albuferon?
答

Albuferon是一种通过基因融合，将干扰素α（IFN-α）与人血清白蛋白（HSA）连接在一起，形成单一多肽链的融合蛋白。 Albuferon is a fusion protein generated by genetically linking interferon-alpha (IFN-α) with human serum albumin (HSA) into a single polypeptide chain.

图1展示了这种基因融合的结构示意，IFN-α和HSA基因串联后在表达系统中一次性表达为一个融合蛋白。 Figure 1 illustrates the schematic of this genetic fusion, where the IFN-α and HSA genes are joined and expressed together as one fusion protein in an expression system.

这一策略的核心是利用白蛋白自身的长半衰期（~19天），以显著延长IFN-α的体内循环时间。 The core strategy is to utilize the naturally long half-life of albumin (~19 days) to significantly extend the circulation time of IFN-α in vivo.

在图2中可以看到，HSA的半衰期约为456小时，而IFN-α的只有约5小时。融合后半衰期随着白蛋白部分显著延长。 Figure 2 shows that the half-life of HSA is about 456 hours, whereas IFN-α alone is about 5 hours. After fusion, the half-life is significantly prolonged due to the albumin part.

采用该策略可以大幅减少给药频率，提高患者依从性和临床效果，适合慢性病长期治疗需求。 Adopting this strategy sharply reduces dosing frequency and improves patient compliance and therapeutic outcomes, which is especially suitable for long-term treatment of chronic diseases.

此外，白蛋白大分子的属性有助于防止通过肾小球过滤途径的快速消除，进一步增强药物体内稳定性。 Furthermore, the large molecular size of albumin helps prevent rapid elimination by glomerular filtration, further enhancing the in vivo stability of the drug.

分析与评价图3

Analyze and Comment on Figure 3

图3为Albuferon的近紫外和远紫外圆二色谱（UV-CD）光谱，比较了Albuferon与纯HSA及IFN-α混合光谱的主链构象。 Figure 3 shows the near- and far-UV circular dichroism (UV-CD) spectra of Albuferon, comparing it to the spectral sum of pure HSA and IFN-α, reflecting the backbone conformations.

光谱重合，表明融合蛋白的空间构象中，HSA和IFN-α各自的主链结构基本保持，不存在重组后失折叠或异常折叠。 The spectra overlap, indicating that the backbone structures of both HSA and IFN-α are largely preserved in the fusion protein, without misfolding or abnormal folding following recombination.

说明该融合蛋白具备良好的构象稳定性，有利于蛋白功能表达和长期体内稳定。 This shows that the fusion protein has good conformational stability, which is advantageous for functional expression and extended stability in vivo.

结构兼容性强，验证了基因层面融合策略的可行性和蛋白质工程设计的合理性。 The strong structural compatibility confirms the feasibility of the genetic fusion strategy and soundness of protein engineering design.

课堂讲解补充内容

Additional Points from Teacher's Lecture

白蛋白融合不仅可延长血中半衰期，还能降低肾脏清除，部分通过FcRn受体再循环，进一步延缓消除。 Albumin fusion not only extends blood half-life, but also reduces renal clearance, and is partly recycled through the FcRn receptor, thus further delaying elimination.

融合后蛋白的免疫原性不会显著上升，且蛋白功能域基本保留原有活性。 The immunogenicity of the fused protein does not significantly increase, and the functional domains largely retain their original activities.

该技术目前被广泛用于多种重组蛋白药物的半衰期延长和生物利用度提升。 This technology is now widely applied to extend the half-life and improve the bioavailability of many recombinant protein drugs.

归纳总结Summary

Albuferon通过基因融合方式，大幅延长干扰素α的体内持续时间，减少给药次数，提升患者依从性和治疗效果。 Albuferon uses a gene fusion approach to greatly extend the in vivo duration of IFN-α, reduce dosing frequency, and enhance both patient compliance and therapeutic outcomes.

融合蛋白的空间结构与功能保持良好，生物药改良具有显著效果，提升临床应用价值。 The spatial structure and function of the fusion protein are well preserved, making this an effective biopharmaceutical improvement with significant clinical value.

融合蛋白与生物改良药物 Fusion proteins and biobetters

1
融合蛋白是通过基因工程将不同的蛋白片段或多肽拼接成同一个蛋白，实现功能增强或药代性质优化。 Fusion proteins are engineered by linking different protein fragments or peptides into a single protein, to enhance function or optimize pharmacokinetic properties.
2
融合蛋白的主要类型包括：与白蛋白融合、与抗体Fc段融合、以及多聚氨基酸（如聚赖氨酸）链融合。 The main types of fusion proteins include albumin fusion, Fc (fragment crystallizable) domain fusion, and polyamino acid (such as poly-lysine) chain fusion.
3
白蛋白融合能显著延长药物体内半衰期，是目前生物药开发的常用策略。 Albumin fusion can markedly extend the in vivo half-life of drugs and is a common strategy in biopharmaceutical development.
4
Fc段融合利用FcRn受体介导的回收机制，进一步延长蛋白药的循环时间。 Fc fusion leverages the FcRn receptor-mediated recycling mechanism to further prolong the circulation time of protein therapeutics.
5
融合多聚氨基酸链则可以提高蛋白分子的空间体积，增加水动力学半径，通过减少肾脏滤过来降低清除率。 Fusion with polyamino acid chains can increase the spatial volume and hydrodynamic radius of the molecule, reducing renal clearance by minimizing glomerular filtration.
6
部分融合蛋白还可以通过唾液酸化等途径增加蛋白表面的负电荷，进一步减慢在体内的清除速率。 Some fusion proteins can be sialylated to increase negative surface charges, which further slows down in vivo clearance.
7
下方实例为FSH与CTP肽段的融合，表明通过CTP融合增加糖基化后可进一步延长蛋白的体内暴露时间。 The example below illustrates FSH fused with a CTP peptide. The CTP fusion increases glycosylation, further prolonging the protein's in vivo exposure time.
8
这些生物创新方式已催生众多biobetter药物，许多产品正在进行临床试验。 These innovative biotechnologies have produced many biobetter drugs, a significant number of which are currently in clinical trials.
9
融合蛋白技术的主要优点包括： The main advantages of fusion protein technology include:

增大分子尺寸和水动力学半径，降低肾脏清除； Enlarged molecular size and hydrodynamic radius, reducing renal clearance;

Fc段融合实现FcRn受体循环回收； Fc fusion enables recycling through the FcRn receptor;

通过唾液酸化修饰提升表面负电荷，减缓清除速率； Sialylation increases negative surface charges, further slowing clearance;

推动众多生物改良药物进入临床试验阶段。 Enabled the clinical development of numerous biobetter drugs.

课堂补充/老师讲解：

Class/Lecturer’s supplement:

老师特别强调，融合蛋白能通过空间体积和表面修饰，在不牺牲生物活性的前提下大幅改善药代动力学，并指出选择不同融合策略需根据靶标特性及临床需求综合权衡。

The teacher especially emphasized that fusion proteins can substantially improve pharmacokinetics via increased volume and surface modifications without sacrificing bioactivity, and choosing the appropriate fusion strategy should be tailored to the target and clinical needs.

图4：抗病毒活性分析和评论

Figure 4: Antiviral Activity Analysis and Commentary

分析 (Analysis)

图4展示了在感染了水泡性口炎病毒 (VSV) 的 Madin-Darby 牛肾 (MDBK) 细胞中，几种不同干扰素 (IFN) 制剂的抗病毒活性。

Figure 4 illustrates the antiviral activity of several different interferon (IFN) formulations against Vesicular Stomatitis Virus (VSV) in Madin-Darby Bovine Kidney (MDBK) cells.

横轴 (X-axis): [IFN] (fmol)，表示干扰素的摩尔浓度（对数刻度）。 X-axis: [IFN] (fmol), representing the molar concentration of interferon (logarithmic scale).

纵轴 (Y-axis): RFUs (Relative Fluorescence Units)，代表相对荧光单位。RFU的增加与抗病毒效果的增强呈正相关。 Y-axis: RFUs (Relative Fluorescence Units). An increase in RFU suggests a stronger antiviral effect.

曲线类型 (Curve Type): 所有曲线都呈现出典型的S形剂量反应关系，表明抗病毒活性是剂量依赖性的。 Curve Type: All curves exhibit a characteristic sigmoidal dose-response relationship, indicating that the antiviral activity is dose-dependent.

被比较的制剂 (Formulations Compared):

Alb-IFN/IFN-α2b/PEG-IFN-α2b/IFN-α2a/PEG-IFN-α2a

相对效价比较 (Relative Potency Comparison):

alb-IFN 的表现 (Performance): alb-IFN (蓝色三角) 的曲线位置明显偏左。这表明它达到最大效应所需的药物浓度最低，因此在摩尔基础上具有最高的相对抗病毒效价 (Potency)。 The alb-IFN curve (blue triangles) is significantly shifted to the left. This indicates it requires the lowest drug concentration to achieve maximal effect, thus possessing the highest relative antiviral potency on a molar basis.

PEG修饰IFN (PEG-modified IFNs): PEG-IFNα-2b 和 PEG-IFNα-2a 的曲线位于最右侧，表明它们的效价最低。 The PEG-IFNα-2b and PEG-IFNα-2a curves are positioned furthest to the right, indicating the lowest potency.

摩尔效价的相对排序 (Relative Molar Potency Ranking):

评论 (Commentary)

1
Alb-IFN的优势 (Alb-IFN Advantage): 图4明确显示，白蛋白融合技术 (alb-IFN) 大幅提高了干扰素在MDBK细胞模型中的摩尔效价。与非修饰的IFN相比，它实现相同抗病毒效果所需的剂量要低得多（曲线左移）。 Figure 4 clearly demonstrates that the albumin fusion technology ($\text{alb-IFN}$) significantly enhances the molar potency of the interferon in this MDBK cell model. It requires a much lower dose to achieve the same antiviral effect compared to unmodified IFNs (curve shift to the left).
2
PEGylation的影响 (Impact of PEGylation): 聚乙二醇化 (PEGylation) 是一种常用的延长体内半衰期的修饰策略。然而，从这张体外图来看，PEGylation (虚线曲线) 降低了 IFNα-2a 和 IFNα-2b 的固有摩尔效价（曲线右移）。这可能是因为PEG基团的体积效应阻碍了IFN与受体的结合。 Polyethylene Glycol (PEG) modification is a common strategy to extend the in vivo half-life. However, this in vitro data shows that PEGylation (dashed curves) reduces the intrinsic molar potency of both $\text{IFN}\alpha\text{-2a}$ and $\text{IFN}\alpha\text{-2b}$ (curve shift to the right). This loss is often attributed to the steric hindrance of the large PEG moiety interfering with receptor binding.
3
技术对比 (Technology Comparison): alb-IFN 的高摩尔效价提示其可能实现了长效药物的理想目标：在延长半衰期的同时，保持甚至提高了体外固有活性。这与 PEG-IFN 形成对比，后者通常以牺牲部分固有活性为代价来实现长效作用。 The high molar potency of $\text{alb-IFN}$ suggests it may achieve the ideal goal for a long-acting drug: extending the half-life while maintaining or even increasing the in vitro intrinsic activity. This contrasts with $\text{PEG-IFN}$, which typically achieves its long-acting profile at the cost of reduced intrinsic activity.
4
生物学相关性 (Biological Relevance): 这是一个体外实验 (in vitro)。尽管 alb-IFN 在体外表现出最高的效价，但在实际临床应用中，药物的体内药代动力学（PK）和在人体内的整体药效才是最终的决定因素。 This is an in vitro experiment. While $\text{alb-IFN}$ shows the highest potency in vitro, clinical success ultimately depends on the drug's in vivo pharmacokinetics (PK) and overall efficacy in humans.

多肽融合延长半衰期的策略与白蛋白/Fc融合半衰期对比

Half-life extension strategies employing polypeptide fusion & Fusion with albumin or Fc

1
多肽融合是一种通过将目标蛋白与特定多肽序列（如白蛋白、Fc区、PAS、ELP、HAP等）基因融合，从而延长蛋白药物体内半衰期的技术。 Polypeptide fusion refers to fusing the target protein gene with specific polypeptide sequences (such as albumin, Fc region, PAS, ELP, HAP, etc.), thereby prolonging the drug’s half-life in vivo.
2
这些多肽融合策略主要机制包括增大分子尺寸、水动力学半径、提高负电荷以及与FcRn等受体的结合来避免肾小球滤过和促进体内循环再回收。 These fusion strategies primarily work by increasing molecular size, hydrodynamic radius, negative charge, or by binding to receptors such as FcRn to prevent glomerular filtration or promote recycling in circulation.
3
表格中总结了各类融合策略，如： The table summarizes various fusion strategies, including:

与人IgG Fc区融合，提高与FcRn受体的结合力，显著延长体内半衰期； Fusion with human IgG Fc region enhances binding to FcRn receptor and significantly prolongs half-life.

与人血清白蛋白（HSA）融合，使蛋白分子获得与白蛋白相似的长半衰期； Fusion with human serum albumin (HSA) allows the fusion protein to acquire a half-life similar to albumin.

与PAS（多聚丙氨酸-丝氨酸）或ELP（弹性蛋白样多肽）等中性疏水序列融合，通过增加分子体积延缓肾脏清除； Fusion with PAS (polyalanine–serine) or ELP (elastin-like polypeptide) increases molecular size and reduces renal clearance.

融合高负电荷或唾液酸化肽以增加蛋白带电性，减少肝脏/肾脏摄取； Fusion with highly charged or sialylated peptides increases negative charge to reduce hepatic/renal uptake.

CTP融合（源自hCG的羧基末端肽段），可高度糖基化，极大延长体内暴露时间。 CTP fusion (carboxy-terminal peptide from hCG) is highly glycosylated, significantly prolonging in vivo exposure.

4
第二页展示了白蛋白和Fc融合的代表性半衰期数据，表明天然IgG、HSA的半衰期约为19到21天，远高于一般小分子蛋白。 The second slide displays representative half-life data for albumin and Fc fusions. Natural IgG and HSA both have half-lives of about 19–21 days, much longer than typical small proteins.
5
通过与白蛋白/Fc融合，许多药物蛋白（如干扰素、因子VIII、G-CSF等）可大幅提高其半衰期，减少用药频率。 By fusing with albumin or Fc, many therapeutic proteins (such as interferons, factor VIII, G-CSF, etc.) achieve much longer half-lives, allowing less frequent dosing.
6
老师补充讲解：这些延长半衰期的策略是解决蛋白类药物因分子小易被肾脏滤过导致体内持续时间短、需高频给药的主要手段。 The instructor emphasized that these half-life extension strategies are the main solution to the issue of rapid renal clearance and short duration for small protein drugs, which otherwise require frequent dosing.
7
另外，Fc和白蛋白的半衰期延长机制核心在于它们通过与FcRn受体结合，实现回收和再利用，显著减缓体内降解速度。 Additionally, the core mechanism for half-life extension with Fc or albumin is their ability to bind the FcRn receptor, enabling recycling and reuse, which greatly slows degradation in the body.
8
结论：多肽融合和白蛋白/Fc融合显著改善了生物药的体内药代动力学，是生物改良药物（biobetter）研发的核心技术。 Conclusion: Polypeptide fusion and albumin/Fc fusion dramatically improve pharmacokinetics of biopharmaceuticals and are key technologies for developing biobetters.

治疗性蛋白的多种优化策略Different strategies to improve Therapeutic proteins

1
优化治疗性蛋白的方法包括编码序列的修饰，如突变、缺失、融合蛋白和抗体人源化，以及糖基化位点的修饰。 Strategies to improve therapeutic proteins include modifications of the coding sequence, such as mutations, deletions, fusion proteins, antibody humanization, and modification of glycosylation sites.
2
非翻译性工程技术包括与亲水性高分子（如PEG）偶联、与活性分子（如细胞毒药物）偶联、对已形成的糖链进行修饰，以及脂质结合。 Non-translational engineering techniques involve conjugation with hydrophilic polymers (like PEG), conjugation to an active molecule (such as cytotoxics), modification of already formed glycan chains, and lipid binding.
3
新的生产工艺包括宿主细胞工程（如提升糖基化能力）及糖工程技术。 New production technologies include host cell engineering, such as improving glycosylation capability (“glycoengineering”).
4
还可以通过新型制剂和新的给药途径进一步优化蛋白药物。 New formulations and new routes of administration can further optimize protein therapeutics.
5
老师补充强调，不同策略的核心目的是提升药效、延长半衰期、降低给药频率、提高病人依从性，并且有些修饰还可降低免疫原性或改善药物稳定性。 The teacher additionally emphasized that the core goals of these strategies are to enhance efficacy, extend half-life, reduce dosing frequency, improve patient compliance, and that some modifications can also lower immunogenicity or improve drug stability.

市售生物改良药（biobetter）实例Example of Commercially available biobetters

6
表格中举例说明多种已上市的biobetter药物，如促红素、粒细胞刺激因子、促卵泡激素、曲妥珠单抗、利妥昔单抗等。 The table provides examples of commercially available biobetters, such as erythropoietin, filgrastim, follicle stimulating hormone, trastuzumab, and rituximab.
7
Biobetter药物通过延长给药间隔（如原型须两周注射一次，改良型可每月一次）、减少注射次数或改变给药途径，从而提升用药便利性和依从性。 Biobetters achieve improved convenience and compliance by extending dosing intervals (for example, from every two weeks to monthly), reducing the number of injections, or changing administration routes.
8
例如，修饰后的促红素和粒细胞刺激因子通过延长半衰期，大大降低了给药频次，减轻了患者负担。 For instance, modified erythropoietin and filgrastim products have an extended half-life, significantly reducing the frequency of administration and alleviating patient burden.
9
一些生物改良药兼具多机制优势，如KADCYLA，通过抗体-药物偶联实现双重抗肿瘤作用，提升疗效。 Some biobetters, such as KADCYLA, combine multiple mechanisms (e.g., antibody-drug conjugates) to enhance antitumor efficacy.
10
这些biobetter实例体现了现代蛋白工程与制剂创新在临床应用中的价值。 These examples illustrate the clinical value brought by modern protein engineering and formulation innovation.
11
总结：通过多种手段修饰和工程化蛋白药物，已成功推出多种biobetter产品，显著改善了原有药物的性能与患者体验。 Summary: Through various modification and engineering techniques, many biobetter products have been developed, significantly improving the performance of original drugs and the experience of patients.

生物改良药总结BIOBETTER SUMMARY

——

1
生物改良药（biobetter）是在原有蛋白药物基础上进行结构或工艺优化，具有更好的药效、安全性或给药便利性。 Biobetters are optimized versions of original protein drugs, improved in structure or process to enhance efficacy, safety, or dosing convenience.
2
图示展示了多种用于改善多肽药物体内药代动力学（PK）的策略，包括PEG化、脂化、糖基化、Fc融合和白蛋白融合等。 The diagram shows strategies for improving the pharmacokinetics (PK) of polypeptide therapeutics, including PEGylation, lipidation, glycosylation, Fc fusion, and albumin fusion.
3
表格归纳了当前提升多肽药物体内表现的常用方法及其优劣势。 The table summarizes current approaches to enhance in vivo performance of polypeptide drugs and their pros and cons.
4
化学/共价修饰（如PEG化、脂化、糖基化）可延长半衰期、减少肌体降解和提高溶解度，但也可能导致组织摄取减少、免疫原性提升、功能活性降低及工艺复杂性增加。 Chemical/covalent modifications (PEGylation, lipidation, glycosylation) can prolong half-life, reduce degradation, and improve solubility, but may also reduce tissue uptake, increase immunogenicity, lower functional activity, and complicate manufacturing.
5
微球/纳米粒递送系统可实现药物缓释和保护，但需控制释放速率，维持生物活性。 Microsphere/nanoparticle delivery allows sustained drug release and protection but requires control over drug release speed and maintenance of bioactivity.
6
蛋白酶耐受变体和融合蛋白（如白蛋白融合）可改善体内稳定性、延长循环时间，但需关注免疫原性与功能维持。 Protease-resistant variants and fusion proteins (e.g., albumin fusion) improve stability and circulation but require attention to immunogenicity and retention of function.
7
案例包括市售PEG化蛋白、脂化蛋白、白蛋白/抗体Fc融合药物等。 Examples include marketed PEGylated proteins, lipidated proteins, albumin/Fc fusion drugs.
8
老师补充，生物改良药的设计要权衡延长半衰期、功能活性保持及免疫原性等多方面，需用功能检测实验逐一验证。 The instructor added that designing biobetters requires balancing prolonged half-life, maintained functional activity, and immunogenicity, with each modification needing experimental validation.

——

生物类似药、生物改良药及新一代抗体药物Biosimilars, Biobetters and New Generations of mAbs

——

9
生物改良药不是生物类似药，二者概念不同。生物类似药指对参比药高度相似的可互换仿制品；生物改良药则是在原有分子基础上进行性能优化。

Biobetters are not biosimilars. Biosimilars are highly similar and interchangeable to reference products, while biobetters are optimized derivatives with improved properties.

10
图示对比了三类抗体药物：生物类似药、生物原研药、生物改良药。 The diagram compares three types of antibody drugs: biosimilars, originator biologics, and biobetters.
11
生物类似药开发周期短、成本低，但创新性弱；生物原研药开发周期长、成本高；生物改良药研发周期和成本介于两者之间，但能获得新活性物质身份，享有市场排他权。 Biosimilars have short development times and low costs but low innovation; originator biologics have long development and high cost; biobetters fall in between, but are recognized as new active substances and enjoy market exclusivity.
12
商业上，生物改良药研发风险较低，研发投入低于新分子实体，同时获批后享有市场独占性。 Commercially, biobetter development carries less risk, incurs lower R&D costs than new molecular entities, and gains market exclusivity upon approval.
13
课堂强调，生物改良药兼具创新和商业价值，是未来生物药物开发的重点方向。 The teacher highlighted that biobetters carry both innovation and commercial value, representing a key direction for future biopharmaceutical development.

072025-10-17

1
本节主要介绍三类常见的病毒载体。 This section mainly introduces three common types of viral vectors.
2
病毒载体是用于表达或递送外源基因的工程病毒，广泛应用于生物技术领域。 Viral vectors are engineered viruses used to express or deliver foreign genes, widely used in biotechnology.
重点讲解包括以下三类病毒载体
The main focus is on the following three types of viral vectors:

逆转录病毒/慢病毒载体 Retroviral / Lentiviral vectors

腺病毒载体 Adenoviral vectors

重组腺相关病毒载体 rAAV vectors (adeno-associated viruses)

4
课程将依次讨论这三种载体的结构、功能和在不同场景下的应用。 The course will discuss in turn the structure, function, and applications of these three types of vectors in different scenarios.
5
此外，老师还会补充每种病毒载体的优势与局限，帮助理解其实际选择与应用依据。 In addition, the instructor will supplement the advantages and limitations of each viral vector to help understand the basis for their selection and use in practice.
6
本节内容为后续深入学习每种载体的具体特性及技术细节做基础铺垫。 This section lays the foundation for further learning about the specific characteristics and technical details of each vector.

病毒载体Viral vector

1
病毒载体的定义 Definition of viral vector 病毒载体是指经过基因修饰，用于转运和表达自身或外源基因的病毒。 A viral vector is a genetically modified virus used to transfer and express its own genes or foreign genes.
2
病毒载体的优势 Advantages of viral vectors 我们能克隆不同病毒的基因组，现在操纵这些病毒基因组已变得容易。 The genome of different viruses can be cloned, and it is now easy to manipulate these viral genomes. 可以在细菌质粒中操作病毒基因组，便于遗传修饰和扩增。 Viral genomes can be handled in bacterial plasmids, making genetic modification and amplification convenient. 通过将改造后的病毒基因组重新导入宿主细胞可以产生重组病毒。 Recombinant viruses can be produced by reintroducing the modified viral genome into their host cells.
3
病毒载体的四个主要应用 Four main objectives of viral vectors （1）在细胞培养中生产特定蛋白质，用于研究或工业制备。 (1) Production of specific proteins in cell culture for research or industrial purposes. （2）用作疫苗，通过表达抗原蛋白诱导免疫应答。 (2) Vaccination, by expressing antigen proteins to induce immune response. （3）基因治疗，通过递送外源或功能基因修复或替换缺陷基因。 (3) Gene therapy, by delivering exogenous or functional genes to repair or replace defective genes. （4）作为抗肿瘤药物，用于肿瘤免疫治疗或直接杀伤肿瘤细胞。 (4) As anticancer agents, for tumor immunotherapy or direct killing of tumor cells.
4
课堂补充和老师强调要点 Additional explanations and key points from lecture 实际应用中，我们常用的病毒载体是为了表达外源基因，而不是表达病毒自身的基因，这在临床和科研中尤为重要。 In practice, viral vectors are mainly used to express foreign genes rather than viral genes themselves, which is especially important in clinical and research settings. 选择并设计病毒载体时，需重视特异性、安全性及表达效率，确保既能发挥作用又不会对患者产生负面影响。 When choosing and designing viral vectors, specificity, safety, and expression efficiency must be considered, ensuring efficacy without harmful effects on patients.

病毒载体理想特征Characteristics sought for a viral vector

1
可调节的细胞趋向性 Adaptable cellular tropism 我们希望病毒载体可以针对不同类型的细胞或组织，实现高度特异性和高效递送。 We want viral vectors to target different types of cells or tissues, achieving high specificity and efficient delivery. 趋向性调整可以通过包膜蛋白改变，提高药物靶向性，减少副作用。 Tropism adjustment can be realized by modifying envelope proteins, improving targeting and reducing side effects.
2
外源基因表达 Expression of the transgene 病毒载体要有效表达外源基因，包括表达量调控和表达稳定性。 Viral vectors need to efficiently express the transgene, including control of expression level and stability. 表达量调控：可以通过选择强启动子或弱启动子来决定蛋白表达的强弱。 Expression level control: the strength of expression is determined by using strong or weak promoters. 表达稳定性：涉及转基因在宿主中的整合（integration）或以游离状态（episomal form）存在。 Expression stability: relates to whether the transgene is integrated into the host or remains in an episomal form. 应用场景决定了需要短期还是长期表达，如疫苗通常需短期表达，基因疗法则需要长期稳定表达。 The application scenario determines if short- or long-term expression is necessary, e.g., vaccines need short-term, gene therapy needs long-term stable expression.
3
可容纳大基因片段 Large genetic material 理想病毒载体可以携带较大的外源基因，提高多基因串联或大基因修复的能力。 An ideal vector can carry large transgenes, increasing the capacity for multi-gene cassettes or large gene corrections. 病毒的载体容量有限，常需根据基因大小选择合适的病毒系统。 The vector’s capacity is limited, so the viral system must be chosen based on the size of the gene.
4
易生产、低成本 Easy to produce vectors, low cost 工业生产需要病毒载体可以大规模、低成本地制备。 For industrial needs, viral vectors must allow large-scale and low-cost production. 合理工艺设计和宿主细胞选择，有利于提高产率并控制成本。 Proper process design and host cell selection help improve yields and control costs.
5
高滴度病毒的制备 Production of high titers of viruses 高滴度保证病毒载体的应用有效性，减少使用剂量与体积。 High titer ensures efficacy of viral vector application and reduces required dosage and volume. 病毒必须是活性的，并且产物尽量避免空粒子或突变体。 The viruses must be viable, and the product should avoid empty particles or mutants.
6
低或无重组反应 Low or absent recombination 理想病毒载体需避免或极大减少重组，防止产生意外产物。 Ideal viral vectors should minimize recombination to avoid unexpected products. 降低基因重组率有助于提高安全性和治疗效果。 Reducing recombination rate helps improve safety and therapeutic outcomes.
7
限制RCV生成 Limiting the generation of RCV (Replication Competent Viruses) 需严格防止病毒在体内或生产过程中恢复复制能力，减少安全隐患。 Strictly avoid the generation of replication competent viruses during production or in vivo to minimize safety risks.
8
最低毒性 Minimal toxicity 病毒载体必须最大程度安全，既不会产生自身致病性，也不因表达蛋白导致毒性。 The vector must be as safe as possible, neither being pathogenic itself nor causing toxicity via the expressed protein.
9
最低免疫反应 Minimal immune response 理想载体应只引发极低甚至不引起免疫反应，避免炎症或过敏反应。 An ideal vector should induce very little or no immune response, avoiding inflammation or allergic reactions. 特别是基因治疗时，需要降低针对病毒或外源蛋白的免疫性。 Especially in gene therapy, immunogenicity against the vector or transgene must be minimized. 注：疫苗策略中反而“适度的免疫原性”可能带来正效应，但其他应用则严格要求低免疫反应。 Note: For vaccines, some immunogenicity is beneficial, but other applications require minimal immune response.

主要病毒载体Main viral vectors

两种病毒载体类型2 types of viral vectors

1
三种常用病毒载体 Three commonly used viral vectors 常用的病毒载体为逆转录病毒/慢病毒载体、腺病毒载体和重组腺相关病毒（rAAV）载体。 The commonly used viral vectors are retroviral/lentiviral vectors, adenoviral vectors, and recombinant adeno-associated virus (rAAV) vectors. 课堂强调这三类是各自有独特结构和应用领域的主流病毒载体。 The class emphasized these three types as the mainstream viral vectors, each with unique structures and applications.
2
逆转录病毒/慢病毒载体结构与特性 Structure and characteristics of retroviral/lentiviral vectors 逆转录病毒/慢病毒属于有包膜病毒，表面有包膜糖蛋白，包裹着壳体和病毒基因组。 Retroviral/lentiviral vectors are enveloped viruses with envelope glycoproteins, enclosing a capsid and viral genome. 其感染过程依靠包膜蛋白与细胞受体结合实现靶向。 Their infection process relies on envelope proteins binding to cellular receptors for targeting.
3
腺病毒载体结构与特性 Structure and characteristics of adenoviral vectors 腺病毒载体是无包膜病毒，外壳为二十面体，表面有典型纤突结构。 Adenoviral vectors are non-enveloped, icosahedral viruses with characteristic fiber structures on the surface. 感染细胞通过纤突与CAR和整合素等细胞受体结合启动。 They infect cells by fiber binding to cellular receptors such as CAR and integrin.
4
rAAV载体结构与特性 Structure and characteristics of rAAV vectors rAAV为小型无包膜病毒，通过其表面蛋白分别和HSPG及整合素实现结合与进入细胞。 rAAV vectors are small, non-enveloped viruses that bind and enter cells via interactions between surface proteins and HSPG/integrin.
5
病毒表面结构决定趋向性和用途 Viral surface structure determines tropism and applications 病毒表面蛋白决定了其对不同细胞类型或组织的选择性。 Viral surface proteins dictate tropism for specific cell types or tissues. 通过改造表面蛋白，可以实现特异性递送，提高疗效。 Modifying surface proteins allows for specific delivery and enhanced therapeutic efficacy.
6
两类病毒载体类型 Two types of viral vectors 病毒载体分为可复制型和不可复制型。 Viral vectors are divided into replicative and non-replicative types. 可复制型病毒载体可以在宿主细胞内自主复制，不在基因治疗中使用，常见于分子生物学、细胞生物学、疫苗学及蛋白表达。 Replicative viral vectors can autonomously replicate in host cells, are not used in gene therapy, but are common in molecular biology, cell biology, vaccinology, and protein expression. 老师强调这些可复制型用于激活更强免疫反应或作为溶瘤病毒靶向肿瘤细胞。 The teacher emphasized that replicative vectors are used to induce stronger immune responses or as oncolytic viruses targeting tumor cells. 不可复制型病毒载体通常经基因修饰，删除了关键复制基因，仅能在补充缺失基因的特定细胞中复制。 Non-replicative viral vectors are usually genetically modified, with essential replication genes deleted, and can only replicate in cells providing the missing gene. 这类载体被广泛用于分子生物学、细胞生物学和基因治疗，尤其适合人体应用，安全性高。 These vectors are widely used in molecular biology, cell biology, and gene therapy, especially suitable and safe for clinical applications.
7
基因治疗严格选用不可复制型载体 Gene therapy strictly uses non-replicative vectors 在基因治疗中只允许使用不可复制型病毒载体，最大限度降低在受体内扩增或再引发感染风险。 In gene therapy, only non-replicative viral vectors are allowed to minimize the risk of amplification or new infection in recipients. 临床生产过程中必须严格检测，确保无可复制型病毒混入，以保障患者安全。 During clinical production, strict screening is required to ensure no replicative viruses are present, ensuring patient safety. 用于治疗肿瘤时，某些安全设计的可复制病毒也可被用于选择性增殖于肿瘤细胞，从而杀伤肿瘤。 In cancer therapy, some safely designed replicative viruses can be used for selective proliferation in tumor cells to kill the tumor.

典型病毒载体的构建与生产Construction and production of a typical viral vector

1
构建病毒载体的基本思路 Basic idea for constructing viral vectors 首先需要识别病毒基因组中对复制非必需或有条件必需的基因。 First, identify genes in the viral genome that are not essential or only conditionally necessary for replication。 这类基因可以被外源基因替换，不影响载体总体功能。 These genes can be replaced by foreign (trans)genes without affecting the overall vector function。
2
外源基因插入与病毒基因的删除 Insertion of transgene and deletion of viral genes 将识别出的、非必需的病毒基因删除，然后在其位置插入目标外源基因。 Delete the identified non-essential viral genes and insert the target transgene in their place。 通过这种方式构建出只包含原始病毒基因组复制和包装信号、且带有外源序列的载体基因组。 In this way, construct a vector genome that contains only the original replication and packaging signals along with the inserted transgene。
3
转染包装细胞系 Transfection of packaging cell line 将上述重组载体基因组转染到具备病毒复制和结构蛋白表达能力的包装细胞系。 Transfect the above recombinant vector genome into a packaging cell line capable of expressing viral replication and structural proteins。 包装细胞提供缺失的蛋白，为病毒载体的组装和生产提供必需的成分。 The packaging cells supply the missing proteins, providing the necessary components for vector assembly and production。
4
收获重组病毒载体 Harvesting recombinant viral vectors 最终，包装细胞系组装形成能够携带外源基因的重组病毒颗粒。 Finally, the packaging cell line assembles recombinant viral particles capable of carrying the transgene。 收获并纯化这些病毒载体，可用于后续细胞或体内实验。 These vectors are harvested and purified for subsequent cell-based or in vivo experiments。
5
关键序列的保留 Retention of critical sequences 保留复制起点（Ori）和包装信号（ψ等）等关键序列，以保证载体基因组能被高效复制和包装进病毒颗粒。 Retain key sequences such as the origin of replication (Ori) and packaging signal (ψ) to ensure that the vector genome can be efficiently replicated and packaged into viral particles。
6
老师补充讲解要点 Key additional points from the lecture 只有带有正确包装信号的基因组才能被病毒衣壳蛋白识别并包裹成颗粒，缺失该信号则病毒无法正常形成。 Only genomes with the correct packaging signal are recognized by capsid proteins and encapsulated; without this signal, the virus cannot be properly assembled。 包装细胞系通过工程手段稳定表达所有缺失的病毒蛋白（如复制蛋白和结构蛋白），因此只需要把重组载体基因组转进来即可。 Packaging cell lines are engineered to stably express all missing viral proteins (such as replication and structural proteins), so only the recombinant vector genome needs to be introduced。 这样能大幅提升病毒载体的生物安全性，确保其不能在目标细胞中再次复制。 This greatly enhances the biosafety of viral vectors, ensuring they cannot replicate again in target cells。

病毒载体与转基因Viral vector and transgene

1
转基因片段的大小是限制因素 The size of the transgene is the main limiting factor 病毒载体能携带的转基因片段有上限，不同载体容量不同。 The transgene that a viral vector can carry is limited, and each vector has a different capacity. 腺病毒的容量为8至35 kb； Adenovirus can carry from 8 to 35 kb; 逆转录病毒6至8 kb； Retrovirus can carry 6 to 8 kb; 慢病毒约8 kb； Lentivirus can carry about 8 kb; 腺相关病毒（AAV）仅4.5 kb。 Adeno-associated virus (AAV) can carry only 4.5 kb. 老师特别强调如转基因过长，会导致无法装配到病毒颗粒，表达等环节失败。 The teacher emphasized that if the transgene is too large, it cannot be packed into the viral particle, leading to expression failure.
2
生产病毒载体 Production of viral vectors 首先将携带转基因的病毒载体在包装细胞中生产出来。 First, the viral vector carrying the transgene is produced in packaging cells. 这个过程中，确保转基因正确插入并能够通过病毒组装、包装。 In this process, the correct insertion and successful assembly/packaging of the transgene are crucial.
3
转导过程 Transduction process 获得的病毒载体用于“感染”靶细胞，将转基因导入受影响（患病）细胞。 The produced viral vectors are used to "infect" the target (diseased) cells, delivering the transgene. 转基因通过病毒基因组进入受体细胞，并实现表达。 The transgene enters the host cell as part of the viral genome and gets expressed.
4
治疗蛋白的表达 Expression of therapeutic protein 病毒载体导入转基因后，靶细胞可以表达新的治疗蛋白，发挥作用。 After the viral vector introduces the transgene, the target cell begins to produce the therapeutic protein, exerting its effect. 课堂补充：表达量和持续时间依赖于病毒类型、调控元件及目标细胞特性。 Supplement from class: The amount and duration of expression depend on the type of virus, regulatory elements, and target cell characteristics.
5
应用选择与考量 Application selection and considerations 选择何种载体需根据转基因的大小、所需表达时间、细胞类型等具体要求决定。 Choosing the appropriate vector depends on the size of the transgene, required expression time, and cell type. 大容量需求时选腺病毒，小容量求高稳定性时选AAV或慢病毒等。 For large inserts, adenovirus is used; for small/highly stable expression, AAV or lentivirus may be preferred.
6
其他注意事项与老师补充 Other considerations and additional lecture points 不同病毒载体的转导效率、免疫原性和基因表达持续性存在关键差异。 Different viral vectors vary in transduction efficiency, immunogenicity, and duration of gene expression. 生产与应用过程中需权衡安全性、免疫反应及表达效果。 Safety, immune response, and gene expression effects must be balanced during production and application.

大规模生产 Large-scale production

1
大规模病毒载体生产的实际需求 Practical demands of large-scale viral vector production 以AAV用于血友病B基因治疗为例，每位患者每公斤体重需要注射 2×10112×1011 个AAV。 Taking AAV for hemophilia B gene therapy as an example, each patient needs to be injected with 2×10112×1011 AAV particles per kilogram. 所以，一个70公斤的病人总共需要 1.5×10131.5×1013（即15,0000亿）个AAV颗粒。 For a 70 kg patient, this totals 1.5×10131.5×1013 (15,000 billion) AAV particles.
2
理论产率与实际产率的巨大差异 Large gap between theoretical and actual yields 理论上如产率Y=100%，只需1升细胞培养基即可获得足量病毒。 In theory, if yield Y = 100%, only 1 liter of culture would be required to obtain enough virus. 但实际产率仅约1%，因此需100升培养基才能满足同一剂量需求。 In practice, the yield is only about 1%, so 100 liters of culture are needed for the same dose.
3
工业化生产设备 Industrial-scale production equipment 生产过程中需使用容量为100-200升的大型生物反应器以达到所需产量。 Large-scale bioreactors with capacities of 100-200 liters are used in the production process to meet the required yield. 老师补充，AAV产量极高，且不同病毒产率差异较大，对技术与设备的要求也不同。 The teacher added that AAV yields are extremely high and that different viruses have varying production efficiencies, requiring different technical and equipment setups.
4
下游纯化与质控 Downstream purification and quality control 获得的病毒颗粒还需经过严格纯化和质控，才能用于临床。 The produced viral particles must undergo strict purification and quality control before clinical use. 实验室标准远不能满足工业与临床需求，需专门的质控体系。 Laboratory standards are far from sufficient for industrial and clinical needs, so specialized quality control systems are necessary.
5
课堂补充—生产与应用面临的挑战 Additional notes from class—challenges in production and application 老师强调，对于AAV类基因治疗，每位患者所需病毒数量极大，生产工艺与成本都是巨大挑战。 The teacher emphasized that for AAV-based gene therapy, the amount of virus needed per patient is enormous, making production processes and costs significant challenges. 此外，每批产品均须严格检测其纯度、感染性与安全性，确保不带有空载或有害病毒。 In addition, each batch must be strictly tested for purity, infectivity, and safety to ensure it contains no empty capsids or harmful viruses.

病毒载体的质量控制 Control of viral vectors

1
身份与纯度的检测 Identity and purity assessment 需要确认病毒载体的DNA身份，常用测序和限制性酶切作图。 The DNA identity of the viral vector must be confirmed by sequencing and restriction mapping. 可通过物理滴定和光度法测定病毒悬液中颗粒数。 Physical titration and photometry can be used to determine the number of particles in the viral suspension. 还需进行生物滴定，如裂解斑/细胞病变效应和(RT-)PCR，评估感染活性。 Biological titration, such as lysis plaques/cytopathic effect and (RT-)PCR, are used to assess infective activity. 需检测病毒纯度，比如是否存在“空”病毒衣壳，可用光度法进一步鉴定。 The purity of the viral vector, including the presence of empty capsids, is checked using photometry.
2
活性检测 Activity evaluation 检测体外转基因转移效率，多采用PCR。 In vitro transgene transfer efficiency is generally evaluated by PCR. 检查目标蛋白的表达，应用SDS-PAGE、Western Blot、ELISA和免疫细胞化学等技术。 Expression of the protein of interest is analyzed by SDS-PAGE, Western-Blot, ELISA, and immunocytochemistry. 还需在细胞或动物模型中评估蛋白功能性。 The functionality of the protein is assessed in cell or animal models.
3
安全性与无菌性检测 Safety and sterility assessment 需检测细菌与真菌污染，一般依靠无菌性检测。 Testing for bacterial and fungal contaminants is done using sterility tests. 检查内毒素含量，采用内毒素检测（LAL法）。 Presence of endotoxins is checked with the endotoxin test (LAL). 必须检测支原体污染，包括特殊培养、细胞培养、荧光与PCR。 Mycoplasma contamination is tested by culture in specific media, cell culture, epifluorescence, and PCR. 检查有无外源病毒，体外用指示细胞或PCR分析。 Adventitious viruses are detected in vitro using indicator cells and PCR. 最后还要排查是否有能复制的病毒粒子，多采用宿主敏感细胞和PCR进行检测。 Search for replicative particles is performed using tests on permissive cells and PCR.
4
课堂补充要点 Additional notes from the lecture 老师强调，所有生产批次都必须按严格流程进行以上检测，确保治疗安全性。 The teacher emphasized that all production batches must undergo the above tests rigorously to ensure therapeutic safety. 还要针对不同应用场景调整检测策略，某些实验根据需要可增加特殊的病毒或转基因表达安全性验证。 Depending on the application, testing protocols can be adapted; additional tests for viral or transgene expression safety may be required for specific situations. 病毒颗粒不一定都具有感染性，空衣壳或失活颗粒比例需控制，否则会影响剂量与疗效评估。 Not all viral particles are infectious; the proportion of empty or inactivated particles must be monitored as it impacts dosing and therapeutic outcome.

生物安全与风险等级（高/低） Biosecurity and risk levels (High / Low)

1
载体复制能力 Replication of the vector 可复制载体属于高风险；非复制载体属于低风险。 Replicative vectors are classified as high risk; non-replicative vectors are low risk. 如果载体能在实验者或动物体内扩增，则对操作人员和环境风险更高。 If the vector can replicate in the operator or animal, there is a higher risk to personnel and the environment.
2
载体设计 Design of the vector 生产过程中使用2个质粒、保留并表达病毒基因属高风险。 High risk: use of 2 plasmids for production and viral genes present/expressed. 使用3个质粒、病毒基因已删除则风险较低。 Low risk: use of 3 plasmids for production and viral genes deleted. 保留病毒基因的载体更易发生重组，增加意外产生复制性病毒的概率。 Vectors with retained viral genes have a higher chance of recombination, increasing the risk of accidentally generating replicative virus.
3
转基因片段 Transgene 芯片（如癌基因、毒素基因、肿瘤抑制基因）为高风险。 Oncogenes, toxin genes, or tumor suppressor genes are high risk. 结构基因或非致癌基因为低风险。 Structural or non-oncogenic genes are low risk. 表达有毒或致癌基因时，操作人员和环境的潜在危险增加。 Expressing toxic or oncogenic genes increases risks to operators and the environment.
4
生产规模 Production scale 大规模生产风险高，实验室小规模为低风险。 Large-scale production is high risk; laboratory scale is low risk. 批量越大，对生物安全措施的要求越高。 The larger the batch, the stricter the biosafety requirements.
5
动物宿主 Animal host 使用易感宿主或人源化动物属高风险；非易感宿主为低风险。 High risk: permissive hosts or humanized animals; low risk: non-permissive hosts. 如果动物能支持病毒复制，操作风险上升。 If animals support virus replication, the operational risk is increased.
6
动物操作 Handling of animals 需要体内注射、用针处理等为高风险。 High risk: administration of vectors via injection or use of needles. 无此操作风险则较低。 No such procedures means lower risk. 针头操作存在自我注射风险，需格外注意实验安全。 Needle use may cause accidental self-injection, requiring special safety precautions.
7
其他补充 Additional notes 风险等级还需考虑操作对象、病毒类型和实验目的等多重因素。 Risk level assessment should also consider the type of virus, target, and experimental purpose. 实施时需选择合适的操作级别和个人防护措施。 Appropriate biosafety levels and personal protection must be implemented accordingly.

病毒载体应用中的难点 Difficulties in using viral vectors

Lentiviral particle及其基因组结构Lentiviral particle and its genome structure

Multiplication cycle of the HIV (Genus Lentivirus)HIV（慢病毒属）的复制周期

1
慢病毒颗粒结构 Lentiviral Particle Structure

HIV属于慢病毒（Lentivirus）属，是对慢病毒载体的原型参考。 HIV belongs to the Lentivirus genus and serves as a prototype for lentiviral vectors.

成熟的慢病毒颗粒由多个核心成分组成，包括包膜、衣壳、基质、核衣壳、逆转录酶、整合酶等。 The mature lentiviral particle contains several key components: envelope, capsid, matrix, nucleocapsid, reverse transcriptase, and integrase.

病毒包膜上有gp120-gp41糖蛋白，负责病毒识别和进入宿主细胞。 The envelope contains gp120-gp41 glycoproteins, responsible for recognition and entry into host cells.

衣壳（capsid）保护病毒基因组，基质（matrix）与包膜连接，核衣壳（nucleocapsid）与RNA结合。 The capsid protects the viral genome, the matrix connects to the envelope, and the nucleocapsid associates with RNA.

内部有逆转录酶（reverse transcriptase）和整合酶（integrase），分别负责逆转录和基因组整合。 Inside, reverse transcriptase converts the RNA genome to DNA, and integrase mediates genomic integration.

2
HIV基因组结构 HIV Genome Organization

基因组两端具有长末端重复序列（LTR），是整合和转录调控必需的元件。 The genome is flanked by long terminal repeats (LTRs), essential for integration and transcriptional regulation.

三大主要基因区块包括GAG、POL、ENV： There are three main gene blocks: GAG, POL, and ENV.

GAG编码结构蛋白如衣壳和基质。 GAG encodes structural proteins like capsid and matrix.

POL编码酶类，包括逆转录酶和整合酶。 POL encodes enzymes, including reverse transcriptase and integrase.

ENV编码包膜糖蛋白gp120和gp41。 ENV encodes the envelope glycoproteins gp120 and gp41.

其他辅助基因如vif、vpr、tat、rev、nef等，调控病毒复制和免疫逃逸。 Additional accessory genes (vif, vpr, tat, rev, nef) regulate viral replication and immune evasion.

GAG、POL、ENV在载体设计时通常会被剔除或在不同质粒中分开表达，以降低风险。 In vector design, GAG, POL, and ENV are often removed or expressed separately to reduce recombination risk.

3
HIV（慢病毒属）复制周期 Multiplication Cycle of HIV (Lentivirus)

HIV通过包膜蛋白与靶细胞膜上的CD4及CCR5/CXCR4受体结合，实现进入。 HIV enters cells by binding its envelope proteins to CD4 and CCR5/CXCR4 receptors on the host cell membrane.

病毒融合后释放核心至胞质，启动逆转录。 After fusion, the viral core is released into the cytoplasm, initiating reverse transcription.

逆转录酶将病毒RNA逆转录为双链DNA。 Reverse transcriptase converts viral RNA into double-stranded DNA.

病毒DNA通过核孔进入细胞核，并由整合酶介导整合到宿主基因组。 Viral DNA enters the nucleus via nuclear pores and integrates into the host genome, mediated by integrase.

转录和翻译生成病毒蛋白，并与基因组组装病毒颗粒。 Transcription and translation produce viral proteins, which, together with the genome, assemble into viral particles.

新病毒颗粒经包装（packaging）、组装（assembly）、芽生（budding）从细胞膜释放，并在胞外成熟。 New viral particles undergo packaging, assembly, and budding from the cell membrane, then mature extracellularly.

4
上课补充讲解 Key Points from Lecture

老师强调HIV基因组中的GAG区编码结构蛋白，POL区编码酶类（如逆转录酶、整合酶），ENV区编码包膜蛋白。 The lecturer emphasized that the GAG region encodes structural proteins, POL encodes enzymes (reverse transcriptase, integrase), and ENV encodes envelope proteins.

LTR区域在基因集成和转录中起重要作用，例如作为整合“锚点”和启动子。 The LTR region is crucial for integration (serves as an "anchor") and acts as a promoter for transcription.

在慢病毒载体设计与生产中，安全性要求通常将主要基因功能模块分开表达，降低恢复复制性病毒的概率。 For lentiviral vector design and production, safety is increased by separating major gene modules, thereby reducing the risk of regenerating replication-competent virus.

慢病毒的优点包括可感染分裂和非分裂细胞，并能实现长期基因表达。 Lentiviruses can transduce both dividing and non-dividing cells, enabling long-term gene expression.

慢病毒载体的制备Production of lentiviral vectors

靶细胞的感染Infection of target cells

1
三质粒系统用于重组慢病毒载体的制备

3-plasmid system for the production of recombinant lentiviral vectors

制备重组慢病毒需转染三种质粒
To produce recombinant lentivirus, three types of plasmids are transfected:   1）载体质粒（如pTrip CMV GFP）：包含治疗基因/目的基因（如GFP），两端为LTR，RRE等调控元件，含Ψ信号，保证被包装进病毒颗粒。 Plasmid vector (e.g., pTrip CMV GFP): contains the therapeutic/target gene (such as GFP), flanked by LTRs and regulatory elements like RRE, and contains the packaging signal Ψ to be encapsidated into the viral particle.   2）包膜蛋白质粒（pVSV-G）：表达VSV-G包膜糖蛋白，使病毒获得广泛的细胞嗜性，提高稳定性。 Envelope plasmid (pVSV-G): expresses the vesicular stomatitis virus envelope glycoprotein (VSV-G), conferring broad tropism and increasing viral stability.   3）包装质粒（p8.91）：提供病毒组装和复制所需的蛋白，包括gag、pol及调控蛋白tat、rev，但删除了Ψ包装信号，避免自身被包装。 Encapsidation (packaging) plasmid (p8.91): provides proteins necessary for virus assembly and replication, including gag, pol, and regulatory proteins tat and rev, but lacks the Ψ signal so it cannot be encapsidated itself.
2
三质粒系统设计要点与安全性

Key features and biosafety of the 3-plasmid system

这种系统是三质粒分开表达，使得野生型病毒重建风险极低，提高安全性。 This system separates the essential viral genes onto three plasmids, thereby minimizing the risk of generating replication-competent wild-type virus and increasing biosafety.

GFP常用于示踪或实验优化，不属于治疗基因，仅作为报告基因使用。 GFP is commonly used as a reporter gene for tracking or process optimization; it is not a therapeutic gene.

LTR在载体两端，能促进基因的整合与表达。 LTRs flank the vector and promote integration and gene expression.

包装质粒无Ψ信号，结构蛋白只在包装细胞中表达，防止病毒自身再次复制。 The packaging plasmid lacks the Ψ signal, so the structural proteins are only expressed in producer cells and cannot be incorporated into new viruses, preventing further replication.

3
慢病毒颗粒的感染及检测

Lentiviral particle infection and detection

病毒颗粒收集后用于感染靶细胞，常用术语为“转导”而非“转染”。 After collection, lentiviral particles are used to infect target cells; the correct term here is "transduction," not "transfection".

成功感染的细胞将表达GFP，可通过荧光显微镜直接观察绿色荧光。 Successfully transduced cells will express GFP, which can be directly visualized as green fluorescence under a microscope.

对于后续实验，常在严格的无菌（洁净）环境下进行操作，以确保生物安全。 All subsequent manipulations are performed in highly sterile (clean room) environments to ensure biosafety.

通过GFP可快速确认病毒制备和感染流程是否顺利及效率高低。 GFP reporting allows rapid confirmation of virus production and infection efficiency.

4
课堂补充与常见问题

Lecture highlights and common issues

该体系的最大优点是高度安全，且载体成分间重组概率极低。 The biggest advantage of this system is its high safety, with an extremely low probability of recombination among vector components.

能通过更换包膜蛋白改变病毒的细胞嗜性，实现特异性转导。 The viral tropism can be altered for cell/organ specificity by changing the envelope plasmid.

由于目的载体含有Ψ信号，只有该质粒能被包装进病毒颗粒；包装和包膜质粒不能被包装进病毒。 Only the transfer plasmid contains the Ψ signal and can be packaged into viral particles; the packaging and envelope plasmids cannot be packaged.

投入的质粒需经过纯度和序列的质量控制，实验操作需防止污染和意外感染。 All plasmids must be quality controlled for purity and sequence integrity; biosafety precautions must be followed to prevent contamination and accidental infection.

靶细胞感染与慢病毒/逆转录病毒载体的优缺点 Infection of Target Cells and the Advantages/Limitations of Lentiviral/Retroviral Vectors

一、靶细胞的感染与检测 Infection and Detection of Target Cells

慢病毒颗粒可用于感染靶细胞，正确术语为“转导”。 Lentiviral particles are used to infect target cells, and the correct term is "transduction".

被成功感染的细胞会表达GFP，可用荧光显微镜观察绿色荧光。 Successfully transduced cells will express GFP, which can be detected as green fluorescence under a microscope.

实验过程中，操作需在严格的生物安全环境（如P2实验室）下进行，防止污染和意外感染。 During the experiment, all steps should be performed under strict biosafety conditions (such as in a P2 lab) to avoid contamination and accidental infection.

检查GFP信号可快速判断病毒制备和感染流程是否顺利。 Checking the GFP signal allows rapid assessment of the efficiency and success of virus production and transduction.

二、慢病毒/逆转录病毒载体的优点 Advantages of Lentiviral/Retroviral Vectors

包膜糖蛋白可以被其他蛋白取代，从而改变感染靶向性（嗜性）。 The envelope glycoprotein can be substituted with other proteins, allowing modification of viral tropism.

常用的VSV-G蛋白能赋予病毒广泛的细胞感染能力和较高稳定性。 The commonly used VSV-G protein gives the virus broad cellular tropism and high stability.

是基因替代的理想载体，转基因可稳定整合到细胞基因组中。 They are ideal vectors for gene replacement, as the transgene can stably integrate into the cellular genome.

慢病毒载体的转基因可整合到分裂及非分裂细胞中。 Lentiviral vectors can integrate the transgene into both mitotic and non-mitotic cells.

可以实现转基因的长期表达，这在基因疗法中很重要。 They can achieve long-term expression of the transgene, which is important for gene therapy.

引发的免疫反应极低，适合长期表达和应用。 They induce very limited immune response, supporting long-term use and expression.

由于结构分离，发生重组和生成复制型病毒的风险极低。 The risk of recombination or generating replication-competent virus is very low due to construction fractionation.

三、慢病毒/逆转录病毒载体的局限性 Limitations of Lentiviral/Retroviral Vectors

插入片段容量较小，一般限制在7-8 kb。 The insert size is small, usually limited to 7-8 kb.

不易制备高滴度、高感染性的病毒颗粒。 Producing high titers of infectious viral particles is a challenge.

对特定细胞的靶向（特异性）有限，部分细胞类型难以感染。 There is a lack of effective cell targeting, and some cell types are difficult to infect.

转基因整合是随机的，可能导致插入性突变（插入致突变风险）。 Integration of the transgene is random, which creates a risk of insertional mutagenesis.

仍存在一定的安全性问题，需要严格的质量控制和生物安全保障。 There are safety concerns that require stringent quality control and biosafety precautions.

补充要点 Additional Key Points from the Lecture

慢病毒/逆转录病毒载体可以感染分裂和非分裂细胞，这是其他病毒载体不具备的独特优势。 Lentiviral/retroviral vectors can infect both dividing and non-dividing cells, a unique advantage not shared by other viral vectors.

包膜蛋白的替换可灵活调节病毒的靶细胞范围。 By switching envelope proteins, the range of target cells can be flexibly adjusted.

为确保实验安全，所有质粒和病毒制备均需进行纯度和序列质控。 To ensure experimental safety, all plasmids and virus preparations must undergo purity and sequence quality control.

腺病毒颗粒结构与增殖周期Adenoviral Particle Structure and Multiplication Cycle

一、腺病毒颗粒的结构 The Structure of Adenoviral Particle

腺病毒为无包膜的二十面体病毒，外壳由多种蛋白质构成。 Adenovirus is a non-enveloped icosahedral virus with a capsid composed of several proteins.

外壳的主要结构蛋白为hexon，形成二十面体的主框架。 The main capsid protein is the hexon, which forms the framework of the icosahedron.

每个顶点有纤突（fiber）和五聚体基座（penton base），纤突参与病毒吸附宿主细胞。 Each vertex has a fiber and a penton base; the fiber is involved in virus attachment to host cells.

内部含有腺病毒基因组，为线性双链DNA，与核心蛋白（V, VII, μ）结合。 Inside is the adenoviral genome, which is linear double-stranded DNA, associated with core proteins (V, VII, μ).

蛋白VI和蛋白IX参与粒子的结构稳定及装配。 Proteins VI and IX contribute to particle stability and assembly.

终末蛋白（Terminal protein）与DNA两端结合，可能对复制有辅助作用。 The terminal protein binds to both ends of the DNA and may assist in replication.

二、腺病毒的增殖周期 Multiplication Cycle of the Adenovirus

腺病毒在细胞核内进行全生命周期。 The entire life cycle of adenovirus occurs in the nucleus of the host cell.

先是早期（early phase）转录，产生E1A、E1B、E2等调控蛋白，调节细胞环境和病毒复制。 First, the early phase transcription produces regulatory proteins such as E1A, E1B, and E2, which modulate the cellular environment and viral replication.

E3和E4也是早期蛋白，参与调控细胞对病毒的反应和免疫逃逸。 E3 and E4 are also early proteins involved in modulating cellular responses and immune evasion.

病毒DNA在细胞核内复制，形成大量新基因组。 The viral DNA is then replicated in the nucleus, generating multiple new viral genomes.

晚期（late phase）合成结构蛋白，包括外壳和核心蛋白，用于组装新病毒颗粒。 In the late phase, structural proteins are synthesized, including capsid and core proteins for assembling new virions.

新合成的结构蛋白与基因组包装形成成熟病毒颗粒。 The newly synthesized structural proteins and genomes are packaged into mature viral particles.

成熟的病毒颗粒经细胞裂解或其他机制释放到胞外，感染新的细胞。 Mature viral particles are released via cell lysis or other mechanisms to infect new cells.
