三、老师口头补充要点 Additional Oral Remarks from the Lecturer

腺病毒基因组为线性双链DNA，并带有特征性的纤突，这是其识别宿主和细胞进入的关键。 The adenoviral genome is linear double-stranded DNA with characteristic fibers, which are key for host recognition and cell entry.

腺病毒的生命周期严格区分为早期和晚期阶段，早期主要是调控和复制，晚期主要是结构蛋白的表达与病毒组装。 The adenoviral life cycle is strictly divided into early and late phases: the early phase focuses on regulation and replication, while the late phase emphasizes structural protein expression and assembly.

腺病毒复制不整合入宿主基因组，因此表达多为短期。 Adenoviruses do not integrate into the host genome, so gene expression is usually transient.

由于其免疫原性较强，体内应用需注意诱发宿主的免疫应答。 Due to their strong immunogenicity, adenoviral vectors can stimulate robust immune responses, which must be considered during therapeutic use.

腺病毒颗粒的结构和生命周期特点使其成为研究基因转导和基因治疗的重要工具。 The structure and life cycle of adenoviruses make them important tools for studies of gene transfer and gene therapy.

腺病毒载体的不同代际变化Different generations of adenoviral vectors

一、腺病毒载体构建的基本要素 Key Elements of Adenoviral Vector Construction

腺病毒基因组两端有反向重复序列（ITR），约100 bp，参与复制和包装。 The adenoviral genome has inverted terminal repeats (ITR) of about 100 bp at both ends, involved in replication and packaging.

ψ信号是5’端的包装信号，用于指示病毒基因组被正确包裹进病毒颗粒。 The ψ sequence at the 5’ end is the encapsidation signal, guiding the correct packaging of the viral genome into virions.

MLP是主要晚期启动子，负责调控结构蛋白等晚期基因的转录。 MLP (Major Late Promoter) regulates the transcription of structural and other late viral genes.

二、不同代腺病毒载体的结构与特点 Structure and Features of Different Generations

第一代腺病毒载体通常去除了E1区（ΔE1），有时还会去除E3区（ΔE3），用于插入外源基因（转基因）。 First generation adenoviral vectors generally delete the E1 region (ΔE1), sometimes also E3 (ΔE3), to insert a transgene.

第二代腺病毒载体在第一代基础上，进一步去除E2和E4等区域（ΔE1, ΔE2, ΔE3, ΔE4），以极大降低病毒自身基因的表达，提高安全性和转基因容量。 Second generation vectors build on the first by deleting additional regions like E2 and E4 (ΔE1, ΔE2, ΔE3, ΔE4), further reducing viral gene expression and increasing safety and capacity for the transgene.

辅助依赖型腺病毒（高容量/第三代）几乎去除了所有病毒编码区，只保留ITR和ψ信号，大大提高了外源基因的插入空间（可达30 kb以上），完全依赖外源系统供给结构蛋白。 Helper-dependent adenoviral (high-capacity/third generation) vectors remove almost all viral coding sequences, retaining only ITR and ψ, which allows large transgenes (over 30 kb), and completely rely on helper systems to supply viral proteins.

三、意义及生产策略 Significance and Production Strategy

所有这些载体的生产依赖于补体细胞如HEK 293，细胞中表达被去除或缺失的病毒蛋白，称为“互补表达”或“功能互补”。 All these vectors are produced in complementing cell lines like HEK 293, which express the deleted or missing viral proteins, a process called "transcomplementation".

四、老师补充口头解读 Additional Key Points from Lecture

更高代次的腺病毒载体可装载更大外源DNA，并极大减少病毒相关毒性和免疫反应。 Higher generation adenoviral vectors can carry larger foreign DNA and greatly reduce viral toxicity and immune response.

第一代因保留较多病毒自身基因表达，容易引起免疫反应和细胞毒性；第三代应用时更安全，适合慢性疾病基因治疗。 The first generation retains more viral gene expression, which may trigger immune response and cytotoxicity; third generation vectors are safer for applications like chronic disease gene therapy.

HEK 293细胞源自人胚胎肾细胞，被工程化以表达腺病毒E1区域蛋白，是最常用的腺病毒互补细胞系。 HEK 293 cells, derived from human embryonic kidney and engineered to express adenoviral E1 proteins, are the most commonly used complementing cell line for adenovirus production.

腺病毒载体通常为非整合型，表达多为短期（转ient）；但大载体容量和高滴度生产，使其在疫苗、基因补充等领域广泛应用。 Adenoviral vectors are typically non-integrating and express genes transiently, but their large capacity and high titer production make them widely used in vaccines and gene supplementation.

腺病毒载体的优势Advantages of adenoviral vectors

对免疫健全个体的致病性低 Low pathogenicity in an immunocompetent person

基因组特征清楚且易于操作 Well characterized and easily manipulated genome

能制备高滴度的感染性病毒 Production of high titers of infectious viruses

对广泛表达的受体具有嗜性 Tropism for widely expressed receptors

可转导静息状态细胞 Vectors transducing quiescent cells

病毒基因组不整合入宿主基因组 No integration of the viral genome

病毒基因组以表观染色体形式在细胞中可持续存在 Persistence of the viral genome in episomal form

外源基因表达量高 High expression of the transgene

能诱导强烈的细胞免疫及体液免疫，包括全身和黏膜免疫反应 Induction of strong cellular and humoral immune, systemic and mucous responses

适合应用于疫苗接种策略（如口服给药途径） Adapted to vaccination strategies (oral route for example)

补充老师讲解和口头强调 Additional Key Points from the Lecturer

腺病毒载体广泛应用于疫苗、基因治疗以及表达外源蛋白，因为其不整合入基因组，所以适合短期表达，降低了基因突变和插入致癌的风险 Adenoviral vectors are widely used in vaccines, gene therapy, and heterologous protein expression. Since they do not integrate into the genome, they are suitable for short-term expression and reduce the risks of mutation and insertional oncogenesis

可在多种分裂和非分裂细胞中递送基因，且病毒本身操作和生产流程非常成熟便捷 They can deliver genes to various dividing and non-dividing cells, and the viral manipulation and production processes are mature and convenient

病毒滴度极高，有利于大规模生产和应用 Viral titers are very high, facilitating large-scale production and application

由于腺病毒广泛存在于人群中，部分人群体内已有抗体，可能会对治疗或再次接种造成免疫快速清除，需在临床应用时引起重视 Due to widespread exposure, some individuals carry pre-existing antibodies against adenovirus, which may cause rapid immune clearance during therapy or repeated vaccination, and this requires attention in clinical applications

腺病毒基因组以环状DNA（表观体）存在于细胞核，表达为短期/中期，适合需要短期蛋白表达或免疫激活的情境 The adenoviral genome exists as episomal circular DNA in the nucleus and supports transient/intermediate expression, suitable for scenarios requiring short-term protein expression or immune activation

腺病毒载体的局限性Limitations of the adenoviral vectors

转基因表达的持续性有限 Persistence of transgene expression limited in time 腺病毒载体主要支持短期或中期的外源基因表达，难以实现长期持续表达。 Adenoviral vectors mainly support short or intermediate-term transgene expression, and it is difficult to achieve long-term sustained expression.

免疫反应是主要障碍 Immune response is a major obstacle 免疫系统能迅速识别并清除腺病毒载体，显著影响治疗或疫苗效果。 The immune system can quickly recognize and clear adenoviral vectors, significantly affecting the efficacy of therapy or vaccines. 普遍人群中腺病毒抗体阳性率高达80%，导致很多人初次甚至再次接受时都可能因已有免疫而快速失效。 The prevalence of adenovirus antibodies in the general population is as high as 80%, so many people may experience rapid vector clearance even at first or repeated administration due to pre-existing immunity.

炎症反应 Inflammatory response 腺病毒载体容易诱导机体产生炎症反应，潜在导致组织损伤或副作用。 Adenoviral vectors can easily trigger inflammatory responses, which may result in tissue damage or side effects.

再次给药困难 Difficult re-administration 因免疫记忆和抗体存在，若需多次用药，后续给药会迅速被机体清除，效果明显下降。 Due to immune memory and existing antibodies, multiple dosing is problematic as subsequent administrations are rapidly cleared, with significantly reduced efficacy.

理论上的与内源性病毒重组风险 Risk of theoretical recombination with endogenous viruses 虽然发生率极低，但存在与人体内源腺病毒或其它病毒发生重组的理论风险，可能造成安全隐患。 Though extremely rare, there is a theoretical risk of recombination between the vector and endogenous or other viruses, which may pose safety concerns.

老师补充讲解与强调 Instructor’s Additional Explanation and Emphasis

老师指出腺病毒载体因易被宿主免疫系统识别，是基因治疗及疫苗设计中一项重大挑战，尤其是在临床上对效果持久性要求较高时。 The instructor emphasized that easy recognition by the host immune system is a major challenge in gene therapy and vaccine design using adenoviral vectors, especially in clinical situations where long-term efficacy is required.

免疫清除极可能在二次给药时造成完全无效，这也是目前在实际应用中亟需解决的问题。 Immune clearance can lead to complete ineffectiveness upon the second administration, a critical issue to address in practice.

发生炎症反应有时会导致急性或严重副作用，需在给药剂量及用法上特别注意。 Inflammatory responses may sometimes lead to acute or severe adverse effects, necessitating careful consideration in dosing and administration.

关于理论重组风险
老师说明虽然现实中罕见，但在病毒设计和生产中需严格监控，确保不会产生复制型病毒或重组产物，以保障患者安全。 Regarding the theoretical risk of recombination, the instructor pointed out that although rare, strict monitoring during viral design and production is essential to ensure no replicative virus or recombinant is produced, keeping patient safety assured.

AAV2基因组结构与增殖周期及重组AAV解析Genome Structure, Multiplication Cycle, and Recombinant AAV Explanation

1
AAV2的野生型基因组结构 Wild-type AAV2 Genome Structure

AAV2基因组为单股DNA，约4.7kb，包含两大开放读码框Rep和Cap。 The AAV2 genome is a single-stranded DNA of about 4.7 kb, containing two open reading frames: Rep and Cap.

Rep区编码四种复制所需的调控蛋白（Rep78, Rep68, Rep52, Rep40），Cap区编码三种衣壳结构蛋白（VP1, VP2, VP3）。 The Rep region encodes four regulatory proteins necessary for replication (Rep78, Rep68, Rep52, Rep40), while the Cap region encodes three capsid structural proteins (VP1, VP2, VP3).

其两端为反向重复序列（ITR），是病毒复制与包装所必需。 Both ends have inverted terminal repeats (ITR) essential for viral replication and packaging.

基因组两侧的ITR还能作为启动子和整合信号。 The ITRs at both ends also function as promoters and integration signals.

2
AAV的增殖周期 Multiplication Cycle of AAV

AAV感染细胞后，若无辅助病毒（如腺病毒）共感染，AAV基因组整合到宿主的19号染色体AAVS1位点，进入溶原周期，处于静默状态，不表达病毒颗粒。 After AAV infects a cell, if no helper virus (like adenovirus) is present, the AAV genome integrates into the AAVS1 site on human chromosome 19, leading to a lysogenic cycle, remaining silent without expression of viral particles.

一旦有辅助病毒共感染，AAV基因组被激活，进入裂解周期，进行基因组复制和病毒颗粒包装，释放新病毒。 Upon co-infection with a helper virus, the AAV genome is activated, entering the lytic cycle with genome replication, viral packaging, and release of new particles.

这种独特的增殖机制确保AAV不引起细胞溶解，安全性较高。 This unique cycle ensures that AAV does not cause cell lysis under normal circumstances, conferring high safety.

3
重组AAV的构建与表达 Recombinant AAV Construction and Expression

重组AAV（AAVr）只保留ITR区域，将病毒的Rep和Cap区域用目标基因表达盒（含启动子、外源基因和多腺苷酸化信号）替换。 Recombinant AAV (AAVr) retains only the ITRs, replacing Rep and Cap with the expression cassette of the desired gene (including promoter, transgene, and polyadenylation signal).

这样得到的AAVr无法自我复制，仅能在包装系统下体外生产。 This engineered AAVr cannot self-replicate and can only be produced in vitro under packaging system conditions.

最终形成的不复制型载体安全，可高效递送目标基因，不表达病毒自身蛋白。 The resulting non-replicative vector is safe, efficiently delivers the transgene, and does not express viral proteins.

4
课堂口头补充 Oral Notes and Emphasis from Class

老师强调AAV2载体因其基因组小、结构简单、免疫原性低、整合位置固定（AAVS1位点），广泛用于基因治疗。 The instructor highlighted that AAV2 vectors are widely used for gene therapy due to their small genome, simple structure, low immunogenicity, and site-specific integration (AAVS1 site).

AAV天然不会引起显著免疫反应，使用安全性较高。 Natural AAV does not provoke significant immune responses, ensuring high safety.

采用重组方式删除携带或表达病毒自身蛋白，进一步减少免疫风险。 Recombinant strategies that remove or avoid expressing viral proteins further decrease immune risks.

AAV载体不能携带过大外源基因（极限约4.7kb），这是设计时需注意的容量限制。 AAV vectors cannot carry excessively large transgenes (limit around 4.7 kb), which should be considered during design.

在实验室或工业生产中，需提供包装细胞系表达Rep、Cap蛋白，才能完成病毒包装。 During laboratory or industrial production, packaging cell lines expressing Rep and Cap are required for virus assembly.

载体设计重点
仅保留ITR确保有效包装和递送，去除其他病毒基因以增强安全性和灵活性。 Key for vector design: retain only the ITR for efficient packaging/delivery, and remove other viral genes to increase safety and flexibility.

AAV的组织特异性与生产方法 Tissue Targeting of AAV and Production Methods

1
AAV血清型及组织特异性 Tropism and Serotypes of AAV

不同AAV血清型具有不同的组织嗜性，可根据目标组织选择相应血清型。 Different serotypes of AAV have distinct tissue tropism, so the serotype can be selected according to the target tissue.

例如，AAV2广泛应用于基因治疗，可感染肌肉、脑、视网膜、肝脏和肺等多种组织。 For example, AAV2 is widely used in gene therapy and can transduce muscle, brain, retina, liver, and lungs.

AAV5和AAV8等其它血清型也因其对特定组织的偏好可用于不同治疗场景。 Other serotypes, such as AAV5 and AAV8, are used in different therapeutic contexts due to their preferences for specific tissues.

选择目标组织对应的血清型极为重要，可以显著提高递送效率与安全性。 Selecting the serotype specific to the target tissue is crucial and can significantly enhance delivery efficiency and safety.

血清型决定AAV在体内感染的广泛性和特异性，可以利用不同血清型实现对不同器官甚至细胞类型的定向递送。 The serotype determines the breadth and specificity of AAV infection in vivo. Different serotypes enable targeted delivery to distinct organs or even cell types.

2
AAV主要受体/协同受体与靶向偏好 Main AAV Receptors/Co-receptors and Tissue Localization

各AAV血清型结合的主要受体和协同受体不同，决定其感染对象。 Each AAV serotype binds to specific main receptors and co-receptors, which determines its infectivity.

例如，AAV2的主要受体是HSPG，它还能与FGFR1、HGFR等协同受体结合，适合转导骨骼肌、中枢神经系统、肝脏和肾脏。 For example, AAV2’s main receptor is HSPG, and it also binds to co-receptors FGFR1, HGFR, etc., making it suitable for transducing skeletal muscle, CNS, liver, and kidneys.

AAV8的受体尚未明确，偏好肝脏、骨骼肌、中枢神经系统、视网膜、胰腺和心脏。 The main receptor for AAV8 is unclear, but it preferentially targets liver, muscle, CNS, retina, pancreas, and heart.

AAV9可通过半乳糖受体和LamR介导，能高效感染肝脏、心脏、脑、骨骼肌、肺、胰腺和肾脏。 AAV9, via galactose receptor and LamR, efficiently infects liver, heart, brain, skeletal muscle, lungs, pancreas, and kidneys.

理解受体、血清型、组织偏好三者关系对于设计疾病相关的靶向治疗至关重要。 Understanding the relationship between receptor, serotype, and tissue preference is essential for designing disease-specific targeted therapies.

3
重组AAV生产方法 Production Methods for Recombinant AAV
经典转染法
通过三重转染或其他方式将ITR片段、Rep/Cap蛋白表达质粒和辅助质粒转入细胞，完成AAV包装。 Classical method of transfection: Co-transfection of ITR-containing plasmid, Rep/Cap expression plasmid, and helper plasmid into cells achieves AAV packaging.
稳定包被与生产细胞系
利用稳定转染的细胞持续表达包装蛋白，实现长期连续生产。 Stable encapsidation and production cell lines: Cell lines stably expressing packaging proteins are used for continuous production.
单纯疱疹病毒RepCap系统
利用HSV感染辅助，帮助AAV复制和包装。 Herpes simplex virus RepCap system: Uses HSV infection as a helper to enable AAV replication and packaging.
杆状病毒/昆虫细胞系统
在昆虫细胞内表达AAV关键蛋白，便于大规模生产。 Baculovirus/insect cell system: AAV key proteins are expressed in insect cells, facilitating large-scale production.

常用三重转染法简单灵活，便于实验室快速小规模制备；工业级生产常优化细胞系或昆虫系统以提升产量与纯度。 Classical triple transfection is simple and flexible for rapid lab-scale preparation; industrial production often prefers optimized cell lines or insect systems for higher yield and purity.

4
总结 Summary

血清型选择、受体结合机制和生产平台决定AAV载体的组织靶向性和应用前景。 The selection of serotype, receptor-binding mechanisms, and production platforms determine AAV vector’s tissue specificity and application prospects.

科学选择和设计AAV血清型可极大提高治疗的有效性和特异性。 Scientific serotype selection and design can greatly improve therapeutic efficacy and specificity.

重组AAV的生产（转染与疱疹病毒辅助法）Production of recombinant AAV (Transfection and Helper Herpes Simplex Virus Methods)

一、转染法生产重组AAV Production of Recombinant AAV by Transfection

实验室常用的方法是将AAV载体与辅助质粒共同转染到HEK293细胞中。 The commonly used laboratory method is to co-transfect the AAV vector and helper plasmids into HEK293 cells.

载体pAAV质粒上含有两个ITR以及希望导入的外源基因（转基因），ITR是包装和复制所必需的。 The pAAV vector plasmid contains two ITRs and the transgene to be delivered; ITRs are essential for packaging and replication.

三重转染法包括
分别转入（1）带ITR的目标质粒（pAAV），（2）提供rep和cap基因的质粒（pRC），（3）提供腺病毒辅助因子E4、E2、VA等的质粒（pAd）。 Triple transfection includes: (1) target plasmid with ITRs (pAAV), (2) plasmid supplying rep and cap genes (pRC), (3) plasmid supplying adenoviral helper factors E4, E2, VA, etc. (pAd).

双重转染法则将rep、cap和腺病毒辅助因子组合在同一质粒（如pDG）中，只需与pAAV共同转入即可。 Double transfection combines rep, cap, and adenoviral helper factors on a single plasmid (e.g., pDG), transfected with pAAV.

HEK293细胞提供腺病毒的E1蛋白，协助病毒组装。 HEK293 cells provide adenoviral E1 protein, aiding virus assembly.

细胞内组装形成重组AAV颗粒，收获纯化后即可获得rAAV产品。 Cells assemble recombinant AAV (rAAV) particles, which are harvested and purified.

老师特别强调
三重转染和双重转染在小规模实验室生产中最常用，操作灵活，可根据实际情况选择。 The professor emphasized that triple and double transfection are most commonly used in laboratory-scale production due to their flexibility and adaptability.

二、使用辅助单纯疱疹病毒生产重组AAV Production of Recombinant AAV Using Helper Herpes Simplex Virus

工业生产中还可利用去除复制能力的单纯疱疹病毒（HSV-1）体系。 In industrial production, a system using replication-deficient herpes simplex virus (HSV-1) can also be employed.

分别构建带有AAV rep/cap基因的辅助HSV（rHSV1-RC）和带有AAV ITR-转基因的HSV（rHSV1-AAV）。 Construct two recombinant HSVs: one carrying AAV rep/cap genes (rHSV1-RC), and one carrying AAV ITRs with the transgene (rHSV1-AAV).

这两种rHSV1同时感染HEK293或BHK21细胞。 Both rHSV1-RC and rHSV1-AAV co-infect HEK293 or BHK21 cells.

HSV-1为辅助病毒，提供AAV组装所需的所有蛋白和因子，且自身设计为不能复制，提高安全性。 HSV-1 acts as the helper virus, providing all necessary proteins and factors for AAV assembly, and is designed to be replication-deficient for safety.

细胞内完成AAV的组装，收获纯化后得到重组AAV。 AAV assembly is completed within the cells, and the recombinant AAV is harvested and purified.

老师补充
该方法大规模生产效率高，但用HSV时产物需严格纯化以去除可能残留的疱疹病毒，成本较高。 The professor added: This method achieves high yields in large-scale production, but product purification must be rigorous to remove any HSV residue, which increases cost.

三、课堂要点补充 Additional Classroom Notes

转染法操作灵活，是实验室最广泛使用的rAAV生产方式。 The transfection method is flexible and is the most widely used laboratory method for rAAV production.

HSV系统适合工业放大生产，但需要关注病毒纯化和安全性。 The HSV system is suitable for industrial-scale production, but requires attention to viral purification and biosafety.

HEK293细胞是生产AAV的标准细胞系，因其内源表达腺病毒E1蛋白，有利于AAV高效组装。 HEK293 cells are the standard cell line for AAV production because their endogenous adenoviral E1 protein expression enhances AAV assembly.

AAV载体的优缺点及与其他病毒载体的比较Summary of the Advantages, Limitations, and Comparison of AAV Vectors with Other Viral Vectors

一、AAV载体优势Advantages of AAV Vectors

AAV在免疫正常个体中无致病性。 AAV is non-pathogenic in immunocompetent individuals.

AAV的复制依赖于辅因子病毒（如腺病毒或疱疹病毒）。 Replication of AAV depends on a helper virus such as adenovirus or herpesvirus.

可感染分裂和非分裂细胞。 AAV can infect both mitotic (dividing) and non-mitotic (non-dividing) cells.

病毒基因组具有持久性，在细胞内可长期存在。 The viral genome persists and can remain in cells long term.

在特定条件下（如保留REP基因时），AAV基因组可以特异性整合到19号染色体AAVS1位点。 Under specific conditions (e.g., when the REP gene is maintained), AAV genome can integrate specifically into the AAVS1 site of chromosome 19.

没有插入突变的风险。 There is no risk of insertional mutagenesis.

老师补充，AAV非常安全，已被广泛用于基因治疗。 The professor added that AAV is very safe and is widely used in gene therapy.

二、AAV载体的局限性Limitations of AAV Vectors

插入片段小，最大可携带4.5kb外源基因。 Small insert size, with a maximum of 4.5kb for the transgene.

病毒生产效率较低，难以大规模工业生产。 Low production efficiency of viruses, making large-scale manufacturing challenging.

转基因表达水平相对较低。 The level of transgene expression is relatively low.

若生产时使用辅因子病毒，存在生产污染风险（如辅病毒残留）。 If a helper virus is used during production, there is a risk of contamination from the helper virus.

老师补充，AAV在高剂量时的安全和免疫反应问题也受到关注。 The professor added that, at high doses, the safety and immune response of AAV are also concerns.

三、与其他病毒载体的比较Comparison with Other Viral Vectors

四、扩展
生产与临床相关补充课堂内容Classroom Extension: Production and Application Points

实际生产AAV时，对纯化、病毒完整性检测、安全性、效价等指标要求严格。 During actual AAV production, strict requirements exist for purification, viral particle integrity, safety, and titer.

需确保无复制能力病毒、无辅因子病毒残留。 It is necessary to guarantee no replication-competent viruses or helper virus residues.

质量控制包括测定空/满颗粒比例、感染效率和功能性表达。 Quality control includes measuring the empty/full capsid ratio, infection efficiency, and functional expression.

五、结论 Conclusion

AAV载体因其安全性高、无插入突变风险、可感染多种细胞，广泛用作基因治疗工具。 Due to its high safety, lack of insertional mutagenesis, and capability to infect multiple cell types, AAV vectors are widely used as gene therapy tools.

但携带基因长度有限制、产量低、转基因表达水平不高，在需要大载体或高效表达时有局限。 Limitations include small carrying capacity, low yield, and modest expression, restricting use when large vectors or high expression levels are needed.

老师提醒，选择载体时需综合考虑实验目的、基因大小、安全性与表达需求。 The professor reminded that vector selection should consider experimental goals, gene size, safety, and expression requirements.

细胞外囊泡（EVs）简介Extracellular Vesicles (EVs): Introduction

1
细胞外囊泡（EVs）是膜性来源的纳米颗粒。 Extracellular vesicles (EVs) are membranous cell-derived nanoparticles.
2
EVs的结构包含磷脂双层，表面有多种蛋白，如转铁蛋白受体、MHC-I、MHC-II、整合素、四跨膜蛋白（如CD9、CD63、CD81）、粘附分子（ICAM）、Flotillin等。内部富含RNA、DNA、脂质、蛋白和分子伴侣。 The structure of EVs includes a phospholipid bilayer, with surface proteins such as transferrin receptor, MHC-I, MHC-II, integrin, tetraspanins (Cd9, Cd63, Cd81), adhesion molecules (ICAM), and flotillin. The interior contains RNA, DNA, lipids, proteins, and chaperones.
3
EVs在静息和应激等多种状态下由细胞分泌。 EVs are secreted by cells in both resting and stressed conditions.
4
所有类型的细胞，包括真核细胞和原核细胞，都可以分泌EVs。 All types of cells, including eukaryotic and prokaryotic cells, can secrete EVs.
5
EVs在所有体液中都可以被检测到，包括泪液、脑脊液、唾液、汗液、乳汁、血液、淋巴和尿液。 EVs are detectable in all body fluids, including tears, cerebrospinal fluid, saliva, sweat, milk, blood, lymph, and urine.
6
EVs的生物发生过程涉及内质网、高尔基体、多泡内体的形成和释放，以及胞吐作用。 The biogenesis of EVs involves the endoplasmic reticulum, Golgi apparatus, formation and release of multivesicular endosomes, and exocytosis.
7
（补充：老师课堂讲授内容）课前老师先让同学们互动来自不同国家，强调全班国际化背景，为引出EVs作为“普遍存在于生物界”的颗粒做好铺垫。 (Supplement: According to the class) The teacher started by interacting with students about their countries of origin, emphasizing the international diversity, which set the stage for introducing EVs as "particles universally present in the biological world".
8
老师指出EVs既不是人类独有，也不是哺乳动物独有，植物、真菌、细菌甚至蔬菜和水果都能分泌EVs。 The teacher emphasized that EVs are not unique to humans or mammals, but are also secreted by plants, fungi, bacteria, and even vegetables and fruits.
9
EVs的分泌既可以在细胞静息状态下发生，也可以在受到化学或物理刺激后诱导分泌。 EV secretion can occur in the resting state and can also be induced after chemical or physical stimuli.
10
EVs的发现代表了细胞间多层次交流的新认知，不再仅被认为是细胞废物的搬运工。 The discovery of EVs represents a new understanding of multi-level intercellular communication, and they are no longer seen merely as carriers of cellular waste.

细胞外囊泡（EVs）的分类Extracellular Vesicles (EVs): Classification

1
细胞外囊泡（EVs）根据形成方式和粒径可分为多种亚型。 Extracellular vesicles (EVs) can be classified into various subtypes according to their mode of biogenesis and particle size.
EVs主要分为三大类
外泌体（Exosome）、微泡（Microvesicle，也称Microparticle）、和凋亡小体（Apoptotic body）。 EVs are mainly divided into three groups: exosomes, microvesicles (also called microparticles), and apoptotic bodies.
三类EVs的形成机制不同
外泌体来源于内吞途径的多泡内体；微泡由细胞膜直接出芽而成；凋亡小体由细胞凋亡时膜起泡形成。 The three types of EVs differ in biogenesis: exosomes originate from multivesicular endosomes/endocytic pathway; microvesicles are formed by direct budding from the plasma membrane; apoptotic bodies are produced via membrane blebbing during apoptosis.
4
外泌体粒径范围为30-200纳米，微泡为100-1000纳米，凋亡小体为500-5000纳米。 Exosomes have a size range of 30-200 nm, microvesicles are 100-1000 nm, and apoptotic bodies are 500-5000 nm in diameter.
5
这三类囊泡形态各异，外泌体多为球形，微泡形态不规则，凋亡小体大小和形状变化较大。 The morphology also differs: exosomes are usually spheroid, microvesicles are irregular, and apoptotic bodies are variable in size and shape.
组成方面
三者都含有蛋白、核酸、脂质和代谢物。外泌体特有标志物包括四跨膜蛋白、ESCRT蛋白（如Alix，TSG101）、Rabs和热休克蛋白；微泡常见整合素、选择素、CD40配体、flotilin-2、磷脂酰丝氨酸；凋亡小体典型标志是Annexin-V和磷脂酰丝氨酸。 In terms of composition: all contain proteins, nucleic acids, lipids, and metabolites. Exosome-specific markers include tetraspanins, ESCRT proteins (Alix, TSG101), Rabs, and HSPs; common microvesicle markers are integrins, selectins, CD40 ligand, flotilin-2, phosphatidylserine; apoptotic bodies are characterized by Annexin-V and phosphatidylserine.
7
凋亡小体内部可以包含DNA片段、组蛋白、染色质残片及降解蛋白。 Apoptotic bodies may contain DNA fragments, histones, chromatin remnants, and degraded proteins.
8
EVs整体属于纳米级颗粒，尺寸在30-5000纳米之间。除EVs外，同一尺度内还存在病毒、细菌、血小板等。 Overall, EVs are nanoparticles within 30-5000 nm. In addition to EVs, viruses, bacteria, and platelets also exist in this size range.
9
（补充课堂讲解）老师强调三种类型EVs在疾病研究和生物标志物检测中的区分意义，并提示同学在实验中要注意根据粒径和标志物进行鉴别。

(Supplement from class) The teacher emphasized the importance of distinguishing between the three types of EVs in disease research and biomarker analysis, and suggested that students should carefully identify them by size and marker proteins in experiments.

10
（补充）老师介绍外泌体形成过程包括早期内体、多泡内体，最后通过胞吐作用释放。微泡则是细胞膜直接外化，而凋亡小体是在细胞死亡的过程中产生。

(Additional) The teacher illustrated that exosome formation involves early endosomes, multivesicular bodies, and exocytosis; microvesicles originate via direct budding from the membrane, while apoptotic bodies form during the process of cell death.

9
（课堂补充）老师强调这些分类标准并非绝对，因为EVs在大小和组成上高度异质，实际操作中难以只分离出单一类型。当前领域推荐以来源机制为主，结合尺寸进行分类。 (Lecture supplement) The teacher emphasized that these classification criteria are not absolute, as EVs are highly heterogeneous in size and composition; in practice, it's nearly impossible to isolate a pure single subtype. The current field recommends mainly classifying by biogenesis mechanisms, complemented by size.
10
（课堂补充）举了一个巧克力豆（如M&M、Smarties）作为类比，不同颜色和大小就像EVs的异质性，帮助同学形象了解各种亚型的混杂。 (Lecture supplement) The teacher used a chocolate candy (like M&M or Smarties) analogy: different colors and sizes represent the heterogeneity of EVs, helping students vividly understand subtype mixtures.

细胞外囊泡（EVs）的作用与货物递送Extracellular Vesicles (EVs): Role and Cargo Delivery

1
细胞外囊泡（EVs）在短距离和长距离的细胞间通讯中起关键作用。 Extracellular vesicles (EVs) play a crucial role in short- and long-distance intercellular communication.
2
EVs可以携带多种分子，包括蛋白质、脂质、RNA、DNA和多种调节分子，且通过包裹于膜内部免受降解。 EVs can carry various molecules including proteins, lipids, RNA, DNA, and regulatory molecules, and protect their cargo from degradation by membrane encapsulation.
3
EVs通过影响靶细胞的功能，实现信号分子、遗传信息的转运，是生理和病理过程中新的信号传递机制。 By modulating recipient cell functions, EVs enable the transfer of signaling molecules and genetic information, representing a novel communication mechanism in both physiological and pathological processes.
4
EV的货物递送方式多样，目前研究证实受体细胞可以通过多种途径摄取EVs。 Multiple EV cargo delivery pathways exist, and studies confirm recipient cells can internalize EVs via various uptake mechanisms.
主要的EVs摄取和递送方式包括
The main mechanisms by which EVs are taken up and deliver cargo include:

直接与受体细胞膜表面受体相互作用，激活下游信号通路（直接相互作用）。 Direct interaction with membrane receptors of recipient cells, activating downstream signaling.

囊泡膜与细胞膜融合，使内容物直接释放到胞质（膜融合）。 Fusion of vesicle and cell membranes, releasing contents into the cytosol (membrane fusion).

脂筏介导的胞吞作用。 Lipid raft-mediated endocytosis.

网格蛋白介导的胞吞作用。 Clathrin-mediated endocytosis.

吞噬作用。 Phagocytosis.

巨胞饮作用。 Macropinocytosis.

穴饮作用（caveolin介导的内吞）。 Caveolin-mediated endocytosis.

6
EVs递送内容物后可通过不同方式影响受体细胞：有的直接与膜上受体结合，有的在胞内释放功能性核酸或蛋白，调控基因表达或信号转导。 After EVs deliver their cargos, effects may include binding to cell surface receptors or releasing functionally active nucleic acids/proteins inside the cell, thereby regulating gene expression or signaling pathways.
7
（课堂补充）老师特别强调，目前关于EVs如何、何时、多少货物被递送进受体细胞，仍然是研究的热点和难点，并在PPT上标注“Cargo delivery??”以突出该过程的复杂性和未解问题。 (Lecture addition) The teacher emphasized that the precise mechanisms—how, when, and how much cargo EVs deliver to recipient cells—remain research hotspots and challenges, highlighting this with “Cargo delivery??” on the slide.
8
老师还介绍，不同递送机制的选择可能取决于EVs的来源、膜表蛋白类型及受体细胞状态，因而在不同生理/病理条件下，递送途径及效率可能差异很大。 The teacher also explained that the choice of delivery mechanism may depend on EV origin, membrane proteins, and recipient cell state, leading to great variability in uptake pathway and efficiency under different physiological/pathological conditions.
9
另外，老师用形象比喻帮助理解EV与受体细胞的关系：就像快递包裹，既有投递到门口的，也有直接送进家的，不同“快递方式”对应不同递送机制。 In addition, the teacher used an analogy: EVs are like packages—some are delivered to the doorstep (membrane interaction), some brought inside (endocytosis/fusion); different “delivery methods” correspond to the different uptake mechanisms.

EVs的组成Composition of EVs

1
EVs的组成依赖于其细胞来源、分泌刺激以及生物发生方式。 The composition of EVs depends on their cell origin, secretion stimulus, and mode of biogenesis.
2
细胞来源不同，EVs的蛋白、脂质、核酸等组分会有所差异。 Different cell origins result in different protein, lipid, and nucleic acid contents in EVs.
3
分泌刺激如静息或应激状态也会显著影响EVs的组成。 The secretion stimulus, such as resting or stress state, significantly affects the composition of EVs.
4
EVs的生物发生方式（例如来源于多泡内体or质膜外芽等）也会决定其成分异同。 The biogenesis pathway of EVs (e.g., multivesicular bodies or plasma membrane budding) also determines their compositional differences.
5
现有数据库如ExoCarta、Vesiclepedia和EVpedia整合了各种EVs组分信息，供研究者查询。 Databases such as ExoCarta, Vesiclepedia, and EVpedia compile comprehensive information about EV compositions for research purposes.

EVs的脂质组成Composition of EVs: Lipids

6
EVs具有典型的磷脂双分子层膜结构。 EVs have a typical lipid bilayer membrane structure.
7
EVs膜富含脂筏成分，如胆固醇、鞘氨醇、鞘磷脂、磷脂酰胆碱和磷脂酰丝氨酸。 The membrane of EVs is enriched in elements of lipid rafts: cholesterol, ceramide, sphingomyelin, phosphatidylcholine, and phosphatidylserine.
8
这些脂质参与EVs与受体细胞之间的膜融合，是实现内容物递送的关键。 These lipids participate in membrane fusion between EVs and recipient cells, which is crucial for cargo delivery.

EVs的蛋白组成Composition of EVs: Proteins

9
蛋白质是EVs的主要成分，并表现出细胞起源特征。 Proteins are main components of EVs and display a signature of the cell of origin.
10
不同类型细胞来源的EVs含有特定蛋白标志物：
血小板来源
CD41，CD31，CD42a/b，CD61，CD62P
内皮细胞来源
CD144，CD31，CD34，CD62E，CD51，CD105
淋巴细胞来源
CD4，CD8，CD45，CD3，CD66b
单核细胞来源
CD14
红细胞来源
CD235a The EVs from different cell types contain specific markers:
Platelets
CD41, CD31, CD42a/b, CD61, CD62P
Endothelial cells
CD144, CD31, CD34, CD62E, CD51, CD105
Lymphocytes
CD4, CD8, CD45, CD3, CD66b
Monocytes
CD14
Red blood cells
CD235a
11
外泌体中富含内溶酶体蛋白（如Rab GTPase、SNARE）、四跨膜蛋白和MHC-II。 Exosomes are rich in endosomal proteins (Rab GTPase, SNAREs), tetraspanins, and MHC-II.
12
微粒含有多样的蛋白，包括糖蛋白、整合素和多种受体。 Microparticles contain diverse proteins, including glycoproteins, integrins, and various receptors.
13
EVs中的蛋白质还参与EV的靶向、摄取与信号传递。 Proteins in EVs also participate in EV targeting, uptake, and signaling.

EVs的核酸组成Composition of EVs: Nucleic Acids

14
EVs中存在多种RNA，包括mRNA、miRNA、rRNA、lncRNAs和snRNAs。 EVs contain multiple types of RNA, including mRNA, miRNA, rRNA, lncRNAs, and snRNAs.
15
EVs还可以含有单链DNA、双链DNA以及线粒体DNA。 EVs may also contain single-stranded DNA, double-stranded DNA, and mitochondrial DNA.
16
不同来源细胞分泌的EVs具有特定的核酸特征，例如MSC来源的EVs富含miR-210和miR-126等特定小RNA。 EVs secreted by different cells show specific nucleic acid profiles, e.g., MSC-derived EVs are enriched in miR-210 and miR-126.
17
EVs中的核酸在调控受体细胞信号传导和功能调节中起作用。 Nucleic acids in EVs play roles in regulating signaling and functional modulation in recipient cells.

课堂补充Class Supplement

18
老师补充强调，EVs成分的多样性和异质性很大程度上取决于细胞类型、分泌前细胞所受刺激和生物发生途径。 The teacher highlighted that EV compositional diversity and heterogeneity are largely determined by cell type, secretion stimulus, and biogenesis pathway.
19
目前国际通用数据库能够帮助追踪和对比不同类型、不同条件下EVs的详细组成。 Currently, international databases help track and compare detailed EV compositions under different conditions and cell types.
课堂互动中老师举例
同一种细胞在静息和受到化学应激后分泌的EVs，其内容物可以完全不同。 In class, the teacher gave an example: EVs secreted by the same cell in resting vs. chemically stressed conditions can have completely different cargo.

EVs的临床应用潜力Clinical potential of EVs

EVs的临床潜力主要体现在以下三方面
作为疾病生物标志物、生物治疗剂和治疗分子的纳米载体。 The clinical potential of EVs mainly lies in three aspects: as biomarkers of diseases, biotherapeutics, and nanovectors of therapeutic molecules.
2
EVs可以作为疾病的生物标志物，因为其组成反映了母细胞的状态，包括健康/疾病、静息/激活或压力状态。 EVs can serve as disease biomarkers because their composition reflects the state of the parent cell—including healthy/diseased, resting/activated, or stressed conditions.
EVs的液体活检优势包括
可通过血液、尿液、唾液等体液无创获取，结合其具有细胞来源特异性与多重生物标志物保护特性。 Advantages of EV-based liquid biopsy include non-invasive collection from fluids like blood, urine, or saliva, plus cell-specific origin and protection of multiple biomarkers.
4
EVs可作为生物治疗剂，源于其能够携带生物活性分子（如蛋白、核酸、脂质），调节靶细胞功能并促进再生医学等应用。 EVs can be used as biotherapeutics due to their ability to carry bioactive molecules (proteins, nucleic acids, lipids), modulating target cell function and supporting regenerative medicine applications.
5
EVs被用于作为治疗分子的纳米载体，可以被人工负载药物，实现靶向递送和细胞间的有效传递。 EVs are used as nanovectors for therapeutic molecules, allowing for artificial drug loading and targeted delivery between cells.
6
EVs可在体内通过近端、远端及系统性方式完成信息交流，并能经过血液循环到达特定组织甚至穿越生物屏障。 EVs can mediate proximal, distal, and systemic exchanges, traveling via the bloodstream to target tissues and even crossing biological barriers.
7
EVs在注射入体后部分会被清除器官代谢（如肝、肺、肾），部分可能在免疫系统中失活，但也能顺利到达靶组织并进入靶细胞内发挥作用。 After administration, some EVs are metabolized by clearance organs (liver, lung, kidney) or inactivated by the immune system, but others reach target tissues and internalize into target cells.
8
教师强调，EVs的临床应用研究正在快速发展，涵盖了疾病诊断、预后、个体化治疗等领域，是精准医疗的重要组成部分。 The teacher emphasized that clinical research on EVs is rapidly progressing, covering disease diagnosis, prognosis, and personalized therapy, making EVs an important part of precision medicine.
EVs的临床应用潜力
研究热度持续增长Clinical potential of EVs: Growing interest in EVs
9
从上世纪40年代开始有EVs相关研究，RNA成分在2007年被发现，此后研究数量迅速增长。 Research on EVs began in the 1940s, and the RNA component was identified in 2007, after which the number of studies grew rapidly.

2011年国际EV学会（ISEV）成立，为全球EVs研究提供了平台，并定期发布研究与实验规范推荐。 The International Society for Extracellular Vesicles (ISEV) was founded in 2011, offering a platform for global EV research and regularly publishing guidelines for studies and experimental standards.

11
ISEV于2018年和2023年发布了标准化建议，对EV研究方法、术语及分类等达成专家共识，促进研究标准化和对比。 ISEV issued standardization recommendations in 2018 and 2023, providing expert consensus on methods, terminology, and classification, thus promoting research standardization and comparison.
12
教师补充指出，EVs作为疾病标志物的相关研究在肿瘤、心血管病、代谢病等多领域均显示出广阔前景，近年发表文章数量呈指数级增长。 The teacher added that research on EVs as disease biomarkers shows great promise in cancer, cardiovascular, and metabolic diseases, and the number of related publications has increased exponentially in recent years.

EVs作为疾病潜在生物标志物EVs as Potential Biomarkers of Diseases

1
EVs的水平在不同的生理和病理状态下会发生变化，可作为多种疾病的生物标志物。 EV levels change under various physiological and pathological conditions, making them potential biomarkers for multiple diseases.
2
在健康个体中，吸烟者的EVs（来源于内皮细胞）上调，妊娠期个体的EVs由各种细胞来源整体上升，体育锻炼时内皮细胞来源的EVs下降，而血小板来源的EVs上升。 In healthy individuals, EVs from endothelial cells increase in smokers; overall EVs increase during pregnancy; EVs from endothelial cells decrease and from platelets increase during physical activities.
3
心血管疾病，如高血压、急性冠脉综合征、动脉粥样硬化和静脉血栓形成均表现为内皮细胞和/或血小板来源的EVs上调，动脉粥样硬化还涉及巨噬细胞、红细胞、肌肉等来源。 In cardiovascular diseases such as hypertension, acute coronary syndrome, atherosclerosis, and venous thrombosis, EV levels from endothelial cells and/or platelets are upregulated; atherosclerosis also involves macrophages, red blood cells, and muscle-derived EVs.
4
Scott综合征患者的血小板来源EVs下调。 In Scott syndrome, EVs from platelets are decreased.
5
多种肿瘤（如胃癌、肺癌、结直肠癌、黑色素瘤）患者的相关来源细胞（如血小板、单核细胞、肿瘤细胞等）的EVs均出现上调现象。 In various cancers (such as gastric, lung, colorectal cancer, melanoma), EVs from relevant cells (platelets, monocytes, tumor cells) are increased.
6
感染性疾病如脓毒症、HIV、疟疾等也表现出单核细胞、T淋巴细胞、血小板来源EVs的增加。 Infectious diseases such as sepsis, HIV, and malaria also show increased EVs from monocytes, T lymphocytes, and platelets.
7
老师重点强调，EVs水平受细胞类型及其所处的生理或病理状态影响巨大。例如，同样是内皮细胞，吸烟、妊娠或缺氧等压力条件下分泌的EVs种类和含量都会不同。 The teacher emphasized that both the type of cell and its physiological or pathological state strongly influence EV levels. For example, endothelial cells secrete EVs of different types and quantities under stress conditions like smoking, pregnancy, or hypoxia.
8
在肿瘤疾病诊断中，EVs作为生物标志物的实用性已被证实，表现在能够区分患者与健康人、不同肿瘤分期之间或评估预后等方面。 In cancer diagnosis, the utility of EVs as biomarkers is well established, enabling distinction between patients and healthy individuals, cancer staging, and prognosis assessment.
9
不同肿瘤的EVs可载有特异的蛋白或核酸。这些分子可通过血液、尿液等非侵入性途径检测，对个体化医疗意义重大。 EVs from different tumors can contain specific proteins or nucleic acids, detectable in blood, urine, etc., providing noninvasive means significant for personalized medicine.
10
非肿瘤疾病方面，EVs也能反映免疫、血栓、炎症、组织损伤等病理过程。通过流式细胞术、RT-PCR等方法检测，可以用于疾病预警、早期诊断、进展监测等。 For non-tumoral diseases, EVs reflect immune, thrombotic, inflammatory, and tissue injury conditions. Detection by flow cytometry, RT-PCR, etc., allows for disease warning, early diagnosis, and monitoring.
11
教师补充，在很多非肿瘤疾病如心脑血管病、系统性红斑狼疮、类风湿、妊娠并发症、结核、慢性阻塞性肺病等，多种类型EVs已经用于临床相关的生物标志物检测。 The teacher noted that for many non-tumoral conditions—including cardiovascular diseases, lupus, rheumatoid arthritis, pregnancy complications, tuberculosis, and COPD—various EV types have been used for clinically relevant biomarker testing.
12
EVs可通过液体活检实现获取，避免了传统组织活检的侵入性和局限性，因此具有广阔的应用前景。 EVs can be obtained through liquid biopsy, avoiding the invasiveness and limitations of traditional tissue biopsy, and thus have broad application prospects.

EVs作为疾病标志物来源的优势Advantages of EVs as Source of Disease Biomarkers

1
EVs可通过液体活检获得，实现无创或微创的疾病监测。 EVs are accessible via liquid biopsy, enabling non-invasive or minimally invasive disease monitoring.
2
EVs内部包裹的生物标志物在体内和储存条件下都能被保护，能避免降解或失活。 The biomarkers encapsulated in EVs are protected both in vivo and during storage, preventing degradation or inactivation.
3
EVs携带有细胞特异性的信息，可追溯其来源细胞，有助于精准识别疾病类型或病灶。 EVs carry cell-specific signatures, allowing tracing back to their parent cells and thus supporting precise disease or lesion identification.
4
一个EV中可同时携带多种生物标志物，包括蛋白质、核酸、脂类等，增强检测的信息量和准确性。 A single EV can carry multiple biomarkers, including proteins, nucleic acids, and lipids, increasing the amount and accuracy of detectable information.
5
EVs能够跨越多种生物屏障，如血脑屏障，因此具有在体内远距离信号传递和标志物检测的独特优势。 EVs can cross various biological barriers, such as the blood-brain barrier, giving them unique advantages in long-distance signaling and biomarker detection within the body.
6
相比传统组织活检，液体活检速度更快（通常1~2天即可完成），并且样本采集更全面、更便捷和更安全。 Compared to standard tissue biopsy, liquid biopsy is faster (usually completed in 1-2 days), with more comprehensive, convenient, and safer sampling.
7
老师强调，EVs中的生物标志物在体液中具有较高的稳定性，即使在冷藏或延时检测的情况下也能保持活性，因此可实现灵活的样本处理和分析。 The teacher highlighted that biomarkers inside EVs are highly stable in body fluids, retaining their activity even after refrigeration or delayed analysis, enabling flexible sample handling and testing.
8
教师补充，EVs的液体活检不仅采样方便，还能大大减少患者的痛苦和风险，尤其适用于多次检测和长期随访的临床场景。 The teacher added that EV-based liquid biopsy is not only convenient for sampling but also greatly reduces patient pain and risk, making it particularly suitable for repeated testing and long-term clinical follow-up.

EVs作为疾病标志物来源的优势（二）Advantages of EVs as Source of Disease Biomarkers (II)

9
已经开发出多种方法用于检测和表征EVs中的生物标志物，包括多组学方法（如蛋白质组学、基因组学等）、微流控芯片、质谱、流式细胞术、ELISA、人工智能等高通量平台。 Multiple approaches have been developed for EV biomarker detection and characterization, including omics methods (such as proteomics, genomics), microfluidic chips, mass spectrometry, flow cytometry, ELISA, and artificial intelligence-based high-throughput platforms.
10
EVs存在于几乎所有体液中，如血液、尿液、唾液、泪液、脑脊液、汗液等，因此可以灵活地从不同类型样本中进行检测。 EVs are present in almost all body fluids, including blood, urine, saliva, tears, cerebrospinal fluid, and sweat, allowing flexible testing from various sample types.
11
教师强调，不同检测方法可针对不同的标志物类型（蛋白、RNA、DNA、脂质等）进行灵敏定量或高通量筛查，适用于疾病早筛、分型与监测。 The teacher emphasized that different methods allow sensitive quantification or high-throughput screening of different biomarker types (proteins, RNA, DNA, lipids), facilitating early disease screening, typing, and monitoring.
12
现代检测技术正持续进步，微流控和人工智能等新技术进一步提升了EVs液体活检的自动化水平和分析精度，有助于推动其在临床中的应用普及。 Modern technologies are evolving, and innovations such as microfluidics and AI further increase the automation and analytical accuracy of EV-based liquid biopsy, accelerating its adoption in clinical practice.

EVs的治疗潜力Therapeutic potential of EVs

EVs的治疗潜力可分为三类
天然EVs、来源于修饰细胞的EVs、分泌后再修饰的EVs。 The therapeutic potential of EVs is categorized into three groups: native EVs, EVs from modified cells, and EVs modified after secretion.
2
天然EVs指直接从细胞分泌出来、未经过外源修饰的EVs。 Native EVs are those secreted directly from cells with no additional modifications.
3
来源于修饰细胞的EVs是指先对细胞进行基因或其他操作后再收集其分泌的EVs，这些EVs带有新的功能分子成分。 EVs from modified cells are collected from cells that have been genetically or otherwise engineered, so the EVs contain new functional molecules.
4
分泌后修饰的EVs是指先分离普通EVs，然后再进行修饰，如包载药物或改变表面结构，实现特定治疗功能。 Post-modified EVs are regular EVs isolated first and then modified, for example by drug loading or surface engineering, to achieve specific therapeutic functions.

天然EVs的治疗潜力Therapeutic potential of native EVs

5
天然EVs可来源于多种细胞，如间充质干细胞（MSC）、造血干细胞（HSC）、诱导多能干细胞（iPSC）、巨噬细胞、血小板等。 Native EVs can originate from various cells, including mesenchymal stem cells (MSC), hematopoietic stem cells (HSC), induced pluripotent stem cells (iPSC), macrophages, and platelets.
6
最常用的MSC来源包括骨髓、脂肪组织及血液。 MSCs from bone marrow, adipose tissue, and blood are used most frequently.
7
天然EVs在免疫调节、促进血管生成、组织再生以及抗微生物等方面显示出治疗作用。 Native EVs have shown therapeutic effects in immunomodulation, promotion of angiogenesis, tissue regeneration, and antimicrobial activities.
8
老师强调，EVs的功能性主要由其蛋白质、mRNA和miRNA等活性成分介导。 The teacher emphasized that the functional effects of EVs are mainly mediated by their proteins, mRNA, and miRNA contents.

天然EVs修复与再生功能Repair & regeneration function of native EVs

9
天然EVs已被应用于多种器官和组织类型的修复与再生，如心血管、肝脏、脾脏、肺、神经系统、皮肤和骨组织。 Native EVs have been used in the repair and regeneration of various organs and tissues, including cardiovascular system, liver, spleen, lung, neural system, skin, and bone.
10
EVs的再生活性主要依赖其传递的蛋白、mRNA、miRNA，通过促进细胞增殖、抑制炎症、减弱细胞凋亡等路径实现。 The regenerative activities of EVs mainly rely on transferred proteins, mRNA, and miRNA, which promote cell proliferation, suppress inflammation, and attenuate apoptosis.
11
教师补充，不同来源的EVs可包含各自特有的生物活性成分，如MSC-EVs富含miR-210、miR-126，部分蛋白如VEGF、bFGF、TGF-β，以及脂质和受体分子也参与功能调控。 The teacher added that EVs from different sources contain distinct bioactive components, for example, MSC-EVs are rich in miR-210 and miR-126; proteins such as VEGF, bFGF, TGF-β, as well as lipids and receptor molecules are also involved in therapeutic modulation.

消化道瘘的治疗Treatment of digestive fistula

1
消化道瘘是指消化道与体内其他器官（如皮肤）之间形成异常通道。 Digestive fistula refers to abnormal connections between the digestive tract and another organ in the body (e.g., skin).
2
瘘管通常由感染、炎症、创伤或外科手术引起。 Fistulas usually result from infection, inflammation, trauma, or surgery.
3
常见类型包括肠皮瘘、肛瘘和食管瘘。 Common types include enterocutaneous fistula, anal fistula, and esophageal fistula.

脂肪来源间充质干细胞用于消化道瘘治疗Treatment of digestive fistula: Adipose MSC

4
Alofisel（达伐司他），是一种从脂肪组织提取的人同种异体间充质干细胞产品。 Alofisel (darvadstrocel) is an expanded human allogeneic mesenchymal adult stem cell product extracted from adipose tissue.
5
主要用于治疗合并克罗恩病的复杂性肛瘘成人患者。 It is mainly used for treating complex anal fistulas in adults with Crohn’s disease.
6
该药物在欧洲不予报销，并于2024年12月从欧盟市场撤回，原因是最新研究数据显示其疗效不优于安慰剂。 This drug is not reimbursed in Europe, and was withdrawn from the EU market in Dec. 2024 as recent studies showed its effect is no better than placebo.
7
制备过程包括脂肪抽吸、酶消化、超速离心及细胞培养，并需冻存、最终以局部注射方式施用，每毫米含500万细胞。 The preparation involves liposuction, enzymatic digestion, ultracentrifugation, cell culture, cryopreservation, and is administered via intralesional injection (5 million cells/ml).

脂肪MSC源外泌体（EVs）在消化道瘘治疗中的应用Treatment of digestive fistula: EVs from adipose MSC

8
以脂肪来源MSC分泌的EVs替代细胞疗法用于瘘管治疗，具有新兴潜力。 Using EVs derived from adipose MSC as an alternative to cell therapy for fistula treatment shows promising potential.
9
动物实验（猪模型）中，采用EVs治疗食管瘘。 In porcine models, EVs were used to treat esophageal fistula.
10
采用热响应性凝胶（PF-127）包埋MSC-EVs，可在4℃下注射，体温下实现局部EVs保留，促进瘘管愈合。 A thermoresponsive gel (PF-127) embedded with MSC-EVs was injected at 4°C, ensuring EV retention at body temperature and promoting fistula healing.

脂肪MSC-EVs治疗效果及优势观察Treatment of digestive fistula: EVs of adipose MSC

11
注射方法为低温（4℃）下将含EVs的凝胶通过外瘘口注射。 The gel containing MSC-EVs is injected at 4°C through the external fistula orifice.
12
EVs粒径分布以138 nm为主，平均175±4 nm，特点明显。 The nanoparticles (EVs) had a mode size of 138 nm and a mean size of 175±4 nm, with clear distribution.
13
动物实验显示，处理后外瘘口100%愈合，疗效优于凝胶单用或对照组。 In animal experiments, 100% healing was achieved after treatment with gel plus EVs, outperforming gel alone or control.
14
结果暗示EVs可能成为优于现有细胞疗法（如Alofisel）的新选择。 These results suggest that EVs may represent an advantageous alternative to existing cell therapies such as Alofisel.

老师补充说明Teacher’s supplement

15
教师特别指出，EVs不具备活细胞的免疫原性，也无微血管阻塞和肿瘤形成等细胞疗法相关风险，可标准化、保存和运输，临床应用前景更广阔。 The teacher particularly noted that EVs lack the immunogenicity of live cells and avoid risks such as microvascular occlusion and tumorigenicity associated with cell therapies. They can be standardized, stored, and transported, offering broader clinical potential.

CSM来源外泌体在缺血性心脏病治疗中的作用

CSM-derived EVs
treatment of ischemic heart diseases
1
缺血性心脏病（如心肌梗死）是人类死亡最主要的原因之一，其发病机制主要为动脉阻塞导致心肌组织损伤甚至坏死。 Ischemic heart diseases, such as myocardial infarction, are among the leading causes of human death, mainly due to arterial blockage resulting in damage or death of cardiac muscle tissue.
2
动物实验通常采用大鼠心肌梗死模型，模拟人类心肌缺血环境。 A rat model of myocardial infarction is commonly used in research to mimic the ischemic conditions of the human heart.
3
实验方案为先通过结扎手术诱导大鼠心肌梗死，然后收集来源于间充质干细胞（MSC）的培养液，分离提取外泌体（exosomes）。 The experimental design involves inducing myocardial infarction in rats by ligation, then collecting culture medium from mesenchymal stem cells (MSCs) and isolating exosomes from it.
4
所提取的外泌体与MA-HA水凝胶混合，采用注射方式直接作用于受损心肌区域（心包腔、心外膜或心包）。 The extracted exosomes are mixed with MA-HA hydrogel and injected directly into the damaged myocardial region (pericardial cavity, epicardium, or pericardium).
5
上述过程中，MA-HA水凝胶有助于局部保留和缓慢释放外泌体，提高作用效率。 In this protocol, the MA-HA hydrogel helps retain and gradually release exosomes at the injection site, improving their therapeutic efficacy.
对比实验分为四组
仅心肌梗死（MI）、单独水凝胶（HA-alone）、单独外泌体（Exo-alone）、水凝胶联合外泌体（HA+Exo）。 Four groups are compared: myocardial infarction only (MI), hydrogel alone (HA-alone), exosomes alone (Exo-alone), and hydrogel combined with exosomes (HA+Exo).
7
结果显示，单独使用水凝胶或外泌体均可促进心脏组织修复，但水凝胶与外泌体联合使用效果最佳，显著改善心肌梗死区域壁厚。 The results show that both hydrogel and exosomes alone promote cardiac tissue repair, but the combination achieves the best outcome, significantly improving infarct wall thickness.
8
组织切片染色及定量分析证实，联合治疗组梗死区域壁厚明显大于其他组，且具有统计学显著性（见图中P值比较）。 Histological staining and quantitative analysis confirm that infarct wall thickness in the combined treatment group is significantly greater than other groups, with clear statistical significance (see P values in the figure).
9
老师补充，间充质干细胞来源外泌体能够通过其携带的生物活性分子（如特定miRNA、蛋白），调节心脏局部细胞生存、凋亡与再生，发挥心脏组织修复和保护作用。 The teacher added that MSC-derived exosomes exert cardiac repair and protective effects via their bioactive contents (such as specific miRNAs and proteins), modulating local cell survival, apoptosis, and regeneration.
10
此类外泌体治疗属于细胞外泌体再生医学的前沿探索，可替代活细胞治疗，具有安全性高、免疫原性低、操作可控等优势。 This exosome-based regenerative approach is a cutting-edge alternative to live-cell therapy, offering advantages such as high safety, low immunogenicity, and controlled administration.

经修饰细胞来源外泌体的治疗潜力ITherapeutic potential of EVs from modified cells

及分泌后修饰外泌体的治疗潜力ITherapeutic potential of post-modified EVs

1
经修饰细胞来源EVs是指通过药物处理或基因工程方法，使分泌细胞预先表达、富集或包载治疗分子后，获得具有特定治疗功能的外泌体。 EVs from modified cells refer to vesicles produced by cells that have been treated with drugs or genetically engineered to express, overexpress, or load specific therapeutic agents before EV production.
2
一种常见方式是用抗癌药物处理分泌母细胞，使外泌体内富含药物，主要用于抗肿瘤治疗。 A common approach is treating parental cells with anticancer drugs, which results in drug-enriched EVs, mainly for cancer therapy.
3
另一种方式是对母细胞进行基因转染，例如转染miRNA或治疗用的蛋白编码质粒，使分泌出的EVs携带特定RNA或功能蛋白。 Another method is transfection of parental cells with miRNA or therapeutic protein-coding plasmids, so the secreted EVs carry specific RNAs or functional proteins.
4
分泌后修饰EVs是指外泌体分泌出来后再进行功能化改造，如药物包载或表面修饰，从而扩展其临床应用范围。 Post-modified EVs refer to vesicles that have been further functionalized after secretion, such as by drug loading or surface modification, to broaden their clinical applications.

分泌后修饰外泌体的药物包载与应用Drug loading and application of post-modified EVs

5
分泌后修饰通常通过药物物理或化学手段包载入外泌体，药物性质（如分子大小、亲水/疏水性）和包载方法对效率有显著影响。 Drug loading into post-modified EVs is usually achieved by physical or chemical methods, and the efficiency depends greatly on the drug’s properties (size, hydrophilicity, etc.) and the loading method.
常用包载方法包括
孵育、超声、反复冻融、皂化、转染试剂、电穿孔等，每种方法适用于不同类型的药物分子。 Common loading methods include incubation, sonication, repeated freeze-thaw cycles, saponin treatment, transfection reagents, and electroporation, with each method suitable for different types of agents.
7
电穿孔、皂化处理等常用于高效包载核酸及蛋白等较大分子，但成本较高。 Methods like electroporation and saponin treatment are commonly used for efficient loading of large molecules (nucleic acids, proteins), though they are more costly.
8
孵育属于被动包载方式，操作简单但包载效率较低，适用于小分子药物。 Incubation is a passive loading method, simple to perform but with lower efficiency, mostly for small molecules.

分泌后修饰外泌体的应用特点Application features of post-modified EVs

9
癌症是药物包载EVs研究最活跃的领域，已覆盖多种化疗药物、siRNA、miRNA等递送。 Cancer is the most active field for drug-loaded EVs, with applications encompassing delivery of various chemotherapeutic agents, siRNAs, and miRNAs.
10
EV来源更加多样，既有来源于人、动物细胞，也包括植物、细菌等。 The sources of EVs are diverse, including human/animal cells, as well as plants and bacteria.
11
教师强调，不同包载方式在包载效率及成本上具有明显区别，需要根据药物类型及预期应用合理选择。 The teacher emphasized that the choice of loading strategy depends on both the efficiency and cost, making it important to match the method to the specific drug and intended use.

治疗性外泌体的规模化生产Mass production of therapeutic EVs

1
治疗性外泌体的大规模生产应借鉴生物制品、脂质体和细胞治疗等现有领域的经验。 The mass production of therapeutic EVs should benefit from the existing fields of biologics, liposomes, and cell-based therapies.
2
整个生产过程分为上游工艺和下游工艺。 The entire manufacturing process is divided into upstream and downstream processes.
3
上游工艺主要包括细胞培养和药物装载等步骤。 The upstream process mainly includes cell culture and drug loading.
4
下游工艺包括外泌体的分离纯化、质量控制、工程修饰、成药性制备和储存等步骤。 The downstream process includes isolation/purification of EVs, quality control, EV engineering, formulation, and storage.
5
教师强调，上游工艺所有针对母细胞的准备、培养和修饰操作均属于上游流程，比如在细胞分泌前对其进行基因或药物处理。 The teacher emphasized that all preparations, culture, and modifications of source cells are part of the upstream process, such as genetic or drug treatment before EV secretion.
6
当外泌体产物被收集出来后，相关的纯化、检测、功能修饰、配制和储存等都归为下游工艺。 Once EV products are collected, subsequent procedures—such as purification, characterization, engineering, formulation, and storage—are categorized as downstream processes.
7
这种工艺流程与重组蛋白、抗体及其他生物药的开发制造高度相似，有利于工业化标准运作和质量可控。 This workflow closely resembles the production of recombinant proteins, antibodies, and other biologics, facilitating standardized industrial operations and quality control.

细胞治疗Cell-based therapy

1
细胞治疗是指通过给体内注射活细胞来治疗人类疾病，从而在体内产生治疗作用。 Cell-based therapy refers to the treatment of human diseases by administration of living cells, leading to an in-vivo therapeutic effect.
2
主要用于组织再生、免疫治疗和癌症等领域。 It is mainly applied in regenerative medicine, immunotherapy, and cancer.

细胞治疗的分类Classification of cell-based therapy

按细胞来源分为自体和异体
自体是使用患者自身的细胞，异体为来源于供体的细胞。 According to the origin of the cells, cell-based therapies can be classified as autologous (using the patient's own cells) or allogenic (from a donor).
按分化程度分为两大类
包括干细胞和分化/专职细胞。 Based on differentiation level, there are two main types: stem cells and differentiated/specialized cells.

多能干细胞与多能干细胞 Pluripotent and multipotent stem cells

5
多能干细胞如ESC和iPSC能够分化为体内所有细胞类型。 Pluripotent stem cells, such as ESC (embryonic stem cells) and iPSC (induced pluripotent stem cells), are capable of giving rise to all cell types of the body.
6
组织特异性干细胞和间充质干细胞（MSC）属于更有限的分化类型，如成骨细胞、软骨细胞、心脏细胞等。 Tissue-specific stem cells and mesenchymal stem cells (MSCs) are multipotent and can give rise to specific cell types, such as osteocytes, chondrocytes, cardiomyocytes etc.
7
造血干细胞能够分化为各种血细胞，包括淋巴细胞、红细胞、血小板和各种粒细胞或单核细胞。 Hematopoietic stem cells can differentiate into all types of blood cells including lymphocytes, erythrocytes, thrombocytes, granulocytes, and monocytes.

临床常用细胞类型及作用Clinical cell types and their functions

8
临床上常见的有T细胞、干细胞、树突状细胞、自然杀伤细胞、单核细胞等，对应不同疾病领域的应用。 Clinically, common cell types used include T cells, stem cells, dendritic cells, natural killer cells, monocytes, etc., for diverse therapeutic applications.
9
干细胞具有多种生物学功能，包括免疫调节、促进血管生成、抗细胞凋亡和促进组织再生。 Stem cells possess several biological functions, such as immunomodulation, promoting angiogenesis, anti-apoptosis, and tissue regeneration.

外泌体治疗的优势Advantages of EVs-based therapy

10
外泌体治疗在物流（来源丰富、易储存、稳定性好）、组织穿透/穿越生物屏障、无自身增殖或分化能力、极低的微血管堵塞风险、低免疫原性、易工程化和低成本等方面具有明显优势。 EVs-based therapy offers clear advantages, including logistics (availability, storage, stability), tissue penetration/crossing biological barriers, no risk of self-replication/differentiation, minimal risk of microvasculature occlusion, low immunogenicity, ease of engineering, and reduced cost.

外泌体治疗临床转化面临的挑战Challenges in clinical translation of EV-based therapy

11
临床转化面临诸多挑战，如大规模生产（细胞培养条件、产量、批间一致性）、外泌体分离与纯化、外泌体特性表征、给药途径选择、剂量优化及法规要求等。 Clinical translation faces several challenges, including mass production (cell culture conditions, yield, reproducibility), isolation and purification of EVs, characterization, selection of administration route, dosage optimization, and regulatory frameworks.

092025-10-23

片剂制备与生产 Tablet formulation and manufacturing

1
片剂制备工艺流程 Tablet manufacturing process flow
片剂制备主要包括如下步骤
结晶、粉碎、混合、制粒、干燥、压片、包衣、调剂、崩解。 Tablet formulation and manufacturing mainly involves the following steps: crystallisation, grinding, blending, granulation, drying, tabletting, coating, conditioning, and disintegration.
结晶
得到药物的固体结晶形态。 Crystallisation: Obtaining the drug in a solid crystalline form.
粉碎
将结晶体粉碎为细小颗粒或粉末，改善其后续工艺表现。 Grinding: Crushing the crystals into finer particles or powders to improve processability.
混合
使主药与辅料充分混合，实现成分均匀分布。 Blending: Ensuring the active pharmaceutical ingredient (API) and excipients are thoroughly mixed for uniform composition.
制粒
把不能直接压片的混合粉末通过加液等方式制成颗粒，提高流动性与可压性。 Granulation: Transforming powder mixtures that cannot be directly compressed into granules by adding liquid, enhancing flowability and compressibility.
干燥
对颗粒进行干燥，减少水分以便于后续压片。 Drying: Drying the granules to reduce moisture for better tabletting.
压片
用压片机将颗粒压制成型得到片剂。 Tabletting: Using a tabletting machine to compress granules into tablets.
包衣
对片剂进行包衣，常用于保护药物、掩味、美观或调节释放。 Coating: Coating the tablets, typically for protection, taste masking, appearance, or to modify drug release.
调剂
对成品片剂进行质量调配和分装。 Conditioning: Quality adjustment and packaging of the final tablets.
崩解
片剂在体液或检测液中崩散为小颗粒或粉末，是药物释放的重要前提。 Disintegration: Tablets break down into small aggregates or powder in body fluids or test liquids, essential for drug release.
2
预制工艺研究需明确的参数 Parameters to include in the preformulation study

需要在预制工艺（preformulation）阶段明确并测试以下参数： The following parameters need to be identified and tested during the preformulation stage:

1
颗粒形状和粒径 Particle shape and size
2
晶体性质（多晶型）Crystalline properties (polymorphism)
3
热行为 Thermal behaviour
4
流动性 Flowing properties
5
可压性 Compressibility
6
可压实性 Compactability
7
易碎性 Friability
8
脆性 Fragility
9
磨蚀性 Abrasiveness
10
润湿性 Wettability
11
黏弹性 Viscoelastic properties
3
结合课堂讲解内容 Combined with lecture highlights

老师强调，不同固体制剂工艺步骤目的性强，每一步对最终片剂质量和剂量均一性至关重要。 The teacher emphasized that each step in the solid dosage form process has a distinct purpose and is crucial for the final tablet’s quality and dosage uniformity.

结晶和粉碎决定了API的初始粒径与形态，这将直接影响后续工艺。 Crystallisation and grinding determine the initial particle size and shape of the API, which directly affects subsequent processing steps.

混合过程要保证成分的均匀性，这样每一片剂的含药量才能保持一致，这是药品质量合格的前提。 Blending must ensure homogeneous distribution of components so that each tablet has a consistent drug content, which is foundational for meeting pharmaceutical quality standards.

制粒的主要目的是改善粉末的流动性和可压性，使其适合压片生产。 The main purpose of granulation is to improve the powder’s flow and compressibility, making it suitable for tabletting.

压片时不可忽视粉末的可压性与可压实性，否则可能造成片剂成形不良或机械强度不足。 During tabletting, the compressibility and compactability of powder should not be overlooked, or the tablets may fail to form properly or have insufficient mechanical strength.

崩解和溶出性能直接关系到药物在体内的释放速率，是药效实现的关键环节。 Disintegration and dissolution characteristics directly relate to the drug’s release rate in the body, which is key for therapeutic efficacy.

课上补充
还需特别注意粉末的流动性，否则在灌装胶囊和压片过程中会导致组分分布不均或剂量不准确。 Additional note from the lecture: Special attention must be paid to powder flow properties; otherwise, there can be non-uniform component distribution or inaccurate dosing during capsule filling or tabletting.

预制工艺中的颗粒形状与粒径 Particle shape and size in Preformulation

1
颗粒形状可能宏观和微观上有很大差异。 Particle shape may vary greatly both macroscopically and microscopically.
2
常见药用粉体（如乳糖、磷酸钙、对乙酰氨基酚、茶碱等）在电子显微镜下显示出明显不同的颗粒形态，有的为球形，有的为针状或不规则形态。 Typical pharmaceutical powders (such as lactose, calcium phosphate, acetaminophen, theophylline, etc.) present distinctly different shapes under electron microscopy—some are spherical, some are needle-like or irregular.
3
颗粒的形状和分布状态会影响其流动性、混合均匀性及制剂性能。 The shape and distribution of particles affect their flow properties, blending uniformity, and formulation behavior.
4
以三水合阿莫西林为例，在不同pH条件下结晶可得到不同形貌，如针状或板状。 Amoxicillin trihydrate, for example, can crystallize into needle-like or plate-like forms under different pH conditions.
5
一些常用辅料如羟丙基纤维素、微晶纤维素、羧甲淀粉钠等，也各自展示不同的形状特征，有些流动性或压缩性较好，有些不佳。 Common excipients such as hydroxypropyl cellulose, microcrystalline cellulose, sodium starch glycolate, etc., each show different shape characteristics; some have good flow or compressibility, while others do not.
6
对于非球形颗粒，直接测量其尺寸较为困难。 For non-spherical particles, it is difficult to directly measure their size.
7
引入等效直径与等效球体的概念，用以简化非球形颗粒的表征。 The concept of equivalent diameter and equivalent sphere is introduced to simplify the characterization of non-spherical particles.
8
等效体积直径（dvdv​）：与颗粒体积相等的球体直径，公式 V=(π/6)⋅dv3V=(π/6)⋅dv3​。 Equivalent volumic diameter (dvdv​): Diameter of a sphere with the same volume as the particle. Formula: V=(π/6)⋅dv3V=(π/6)⋅dv3​.
9
等效表面积直径（dsds​）：与颗粒表面积相等的球体直径，公式 S=π⋅ds2S=π⋅ds2​。 Equivalent surface diameter (dsds​): Diameter of a sphere with the same surface area as the particle. Formula: S=π⋅ds2S=π⋅ds2​.
10
表面积-体积比等效直径（dsvdsv​）：具有相同表面积与体积比的球体直径，公式 dsv=ds2/dvdsv​=ds2​/dv​。 Equivalent surface-volume diameter (dsvdsv​): Diameter of a sphere with the same surface-to-volume ratio as the particle. Formula: dsv=ds2/dvdsv​=ds2​/dv​.
11
筛分直径（dSCRdSCR​）：指颗粒能够通过筛孔的最小正方形开口的尺寸。 Screening diameter (dSCRdSCR​): Minimum square opening of the sieve through which the particle can pass.
课堂补充
老师强调了颗粒的各向异性（如针状）使得其物理性质（如流动性）沿不同方向表现不同，需要统计大量样品获得平均参数。 Additional from lecture: The teacher emphasized that particle anisotropy (e.g. needle shape) results in physical properties (such as flowability) behaving differently in different directions, requiring statistics on numerous samples to obtain average parameters.
13
可通过体积、表面积等不同等效直径综合描述非球形颗粒的平均尺寸，便于粉体工程中的比较与计算。 The average size of non-spherical particles can be described by various equivalent diameters (volume, surface area, etc.), which facilitates comparison and calculations in powder engineering.
14
颗粒形状测量常用方法包括光学/电子显微镜观察和图像分析，也可结合筛分、激光粒径分析等多种手段。 Common techniques for measuring particle shape include optical/electron microscopy and image analysis, as well as methods like sieving or laser particle sizing.

预制工艺中的颗粒形状与粒径测量 Particle Shape and Size Measurement in Preformulation

1
颗粒形状与尺寸是预制工艺研究中的基础参数。 Particle shape and size are fundamental parameters in preformulation studies.
2
Feret直径（dFdF​）：指的是与给定方向平行的平面上，能完全包容颗粒图像的最小宽度。 Feret's diameter (dFdF​): The width of the smallest strip of plane, parallel to a given direction, which entirely contains the image of the particle.
3
Martin直径（dMdM​）：指颗粒轮廓投影弦的平均长度。 Martin's diameter (dMdM​): Average length of the chord of the projection of the contour of the particle.
4
对非球形颗粒需要补充形状因子，考虑表面积或体积。 For non-spherical particles, it is necessary to add a shape factor considering surface or volume.
表面积和体积计算公式如下
The formulas for surface area and volume are as follows: S=φsd2S=φs​d2 V=φVd3V=φV​d3
颗粒粒径测量常用方法包括
Common particle size measurement methods include:   （1）光学显微镜（OM）、扫描电镜（SEM）及图像分析，适用于 >0.5 μm>0.5μm 或 <0.5 μm<0.5μm 粒度，可以获得形状信息但统计量不大。 (1) Optical microscopy (OM), scanning electron microscopy (SEM) and image analysis, applicable for >0.5 μm>0.5μm or <0.5 μm<0.5μm sizes, can provide shape information but with low statistics.   （2）筛分法，适用粒径 >40 μm>40μm，不反映形状，只测量大量样品。 (2) Sieving, suitable for >40 μm>40μm particle size, does not reflect shape and requires a large powder quantity.   （3）激光衍射法，适用 0.1∼2000 μm0.1∼2000μm，基于体积百分比，分析快速且可重复。 (3) Laser diffraction, suitable for 0.1∼2000 μm0.1∼2000μm range, uses volume percentage, with fast and repeatable analysis.
7
SEM（扫描电子显微镜）可详细观察颗粒形态，常结合粒径分布图（直方图）分析微观结构。 SEM (scanning electron microscopy) enables detailed visualization of particle morphology and is often used with particle size distribution histograms for analyzing microstructure.
8
频数分布（柱状图）与累积频率分布曲线常用于展示粒径分布特征。 Frequency distribution (histogram) and cumulative frequency distribution curves are commonly used to display particle size distribution characteristics.
课堂补充
Additional from lecture:

颗粒形状和尺寸影响粉体的物理及加工性质，如流动性、可均匀分散性等，因此在药物制剂前需充分测定这两个参数。 Particle shape and size influence the physical and processing properties of powders, such as flowability and dispersibility, so these parameters must be well characterized before formulation.

不同测量方法各有优劣，应根据样品粒径范围和需要的信息类型选用合适方法。 Each measurement method has its own advantages and disadvantages; the appropriate technique should be chosen based on the particle size range and the desired information.

老师强调，对于数量统计不足的方法，可以采用多样本、多视角测量提高准确性。 The instructor emphasized that for methods with low statistics, using multiple samples and perspectives can improve accuracy.

预制工艺中的颗粒形状与粒径测量 Particle Shape and Size Measurement in Preformulation

1
颗粒的形状和尺寸是预制工艺研究中的基础参数。 Particle shape and size are fundamental parameters in preformulation studies.
2
一旦颗粒不是球形，就需要用到不同的等效直径和补充形状因子。 Once particles are non-spherical, different equivalent diameters and shape factors are needed.
3
镜检时可用体积等效直径（颗粒体积与球体体积等同时的直径）、表面积等效直径（颗粒表面积等同球体时的直径）或表面积体积等效直径（表面积与体积比等同球体时的直径）等参数。 Under microscopy, parameters such as equivalent volumetric diameter (diameter of sphere with same volume), equivalent surface diameter (diameter of sphere with same surface area), or equivalent surface-volume diameter (diameter of sphere with same surface to volume ratio) can be used.
4
Feret直径是与给定方向平行的平面上，能完全包容颗粒图像的最小宽度。 Feret’s diameter is the width of the smallest strip of plane, parallel to a given direction, which entirely contains the image of the particle.
5
Martin直径是颗粒轮廓投影弦的平均长度。 Martin’s diameter is the average length of the chord of the projection of the contour of the particle.
6
对于有各向异性的颗粒（如针状晶体、片状等），形状的统计要结合多样本并配合几何学方法分析。 For anisotropic particles (e.g., needle-shaped, plate-like), geometric analysis and statistics over many samples are needed.
7
颗粒形状和尺寸直接影响药物粉体的物理及工艺参数，如流动性和压片过程中的性能。 Particle shape and size directly affect the physical and process parameters of pharmaceutical powders, such as flowability and tableting behavior.
常用粒径与形状测定方法有
Common particle size and shape measurement methods include:

光学显微镜（OM）：适用于大于0.5微米颗粒，能观察形状但数据量有限。 Optical microscopy (OM): suitable for particles >0.5 microns, provides shape info but low statistics.

扫描电镜（SEM）：适合观察小于0.5微米颗粒，分辨率高，能详尽显示外形，但统计量有限。 Scanning electron microscopy (SEM): suitable for <0.5 microns, high resolution and detailed morphology, but low statistics.

筛分法
适于大于40微米颗粒，无法反映形状，需大量样品。 Sieving: suitable for >40 microns, doesn’t give shape, needs large quantity of sample.
激光衍射法
测量范围广（0.1至2000微米），数据量大，分析快速。 Laser diffraction: broad range (0.1 to 2000 microns), large data, fast analysis.
9
颗粒分布常用直方图表示频数分布，累计频率分布曲线辅助分析颗粒分布特征。 Frequency distribution (histogram) and cumulative frequency curves are commonly used to analyze particle size distribution.
课堂补充内容
Additional lecture points:

对于形状各异且分布不均的颗粒，要采取多样本、多视角测量以保证统计学准确性。 For irregular-shaped particles with uneven distribution, multisample and multiperspective measurements are needed for statistical accuracy.

颗粒的形状与尺寸还能影响粉体的分散性、可压性及成片后的质量，制剂开发中需优先考虑。 Particle shape and size influence powder dispersibility, compressibility, and tablet quality, and must be prioritized in formulation development.

不同测量法各有优缺点，应根据粒径范围与需要的信息类型选择合适方法。 Each measurement method has its own pros and cons; appropriate methods should be selected based on particle size range and required info.

表面积 S = φs × d²

体积 V = φV × d³

多晶型实例
对乙酰氨基酚与无水咖啡因 Polymorphism Examples: Acetaminophen and Anhydrous Caffeine
1
多晶型是指同一种物质可以形成多种固态的晶体结构。 Polymorphism refers to the ability of a substance to form multiple solid-state crystal structures.
2
对乙酰氨基酚的多晶型 Polymorphism of Acetaminophen
对乙酰氨基酚有两种晶型
I型（单斜）和II型（斜方）。 Acetaminophen has two crystal forms: Form I (monoclinic) and Form II (orthorhombic).
饱和度的影响
溶液的饱和度会对生成哪种晶型产生影响。 The effect of solution saturation: the saturation of solution affects which polymorph is obtained.

晶型的稳定性不同，存在稳定型和亚稳型。 The crystal forms differ in stability, with stable and metastable forms.

经过30分钟后只剩下I型，但II型更适合用于压片。 Only Form I remains after 30 minutes, but Form II is better for tableting.

II型的可压性较好，便于直接压片，而I型虽然稳定但不易直接压片。 Form II has better compressibility, which is suitable for direct tableting, whereas Form I is stable but difficult for direct compression.

3
无水咖啡因的多晶型 Polymorphism of Anhydrous Caffeine
无水咖啡因有两种晶型
II型（单斜）和I型（六方）。 Anhydrous caffeine has two forms: Form II (monoclinic) and Form I (hexagonal).

这两种晶型转变是可逆的。 The transition between these two forms is reversible.

II型在室温下稳定。 Form II is stable at room temperature.

加热到160℃时，II型会转变为I型。 When heated to 160°C, Form II transforms into Form I.

I型的熔点为235℃。 Form I melts at 235°C.

4
课堂补充讲解 Additional Points from the Lecture

老师强调，不同多晶型的存在和转变会直接影响药物的加工工艺与最终性能。 The instructor emphasized that the presence and transformation of different polymorphs directly affect drug manufacturing processes and performance.

稳定型通常能长期维持而不转变，但亚稳型在外界条件变化（如温度、溶剂变化）下可能发生转化。 Stable forms can remain unchanged for long periods, while metastable forms may convert to stable forms under changes like temperature or solvent.

多晶型还能决定溶解度和生物利用度，有些亚稳型虽然溶解度较高，但不稳定，最终会转变为稳定型。 Polymorphism also determines solubility and bioavailability; some metastable forms have higher solubility but will eventually convert to the stable form.

药物开发时应根据生产方法和使用目标选择合适的晶型，并设计工艺条件以控制晶型发生和转化。 The appropriate polymorph should be selected based on manufacturing and therapeutic needs, with process conditions designed to control polymorphic formation and transformation.

多晶型对固体理化性质的影响及多晶型溶出曲线 Polymorphism: Effect on Solid Physicochemical Properties & Dissolution Profiles

1
多晶型会显著影响固体的理化性质，尤其是溶解度。 Polymorphism significantly affects the physico-chemical properties of solids, especially solubility.
奥斯特瓦尔德法则指出
亚稳型首先结晶，随后转变为稳定型。 The Ostwald rule states: the metastable form crystallizes first and then evolves into the stable form.
3
通常，亚稳型的溶解度比稳定型高，溶出速度也更快。 Usually, metastable forms have higher solubility and faster dissolution rates than stable forms.
4
非晶态物质的溶解度往往高于晶体型物质。 Amorphous materials are generally more soluble than crystalline forms.
5
溶解速率的不同不仅仅影响药物的吸收，还会影响药效和制造工艺选择。 The difference in dissolution rates not only affects drug absorption but also impacts therapeutic effect and manufacturing choices.
讲解中补充
老师强调，药物在使用过程中如果多晶型转化，会造成溶解度和生物利用度的突变，影响药效和安全性。 As added by the lecturer: If polymorphic transformation occurs during drug use, it can cause sudden changes in solubility and bioavailability, affecting efficacy and safety.
7
药品开发时应准确表征多晶型，控制结晶条件，确保产品稳定性。 During drug development, polymorphs must be accurately characterized, and crystallization conditions controlled to ensure product stability.
PPT曲线说明
Explanation of PPT curves:

曲线1代表稳定型，多晶型最稳，溶解度最低，溶出曲线平缓。 Curve 1 represents the stable form, which is the most stable polymorph, has the lowest solubility, and displays a flat dissolution curve.

曲线2代表亚稳型，但在实验期间不会转化为稳定型，因此溶解度更高但保持不变。 Curve 2 represents a metastable form that does not convert into the stable form during the experiment, so it maintains higher solubility.

曲线3代表亚稳型，且在实验期间发生向稳定型的转化，开始时溶解度升高，随后下降趋于稳定型水平。 Curve 3 is for a metastable form that transforms into the stable form during the experiment; initially, solubility increases, then drops to the level of the stable form.

教师补充
亚稳型虽然溶解度高，但实际长期储存或生产过程中可能随着时间和条件转为稳定型，失去初始的高溶解度。 Lecturer’s addition: While metastable forms are more soluble, in real storage or production they may transform into the stable form over time, losing their initial high solubility.

多晶型对溶解和吸收曲线的影响Polymorphism: Effect on Dissolution and Absorption Profiles

1
多晶型会导致同一API不同晶型具有不同的溶解度和溶出曲线。 Polymorphism results in different polymorphs of the same API having distinct solubilities and dissolution profiles.
2
在左侧图中，晶型A和B的溶出曲线对比显示，B型的溶解度高于A型。 In the left graph, comparison of forms A and B shows that B has a higher dissolution than A.
3
溶解度高的多晶型往往能更快、更大量地释放API。 Polymorphs with higher solubility usually release the API more quickly and at higher amounts.
4.右侧图为吸收曲线，说明高溶解度多晶型　的吸收更迅速、峰值更高。 The right graph shows absorption: polymorph B with higher solubility is absorbed more rapidly and reaches a higher peak.
答案 B
5
刚生产的B型片剂吸收峰值最高，浓度-时间曲线最大。 The freshly manufactured tablet B provides the highest absorption peak on the concentration-time curve.
6
但经过6个月储存后，B型片剂的吸收峰值明显下降。 After 6 months of storage, the absorption peak of B-type tablet decreases markedly.
老师讲解
这是因为高溶解度的B型在储存过程中部分转变为较为稳定、但溶解度较低的A型，导致实际吸收效果降低。 Lecture note: This occurs because the higher-solubility form B partly transforms into more stable, less soluble form A during storage, reducing the actual absorption in practice.
8
多晶型的选择和控制直接影响药物产品的疗效和安全性。 The choice and control of polymorphs directly affects the drug’s efficacy and safety.

多晶型的溶出曲线差异及案例解读Polymorphism: Dissolution Profiles and Case Study

9
不同批次、不同国家来源的furosémide片剂溶出曲线存在差异。 Dissolution profiles of furosemide tablets from different batches and countries show differences.
10
上图展示了棒状颗粒的大型团聚物，这类微观结构导致溶解度降低。 The top image shows large agglomerates of rod-like particles, which result in lower solubility.
11
下图颗粒更大，比表面积更低，溶解性更差。 The lower image shows even bigger particles with lower specific surface area and even worse dissolution.
12
溶出曲线显示，颗粒大且团聚的片剂溶解缓慢，可能影响药效。 The dissolution curves reveal that tablets containing large and agglomerated particles dissolve slowly, which may affect drug efficacy.
13
教师强调，必须控制和表征多晶型和颗粒形貌，以保证产品质量一致性。 The lecturer emphasized the need to control and characterize polymorphism and particle morphology to ensure product quality consistency.
14
由于API的多晶型和颗粒结构不同，不同生产厂家和批次的药品实际疗效可能波动，甚至存在生物利用度不足的风险。 Due to different polymorphs and particle structures, actual effectiveness of the same drug may vary by manufacturer or batch, with risk of insufficient bioavailability.

多晶型对片剂溶出与吸收性能的影响Impact of Polymorphism on Tablet Dissolution and Absorption

1
多晶型差异会导致API不同溶出曲线，从而影响药物释放速率。 Differences in polymorphism lead to varying API dissolution profiles, thus affecting drug release rates.
2
左图展示了同一API的A型和B型两种多晶型片剂的溶出曲线，B型溶解度更高，释放更快。 The left graph shows dissolution profiles of Form A and Form B tablets of the same API; Form B dissolves faster and reaches a higher concentration.
3
药物溶出速度的提高通常意味着药物吸收更快，人体内达到更高峰值。 Improved dissolution rate generally means faster drug absorption and higher peak concentrations in the body.
4
右图为吸收曲线，说明刚生产时B型片剂在体内吸收峰值高于A型。 The right figure displays the absorption curves, showing that freshly manufactured B-type tablets achieve a higher absorption peak than A-type.
5
但是，B型片剂经过6个月储存，吸收峰值明显下降，低于初始值。 However, after 6 months of storage, the absorption peak of B-type tablets significantly declines, falling below the original value.
课堂补充
老师强调，这种下降是因为储存过程中，部分高溶解度的B型逐渐转化为较为稳定但溶解度低的A型，导致实际疗效下降。 Additional lecture note: The lecturer highlighted that the decrease is due to the partial transformation of high-solubility Form B into more stable, but less soluble, Form A during storage, resulting in reduced real efficacy.
7
多晶型的选择、控制和稳定性管理是药物开发和工业生产中的关键环节。 The selection, control, and stability management of polymorphs are critical steps in drug development and manufacturing.

多晶型对不同批次药品质量一致性的影响Polymorphism and Batch-to-Batch Consistency of Drug Quality

8
不同批次、不同国家来源的呋塞米片剂溶出曲线可能差异显著，表现出质量波动。 Dissolution profiles of furosemide tablets from different batches and countries may differ significantly, reflecting quality variations.
9
上方显微图片显示大量棒状颗粒团聚，这种微观结构会降低溶解度。 The upper micrograph shows large agglomerates of rod-like particles, and these microstructures lower solubility.
10
下方图片颗粒更大，比表面积更低，溶解性进一步下降。 The lower image contains even larger particles with lower specific surface area, resulting in even worse dissolution.
11
溶出曲线显示，这些团聚或粒径大的片剂溶解速度慢，API释放受限，可能影响疗效。 The dissolution curves indicate that tablets with agglomerated or larger particles dissolve slowly, limiting API release and potentially affecting efficacy.
教师补充
API的多晶型及颗粒结构不受控，导致不同厂家或批次之间药品溶解、生物利用度及临床效果波动甚至不可控。 Lecturer’s addition: Uncontrolled polymorphism and particle structure of the API can cause unpredictable variations in dissolution, bioavailability, and clinical outcomes across manufacturers or batches.
13
因此，药品开发和生产需规范表征、严格过程控制，以保证成品一致性与患者用药安全。 Therefore, thorough characterization and strict process control during drug development and manufacturing are necessary to ensure product consistency and patient safety.

多晶型对药物工艺过程的影响Polymorphism: Impact on Pharmaceutical Processes

1
多晶型的存在会影响药物制备工艺，如微球的生产和性能。 The existence of polymorphism affects pharmaceutical manufacturing processes, such as the production and properties of microspheres.
2
以α-黄体酮为例，在不同含量（A:10%，B:28%，C:35%，D:50%）掺杂于聚乳酸-羟基乙酸共聚物（85:15）中，可制备不同结构的微球。 Taking α-progesterone as an example, different proportions (A:10%, B:28%, C:35%, D:50%) compounded in poly(lactic-co-glycolic acid, 85:15) yield microspheres with various structures.
3
不同比例API会影响微球的形貌和包载状态，如显微镜照片A-D所示。 Varying API ratios affect the morphology and drug encapsulation status, as illustrated by micrographs A-D.
4
右侧示意热分析（如DSC）结果，显示随API含量变化，出现不同的熔点（Tm）和玻璃转变温度（Tg）。 The right side shows thermograms (such as DSC) indicating that as API proportion changes, different melting points (Tm) and glass transition temperatures (Tg) appear.
5
不同多晶型对应不同的吸热（熔融）峰，如α型和β型熔点不同，表明多晶型对药物在载体中的物理状态有直接影响。 Different polymorphs give distinct endothermic (melting) peaks, such as different Tm for α and β forms, showing polymorphism directly affects the physical state of the drug in the carrier.
6
随API含量提高，微球中出现β型结晶峰，且α型峰逐渐弱化甚至消失，说明晶型的演变受配比控制。 When API content increases, a β-form crystalline peak appears in the microspheres, while the α-form peak weakens or disappears, indicating polymorph evolution is composition-dependent.
教师补充
制备和存储条件（如温度、溶剂、干燥过程）都可能导致多晶型转换，从而改变药物释放行为或稳定性。 Lecture addition: Manufacturing and storage conditions (such as temperature, solvent, drying) can trigger polymorph interconversion, altering drug release or stability.

多晶型的表征方法Characterization of Polymorphs

8
多晶型的识别需通过多种手段表征，包括粒径、熔点和晶格参数。 Identification of polymorphism requires multiple characterization methods, including particle size, melting point, and crystal lattice parameters.
9
粒子形貌可用扫描电子显微镜（SEM）精确观察。 Particle morphology can be precisely examined by scanning electron microscopy (SEM).
10
熔点和玻璃化转变温度分析常用差示扫描量热法（DSC）和热显微镜法。 Melting point and glass transition analysis commonly use differential scanning calorimetry (DSC) and thermomicroscopy.
11
晶体结构参数常通过X射线衍射（XR）测定。 Crystal lattice parameters are usually determined by x-ray diffraction (XR).
教师强调
只有结合多种分析手段，才能全面评估药物的多晶型情况，确保药品质量与工艺稳定。 Lecturer emphasized: Only by combining various analytical methods can the polymorphic status be comprehensively evaluated, ensuring drug product quality and process robustness.

片剂制备与生产工艺Tablet Formulation and Manufacturing

片剂制备需满足以下三个基本条件
Tablet manufacturing must meet the following three basic conditions:
2
首先，必须得到均匀的混合物，保证每个单位片剂药物剂量的均匀性。 Firstly, a homogeneous mix is needed to ensure dosage uniformity in each tablet unit.
3
其次，混合物还需具备良好的流动性，保证每个单位片剂质量的一致性。 Secondly, the mixture should be fluid to maintain uniformity of mass per unit dose.
4
通过造粒，能进一步改善这两种属性，使物料便于后续压片。 Granulation further improves these two properties, making the powder suitable for tableting.
5
最后，混合物要适合工业规模的压片，包括具备良好的可压缩性（在机械压力下体积可减小、空气可移除）、以及可压性（颗粒间有足够内聚力形成坚固片剂）。 Finally, the mix must be suitable for industrial-scale tableting, with good compressibility (volume reduction and air removal under pressure), and compactability (adequate cohesion between particles to form a strong tablet).
6
可压性体现为粉末经过压制后形成具有机械强度的固体片剂。 Compactability refers to the ability of compressed powder to form a solid tablet with mechanical strength.

预配制中的流动性属性Flowing Properties in Preformulation

7
药粉或颗粒的流动性对混合、造粒、压片过程有直接影响。 The flowability of powders or granules directly affects mixing, granulation, and tableting processes.
8
流动性能主要受颗粒形状影响，形状越规则、越接近球形，流动性通常越好。 Flowing properties mainly depend on particle shape; more regular or spherical particles generally flow better.
9
流动性可通过多种方法检测，包括直接法如Jenike cell（测量颗粒间内聚力）， Flowability can be measured by various methods, such as direct methods like Jenike cell (which measures cohesion between particles),
10
也有间接法，比如休止角、Hosokawa粉末测试仪（Carr指数）、可压缩性测试（Hausner比）。 And indirect methods such as angle of repose, Hosokawa powder tester (Carr’s index), and compressibility testing (Hausner ratio).
11
休止角越小，粉末流动性越好；Carr指数和Hausner比越低表明流动性能越佳。 A smaller angle of repose indicates better flow; lower Carr’s index and Hausner ratio also imply better flowability.
12
流动性不足可能导致混合不均、制粒困难和压片重量/含量差异。 Poor flow can lead to uneven mixing, granulation difficulties, and variations in tablet weight/content.

>

流动性与可压缩性间接表征方法Flowing and Compressibility: Indirect Characterization Methods

1
粉体的流动性可通过间接法评价，其中休止角法是常用方法之一。 The flowability of powders can be assessed by indirect methods; the angle of repose is a commonly used approach.
2
休止角指粉末自高处落下形成堆体时，其堆顶与水平方向间的夹角。 The angle of repose refers to the angle between the horizontal plane and the slope of the pile formed by freely falling powder.
正确的休止角计算公式为
The correct formula for angle of repose is:
5
其中 h 为堆高，r 为底部半径。 Here, hh is the height of the heap and rr is the radius of the base.
6
休止角越小，粉体流动性越好。一般评价标准如下：25-30°为极佳，31-35°为良好，36-40°为一般，41-45°为可用但易卡住，45°以上则流动性差。 The smaller the angle, the better the flow. Typical evaluation: 25-30° excellent, 31-35° good, 36-40° fair, 41-45° passable (may hang up), above 45° poor flow.
老师补充
药厂实际常采用漏斗法和休止角测定作为快速筛查工具，如果休止角过大，则需通过配方改进或造粒等方式提升流动性。 Additional: In practice, the funnel and angle-of-repose methods are commonly used as quick screening tools; if poor flow is observed (high angle), formulation adjustments or granulation may be needed to improve flow.
8
可压缩性评价基于体积变化，常用Hosokawa粉体测试仪计算卡氏指数（Carr's index）和豪斯纳比（Hausner ratio）。 Compressibility is evaluated based on powder volume changes, using the Hosokawa powder tester to calculate Carr’s Index and Hausner ratio.
测定方法为
The method is as follows:

首先称取一定质量粉末，测量自然堆积体积 V0V0​，得松装密度： First, weigh a fixed powder mass, measure its poured (bulk/unsettled) volume V0V0​, get bulk density:

反复机械拍打至体积恒定后测量终体积 VfinalVfinal​，得振实密度： Tap repeatedly until volume is constant, then measure tapped (packed) volume VfinalVfinal​ for tapped density:

计算豪斯纳比（Hausner ratio）： Calculate Hausner ratio: Hausner ratio=ρtappedρpouredHausner ratio=ρpoured​ρtapped​​

计算卡氏指数（Carr's index）： Calculate Carr's Index:
