Hausner比小于1.25通常表示流动性好，大于1.25表示流动性差；Carr's指数越小表示流动性越好（例如5-10为极佳，>23为差）。 A Hausner ratio less than 1.25 indicates good flowability, above 1.25 indicates poor flow; a lower Carr's Index means better flow (5-10 excellent, >23 poor).

课堂补充
老师提到休止角、Carr's指数与Hausner比只是初步定量指标，不能替代最终工艺测试。部分API或辅料需结合造粒、配伍等工艺优化。 Lecture addition: The teacher emphasized that these indices are preliminary quantitative indicators and cannot replace actual process testing; some APIs or excipients may require granulation or excipient adjustments for optimization.

紧密性与润湿模式在预配制中的作用 Role of Compactability and Wetting Modes in Preformulation

1
紧密性（可压性）是指粉末能否在压力作用下形成具有足够机械强度的片剂。 Compactability refers to the ability of a powder to form tablets with sufficient mechanical strength under pressure.
2
研究可压性时，需要用带有测力装置的压片机，通过测量上冲与下冲施加的压力及移动距离，记录粉末的压缩与回弹过程。 To study compactability, an instrumented tablet press is needed, measuring the force applied by the punches and their movement, and recording the compression and relaxation of the powder.
3
粉体压缩过程中，压头首先对粉末颗粒进行重新排列（A到B），然后压缩颗粒（B到C），最后释放压力（C到D），部分能量由于摩擦耗散。 During compression, the upper punch first rearranges the powder particles (A to B), then compresses them (B to C), and finally releases the pressure (C to D), with some energy dissipated due to friction.
4.其中，曲线下A到B到C的区域表示净压缩功ABC，BC段为弹性回弹功，二者相减可知材料因摩擦损失的能量。 The area under curve from A to C 　 represents net compression work, BC segment is the elastic relaxation work, and their difference shows the energy lost to friction.
答案 ABC
老师补充
评估可压性最终要通过实际压片，观察压片成品是否能够保持完整。如果压片后片剂易碎、易裂，则表明可压性不足。 Teacher’s note: Ultimately, compactability must be tested by making real tablets and checking whether they remain intact. If tablets are fragile or cracked, compactability is insufficient.
6
实验室可以用小型压片机做初步测试，大型生产则用工业级压片机。 Lab-scale tablet presses are used for preliminary tests, while industrial presses are used for large-scale production.
7
润湿模式描述液体如何与固体表面相互作用，影响片剂的崩解和溶出。 Wetting modes describe how a liquid interacts with a solid surface, influencing tablet disintegration and dissolution.
常见润湿模式包括
铺展（液滴在固体表面铺开）、浸润（液体部分或全部包围固体）、冷凝（气态水或其他液体在固体表面凝结）、毛细上升（液体沿毛细孔上升）。 Common wetting modes include: spreading (droplet spreads on solid), immersion (liquid envelops solid), condensation (vapor condenses on surface), and capillary rise (liquid rises in capillaries).
9
润湿行为受液体和固体的极性或非极性影响，极性溶液易于润湿极性表面，非极性则润湿非极性表面。 Wetting is influenced by the polarity of the liquid and solid; polar liquids wet polar surfaces, nonpolar liquids wet nonpolar surfaces.
10
滋润性直接影响片剂接触水后能否及时崩解、释放API。片剂表面润湿性差，会导致溶出速率下降。 Wettability directly affects whether a tablet can disintegrate and release API in contact with water. Poor wettability leads to slowed dissolution.
教师补充
药片进入体内首先与水接触，润湿和崩解是药物释放的关键步骤。常用评估方法有接触角测量与Washburn毛细吸液法。 Teacher’s note: Tablets first contact water in the body; wetting and disintegration are key for drug release. Contact angle measurement and the Washburn method are commonly used to assess wettability.

固体制剂的配制Formulation of solid pharmaceuticals

片剂制备与生产工艺Tablet formulation and manufacturing

1
药用辅料（Excipients for tabletting）是片剂制备中必不可少的组分，不同种类辅料各有其作用。 Excipients for tabletting are essential components in tablet formulation, each category serving a specific function.
2
填充剂（Fillers，0-95%）：作用是填充体积，使片剂达到合适大小，同时应惰性、可压、非吸湿，可溶或不可溶于水。 Fillers (0-95%) are used to increase the bulk and reach a reasonable tablet size, and should be inert, compactable, nonhygroscopic, and water-soluble or insoluble.
3
常见填充剂包括淀粉、乳糖、蔗糖、葡萄糖、甘露醇、山梨醇、磷酸钙、碳酸钙、纤维素衍生物等。 Common fillers are starch, lactose, sucrose, glucose, mannitol, sorbitol, calcium phosphate, calcium carbonate, cellulose derivatives.
4
粘合剂（Binders，3-5%，淀粉可高达15-20%）：用于增强片剂内部结构的结合力，保证压片成型。 Binders (3-5%, up to 15-20% for starch) strengthen the cohesion within tablets and ensure successful tableting.
5
湿法制粒用的粘合剂有纤维素衍生物、聚乙烯吡咯烷酮（PVP）、预糊化淀粉（干或溶液形式）；干法制粒常用聚乙二醇（PEG）、泊洛沙姆等。 In wet granulation, binders include cellulose derivatives, PVP, pregelatinized starch (dry or in solution); in dry granulation, polyethylene glycol (PEG) and poloxamer are common.
6
润滑剂（Lubricants，0.5-3%）：减少颗粒及与模具接触时摩擦，便于脱模。 Lubricants (0.5-3%) reduce friction between particles and tablet tools, aiding in ejection.
7
常用润滑剂包括硬脂酸镁、硬脂酸、富马酸钠（PRUV®）。 Typical lubricants are magnesium stearate, stearic acid, sodium stearyl fumarate (PRUV®).
老师补充
润滑剂加得太多会降低片剂可压性，延缓崩解和溶出速率，因此一般建议添加量应控制在极低比例。 Teacher's note: Excess lubricant will reduce compactability and prolong disintegration and dissolution, so amounts should be minimized.
9
助流剂（Glidant，0.1-0.25%）：改善粉末流动性，多采用滑石粉或胶体二氧化硅。 Glidants (0.1-0.25%) improve powder flow, usually talc or colloidal silica is used.
10
崩解剂（Disintegrants，3-5%）：加速片剂遇水崩解，常用的有羧甲基淀粉钠、羧甲基纤维素钠（CMC）、淀粉等。 Disintegrants (3-5%) speed up tablet disintegration in water, such as sodium starch glycolate, sodium carboxymethylcellulose (CMC), and starch.
老师补充
崩解剂加量过多会形成凝胶层，反而降低API释放速率，因此需适量添加。 Teacher's note: Excess disintegrant may form a gel, slowing down API release rate, so only optimal quantity should be used.
辅料的加入顺序一般是
先加入填充剂、助流剂（如需要），再加入粘合剂和崩解剂（缓释片除外），最后加入润滑剂（一定放最后）。 The order of excipient addition is typically: filler, glidant (if needed), then binder and disintegrant (not in sustained release tablets), and lubricant at the very end.
老师补充
润滑剂必须最后加入，过早混合会包裹颗粒表面影响压片和其它辅料作用；每步搅拌要保证均匀。 Teacher's note: Lubricants must be added last, as early mixing may coat particle surfaces, hindering tablet formation and function of other excipients; every mixing step must ensure uniformity.

102025-10-30

1
The plasma donation system in France is organized and overseen by EFS. 法国的血浆捐赠体系由EFS（法国血液机构）组织和监管。
2
The EFS manages the blood donation operations and ensures the distribution of plasma drugs primarily to French healthcare facilities. EFS负责血液捐赠的运营，并优先向法国医疗机构配送血浆药物。
3
Hospitals in France can purchase plasma-derived drugs, and the price is regulated by French authorities, regardless of whether the drug is of local or foreign origin. 法国医院可以购买血浆药物，无论这些药物是本地还是进口的，价格都由法国政府监管。
4
EFS supplies plasma to hospitals and also sells plasma for fractionation to pharmaceutical companies like LFB. EFS向医院供应血浆，同时也向制药公司（如LFB）出售用于分离的血浆。
5
Hospitals are allowed to purchase plasma-derived medicinal products from outside France, creating a competitive market. 医院有权从法国以外采购血浆药物，这形成了一个竞争性的市场。
6
Plasma is the liquid component of blood, making up about 55% of total blood volume. 血浆是血液中的液体成分，大约占据血液总体积的55%。
7
The rest of the blood consists of white blood cells (about 1%) and red blood cells (about 45%). 血液的其余部分由白细胞（约占1%）和红细胞（约占45%）组成。
8
EFS separates white blood cells, red blood cells, platelets, and plasma to be used for different therapeutic purposes. EFS会分离白细胞、红细胞、血小板和血浆，用于不同的医疗用途。
9
Plasma contains about 90% water, with salts, lipids, hormones, proteins, and coagulation factors. 血浆约含有90%的水分，还包括盐类、脂质、激素、蛋白质和凝血因子。
10
Key plasma proteins include albumin (for transport), immunoglobulins/IgG (IVIG, for immunity), and fibrinogen (for coagulation). 主要的血浆蛋白包括白蛋白（用于运输）、免疫球蛋白（如IgG，用于免疫）、和纤维蛋白原（用于凝血）。
11
Plasma plays roles in nutrient transport, water regulation, tissue irrigation, immunity, and blood coagulation. 血浆的功能包括营养运输、水分调节、组织灌注、免疫防御和血液凝固。
12
To manufacture plasma-derived drugs, the first major step is cryoprecipitation. 生产血浆药物的第一大步骤是冷下析。
13
Donated plasma is quarantined for 35 days for viral screening before processing. 捐赠的血浆会隔离35天，用于病毒筛查再进行处理。
14
Plasma is pooled in large quantities (up to 4,000 liters), then cooled, causing some proteins to precipitate. 血浆会被大批量混合（多达4,000升），然后冷却，导致部分蛋白质沉淀。
15
Centrifugation separates the solid cryoprecipitate (rich in factor VIII) from the liquid component. 通过离心将富含凝血因子Ⅷ的固体冷下析物与液体成分分离。
16
This process yields medical products like albumin, immunoglobulins, and fibrinogen for therapeutic use. 该过程可产生如白蛋白、免疫球蛋白和纤维蛋白原等治疗用途的药物。
1
The main coagulation factor extracted from plasma for drug manufacturing is Factor VIII, which is crucial for treating hemophilia. 从血浆中提取的主要凝血因子是第八因子（Factor VIII），它对治疗血友病非常重要。
2
During processing, plasma proteins are separated by cooling; for instance, IgG stays in the solution while Factor VIII accumulates in the precipitate. 在处理过程中，通过降温使血浆蛋白分离，例如，IgG留在溶液中，而第八因子则沉淀出来。
3
The pharmaceutical industry uses standard purification techniques such as precipitation, extraction, and chromatography to purify plasma proteins. 制药行业使用标准的纯化技术，例如沉淀、萃取和色谱法来纯化血浆蛋白。
4
Most plasma-derived medicinal products are lyophilized (freeze-dried) to ensure stability and shelf life of 2–3 years. 大多数血浆药物被冻干处理，以保证稳定性，能保存2到3年。
5
Different plasma-derived drugs are made for distinct diseases, such as hemophilia, hepatitis B, and tetanus; immunoglobulin concentrates can be injected for passive immunity. 不同的血浆药物用于治疗不同疾病，如血友病、乙肝和破伤风；免疫球蛋白浓缩物可注射以提供被动免疫。
6
The main plasma proteins separated are albumin (about 60% of plasma proteins), immunoglobulins (IVIG), and fibrinogen. 主要分离的血浆蛋白包括白蛋白（占血浆蛋白约60%）、免疫球蛋白（IVIG）和纤维蛋白原。
7
Gel electrophoresis can separate plasma proteins by size, helping to identify and purify specific proteins such as immunoglobulins and fibrinogen. 凝胶电泳可按大小分离血浆蛋白，有助于鉴定和纯化特定蛋白，如免疫球蛋白和纤维蛋白原。
8
Safety concerns in the 1980s and 1990s, such as HIV and BSE crises, led to improved viral and prion inactivation steps in plasma product manufacturing. 20世纪80至90年代的安全事件（如艾滋病毒和疯牛病危机）推动了血浆产品生产中病毒和朊病毒灭活步骤的改进。
9
The solvent/detergent treatment is now widely used to inactivate envelope viruses in plasma products, improving patient safety. 如今，溶剂/去污剂处理被广泛用于灭活血浆产品中的包膜病毒，提高了患者安全性。
10
Analytical techniques identify the purity and composition of each plasma fraction, ensuring targeted medicines are produced. 分析技术用于鉴定每个血浆组分的纯度和成分，从而保证生产针对性的药物。
1
The main coagulation factor extracted from plasma is Factor VIII, which is essential for producing hemophilia medications. 从血浆中提取的主要凝血因子是第八因子（Factor VIII），它对生产治疗血友病的药物至关重要。
2
When plasma is processed at low temperature, proteins like IgG remain dissolved, whereas Factor VIII becomes enriched in the precipitate. 当血浆在低温下加工时，IgG这类蛋白质保持溶解，而第八因子则富集在沉淀中。
3
Standard pharmaceutical purification techniques such as precipitation, extraction, and chromatography further isolate and purify the desired plasma proteins for medical use. 制药工业采用沉淀、萃取和色谱等标准纯化技术，进一步分离并纯化目标血浆蛋白用于医疗用途。
4
Most plasma-derived medicines are processed into a lyophilized (freeze-dried) state to ensure stability for 2–3 years. 大多数血浆药物通过冻干处理，以确保其在2到3年内的稳定性。
5
Different purified plasma-derived drugs target specific diseases, like hemophilia, hepatitis B, or tetanus; immunoglobulin concentrates are used for passive immunity. 不同的纯化血浆药物用于特定疾病，如血友病、乙肝或破伤风；免疫球蛋白浓缩物用于被动免疫。
6
The three major plasma proteins separated are albumin (about 60% of plasma proteins), immunoglobulins (IVIG), and fibrinogen. 分离的三类主要血浆蛋白是白蛋白（约占血浆蛋白总量60%）、免疫球蛋白（IVIG）和纤维蛋白原。
7
Gel electrophoresis is used to separate plasma proteins by size, assisting in the identification and purification of immunoglobulins and fibrinogen. 凝胶电泳用于按分子大小分离血浆蛋白，有助于鉴定和纯化免疫球蛋白及纤维蛋白原。
8
In the 1980s and 1990s, severe contamination incidents (like HIV and BSE) led to strengthened viral and prion inactivation measures in plasma product manufacture. 20世纪80和90年代，严重的污染事件（如艾滋病毒和疯牛病）促使血浆制品生产中加强了病毒和朊病毒灭活措施。
9
The solvent/detergent method was introduced to inactivate enveloped viruses in plasma products, significantly improving safety. 溶剂/去污剂法被引入以灭活血浆制品中的包膜病毒，大大提高了产品安全性。
10
However, the solvent/detergent treatment is not effective against certain non-enveloped viruses like hepatitis B and C. 然而，溶剂/去污剂处理对一些无包膜病毒（如乙肝和丙肝病毒）无效。
11
Analytical methods, like gel separation and protein sizing, ensure the identity and purity of each plasma fraction, confirming specific medicines are produced. 凝胶分离和蛋白测定等分析方法确保各血浆组分的身份和纯度，从而验证所生产的是特定药物。
1
Factor VIII is the primary coagulation factor extracted from plasma to manufacture hemophilia drugs. 第八因子（Factor VIII）是从血浆中提取的主要凝血因子，用于生产治疗血友病的药物。 1
2
By cooling plasma, proteins such as IgG remain in solution, while Factor VIII concentrates in the precipitate, enabling separation. 通过冷却血浆，IgG等蛋白质保持溶解状态，而第八因子则集中在沉淀中，从而实现分离。 1
3
Standard pharmaceutical purification methods—precipitation, extraction, chromatography—are applied to obtain injectable medicinal products. 采用沉淀、萃取、色谱等制药行业标准纯化方法以获得可注射的药品。 1
4
Most plasma-derived drugs are formulated as lyophilized (freeze-dried) products to ensure a shelf life of 2-3 years without loss of quality. 大多数血浆药品被制成冻干制剂，以确保能在2到3年内保持质量稳定。 1
5
Each type of plasma-derived drug targets a different disease; for example, hemophilia, liver disease, hepatitis B, and tetanus. 每种血浆药物针对不同疾病，例如血友病、肝脏疾病、乙肝和破伤风。 1
6
For people with antibody deficiencies, concentrated immunoglobulin (IVIG) solutions can be injected to provide passive immunity. 对于抗体缺乏者，可注射浓缩免疫球蛋白（IVIG）溶液以提供被动免疫。 1
7
Plasma fractionation and protein migration techniques (such as gel electrophoresis) are used to separate and identify plasma proteins like albumin, immunoglobulin, and fibrinogen by size. 通过血浆分离和蛋白迁移技术（如凝胶电泳），可按照蛋白质大小分离并鉴定出白蛋白、免疫球蛋白和纤维蛋白原等血浆蛋白。 1
8
Albumin constitutes about 60% of total plasma proteins; immunoglobulin and fibrinogen are the next most abundant major plasma proteins. 白蛋白约占血浆蛋白总量的60%；其次是免疫球蛋白和纤维蛋白原。 1
9
Historical safety crises, such as HIV contamination in the 1980s and BSE ("mad cow disease") in the 1990s, led to strengthened virus and prion inactivation steps in plasma drug production. 历史上的安全危机，如20世纪80年代的HIV污染事件和90年代的疯牛病危机，促使血浆药物生产中加强了病毒和朊病毒灭活步骤。 1
10
The introduction of solvent/detergent treatments effectively inactivates enveloped viruses in plasma products, although it is ineffective against non-enveloped viruses like hepatitis B and C. 溶剂/去污剂处理方法能高效灭活血浆制品中的有包膜病毒，但对无包膜病毒（如乙肝和丙肝）无效。 1
11
Analytical techniques ensure the quality, purity, and correct identification of each plasma fraction in the production process. 分析技术确保生产过程中各血浆组分的质量、纯度和正确鉴定。 1
12
European regulations now require all plasma processing to include validated steps for the inactivation or removal of viruses and prions to ensure patient safety. 欧洲法规现要求所有血浆加工过程必须包括经过验证的病毒和朊病毒灭活或去除步骤，以保障患者安全。 2
1
免疫球蛋白（Ig）种类及结构 There are five different types of immunoglobulins (Ig), with IgG accounting for about 85% of the total in plasma. IgG is the most used blood product in clinical practice. IgA is involved in allergic responses, while other types (IgM, IgD, IgE) are less characterized. An immunoglobulin has a Y-shaped structure, with the FAB region (arms) recognizing antigens and the FC region activating plasma cells to remove contaminants. 免疫球蛋白分为五种，IgG约占血浆免疫球蛋白总量的85%，也是临床应用最广泛的血液制品。IgA参与过敏反应，其他类型如IgM、IgD、IgE特征较少。Ig分子为Y型，FAB区（两臂）识别抗原，FC区激活浆细胞清除异物。 1
IVIG 的主要临床用途
免疫替代治疗及免疫调节治疗 The main clinical uses of IVIG are for immunosubstitution (replacing missing antibodies) and immunomodulatory therapy. IVIG临床主要用于免疫替代治疗（补充缺失抗体）和免疫调节治疗。

Babies who do not produce (enough) antibodies require IVIG infusions to prevent infection. Later, bone marrow transplantation may help them generate their own antibodies. 对于不能产生足够抗体的婴儿，要定期通过IVIG注射防止感染，后期可通过骨髓移植恢复自体抗体生成。

Patients with secondary antibody deficiency (e.g. after cancer treatment or diseases impairing immune function) also require IVIG to reduce infection risk. 因肿瘤化疗或疾病导致继发性抗体缺乏的患者，也需使用IVIG降低感染风险。 1

3
IVIG在自身免疫疾病中的调节作用 IVIG is also used in autoimmune diseases, where the immune system attacks the body's own proteins (e.g., platelets or myelin in nerves). Injecting IVIG can help regulate the immune response and reduce the destruction of patient’s own cells. IVIG还用于自身免疫疾病治疗，如免疫系统攻击自身蛋白（如血小板或神经髓鞘）时，注射IVIG可帮助调节免疫反应，减少机体自身细胞被破坏。 1
4
初生免疫耐受性调节及异常 During early postnatal life, T cells that react against self-proteins are normally eliminated (“immune tolerance”). If this process fails, autoantibodies can develop, leading to autoimmune diseases, which may be treated using IVIG. 新生儿早期通过免疫耐受机制清除对自身蛋白产生应答的T细胞，若该过程异常则会出现自身免疫病，可用IVIG治疗。 1
5
IVIG的注射方式及周期 IVIG is typically administered intravenously every 2–4 weeks, mainly in hospitals. IVIG多在医院每两至四周静脉注射一次。
1
免疫球蛋白种类与结构

There are five types of immunoglobulins (IgG, IgA, IgM, IgD, IgE).

IgG accounts for about 85% of the total and is the most used clinically.

IgA participates in allergic reactions; the other types are less characterized.

The immunoglobulin molecule is Y-shaped: FAB region (arms) recognizes antigens; FC region activates plasma cells for clearance.

免疫球蛋白有五种（IgG、IgA、IgM、IgD、IgE）。

IgG约占总量的85%，是临床应用最多的类型。

IgA参与过敏反应，其他类型较少被研究。

Ig分子为Y型，FAB区域识别抗原，FC区激活浆细胞用于清除异物。 1

2
IVIG（静脉注射免疫球蛋白）的临床用途
Main uses
immunosubstitution (for people lacking antibodies) and immunomodulation (for autoimmune diseases).

For infants or patients not producing antibodies, IVIG is infused to prevent infection; bone marrow transplantation can later restore antibody production.

Secondary antibody deficiency (e.g., post-cancer therapy) also requires IVIG.

IVIG is usually infused in hospitals every 2–4 weeks.

IVIG的主要用途包括
免疫替代（用于缺乏抗体的人群）和免疫调节（用于自身免疫病）。

婴儿或无法产生抗体的患者需输注IVIG以防感染，后续可通过骨髓移植恢复自身抗体生成能力。

继发性抗体缺乏（如肿瘤治疗后）同样需要IVIG。

IVIG通常每2-4周在医院静脉注射一次。 1

3
IVIG在自身免疫病中的调节作用

Some diseases are due to the immune system attacking self-proteins (e.g., platelets/myelin).

Injecting high-dose IVIG can help regulate immune response and reduce self-cell destruction, though mechanisms are not fully understood.

某些疾病如免疫系统攻击自身蛋白（如血小板或神经髓鞘），注射大量IVIG可调节免疫反应，减少自体细胞破坏，尽管具体机制尚不完全清楚。 1

4
制药过程与安全措施的强化

After severe incidents (HIV, BSE), plasma drug production adopted virus/prion inactivation steps (including nano-filtration, solvent/detergent treatment).

Solvent/detergent treatment is highly effective for enveloped viruses, but not for non-enveloped viruses like HBV and HCV.

EU regulations now mandate validated processes for inactivation/removal of viruses and prions in plasma drug manufacturing.

严重安全事件（如HIV、疯牛病）后，血浆制品生产中引入了病毒和朊病毒灭活环节（如纳滤、溶剂/去污剂处理）。

溶剂/去污剂法对有包膜病毒灭活高效，对无包膜病毒（如乙肝、丙肝）无效。

欧盟现规定血浆药品制造工艺必须包括病毒和朊病毒的有效灭活或去除步骤。 236

5
大批量生产与分离细节

Fractionation process: After collecting and freezing plasma, stepwise additions of ethanol allow for targeted precipitation and separation of specific proteins (albumin, immunoglobulin, fibrinogen etc).

Purification involves controlled buffer exchanges and nanofiltration to ensure product safety.

分步分离
采集并冷冻血浆后，逐步增加乙醇浓度以实现特定蛋白（如白蛋白、免疫球蛋白、纤维蛋白原）按步骤沉淀分离。

纯化阶段还包括缓冲液更换和纳滤，确保制品安全。

1
新一代IVIG制剂的质量与安全性优化措施

The new IVIG manufacturing process aims to: improve product yield, reduce side effects, and enhance safety (especially against viruses and prion contamination).

新一代IVIG生产流程的目标包括
提高产量、减少副作用、提升安全性（尤其是针对病毒及朊病毒污染的防护）。1
2
副作用与改良方案

Side effects described for previous generations: hypotension, hemolytic anemia (especially in patients with certain blood types), thrombotic events, and renal intolerance.

特别副作用包括
低血压、溶血性贫血（见于特定血型患者）、血栓并发症及肾脏不耐受。1

To reduce these, new steps include: a) Removal of IgA (to lower allergic/anaphylactic risk) b) Removal of coagulation factors (to reduce thrombosis) c) Removal of anti-A/anti-B antibodies (to lower hemolytic risk) d) Minimize vasoactive substances

为减少副作用，新工艺增加： a）去除IgA（降低过敏反应风险） b）去除凝血因子（减少血栓） c）去除抗A/B抗体（降低溶血风险） d）减少血管活性物质 1

3
工艺技术升级——新型沉淀步骤与分离

A new precipitation method using caprylic acid replaces part of the ethanol precipitation, allowing higher yields and more selective contaminant removal.

工艺引入辛酸沉淀法，替代部分乙醇沉淀，提高产率并更好地去除杂质。1

4
病毒与朊病毒去除和灭活措施加强

Process now integrates solvent/detergent (SD) treatment targeting enveloped viruses (like HIV), plus nanofiltration as a physical step for virus/prion removal.

新工艺加入溶剂-去污剂处理（针对有包膜病毒如HIV）以及纳滤（物理法去除病毒和朊病毒）。1

5
层析纯化新环节

Application of ion exchange chromatography: separates IgG from residual IgA and removes solvent/detergent. This step refines the product, achieving high purity IVIG.

应用离子交换层析，从IgG中进一步去除IgA并清除溶剂/去污剂，使IVIG高度纯净。5

6
稳定化和终产品处理

Further steps include acid/pepsin treatment to break down aggregates and enhance product stability, followed by buffer exchange for final formulation.

后续还包括酸/胃蛋白酶处理以消除聚集体、提升稳定性，并进行缓冲液更换最终制成品。9

1. 新一代IVIG生产的目标与副作用控制

EN
The main objectives for new IVIG production are:

To increase yield (produce more IVIG from the same plasma amount, for economies of scale);

To simplify usage for nurses (ready-to-inject liquid formulation);

To maintain the same clinical efficacy as the previous generation;

Most importantly, to further improve safety by reducing side effects:

Lower IgA (reducing allergy risk);

Fewer active coagulation factors (reducing thrombosis risk);

Less anti-A/anti-B antibodies (reducing hemolytic anemia risk);

Less vasoactive substances (lowering hypotension/renal effects) 1.

中
新一代IVIG生产主要目标包括：

提高产率（用同样多的血浆产出更多IVIG、经济优化）；

简化操作流程（直接注射液体制剂，方便护士操作）；

保持与上一代产品相同的临床疗效；

重点在于通过减少副作用进一步提升用药安全：

更低IgA含量（减少过敏风险）；

更少活性凝血因子（降低血栓风险）；

更少抗A/B抗体（降低溶血性贫血风险）；

更少血管活性物质（减轻低血压及肾损害）1。

2. 生产工艺创新——辛酸法沉淀

EN:

The new process replaces some ethanol precipitation steps with caprylic acid (caprylic precipitation), enhancing yield and allowing selective removal of contaminants (especially IgA, active coagulation factors) 1.

中:

新工艺用辛酸沉淀法替代部分乙醇沉淀步骤，使产率更高，并能更有选择性地去除杂质（如IgA、活性凝血因子）1。

3. 病毒和朊病毒安全的强化

EN:

A dual safety system is established: (a) Solvent/detergent (SD) treatment (for enveloped viruses such as HIV) and (b) nanofiltration (physical filtering for viruses/prions much larger than antibodies) 15.

Both SD and nanofiltration are now required for European plasma products.

中:

建立“双重安全”体系
（a）溶剂-去污剂SD处理（针对包膜病毒如HIV），（b）纳滤（利用病毒/朊病毒比抗体大、物理分离）。

在欧盟法规下，两者均为血浆类产品必备工艺。

4. 层析纯化与抗A/B抗体去除

EN:

New affinity and ion-exchange chromatography steps are implemented to separate IgA and anti-A/anti-B antibodies efficiently, resulting in high-purity IVIG that minimizes immune reactions 36.

The process uses synthetic sugar sequences mimicking A/B antigens grafted on chromatography resin to selectively capture and remove anti-A/anti-B immunoglobulins 3.

中:

新增亲和层析与离子交换层析步骤，有效分离IgA及抗A/B抗体，提高IVIG纯度，减少免疫相关不良反应。

工艺使用模拟A/B抗原序列的合成糖基功能化树脂，选择性吸附和去除抗A/B抗体3。

5. 稳定剂与终产品制备

EN:

Final formulation improvements include optimization of excipients for better stability/solubility and use of acid/pepsin treatment to destroy aggregates 9.

Liquid IVIG is now stable without added sugar and does not show increased aggregation or cause pressure drops in patients 3.

中:

最终成品制备优化，调整辅料配方保证溶液稳定及可注射性，并采用酸/胃蛋白酶处理去除聚集体9。

新IVIG液体制剂无须加糖，稳定性更好，未观察到聚集上升及低血压副作用3。

6. 工艺验证与上市进展

EN:

The new process enables compliance with clinical and regulatory safety standards, has been approved in multiple countries, and shows equivalent efficacy in trials; poised for global market expansion 3.

中:

新工艺确保产品满足临床与法规安全标准，已获多国批准并通过疗效等效性试验，正加速全球上市进程3。

112025-11-06

问What is the physiological effect of EPO? What are the indications for using EPO therapy?
问EPO的生理作用是什么？EPO治疗的适应症有哪些？
答
1
EPO的主要生理作用

Main Physiological Effect of EPO

EPO刺激骨髓中红系前体细胞的增殖与分化，促进网织红细胞和成熟红细胞的产生，从而提升外周血红细胞数量和氧输送能力。 EPO stimulates the proliferation and differentiation of erythroid precursor cells in the bone marrow, promoting the production of reticulocytes and mature red blood cells, thereby increasing peripheral RBC count and oxygen delivery.

在正常生理状态下，肾脏检测到缺氧时分泌EPO，调节红细胞生成，维持组织氧合。 Under normal physiology, the kidney secretes EPO in response to hypoxia, regulating erythropoiesis to maintain tissue oxygenation.

2
EPO治疗的适应症

Indications for EPO Therapy

适用于内源性红细胞生成素分泌不足造成的贫血，如慢性肾功能衰竭、炎症相关性贫血、癌症及化疗相关性贫血、慢性病伴发的贫血等。 Indicated for anemias due to insufficient endogenous erythropoietin production, such as chronic renal failure, inflammatory anemia, anemia associated with cancer and chemotherapy, and anemias related to chronic diseases.

主要FDA批准适应症包括：1）慢性肾病相关贫血 2）肿瘤患者化疗引起的贫血。 The main FDA-approved indications are: 1) anemia secondary to chronic kidney disease, and 2) chemotherapy-induced anemia in cancer patients.

其他适应症还包括重大外科手术和骨髓移植诱导的红细胞生成不足。 Other indications include major surgery and bone marrow transplantation leading to insufficient red blood cell production.

3
重组EPO的治疗作用

Therapeutic Role of Recombinant EPO

重组EPO通过增强红细胞生成，有效纠正各种原因引起的贫血。 Recombinant EPO effectively corrects anemia of various causes by stimulating erythropoiesis.

在肾功能损伤时，需外源性补充重组EPO以防止慢性贫血发生。 In cases of kidney damage, external supplementation with recombinant EPO is required to prevent chronic anemia.

4
课堂/老师补充重点

Extra Points from Lecture/Instructor

EPO不仅提高红细胞数量，还带来氧合水平的提升，并可能对巨核细胞有一定作用，影响血小板生成。 EPO increases red blood cell numbers, raises tissue oxygenation, and may also act on megakaryocytes to influence platelet production.

使用EPO需警惕可能的促凝血（血栓）风险。 Monitoring for potential pro-thrombotic risks is crucial when using EPO.

小结 Summary

EPO是调节红细胞生成的关键激素，治疗以纠正各种红细胞生成受损相关的贫血，主要应用于慢性肾病和化疗相关贫血患者。

EPO is a key hormone regulating erythropoiesis, and its therapeutic use is to correct anemias related to impaired red blood cell production—especially in chronic kidney disease and chemotherapy-induced anemia.

1
EPO的结构特征
1
Structural Characteristics of EPO

EPO是一种含有165个氨基酸残基的糖蛋白，单一多肽链结构。 EPO is a glycoprotein of 165 amino acids in a single polypeptide chain.

分子链内含有多个α螺旋，通过二硫键（如图红色虚线所示）稳定三维构象。 Its chain folds into several α-helices, stabilized by disulfide bonds (as shown by the red dashed lines in the figure).

EPO的一级结构中有多个糖基化位点，N端和C端都参与功能结构的形成。 The primary structure has several glycosylation sites, and both N- and C-termini are involved in forming the functional structure.

问2. EPO是否有翻译后修饰？哪些类型？
问2. Does EPO undergo post-translational modifications? Which ones?
答

EPO分子主要进行的翻译后修饰是糖基化，包括三个位于天冬酰胺的N-连接糖基化和一个丝氨酸的O-连接糖基化。 The main post-translational modification is glycosylation: three N-linked glycosylation at asparagine residues, and one O-linked glycosylation at a serine.

糖链种类有N-乙酰葡萄糖胺、甘露糖、半乳糖和N-乙酰神经氨酸等（图例中四种色彩图标）。 The carbohydrate chains include N-acetylglucosamine, mannose, galactose, and N-acetylneuraminic acid (as shown by the four colored symbols in the legend).

多个二硫键形成，进一步稳定蛋白空间结构。 Multiple disulfide bonds are formed to further stabilize the protein’s spatial structure.

这些修饰极大地影响EPO的稳定性、半衰期和生物活性。 These modifications significantly affect EPO’s stability, half-life, and biological activity.

问3. 上述修饰需要哪种宿主细胞？
问3. What type of host cell is required for this post-translational modification?
答

正确的人源化糖基化修饰只能在哺乳动物细胞中实现（下图对比植物、酵母、昆虫和哺乳动物细胞的糖基化结构）。 Correct human-like glycosylation can only be achieved in mammalian cells (the diagram compares glycosylation patterns among plant, yeast, insect, and mammalian cells).

常用的宿主细胞有CHO细胞（中国仓鼠卵巢细胞）、COS、BHK、Namalwa和HeLa等。首选CHO细胞。 Common host cells include CHO (Chinese Hamster Ovary), COS, BHK, Namalwa, and HeLa; CHO cells are preferred.

酵母、昆虫和植物细胞虽然也可以进行糖基化，但结构不同于人类，不利于EPO活性表达；而细菌无法进行糖基化（如图右侧bacteria区空白）。 Yeast, insect, and plant cells can glycosylate proteins but produce non-human glycan structures, impairing EPO activity. Bacteria cannot glycosylate proteins (see empty box in 'bacteria' in the figure).

4
图示要点解读
4
Diagram Interpretation Key Points

主图显示了EPO多肽链的氨基酸序列和三个N-连接糖基化位点、一个O-连接糖基化位点，以及多个二硫键。 The main figure shows the EPO polypeptide sequence with three N-linked glycosylation sites, one O-linked glycosylation site, and several disulfide bonds.

下方小图直观展示了不同生物体系的糖基化能力与类型，说明只有哺乳动物细胞能生成符合人体需求的糖链修饰。 The smaller diagrams below illustrate the differing glycosylation patterns among expression systems, highlighting that only mammalian cells generate human-compatible glycoforms.

1
质粒的特异性结构
1
Specific Structural Features of the Plasmid

该质粒包含人EPO的编码序列（cDNA）。 The plasmid contains the coding sequence of human EPO (cDNA).

具有限制性内切酶切位点，方便插入感兴趣的目标基因序列。 It has restriction enzyme sites to conveniently insert the target gene sequence of interest.

质粒上有细胞巨病毒（CMV）启动子，可实现高水平的外源基因转录表达。 It carries a cytomegalovirus (CMV) promoter, enabling high-level transcription of the adjacent cDNA.

启动子后还有一个CMV 5'非翻译区和内含子，进一步增强基因表达。 Behind the promoter, there is also a CMV 5' untranslated region and intron, further enhancing gene expression.

3'端含有牛生长激素（bGH）polyA尾信号，有效终止mRNA转录并促进稳定性。 At the 3’ end, it includes the bovine growth hormone (bGH) polyadenylation signal for proper termination and transcript stability.

带有抗生素抗性基因（如卡那霉素抗性基因），用于宿主细胞筛选。 It harbors an antibiotic resistance gene (e.g., kanamycin resistance) for selection in host cells.

2
关键功能元件
2
Key Functional Elements

启动子（Promoter）：来自CMV，驱动强表达。 Promoter: Derived from CMV, drives strong expression.

插入位点（Restriction site）：便于插入外源基因。 Restriction site: Facilitates insertion of exogenous genes.

多聚腺苷酸化（Polyadenylation）信号： bGH polyA信号稳定转录本。 Polyadenylation signal: bGH polyA stabilizes transcripts.

筛选标记（Selection gene）：抗生素抗性，便于筛选阳性克隆。 Selection gene: Antibiotic resistance, allows screening for positive clones.

3
文献与课堂补充
3
Literature & Class Highlights

该质粒常用于真核表达系统，对实现EPO等重组蛋白高效分泌和表达至关重要。 This plasmid is commonly used in eukaryotic expression systems and is crucial for the efficient secretion and expression of recombinant proteins like EPO.

CMV启动子保证了异源基因在宿主细胞中持续、高效率表达。 The CMV promoter ensures sustained and high-efficiency expression of exogenous genes in host cells.

bGH polyA尾序列有利于mRNA的成熟和稳定，确保蛋白表达效率。 The bGH polyA tail sequence facilitates mRNA maturation and stability, guaranteeing efficient protein expression.

抗性基因便于通过抗生素筛选得到重组阳性克隆。 The resistance gene allows selection of recombinant positive clones via antibiotic screening.

课件及文献总结 Summary

该表达质粒设计精巧，集成了高效启动子、插入位点、polyA信号和抗性筛选基因等，能够在哺乳动物细胞中高效表达和筛选目标蛋白（如EPO），特别适用于生产生物药物和细胞工程用途。

This expression plasmid is well-designed, integrating a strong promoter, insertion sites, polyA signal, and a resistance gene. It enables efficient expression and selection of target proteins (e.g., EPO) in mammalian cells, making it especially suitable for biopharmaceutical production and cell engineering.

结构特点、翻译后修饰及所需宿主细胞 - 听课笔记（中英对照）

Structural Characteristics, Post-translational Modifications, and Required Host Cell – Class Notes (Chinese-English)

1
EPO的结构特点
1
Structural Features of EPO

EPO是由165个氨基酸组成的糖蛋白，具有单条多肽链。

EPO is a glycoprotein consisting of 165 amino acids, forming a single polypeptide chain.

内含多个α螺旋，并有二硫键稳定结构。

It contains several α-helices and is stabilized by disulfide bonds.

蛋白表面有多个糖基化位点。

There are multiple glycosylation sites on the protein.

2
是否有翻译后修饰及类型
2
Post-translational Modifications and Types

有重要的翻译后修饰，主要是糖基化，包括3个N-糖基化和1个O-糖基化。

Yes, there are critical post-translational modifications, mainly glycosylation: 3 N-linked and 1 O-linked.

糖基化对蛋白的功能和稳定性至关重要。

Glycosylation is vital for protein function and stability.

3
所需宿主细胞
3
Required Host Cell

需要哺乳动物细胞（如CHO细胞）来获得与人体相似的糖基化修饰。

Mammalian cells (like CHO cells) are required to obtain human-like glycosylation.

其他系统（如细菌、酵母等）无法实现相同类型或水平的糖基化。

Other systems (such as bacteria or yeast) cannot achieve the same type or degree of glycosylation.

小结

Summary

EPO结构包含二硫键和糖基化，是糖蛋白。

EPO structure includes disulfide bonds and glycosylation, making it a glycoprotein.

这些修饰必须依赖哺乳动物细胞完成。

These modifications require mammalian cells.

糖基化对于EPO的生物活性和医疗用途非常关键。

Glycosylation is crucial for EPO's biological activity and medical use.

重组载体的构建与验证（简明听课笔记，中英对照）

Construction and Verification of Recombinant Vector (Concise Class Notes, Chinese-English)

一、结构特点 / Structural Features

商业质粒包含强启动子、多克隆位点和筛选标记。 Commercial plasmids contain strong promoters, multiple cloning sites (MCS), and selection markers.

二、克隆步骤 / Cloning Steps

1
用限制性内切酶切开质粒和目的基因。 Cut the plasmid and target gene with restriction enzymes.
2
用T4连接酶连接外源基因和质粒。 Ligate the foreign gene and plasmid using T4 ligase.
3
转化至大肠杆菌感受态细胞。 Transform into competent E. coli cells.
4
抗生素筛选阳性克隆。 Select positive clones with antibiotics.

三、验证方法 / Verification Methods

1
限制性酶切分析 Restriction enzyme digestion analysis
2
PCR鉴定 PCR identification
3
Sanger测序 Sanger sequencing

四、注意事项 / Notes

通常需结合多种方法确认构建正确。 Usually, multiple methods are combined to confirm correct construction.

关键环节包括启动子、抗性基因和正确重复片段。 Key elements include promoter, resistance gene, and correct insert.

五、推荐宿主细胞 / Recommended Host Cells

哺乳动物细胞（如CHO细胞）用于表达带有正确糖基化的蛋白质。 Mammalian cells (such as CHO cells) are used to express proteins with proper glycosylation.

1
表达质粒构建 / Expression Plasmid Construction

制备多个候选表达质粒。 Prepare multiple candidate expression plasmids.

2
转染 / Transfection

外源基因导入宿主细胞（如CHO）。 Introduce gene of interest into host cells (e.g., CHO) via electroporation.

3
主孔板建立 / Master Well Development

转染细胞汇总分装入96孔板，初步筛选高表达孔。 Pool transfected cells, plate into 96-well plates, select top wells for expression.

4
单细胞克隆 / Single Cell Cloning

从优选孔分离单细胞，FACS分选获得单克隆。 Isolate single cells from top wells, use FACS to obtain monoclonal cell lines.

5
建立研究细胞库 / Research Cell Bank (RCB) Setup

挑选克隆建立研究级细胞库（RCB），做初步筛选。 Establish RCBs with selected clones and perform initial screening.

6
RCB检测 / RCB Testing

检测无菌、支原体、病毒等安全性指标。 Test RCBs for sterility, mycoplasma, and viral contaminants.

7
确定主导克隆与备选克隆 / Lead and Back-up Clone Selection

综合分值筛选主克隆和备选克隆（产量、稳定性等）。 Select lead and back-up clones based on yield, stability, and quality.

8
建立主细胞库 / Master Cell Bank Setup

以主克隆制备主细胞库（MCB），用于GMP生产。 Prepare Master Cell Bank (MCB) from lead clone for GMP production.

9
细胞库质量控制和储存 / Cell Bank QC & Storage

进行质控，合格后低温冻存，可全球运输。 Perform QC; after passing, store cryogenically and distribute as needed.

生物反应器需要控制的参数（中英对照）

Parameters to be Controlled in Bioreactors (Chinese-English)

1
温度（通过温度探头 Thermocouple）
1
Temperature (via thermocouple):

控制细胞最佳生长和产物表达，温度影响代谢反应速率。

Maintain optimal cell growth and product expression; temperature affects metabolic rates.14

2
pH值（通过pH传感器）
2
pH Value (via pH sensor):

细胞生长、代谢及蛋白表达都对pH高度敏感，需要及时调节酸/碱。

Cell growth, metabolism, and protein expression are highly sensitive to pH, requiring acid/base adjustment.14

3
溶解氧（DO）（通过溶解氧传感器）
3
Dissolved Oxygen (DO) (via dissolved O₂ sensor):

充分供氧保障有氧代谢，避免细胞因缺氧死亡或产量下降。

Adequate oxygen supply ensures aerobic metabolism, prevents cell death or productivity loss from hypoxia.14

4
液位（通过液位传感器）
4
Liquid Level (via level sensor):

保证营养/反应物稳定供应，也避免过量泡沫或溢出。

Ensures stable supply of nutrients/reagents and prevents excess foam or overflow.14

5
通气与搅拌速率（进气流量、搅拌器）
5
Aeration & Agitation (Inlet flow, agitator):

调节进气量和搅拌速率，保证氧气和营养均匀分布，防止局部养分或氧气不足。

Control air flow and agitation speed for even oxygen/nutrient distribution and to avoid local depletion.14

6
泡沫控制（加消泡剂）
6
Foam Control (addition of antifoam):

泡沫过多会堵塞传感器或引起污染，需定期添加消泡剂。

Excess foam can block sensors or cause contamination, so antifoam is added as needed.14

7
新鲜培养基/出料流速
7
Fresh Media Feed / Exit Liquid Flow:

调节进料或出料速率，维持细胞生长所需营养物浓度和废物清除。

Adjust feed/outflow rates to maintain required nutrient levels and remove waste.14

8
气体出口与废气调控
8
Exit Gas Flow and Off-gas Control:

排出二氧化碳及代谢废气，防止反应器内有害气体积累。

Expel CO₂ and metabolic waste gases to prevent accumulation of harmful gases in the reactor.14

补充说明（结合课堂讲解要点）

Additional Notes (with lecture highlights):

老师强调，所有这些参数都要自动化精准监控，随细胞代谢情况实时调节，以维持生物反应系统的最优稳定状态。 The instructor stressed that all these parameters must be precisely and automatically monitored and adjusted in real time according to cellular metabolism, ensuring optimal and stable bioprocessing.14

搅拌和通气方式需根据细胞类型和培养规模灵活选择，悬浮培养与贴壁培养所用设备不同。 Agitation and aeration modes must be tailored to cell type and culture scale; equipment differs for suspension versus adherent cultures.14

问Fed-batch是什么意思？ / What does fed-batch mean?
答
1
基本定义 / Basic Definition

Fed-batch是一种补料分批培养工艺，在细胞或微生物培养过程中，随着培养的进行，定时或持续地向反应器中补充新鲜底物（营养物），但一般不移出培养液，直到培养结束一次性收获。

Fed-batch is a type of bioprocess where fresh substrate (nutrients) is periodically or continuously added to the reactor during cell or microbial cultivation, but generally, the culture broth is not removed until harvest at the end.14

2
与批次和连续培养的对比 / Comparison with Batch and Continuous Cultures

批次培养：只在开始时加入底物，底物被消耗后细胞生长停止，代谢产物积累。 Batch: Substrate is only added at the start; after depletion, cell growth stops and metabolic byproducts accumulate.

Fed-batch培养：持续/定期补充底物，细胞持续生长，能获得更高密度。 Fed-batch: Substrate is fed continuously or periodically, allowing cells to keep growing and reach higher densities.

连续/灌流培养：底物持续供给，同时等量取出产物和/或细胞。 Continuous/perfusion: Substrate is continuously supplied and an equal amount of culture (product and/or cells) is withdrawn.

3
主要特点 / Main Features

可以提高细胞密度和产物产量。 Can increase cell density and product yield.

便于自动化控制和生产放大。 Easier for automation and scale-up.

易积累生物产品。 Facilitates bioproduct accumulation.

但代谢废物可能积累，需合理设计补料方案。 However, toxic metabolites may still accumulate if feed strategy is not well designed.14

4
教师讲解要点 / Teacher’s Lecture Highlights

老师强调fed-batch是实际生产中最常用的模式之一，既能避免批次培养底物耗尽，又较为经济、便于操作与放大。 The teacher emphasized that fed-batch is one of the most widely used modes in industry; it avoids nutrient exhaustion in batch mode, and is more cost-effective and operable for scale-up.14

主要风险是底物补料方案需优化，避免环境剧变或抑制物积累。 Main risks relate to feed strategy optimization to avoid sudden environmental changes or toxic byproduct accumulation.14

在fed-batch过程中，培养体积可能会随着补料而缓慢增加。 During fed-batch, the culture volume may slowly increase as substrate is fed in.17

适用于产量高、对营养和代谢状态要求严格的生物制品（如重组蛋白）。 Useful for high yield bioproducts that need precise nutrient and metabolic control (such as recombinant proteins).14

5
图解内容简析 / Figure Explanation

图中fed-batch曲线显示底物浓度通过补料保持较稳定，细胞数目持续上升。 The fed-batch curve in the figure shows substrate concentration stabilized by feeding, and the cell number continually increases.

优点：更易控制环境、防止底物耗竭、产物得率高。 Advantages: easier environmental control, prevention of substrate depletion, higher product yield.

简明总结 / Summary

Fed-batch是一种常用的、通过补充营养底物、使细胞持续高密度生长但不实时取出产物的生物工艺，既适合实验室也适合工业大规模生产。

Fed-batch is a commonly used bioprocess where nutrients are supplemented, allowing continuous high-density cell growth without immediate product withdrawal; it suits both laboratory and industrial scale production.
